            payload["model_snapshot_issue"] = "；".join(
                text for text in issues if text
            ) or None
    return payload


def _working_copy_payload(row) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "id": row.id,
        "workflow_definition_id": row.workflow_definition_id,
        "base_workflow_version_id": row.base_workflow_version_id,
        "manifest": json.loads(row.manifest_json),
        "lock_version": row.lock_version,
        "updated_by": row.updated_by,
        "updated_at": row.updated_at,
    }


def _reject_direct_v3_write(manifest: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    if manifest.get("schema_version") == "workflow-v3":
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=409,
            detail={
                "code": "workflow_v3_working_copy_required",
                "message": "workflow-v3 必须通过工作副本生成不可变评测机制版本",
            },
        )


def _reject_v3_lifecycle(row: WorkflowVersion) -> None:
    if row.workflow_schema_version == "workflow-v3":
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=409,
            detail={
                "code": "workflow_v3_lifecycle_not_supported",
                "message": "评测机制版本没有草稿、发布或退役状态",
            },
        )


def build_workflow_registry_router(
    require_user: Callable[..., Any],
) -> APIRouter:
    router = APIRouter(prefix="/api/workflows", tags=["workflow-registry"])

    @router.get("/catalog/node-kinds")
    def composer_catalog(
        _user: User = Depends(require_user),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        configs = {
            "upstream_connector": ["connector_key"],
        "prompt": ["asset_key", "prompt_ref"],
        "foreach_image": [
            "items_path",
            "item_identity_path",
            "max_items",
            "max_parallel_items",
            "failure_policy",
            "item_executor",
        ],
        "aggregate_case": [
            "foreach_node_id",
            "included_statuses",
            "score_path",
            "feature_paths",
            "empty_policy",
            "failure_projection",
        ],
            "rule_contract": ["asset_key", "contract_ref"],
            "level_matcher": ["asset_key", "matcher_ref"],
            "downstream_projection": ["connector_key"],
            "branch": ["branch_key", "branches"],
            "join": ["join_for", "mode"],
        }
        return {
            "items": [
                {"kind": kind, "required_config": configs[kind]}
                for kind in sorted(COMPOSER_STEP_KINDS)
            ],
            "run_modes": sorted(COMPOSER_RUN_MODES),
            "schema_version": "workflow-v2",
            "executor_policy": "deterministic_fixture_only",
        }

    @router.post("/compose", status_code=201)
    def compose_first_version(
        payload: WorkflowComposeWrite,
        user: User = Depends(require_user),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """Atomically create one definition and its first immutable version."""

        _require_write(user)
        _reject_direct_v3_write(payload.version.manifest)
        try:
            definition = create_workflow_definition(
                db,
                workflow_key=payload.definition.workflow_key,
                name=payload.definition.name,
                description=payload.definition.description,
                owner=payload.definition.owner,
                allowed_categories=payload.definition.allowed_categories,
                created_by=user.username,
            )
            version = create_workflow_version(
                db,
                definition=definition,
                version=payload.version.version,
                manifest=payload.version.manifest,
                created_by=user.username,
            )
            db.commit()
            db.refresh(definition)
            db.refresh(version)
            return {
                "definition": _definition_payload(definition),
                "version": _version_payload(version),
            }
        except WorkflowRegistryError as exc:
            db.rollback()
            raise _http_error(exc) from exc

    @router.post("/{workflow_key}/validate-manifest")
    def validate_draft_manifest(
        workflow_key: str,
        payload: WorkflowManifestValidationWrite,
        user: User = Depends(require_user),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        del workflow_key
        report = validate_managed_workflow_manifest(
            db,
            payload.manifest,
            actor=user.username,
        )
        return {"validation_report": report.as_dict()}

    @router.get("/")
    def list_workflows(
        _user: User = Depends(require_user),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        rows = db.scalars(
            select(WorkflowDefinition).order_by(
                WorkflowDefinition.updated_at.desc(),
                WorkflowDefinition.id.desc(),
            )
        ).all()
        return {"items": [_definition_payload(row) for row in rows]}

    @router.post("/", status_code=201)
    def create_definition(
        payload: WorkflowDefinitionWrite,
        user: User = Depends(require_user),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        _require_write(user)
        try:
            row = create_workflow_definition(
                db,
                workflow_key=payload.workflow_key,
                name=payload.name,
                description=payload.description,
                owner=payload.owner,
                allowed_categories=payload.allowed_categories,
                created_by=user.username,
            )
            db.commit()
            db.refresh(row)
            return _definition_payload(row)
        except WorkflowRegistryError as exc:
            db.rollback()
            raise _http_error(exc) from exc

    @router.get("/{workflow_key}/versions")
    def list_versions(
        workflow_key: str,
        _user: User = Depends(require_user),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        try:
            definition = _definition(db, workflow_key)
        except WorkflowRegistryError as exc:
            raise _http_error(exc) from exc
        rows = db.scalars(
            select(WorkflowVersion)
            .where(WorkflowVersion.workflow_definition_id == definition.id)
            .order_by(WorkflowVersion.created_at.desc(), WorkflowVersion.id.desc())
        ).all()
        return {"items": [_version_payload(row, db=db) for row in rows]}

    @router.get("/{workflow_key}/editable-template")
    def get_editable_template(
        workflow_key: str,
        _user: User = Depends(require_user),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        try:
            definition = _definition(db, workflow_key)
            template = editable_workflow_template(db, definition.id)
            return {
                "manifest": template.manifest,
                "source_workflow_version_id": template.source_workflow_version_id,
                "compatibility_applied": template.compatibility_applied,
            }
        except WorkflowRegistryError as exc:
            raise _http_error(exc) from exc
        except WorkflowWorkingCopyError as exc:
            raise _working_copy_error(exc) from exc

    @router.get("/{workflow_key}/working-copy")
    def get_working_copy(
        workflow_key: str,
        _user: User = Depends(require_user),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        try:
            definition = _definition(db, workflow_key)
            return _working_copy_payload(
                require_workflow_working_copy(db, definition.id)
            )
        except WorkflowRegistryError as exc:
            raise _http_error(exc) from exc
        except WorkflowWorkingCopyError as exc:
            raise _working_copy_error(exc) from exc

    @router.put("/{workflow_key}/working-copy")
    def put_working_copy(
        workflow_key: str,
        payload: WorkflowWorkingCopyWrite,
        user: User = Depends(require_user),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        _require_write(user)
        try:
            definition = _definition(db, workflow_key)
            if payload.expected_lock_version == 0:
                try:
                    require_workflow_working_copy(db, definition.id)
                except WorkflowWorkingCopyError as exc:
                    if exc.code != "workflow_working_copy_not_found":
                        raise
                    row = get_or_create_workflow_working_copy(
                        db,
                        workflow_definition_id=definition.id,
                        initial_manifest=payload.manifest,
                        actor=user.username,
                    )
                else:
                    raise WorkflowWorkingCopyError(
                        "workflow_working_copy_conflict",
                        "工作流工作副本已经存在，请刷新后保存",
                        status_code=409,
                    )
            else:
                row = save_workflow_working_copy(
                    db,
                    workflow_definition_id=definition.id,
                    expected_lock_version=payload.expected_lock_version,
                    manifest=payload.manifest,
                    actor=user.username,
                )
            db.commit()
            db.refresh(row)
            return _working_copy_payload(row)
        except WorkflowRegistryError as exc:
            db.rollback()
            raise _http_error(exc) from exc
        except WorkflowWorkingCopyError as exc:
            db.rollback()
            raise _working_copy_error(exc) from exc

    @router.post("/{workflow_key}/working-copy/validate")
    def validate_working_copy(
        workflow_key: str,
        user: User = Depends(require_user),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        _require_write(user)
        try:
            definition = _definition(db, workflow_key)
            row = require_workflow_working_copy(db, definition.id)
            errors = validate_managed_workflow_v3(
                db, json.loads(row.manifest_json)
            )
            return {
                "validation_report": {
                    "ok": not errors,
                    "errors": [item.as_dict() for item in errors],
                }
            }
        except WorkflowRegistryError as exc:
            raise _http_error(exc) from exc
        except WorkflowWorkingCopyError as exc:
            raise _working_copy_error(exc) from exc

    @router.post("/{workflow_key}/generate-version", status_code=201)
    def generate_version_from_working_copy(
        workflow_key: str,
        payload: WorkflowGenerateVersionWrite,
        user: User = Depends(require_user),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        _require_mechanism_generation(user)
        try:
            definition = _definition(db, workflow_key)
            row = generate_evaluation_mechanism_version(
                db,
                workflow_definition_id=definition.id,
                expected_lock_version=payload.expected_lock_version,
                created_by=user.username,
            )
            db.commit()
            db.refresh(row)
            return _version_payload(row)
        except WorkflowRegistryError as exc:
            db.rollback()
            raise _http_error(exc) from exc
        except WorkflowWorkingCopyError as exc:
            db.rollback()
            raise _working_copy_error(exc) from exc

    @router.post("/{workflow_key}/versions", status_code=201)
    def create_version(
        workflow_key: str,
        payload: WorkflowVersionWrite,
        user: User = Depends(require_user),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        _require_write(user)
        _reject_direct_v3_write(payload.manifest)
        try:
            definition = _definition(db, workflow_key)
            row = create_workflow_version(
                db,
                definition=definition,
                version=payload.version,
                manifest=payload.manifest,
                created_by=user.username,
            )
            db.commit()
            db.refresh(row)
            return _version_payload(row)
        except WorkflowRegistryError as exc:
            db.rollback()
            raise _http_error(exc) from exc

    @router.get("/{workflow_key}/versions/{version}")
    def get_version(
        workflow_key: str,
        version: str,
        _user: User = Depends(require_user),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        try:
            definition = _definition(db, workflow_key)
            return _version_payload(_version(db, definition.id, version))
        except WorkflowRegistryError as exc:
            raise _http_error(exc) from exc

    @router.post("/{workflow_key}/versions/{version}/validate")
    def validate_version(
        workflow_key: str,
        version: str,
        user: User = Depends(require_user),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        _require_write(user)
        try:
            definition = _definition(db, workflow_key)
            row = _version(db, definition.id, version)
        except WorkflowRegistryError as exc:
            raise _http_error(exc) from exc
        _reject_v3_lifecycle(row)
        if row.status not in {"validating", "blocked"}:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=409,
                detail={
                    "code": "workflow_validation_state_invalid",
                    "message": "只有 validating 或 blocked 版本可以校验",
                },
            )
        report = validate_workflow_manifest(db, persisted_workflow_manifest(row))
        row.validation_report_json = json.dumps(
            report.as_dict(),
            ensure_ascii=False,
            sort_keys=True,
            separators=(",", ":"),
        )
        if not report.ok:
            row.status = "blocked"
        db.commit()
        db.refresh(row)
        return _version_payload(row)

    @router.post("/{workflow_key}/versions/{version}/transition")
    def transition_version(
        workflow_key: str,
        version: str,
        payload: WorkflowTransitionWrite,
        user: User = Depends(require_user),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        _require_write(user)
        try:
            definition = _definition(db, workflow_key)
            row = _version(db, definition.id, version)
            _reject_v3_lifecycle(row)
            transition_workflow_version(
                db,
                row.id,
                payload.target_status,
                actor=user.username,
            )
            db.commit()
            db.refresh(row)
            return _version_payload(row)
        except WorkflowRegistryError as exc:
            db.rollback()
            raise _http_error(exc) from exc

    return router

===== FILE backend/app/workflow_result_contract.py =====
"""Canonical result helpers for native workflow node execution."""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from typing import Any, Mapping

from .api_datetime_encoding import platform_isoformat
from .audit import canonical_json


def hash_manifest(value: Any) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(canonical_json(value).encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()


def find_evidence_value(value: Any, field: str) -> tuple[bool, Any]:
    if isinstance(value, Mapping):
        if field in value:
            return True, value[field]
        for child in value.values():
            found, result = find_evidence_value(child, field)
            if found:
                return True, result
    elif isinstance(value, list):
        for child in value:
            found, result = find_evidence_value(child, field)
            if found:
                return True, result
    return False, None


def canonical_node_result(
    *,
    node: Mapping[str, Any],
    input_manifest: Mapping[str, Any],
    input_fingerprint: str,
    output: Mapping[str, Any],
    attempt_no: int,
    started_at: datetime | None,
    extra: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    config = node.get("config")
    config = config if isinstance(config, Mapping) else {}
    component = config.get("component_snapshot")
    component = component if isinstance(component, Mapping) else {}
    component_version_id = config.get("prompt_version_id")
    if component_version_id is None:
        component_version_id = config.get("rule_module_version_id")
    upstream = input_manifest.get("upstream")
    upstream_refs = sorted(upstream) if isinstance(upstream, Mapping) else []
    evidence: dict[str, Any] = {}
    evidence_fields = node.get("evidence_fields")
    if isinstance(evidence_fields, list):
        for field in evidence_fields:
            if not isinstance(field, str):
                continue
            found, value = find_evidence_value(input_manifest, field)
            if found:
                evidence[field] = value
    finished_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    result: dict[str, Any] = {
        "schema_version": "workflow-node-result-v1",
        "node_instance_id": node.get("node_instance_id"),
        "node_kind": node.get("kind"),
        "status": "succeeded",
        "input_fingerprint": input_fingerprint,
        "upstream_refs": upstream_refs,
        "component_version_id": component_version_id,
        "component_hash": component.get("content_hash"),
        "model_snapshot": (
            dict(config["model_snapshot"])
            if isinstance(config.get("model_snapshot"), Mapping)
            else None
        ),
        "module_type_key": config.get("module_type_key"),
        "output": dict(output),
        "evidence": evidence,
        "attempt": attempt_no,
        "started_at": platform_isoformat(started_at or finished_at),
        "finished_at": platform_isoformat(finished_at),
    }
    if extra:
        result.update(dict(extra))
    return result

===== FILE backend/app/workflow_result_finalizer.py =====
"""Finalize successful workflow-v3 runs into existing evaluation read models."""

from __future__ import annotations
from .workflow_snapshot_store import read_run_snapshot_text

import json
import logging
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from typing import Any, Mapping

from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .audit import canonical_json
from .evaluation_component_catalog import MODULE_TYPE_KEYS
from .evaluation_mechanism_migration import (
    LEGACY_FIELD_OWNERS,
    LegacyModuleSlices,
    project_modules_to_legacy_bundle,
)
from .evaluation_v3_pipeline import V3PipelineError, recompute_qualified_v3
from .inspiration_metadata_generation import metadata_node_ids

    Asset,
    BaselineRegressionItem,
    BaselineRegressionRun,
    EvaluationJob,
    EvaluationResult,
    ProductionRun,
    ProductionStepAttempt,
)
