    ShortCircuitError,
    enabled_levels_from_manifest,
    short_circuit_decision_from_payload,
    short_circuit_decision_payload,
)


logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

_AUTHORITATIVE_LEVEL_CATEGORIES = frozenset(
    {"inspiration_image", "model_3d_su"}
)


class NativeResultFinalizationError(ValueError):
    def __init__(self, code: str, message: str):
        super().__init__(message)
        self.code = code


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class NativeEvaluationFact:
    normalized_result: dict[str, Any]
    result_hash: str
    workflow_version_id: int
    run_id: int
    node_outputs: dict[str, dict[str, Any]]
    evaluation_job_id: int
    evaluation_result_id: int


def _json_object(value: str | None, *, code: str, message: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    try:
        parsed = json.loads(value or "{}")
    except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
        raise NativeResultFinalizationError(code, message) from exc
    if not isinstance(parsed, dict):
        raise NativeResultFinalizationError(code, message)
    return parsed


def _latest_node_outputs(
    db: Session,
    run: ProductionRun,
) -> dict[str, dict[str, Any]]:
    attempts = db.scalars(
        select(ProductionStepAttempt)
        .where(ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == run.id)
        .order_by(
            ProductionStepAttempt.step_key,
            ProductionStepAttempt.attempt_no.desc(),
        )
    ).all()
    latest: dict[str, ProductionStepAttempt] = {}
    for attempt in attempts:
        latest.setdefault(attempt.step_key, attempt)
    if not latest or any(
        attempt.status not in {"succeeded", "skipped"}
        for attempt in latest.values()
    ):
        raise NativeResultFinalizationError(
            "native_run_node_outputs_incomplete",
            "原生运行缺少完整的节点终态证据",
        )
    node_outputs: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
    for step_key, attempt in sorted(
        latest.items(), key=lambda item: (item[1].sequence, item[0])
    ):
        output = _json_object(
            attempt.output_manifest_json,
            code="native_run_node_output_invalid",
            message=f"节点 {step_key} 的冻结输出损坏",
        )
        node_outputs[step_key] = output
    return node_outputs


def _terminal_node_ids(manifest: Mapping[str, Any]) -> list[str]:
    nodes = manifest.get("nodes")
    edges = manifest.get("edges")
    if not isinstance(nodes, list) or not isinstance(edges, list):
        return []
    ids = [
        node.get("node_instance_id")
        for node in nodes
        if isinstance(node, Mapping)
        and isinstance(node.get("node_instance_id"), str)
    ]
    sources = {
        edge.get("from")
        for edge in edges
        if isinstance(edge, Mapping) and isinstance(edge.get("from"), str)
    }
    return [node_id for node_id in ids if node_id not in sources]


def _business_output(node_result: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    if node_result.get("status") != "succeeded":
        return None
    output = node_result.get("output")
    if not isinstance(output, Mapping):
        return None
    normalized = output.get("normalized_result")
    if isinstance(normalized, Mapping):
        return dict(normalized)
    return dict(output)


def _reduce_native_result(
    manifest: Mapping[str, Any],
    node_outputs: Mapping[str, Mapping[str, Any]],
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    for node_id in _terminal_node_ids(manifest):
        result = node_outputs.get(node_id)
        if isinstance(result, Mapping):
            output = _business_output(result)
            if output is not None and "normalized_result" in (
                result.get("output") if isinstance(result.get("output"), Mapping) else {}
            ):
                return output

    nodes = manifest.get("nodes")
    nodes = nodes if isinstance(nodes, list) else []
    by_id = {
        node.get("node_instance_id"): node
        for node in nodes
        if isinstance(node, Mapping)
        and isinstance(node.get("node_instance_id"), str)
    }
    for node_id, result in reversed(list(node_outputs.items())):
        node = by_id.get(node_id)
        if not isinstance(node, Mapping) or not isinstance(result, Mapping):
            continue
        output = _business_output(result)
        if output is None:
            continue
        config = node.get("config")
        config = config if isinstance(config, Mapping) else {}
        if result.get("output_contract_key") or isinstance(
            config.get("output_contract_snapshot"), Mapping
        ):
            return output
        if node.get("kind") == "rule_module" and (
            "level" in output or "score" in output
        ):
            return output
        if node.get("kind") == "prompt" and output and "fixture" not in output:
            return output

    terminal_outputs = {
        node_id: output
        for node_id in _terminal_node_ids(manifest)
        if isinstance((result := node_outputs.get(node_id)), Mapping)
        and (output := _business_output(result)) is not None
    }
    if not terminal_outputs:
        raise NativeResultFinalizationError(
            "native_run_result_missing",
            "原生运行没有可归并的最终结果",
        )
    if len(terminal_outputs) == 1:
        return next(iter(terminal_outputs.values()))
    return {"route_results": terminal_outputs}


def _knowledge_graph_result(
    manifest: Mapping[str, Any],
    node_outputs: Mapping[str, Mapping[str, Any]],
) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    nodes = manifest.get("nodes")
    if not isinstance(nodes, list):
        return None
    for node in nodes:
        if not isinstance(node, Mapping) or node.get("kind") != "prompt":
            continue
        config = node.get("config")
        config = config if isinstance(config, Mapping) else {}
        if not isinstance(config.get("output_contract_snapshot"), Mapping):
            continue
        node_id = node.get("node_instance_id")
        result = node_outputs.get(str(node_id))
        if isinstance(result, Mapping):
            output = _business_output(result)
            if output is not None:
                return output
    return None


def _short_circuit_level_result(
    manifest: Mapping[str, Any],
    node_outputs: Mapping[str, Mapping[str, Any]],
) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    by_rule: dict[str, tuple[int, Any]] = {}
    metadata_ids = metadata_node_ids(manifest)
    for node_id, result in node_outputs.items():
        if node_id in metadata_ids:
            continue
        if not isinstance(result, Mapping):
            continue
        payload = result.get("short_circuit_decision")
        if not isinstance(payload, Mapping):
            continue
        try:
            decision = short_circuit_decision_from_payload(payload)
        except ShortCircuitError as exc:
            raise NativeResultFinalizationError(
                exc.code, f"冻结短路决定损坏：{exc}"
            ) from exc
        priority = decision.evidence.get("priority", 0)
        if isinstance(priority, bool) or not isinstance(priority, int):
            raise NativeResultFinalizationError(
                "short_circuit_decision_invalid", "冻结短路决定缺少整数优先级"
            )
        previous = by_rule.get(decision.rule_key)
        if previous is not None:
            previous_priority, previous_decision = previous
            if (
                previous_priority != priority
                or previous_decision.target_level != decision.target_level
                or previous_decision.rule_version != decision.rule_version
            ):
                raise NativeResultFinalizationError(
                    "short_circuit_rule_key_conflict",
                    "同一短路规则键出现不一致的冻结决定，必须转人工复核",
                )
            continue
        by_rule[decision.rule_key] = (priority, decision)
    if not by_rule:
        return None
    valid_levels = enabled_levels_from_manifest(manifest)
    ranked = sorted(
        by_rule.values(), key=lambda item: (-item[0], item[1].rule_key)
    )
    highest = ranked[0][0]
    winners = [item[1] for item in ranked if item[0] == highest]
    levels = {decision.target_level for decision in winners}
    if any(level not in valid_levels for level in levels):
        raise NativeResultFinalizationError(
            "short_circuit_target_level_invalid",
            "冻结短路决定的等级不在品类等级表中",
        )
    if len(levels) > 1:
        raise NativeResultFinalizationError(
            "short_circuit_conflict",
            "同优先级短路决定给出不同等级，必须转人工复核",
        )
    winner = winners[0]
    payload = short_circuit_decision_payload(winner)
    return {
        "level": winner.target_level,
        "score": None,
        "confidence": None,
        "needs_review": False,
        "server_authoritative": True,
        "decision_source": "frozen_short_circuit",
        "short_circuit_decision": payload,
        "evidence": {"short_circuit_decision": payload},
    }


def _bonus_cap_shaped(payload: Mapping[str, Any]) -> bool:
    """判断调用 B 输出是否为规则命中/纯基础分合同形状（v15 起的正文系）。

    最低要求：0-100 整数的 aesthetic_score + dimensions 对象。红线短路标记
    也放行——短路素材在重算引擎第一步的红线判定就会终结，不会读维度。
    """
    if payload.get("skipped_by_redline") is True:
        return True
    score = payload.get("aesthetic_score")
    if isinstance(score, bool) or not isinstance(score, int):
        return False
    if not 0 <= score <= 100:
        return False
    return isinstance(payload.get("dimensions"), Mapping)



# 调用 B（美感基座）严格合同允许的顶层字段；单提示词输出里其余字段都属于调用 A。
_SINGLE_PROMPT_B_FIELDS: frozenset[str] = frozenset(
    {
        "contract_version",
        "aesthetic_score",
        "dimensions",
        "overall_evidence",
        "confidence",
        "source_deductions",
        "overall_aesthetic",
    }
)


_SINGLE_PROMPT_A_MARKERS: frozenset[str] = frozenset(
    {"redline_triggered", "track_classification", "decisive_evidence", "hard_defects"}
)


def _is_merged_single_prompt_output(payload: Mapping[str, Any]) -> bool:
    """只有同时带前检标志字段与美感基座字段的输出才是 A+B 并集，需要拆分。"""
    keys = set(payload)
    return bool(keys & _SINGLE_PROMPT_A_MARKERS) and bool(
        keys & {"contract_version", "aesthetic_score"}
    )


def split_single_prompt_output(
    payload: Mapping[str, Any],
) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], dict[str, Any]]:
    """把单提示词的 A+B 并集输出拆成前检段与美感段。

    只按字段归属拆分，不改写任何值；没有 B 段字段时美感段为空字典，
    让收尾按"缺少调用 B 结果"的既有路径处理。
    """
    precheck = {
        key: value for key, value in payload.items() if key not in _SINGLE_PROMPT_B_FIELDS
    }
    dimensions = {
        key: value for key, value in payload.items() if key in _SINGLE_PROMPT_B_FIELDS
    }
    return precheck, dimensions

def _foundation_shaped(payload: Mapping[str, Any]) -> bool:
    """判断调用 B 输出是否为老基座的冻结八维 grade 合同形状。

    合同里残留 aesthetic_foundation 键不代表运营在用基座管线：rev51 起的修订
    一路带着这个历史块，而运营现役的 v24-v29 锚点正文全部输出纯基础分形状
    （aesthetic_score + dimensions{hit_rules} + aesthetic_evidence）。按键存在
    路由会把这些输出全部送进 grade 校验——run137 里 79 条 B 成功的素材因此
    在收尾被「顶层字段不符合冻结合同」拒绝，只有不依赖 B 的红线 L5 活了下来。
    路由必须看输出的实际形状。
    """
    if "contract_version" in payload:
        return True
    dimensions = payload.get("dimensions")
    if not isinstance(dimensions, Mapping) or not dimensions:
        return False
    return all(
        isinstance(item, Mapping) and "grade" in item
        for item in dimensions.values()
    )


def _is_documented_legacy_chain(manifest: Mapping[str, Any]) -> bool:
    policy = manifest.get("resource_policy")
    if not isinstance(policy, Mapping):
        return False
    return policy.get("legacy_semantics") == "documented_php_chain"


def _classification_rule_decision(
    manifest: Mapping[str, Any],
    module_slices: Mapping[str, Mapping[str, Any]],
    precheck: Any,
) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    """分类定级表（规则模块）命中固定等级 → 规则直接定级，不进美感打分。

    与冻结短路决定同形（short_circuit_decision），只是 decision_source 标明来自规则模块；
    运行时若提示词节点已按本表短路，会先被 _short_circuit_level_result 接住，这里是规则层的兜底与权威。
    """
    content = module_slices.get("classification_level_map")
    if not isinstance(content, Mapping) or not isinstance(precheck, Mapping):
        return None
    from .classification_level_map import resolve_classification_level

    hit = resolve_classification_level(content, precheck)
    if hit is None or not hit.get("level"):
        return None
    level = str(hit["level"])
    if level not in enabled_levels_from_manifest(manifest):
        raise NativeResultFinalizationError(
            "classification_level_invalid",
            f"分类定级表给出的等级 {level} 不在品类等级表中",
        )
    payload = {
        "schema_version": "short-circuit-decision-v1",
        "rule_key": hit["rule_key"],
        "rule_version": "classification-level-map-v1",
        "target_level": level,
        "skipped_nodes": [],
        "evidence": {
            "priority": 700,
            "matched_row": hit["row"],
            "group": hit["group"],
            "source": "rule_module:classification_level_map",
        },
    }
    return {
        "level": level,
        "score": None,
        "confidence": None,
        "needs_review": False,
        "server_authoritative": True,
        "decision_source": "rule_module:classification_level_map",
        "classification_group": hit["group"],
        "track_key": hit.get("track_key"),
        "short_circuit_decision": payload,
        "evidence": {"short_circuit_decision": payload},
    }


def _vote_sample_outputs(node_result: Mapping[str, Any]) -> list[dict[str, Any]] | None:
    """提示词节点多次采样时，执行器留在 vote.sample_outputs 里的逐样本校准字段。"""
    invocation = node_result.get("model_invocation")
    vote = invocation.get("vote") if isinstance(invocation, Mapping) else None
    if not isinstance(vote, Mapping):
        vote = node_result.get("vote")
    if not isinstance(vote, Mapping):
        return None
    samples = vote.get("sample_outputs")
    if not isinstance(samples, list) or len(samples) < 2:
        return None
    if not all(isinstance(sample, Mapping) for sample in samples):
        return None
    return [dict(sample) for sample in samples]


def _authoritative_level_result(
    manifest: Mapping[str, Any],
    node_outputs: Mapping[str, Mapping[str, Any]],
    *,
    model_execution_mode: str,
    calibration: tuple[dict, dict] | None = None,
) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    category_key = manifest.get("category_key")
    if category_key not in _AUTHORITATIVE_LEVEL_CATEGORIES:
        return None
    if _is_documented_legacy_chain(manifest):
        # 旧豆包链路（upstream → 单提示词 → downstream，无任何规则模块）按文档化的
        # PHP 旧链语义原样投影模型输出，不做原生等级归并；否则 137 run 193 那样
        # 直接报「缺少规则模块」。
        return None
    nodes = manifest.get("nodes")
    if not isinstance(nodes, list):
        raise NativeResultFinalizationError(
            "native_authoritative_manifest_invalid",
            "原生等级评测工作流缺少节点定义",
        )
    module_slices: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
    prompt_outputs: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
    prompt_vote_outputs: dict[str, list[dict[str, Any]]] = {}
    ordered_prompt_outputs: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    metadata_ids = metadata_node_ids(manifest)
    for node in nodes:
        if not isinstance(node, Mapping):
            continue
        node_id = node.get("node_instance_id")
        if node_id in metadata_ids:
            continue
        result = node_outputs.get(str(node_id))
        output = _business_output(result) if isinstance(result, Mapping) else None
        config = node.get("config")
        config = config if isinstance(config, Mapping) else {}
        asset_key = config.get("asset_key")
        if node.get("kind") == "prompt" and isinstance(asset_key, str):
            if output is not None:
                prompt_outputs[asset_key] = output
                ordered_prompt_outputs.append(output)
                vote_outputs = _vote_sample_outputs(result)
                if vote_outputs is not None:
                    prompt_vote_outputs[asset_key] = vote_outputs
        if node.get("kind") != "rule_module":
            continue
        module_type = config.get("module_type_key")
        component = config.get("component_snapshot")
        component = component if isinstance(component, Mapping) else {}
        raw_content = component.get("content_json")
        if not isinstance(module_type, str) or not isinstance(raw_content, str):
            continue
        try:
            content = json.loads(raw_content)
        except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
            raise NativeResultFinalizationError(
                "native_authoritative_component_invalid",
                f"规则模块 {module_type} 的冻结内容损坏",
            ) from exc
        if isinstance(content, dict):
            module_slices[module_type] = content
    missing = sorted(set(MODULE_TYPE_KEYS) - set(module_slices))
    if missing:
        raise NativeResultFinalizationError(
            "native_authoritative_component_missing",
            "原生等级评测缺少规则模块：" + "、".join(missing),
        )
    resource_policy = manifest.get("resource_policy")
    resource_policy = resource_policy if isinstance(resource_policy, Mapping) else {}
    projection = resource_policy.get("legacy_projection_snapshot")
    projection = projection if isinstance(projection, Mapping) else {}
    workflow_fields = projection.get("workflow_fields")
    workflow_fields = workflow_fields if isinstance(workflow_fields, Mapping) else {}
    presence = projection.get("presence")
    if not (isinstance(presence, list) and all(isinstance(path, str) for path in presence)):
        # Older frozen workflows omitted presence. Reconstruct only from their
        # frozen modules, never the current mutable category configuration.
        presence = list(LEGACY_FIELD_OWNERS)
        matcher = module_slices["score_level_matcher"]
        from .level_scale import resolve_level_scale

        if matcher.get("levels"):
            scale = resolve_level_scale({"level_scale": matcher})
            legacy = matcher.get("legacy_level_thresholds")
            if legacy is not None and scale["thresholds"] != resolve_level_scale(
                {"level_thresholds": legacy}
            )["thresholds"]:
                raise NativeResultFinalizationError(
                    "native_authoritative_level_scale_ambiguous",
                    "冻结等级表与旧阈值不一致，需要生成明确的新机制版本",
                )
            # A legacy list may reserve L5 for redlines without a score band;
            # retain that original meaning when the migration marker exists.
            presence.remove("contract.level_scale" if legacy is not None else "contract.level_thresholds")
        else:
            presence.remove("contract.level_scale")
    slices = LegacyModuleSlices(
        module_slices,
        workflow_fields=workflow_fields,
        unclassified_paths=(),
        multiply_owned_paths=(),
        presence=presence,
    )
    v3_context = project_modules_to_legacy_bundle(slices)
    precheck_output = prompt_outputs.get("prompt_a")
    dimension_output = prompt_outputs.get("prompt_b")
    if len(ordered_prompt_outputs) == 1:
        precheck_output = ordered_prompt_outputs[0]
        dimension_output = ordered_prompt_outputs[0]
    else:
        if precheck_output is None and ordered_prompt_outputs:
            precheck_output = ordered_prompt_outputs[0]
        if dimension_output is None and len(ordered_prompt_outputs) > 1:
            dimension_output = ordered_prompt_outputs[1]
    if precheck_output is None or dimension_output is None:
        raise NativeResultFinalizationError(
            "native_authoritative_prompt_output_missing",
            "原生等级评测缺少可供服务端评分的冻结提示词输出",
        )
    precheck = precheck_output.get("precheck", precheck_output)
    rule_decision = _classification_rule_decision(manifest, module_slices, precheck)
    if rule_decision is not None:
        return rule_decision
    dimensions = dimension_output.get(
        "dimension_output",
        dimension_output.get("dimension_result", dimension_output),
    )
    # 只有 prompt_b 自己的多次采样才参与逐样本校准；单提示词并集或按顺序兜底的输出不算。
    vote_dimensions = (
        prompt_vote_outputs.get("prompt_b")
        if len(ordered_prompt_outputs) > 1 and dimension_output is prompt_outputs.get("prompt_b")
        else None
    )
    if (
        len(ordered_prompt_outputs) == 1
        and isinstance(precheck, dict)
        and _is_merged_single_prompt_output(precheck)
    ):
        # 单提示词一次产出 A+B 两段职责的并集：收尾前按职责拆开，
        # 否则 B 段严格校验会因多出前检字段而拒绝整份输出。
        precheck, dimensions = split_single_prompt_output(precheck)
    if not isinstance(precheck, dict) or not isinstance(dimensions, dict):
        raise NativeResultFinalizationError(
            "native_authoritative_prompt_output_invalid",
            "原生等级评测提示词输出不是结构化对象",
        )
    try:
        contract = v3_context.get("contract")
        subcategory_dimensions = v3_context.get("subcategory_dimensions")
        if (
            model_execution_mode == "simulated"
            and precheck.get("fixture") == "deterministic_prompt"
            and dimensions.get("fixture") == "deterministic_prompt"
        ):
            # Only legacy opaque fixtures need the generic smoke-test fallback.
            # Structured simulation must use the same frozen-contract dispatch
            # as real output (including compact evidence expansion and A
            # normalization), otherwise valid foundation output is misread as
            # deduction output and either fails or scores another track.
            aggregate = recompute_qualified_v3(
                v3_context=v3_context,
                precheck=precheck,
                dimension_output=dimensions,
            )
        elif (
            category_key == "inspiration_image"
            and isinstance(contract, dict)
            and isinstance(contract.get("aesthetic_foundation"), dict)
            and not _foundation_shaped(dimensions)
        ):
            # 合同里残留的 aesthetic_foundation 键不代表运营在用基座管线：
            # 运营现役 v24-v29 锚点正文全部输出纯基础分形状。按键存在硬路由
            # 会把这些输出全部送进 grade 校验——run137 里 79 条 B 成功素材
            # 因此被「顶层字段不符合冻结合同」拒绝。真实执行下先做最低形状
            # 检查，再交给与快速模拟同一个重算引擎；两种合同都不像的输出
            # 仍然 fail-closed。
            if not _bonus_cap_shaped(dimensions):
                raise ValueError(
                    "调用B输出既不符合基座八维合同，也不符合规则命中/纯基础分合同"
                )
            # 调用 A 的输出是扁平结构（顶层 track_classification / media_type /
            # primary_category），而赛道解析、媒介降权读的是 classification /
            # media 嵌套块——必须先经适配器归一。run139 漏了这一步：A 明明判出
            # 一类 158 / 二类 84 / 三类 8、实拍 124 / 3D 115，收尾却一律按
            # 「三类 + other」计算，赛道扣 30 叠加媒介扣 10，247 条全压进 L4/L5。
            from .schema_adapter import (
                adapt_inspiration_call_a_precheck,
                inspiration_image_defects_enabled,
            )

            precheck = adapt_inspiration_call_a_precheck(
                precheck,
                redline_policy=contract.get("redline_policy"),
                image_defects_enabled=inspiration_image_defects_enabled(contract),
            )
            aggregate = recompute_qualified_v3(
                v3_context=v3_context,
                precheck=precheck,
                dimension_output=dimensions,
            )
        elif (
            category_key == "inspiration_image"
            and isinstance(contract, dict)
            and isinstance(contract.get("aesthetic_foundation"), dict)
        ):
            from .schema_adapter import (
                adapt_inspiration_call_a_precheck,
                inspiration_image_defects_enabled,
            )

            precheck = adapt_inspiration_call_a_precheck(
                precheck,
                redline_policy=contract.get("redline_policy"),
                image_defects_enabled=inspiration_image_defects_enabled(contract),
            )
            authoritative_precheck = contract.get(
                "authoritative_precheck_contract"
            )
            if isinstance(authoritative_precheck, dict):
                validation = precheck.get("decisive_signal_validation")
                required_status = authoritative_precheck.get(
                    "required_validation_status"
                )
                if (
                    authoritative_precheck.get("format_version")
                    != "inspiration-authoritative-precheck-v1"
                    or not isinstance(validation, dict)
                    or validation.get("status") != required_status
                ):
                    raise ValueError(
                        "调用A决定性信号缺失、不确定或证据冲突"
                    )
            from .category_evaluation_contract import (
                resolve_scoring_capabilities,
            )
            from .inspiration_aesthetic_foundation import (
                apply_aesthetic_v3_rules,
            )

            classification_map = v3_context.get("classification_map")
            if not isinstance(classification_map, dict) or not isinstance(
                subcategory_dimensions, dict
            ):
                raise ValueError("原生灵感图工作流缺少冻结评分配置")
            aggregate = apply_aesthetic_v3_rules(
                contract=contract,
                classification_map=classification_map,
                precheck=precheck,
                foundation=dimensions,
            )
            aggregate["scoring_capabilities"] = resolve_scoring_capabilities(
                contract,
                subcategory_dimensions,
            )
        elif category_key == "model_3d_su":
            classification_map = v3_context.get("classification_map")
            if (
                not isinstance(contract, dict)
                or not isinstance(classification_map, dict)
                or not isinstance(subcategory_dimensions, dict)
            ):
                raise ValueError("原生 3D/SU 工作流缺少冻结评分配置")
            from .subcategory_resolver import resolve_subcategory

            resolved = resolve_subcategory(
                precheck,
                classification_map=classification_map,
                track_classification=contract["track_classification"],
            )
            track_key = str(resolved["track_key"])
            track_config = subcategory_dimensions.get(track_key)
            raw_grade_dimensions = dimensions.get("dimensions")
            grade_payload = (
                isinstance(track_config, dict)
                and isinstance(track_config.get("grade_output_contract"), dict)
                and isinstance(raw_grade_dimensions, dict)
                and bool(raw_grade_dimensions)
                and all(
                    isinstance(item, dict) and "grade" in item
                    for item in raw_grade_dimensions.values()
                )
            )
            if grade_payload:
                from .b_aesthetic_foundation import (
                    normalize_b_aesthetic_foundation,
                )
                from .category_evaluation_contract import (
                    resolve_scoring_capabilities,
                )
                from .dimension_deduction_bridge import dimension_definitions
                from .inspiration_category_seed import evaluate_one
                from .worker_v3_authoritative import _strict_static_grades

                expected_keys = [
                    item["key"] for item in dimension_definitions(track_config)
                ]
                grades = _strict_static_grades(
                    dimensions,
                    track_config=track_config,
                    expected_keys=expected_keys,
                )
                if grades is None:
                    raise ValueError("调用B未匹配冻结的五维 grade 输出合同")
                foundation = normalize_b_aesthetic_foundation(
                    {
                        "aesthetic_score": dimensions.get("aesthetic_score"),
                        "overall_evidence": dimensions.get(
                            "aesthetic_evidence"
                        )
                        or (
                            [dimensions.get("overall_note")]
                            if dimensions.get("overall_note")
                            else []
                        ),
                        "confidence": dimensions.get(
                            "aesthetic_confidence"
                        ),
                    }
                )
                outcome = evaluate_one(
                    contract=contract,
                    classification_map=classification_map,
                    subcategory_dimensions=subcategory_dimensions,
                    precheck=precheck,
                    common_grades_by_track={track_key: grades},
                    specific_grades_by_track={},
                    initial_score=foundation["aesthetic_score"],
                )
                aggregate = outcome["result"]
                aggregate["dimension_scoring_mode"] = "grade_fallback"
                aggregate["resolved_track_key"] = track_key
                aggregate["scoring_capabilities"] = (
                    resolve_scoring_capabilities(
                        contract,
                        subcategory_dimensions,
                    )
                )
            else:
                aggregate = recompute_qualified_v3(
                    v3_context=v3_context,
                    precheck=precheck,
                    dimension_output=dimensions,
                )
        else:
            aggregate = recompute_qualified_v3(
                v3_context=v3_context,
                precheck=precheck,
                dimension_output=dimensions,
            )
        from .worker_v3_authoritative import build_v3_authoritative_scoring

        scoring = build_v3_authoritative_scoring(aggregate, precheck=precheck)
        scoring.pop("_dimension_deduction_raw_payload", None)
        if calibration is not None:
            from .recommendation_calibration import apply_calibration

            profile, matcher = calibration
            scoring = apply_calibration(scoring, profile=profile, matcher=matcher,
                                        precheck=precheck, dimensions=dimensions,
                                        vote_dimensions=vote_dimensions)
        return scoring
    except (V3PipelineError, KeyError, TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
        raise NativeResultFinalizationError(
            "native_authoritative_reduction_failed",
            f"原生等级评测权威归并失败：{exc}",
        ) from exc


def reduce_native_workflow_result(
    manifest: Mapping[str, Any],
    node_outputs: Mapping[str, Mapping[str, Any]],
    *,
    model_execution_mode: str = "real",
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Reduce one frozen native DAG without persisting a second run fact."""

    if model_execution_mode not in {"simulated", "real"}:
        raise NativeResultFinalizationError(
            "native_model_execution_mode_invalid",
            "原生结果归并缺少合法的模型执行方式",
        )

    from .recommendation_calibration import manifest_profile

    try:
        calibration = manifest_profile(manifest)
    except ValueError as exc:
        raise NativeResultFinalizationError(
            "native_recommendation_calibration_invalid", str(exc),
        ) from exc

    if manifest.get("category_key") == "model_3d_su_joint":
        from .joint_model_workflow import validate_joint_knowledge

        stage_a = node_outputs.get("prompt_a", {})
        payload = _business_output(stage_a)
        if payload is None:
            raise NativeResultFinalizationError("joint_knowledge_graph_missing", "联合工作流缺少完整知识图谱结果")
        knowledge = validate_joint_knowledge(payload)
        source_manifest = dict(manifest, category_key="model_3d_su")
        normalized = reduce_native_workflow_result(source_manifest, node_outputs, model_execution_mode=model_execution_mode)
        normalized["knowledge_graph"] = knowledge
        return normalized

    normalized = _short_circuit_level_result(manifest, node_outputs)
    if normalized is None:
        normalized = _knowledge_graph_result(manifest, node_outputs)
    if normalized is None:
        normalized = _authoritative_level_result(
            manifest,
            node_outputs,
            model_execution_mode=model_execution_mode,
            calibration=calibration,
        )
    if normalized is None:
        normalized = _reduce_native_result(manifest, node_outputs)
    # 字典表撞库：命中项按规范词回写目标字段，未命中项记入 dictionary_filter 审计块
    # （finalize_native_run 再把未命中落到候选表供运营决策）。
    from .dictionary_filter import apply_dictionary_filters

    normalized, _misses = apply_dictionary_filters(manifest, normalized)
    normalized = _attach_material_summary(manifest, node_outputs, normalized)
    normalized = _attach_image_grades(manifest, node_outputs, normalized)
    return normalized


def _attach_material_summary(
    manifest: Mapping[str, Any],
    node_outputs: Mapping[str, Mapping[str, Any]],
    normalized: dict[str, Any],
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """把案例聚合摘要并进终态结果，供素材维度真值逐项比对。

    只有显式开了 ``emit_material_summary`` 的聚合节点才参与；聚合节点自身的
    输出与冻结哈希不变，所以旧版本重放结果完全一致。
    """

    from .workflow_case_aggregation import build_case_aggregate_summary

    nodes = manifest.get("nodes")
    for node in nodes if isinstance(nodes, list) else []:
        if not isinstance(node, Mapping) or node.get("kind") != "aggregate_case":
            continue
        config = node.get("config")
        if not isinstance(config, Mapping) or config.get("emit_material_summary") is not True:
            continue
        result = node_outputs.get(node.get("node_instance_id"))
        aggregate = _business_output(result) if isinstance(result, Mapping) else None
        if not isinstance(aggregate, Mapping):
            continue
        summary = build_case_aggregate_summary(aggregate)
        # 终态结果里已有的同名字段优先，聚合摘要只补不覆盖。
        return {
            **{
                key: value
                for key, value in summary.items()
                if key != "schema_version" and key not in normalized
            },
            **normalized,
            "material_summary_schema_version": summary["schema_version"],
        }
    return normalized


def _attach_image_grades(
    manifest: Mapping[str, Any],
    node_outputs: Mapping[str, Mapping[str, Any]],
    normalized: dict[str, Any],
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """把逐图模型分按档位阈值投影成逐图档位，并进终态结果。

    ``images`` 与图片维度真值同形状（只有 cloud_id 和 level），指标层的集合
    比对才能逐张对上；分数和用过的阈值另放在 ``image_grade_detail`` 里，
    供纠偏时回看「这张图为什么被判成这档」。

    只有显式开了 ``emit_image_grades`` 的聚合节点才参与；聚合节点自身的输出
    与冻结哈希不变，旧版本重放结果完全一致。
    """

    from .design_case_image_grade import (
        DesignCaseImageGradeError,
        build_image_grade_projection,
    )

    nodes = manifest.get("nodes")
    for node in nodes if isinstance(nodes, list) else []:
        if not isinstance(node, Mapping) or node.get("kind") != "aggregate_case":
            continue
        config = node.get("config")
        if not isinstance(config, Mapping) or config.get("emit_image_grades") is not True:
            continue
        result = node_outputs.get(node.get("node_instance_id"))
        aggregate = _business_output(result) if isinstance(result, Mapping) else None
        if not isinstance(aggregate, Mapping):
            continue
        try:
            projection = build_image_grade_projection(
                aggregate, bands=config.get("image_grade_bands")
            )
        except DesignCaseImageGradeError as exc:
            raise NativeResultFinalizationError(
                exc.code,
                f"逐图档位投影失败：{exc}",
            ) from exc
        # The frozen server thresholds own predicted grades. Original model
        # output remains in node evidence; do not mutate it or trust its grade.
        return {
            **normalized,
            "images": [
                {"cloud_id": image["cloud_id"], "level": image["level"]}
                for image in projection["images"]
            ],
            "image_grade_detail": projection,
        }
    return normalized


def _resolve_asset_id(
    db: Session,
    run: ProductionRun,
    snapshot: Mapping[str, Any],
) -> int:
    if run.source_type == "baseline_regression_item" and run.source_id is not None:
        item = db.get(BaselineRegressionItem, run.source_id)
        if item is not None:
            return item.asset_id
    runtime_context = snapshot.get("runtime_context")
    input_manifest = (
        runtime_context.get("input_manifest")
        if isinstance(runtime_context, Mapping)
        else None
    )
    candidates = []
    if isinstance(input_manifest, Mapping):
        candidates.append(input_manifest.get("asset_id"))
    if run.source_type in {"native_evaluation", "asset"}:
        candidates.append(run.source_id)
    for candidate in candidates:
        if isinstance(candidate, int) and not isinstance(candidate, bool):
            if db.get(Asset, candidate) is not None:
                return candidate
    raise NativeResultFinalizationError(
        "native_run_asset_missing",
        "原生运行没有绑定可读取的素材",
    )


def _prompt_stage_payloads(
    node_outputs: Mapping[str, Any],
    manifest: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], dict[str, Any] | None]:
    """Project prompt node outputs onto the legacy precheck/aesthetic columns.

    兼容行历来只写空壳，导致纠偏页与结果页读不到调用 A/B 的字段结果。
    这里按节点键定位 prompt_a / prompt_b，取不到再按出现顺序兜底。
    """

    by_key: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
    ordered: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    metadata_ids = metadata_node_ids(manifest)
    for key, entry in node_outputs.items():
        if key in metadata_ids or not isinstance(entry, Mapping) or entry.get("node_kind") != "prompt":
            continue
        output = entry.get("output")
        if not isinstance(output, Mapping):
            continue
        payload = dict(output)
        by_key[str(key)] = payload
        ordered.append(payload)
    precheck = by_key.get("prompt_a")
    if precheck is None:
        precheck = ordered[0] if ordered else {}
    aesthetic = by_key.get("prompt_b")
    if aesthetic is None:
        remaining = [item for item in ordered if item is not precheck]
        aesthetic = remaining[0] if remaining else None
    return precheck, aesthetic


def _legacy_summary(manifest: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    prompts: list[Mapping[str, Any]] = []
    rule_versions: list[str] = []
    nodes = manifest.get("nodes")
    metadata_ids = metadata_node_ids(manifest)
    if isinstance(nodes, list):
        for node in nodes:
            if not isinstance(node, Mapping):
                continue
            if node.get("node_instance_id") in metadata_ids:
                continue
            config = node.get("config")
            config = config if isinstance(config, Mapping) else {}
            component = config.get("component_snapshot")
            component = component if isinstance(component, Mapping) else {}
            if node.get("kind") == "prompt":
                prompts.append(config)
            elif node.get("kind") == "rule_module":
                version = component.get("version")
                if version is not None:
                    rule_versions.append(str(version))
    first_prompt = prompts[0] if prompts else {}
    second_prompt = prompts[1] if len(prompts) > 1 else {}
    first_component = first_prompt.get("component_snapshot")
    first_component = (
        first_component if isinstance(first_component, Mapping) else {}
    )
    second_component = second_prompt.get("component_snapshot")
    second_component = (
        second_component if isinstance(second_component, Mapping) else {}
    )
    first_model = first_prompt.get("model_snapshot")
    first_model = first_model if isinstance(first_model, Mapping) else {}
    content = first_component.get("content")
    content = content if isinstance(content, Mapping) else {}
    return {
        "model_id": str(first_model.get("model_id") or "native-workflow"),
        "prompt_a_version": str(first_component.get("version") or "workflow-v3"),
        "prompt_b_version": (
            str(second_component.get("version"))
            if second_component.get("version") is not None
            else None
        ),
        "rubric_version": str(content.get("rubric_version") or "workflow-v3"),
        "engine_version": "native-workflow-v1",
        "rule_versions": rule_versions,
    }


def _attach_short_circuit_savings(
    db: Session,
    run: ProductionRun,
    manifest: Mapping[str, Any],
    normalized_result: Mapping[str, Any],
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """短路命中时估算被跳过的模型调用节点省下的费用，写进 short_circuit_decision。

    估算口径：同一工作流版本、同一节点最近 20 次成功调用的 actual_cost_micros 均值；
    没有历史时退化为同类目该节点均值；仍没有则记 None（只记录 skipped_prompt_nodes）。
    这是估算不是账单，字段名带 estimated 前缀。
    """
    decision = normalized_result.get("short_circuit_decision")
    if not isinstance(decision, Mapping):
        return dict(normalized_result)
    from sqlalchemy import func

    from .models import WorkflowModelInvocation

    skipped_nodes = set(decision.get("skipped_nodes") or [])
    evidence = decision.get("evidence") if isinstance(decision.get("evidence"), Mapping) else {}
    skip_roles = set(evidence.get("skip_roles") or decision.get("skip_roles") or [])
    prompt_nodes: list[str] = []
    for node in manifest.get("nodes", []) or []:
        if not isinstance(node, Mapping) or node.get("kind") not in {"prompt", "foreach_image"}:
            continue
        node_id = str(node.get("node_instance_id") or "")
        config = node.get("config") if isinstance(node.get("config"), Mapping) else {}
        role = str(config.get("execution_role") or "")
        if node_id in skipped_nodes or (skip_roles and role in skip_roles):
            prompt_nodes.append(node_id)
    estimates: dict[str, int | None] = {}
    total: int | None = 0
    for node_id in prompt_nodes:
        avg = db.execute(
            select(func.avg(WorkflowModelInvocation.actual_cost_micros))
            .select_from(WorkflowModelInvocation)
            .join(ProductionRun, ProductionRun.id == WorkflowModelInvocation.production_run_id)
            .where(
                ProductionRun.workflow_version_id == run.workflow_version_id,
                WorkflowModelInvocation.node_instance_id == node_id,
                WorkflowModelInvocation.status == "succeeded",
                WorkflowModelInvocation.actual_cost_micros > 0,
            )
        ).scalar()
        if avg is None and run.category_key:
            avg = db.execute(
                select(func.avg(WorkflowModelInvocation.actual_cost_micros))
                .select_from(WorkflowModelInvocation)
                .join(ProductionRun, ProductionRun.id == WorkflowModelInvocation.production_run_id)
                .where(
                    ProductionRun.category_key == run.category_key,
                    WorkflowModelInvocation.node_instance_id == node_id,
                    WorkflowModelInvocation.status == "succeeded",
                    WorkflowModelInvocation.actual_cost_micros > 0,
                )
            ).scalar()
        if avg is None:
            estimates[node_id] = None
            total = None if total is None else None
        else:
            estimates[node_id] = int(avg)
            if total is not None:
                total += int(avg)
    updated = dict(normalized_result)
    updated["short_circuit_decision"] = {
        **dict(decision),
        "skipped_prompt_nodes": prompt_nodes,
        "estimated_saved_cost_micros": total if prompt_nodes else 0,
        "estimated_saved_cost_by_node": estimates,
    }
    return updated


#: dictionary_miss_candidates 列宽（String(40/160/200)）：超长的词多半是模型吐出的句子，
#: 截断会造出错词供运营「接受」，所以直接跳过而不是截断。
_MISS_CATEGORY_MAX = 40
_MISS_FIELD_PATH_MAX = 160
_MISS_TERM_MAX = 200


def _bump_dictionary_miss(
    db: Session,
    *,
    category_key: str,
    field_path: str,
    term: str,
    run_id: int | None,
    now: datetime,
) -> bool:
    """原子累加一个已存在的候选词；返回是否命中了已有行。

    ``hit_count = hit_count + 1`` 在数据库里算，两个收尾事务同时见到同一个词也不丢计数。
    """
    from sqlalchemy import update

    from .models import DictionaryMissCandidate

    result = db.execute(
        update(DictionaryMissCandidate)
        .where(
            DictionaryMissCandidate.category_key == category_key,
            DictionaryMissCandidate.field_path == field_path,
            DictionaryMissCandidate.term == term,
        )
        .values(
            hit_count=DictionaryMissCandidate.hit_count + 1,
            last_seen_run_id=run_id,
            last_seen_at=now,
        )
        .execution_options(synchronize_session=False)
    )
    return bool(result.rowcount)


def _record_dictionary_misses(
    db: Session,
    run: ProductionRun,
    normalized_result: Mapping[str, Any],
    *,
    now: datetime,
) -> None:
    """把字典撞库未命中的词落到候选表（同类目+字段+词去重，累计命中次数）。

    同一结果内先按 casefold 去重（MySQL 默认排序规则大小写不敏感，"Loft"/"loft" 是同一
    唯一键）；每个词先原子累加，没有行再在保存点里插入，并发插入撞唯一约束时退回累加。
    这些都不能让收尾崩溃：候选表只是运营线索，不是评测事实。
    """
    from sqlalchemy.exc import DataError, IntegrityError

    from .models import DictionaryMissCandidate

    audit = normalized_result.get("dictionary_filter")
    if not isinstance(audit, list) or not audit:
        return

    category_key = str(run.category_key or "")
    if len(category_key) > _MISS_CATEGORY_MAX:
        logger.warning("dictionary miss skipped: category_key too long run_id=%s", run.id)
        return
    unique: dict[tuple[str, str], tuple[str, str]] = {}
    for block in audit:
        if not isinstance(block, Mapping):
            continue
        field_path = str(block.get("field_path") or "")
        if len(field_path) > _MISS_FIELD_PATH_MAX:
            logger.warning("dictionary miss skipped: field_path too long run_id=%s", run.id)
            continue
        for raw in block.get("unmatched") or []:
            if not isinstance(raw, str) or not raw.strip():
                continue
            term = raw.strip()
            if len(term) > _MISS_TERM_MAX:
                logger.warning(
                    "dictionary miss skipped: term longer than %s run_id=%s field=%s",
                    _MISS_TERM_MAX, run.id, field_path,
                )
                continue
            unique.setdefault((field_path, term.casefold()), (field_path, term))

    for field_path, term in unique.values():
        identity = dict(category_key=category_key, field_path=field_path, term=term)
        if _bump_dictionary_miss(db, **identity, run_id=run.id, now=now):
            continue
        try:
            with db.begin_nested():
                db.add(
                    DictionaryMissCandidate(
                        **identity,
                        hit_count=1,
                        status="pending",
                        first_seen_run_id=run.id,
                        last_seen_run_id=run.id,
                        last_seen_at=now,
                    )
                )
        except IntegrityError:
            # 另一个事务刚插入了同一个词（或排序规则判为同一个词）：改为累加。
            _bump_dictionary_miss(db, **identity, run_id=run.id, now=now)
        except DataError:
            logger.warning(
                "dictionary miss skipped: value rejected by database run_id=%s field=%s",
                run.id, field_path,
            )


def finalize_native_run(
    db: Session,
    run: ProductionRun,
) -> NativeEvaluationFact:
    """Reduce and persist one successful workflow-v3 run exactly once."""

    if run.status != "succeeded":
        raise NativeResultFinalizationError(
            "native_run_not_succeeded",
            "只有成功的原生运行可以生成最终评测事实",
        )
    if run.source_type == "baseline_regression_item":
        baseline_run_id = db.scalar(
            select(BaselineRegressionItem.run_id).where(
                BaselineRegressionItem.id == run.source_id
            )
        )
        baseline_run = (
            db.scalar(
                select(BaselineRegressionRun)
                .where(BaselineRegressionRun.id == baseline_run_id)
                .with_for_update()
            )
            if baseline_run_id is not None
            else None
        )
        if baseline_run is None:
            raise NativeResultFinalizationError(
                "native_baseline_source_invalid",
                "原生运行绑定的存量回归不存在",
            )
    snapshot = _json_object(
        read_run_snapshot_text(run),
        code="native_run_snapshot_invalid",
        message="原生运行冻结快照损坏",
    )
    workflow = snapshot.get("workflow")
    manifest = workflow.get("manifest") if isinstance(workflow, Mapping) else None
    if not isinstance(manifest, Mapping) or manifest.get("schema_version") != "workflow-v3":
        raise NativeResultFinalizationError(
            "native_run_snapshot_invalid",
            "运行不是可归并的 workflow-v3",
        )
    asset_id = _resolve_asset_id(db, run, snapshot)
    node_outputs = _latest_node_outputs(db, run)
    normalized_result = reduce_native_workflow_result(
        manifest,
        node_outputs,
        model_execution_mode=run.model_execution_mode,
    )
    normalized_result = _attach_short_circuit_savings(db, run, manifest, normalized_result)
    result_hash = hash_manifest(normalized_result)
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    _record_dictionary_misses(db, run, normalized_result, now=now)

    job = db.scalar(
        select(EvaluationJob).where(EvaluationJob.production_run_id == run.id)
    )
    baseline_item_id = (
        run.source_id if run.source_type == "baseline_regression_item" else None
    )
    if job is None:
        job = EvaluationJob(
            asset_id=asset_id,
            category_key=run.category_key or str(manifest.get("category_key") or ""),
            baseline_regression_item_id=baseline_item_id,
            workflow_version_id=run.workflow_version_id,
            production_run_id=run.id,
            queue_class=run.queue_class,
            origin_queue_class=run.queue_class,
            status="completed",
            stage="completed",
            progress=100,
            attempts=max(run.attempt_count, 1),
            error_message="",
            started_at=run.started_at or run.created_at,
            finished_at=run.finished_at or now,
        )
        db.add(job)
        db.flush()
    elif (
        job.asset_id != asset_id
        or job.workflow_version_id != run.workflow_version_id
        or job.production_run_id != run.id
    ):
        raise NativeResultFinalizationError(
            "native_evaluation_job_conflict",
            "原生运行已绑定不同的兼容任务",
        )

    result = db.scalar(
        select(EvaluationResult).where(EvaluationResult.production_run_id == run.id)
    )
    if result is None:
        legacy = _legacy_summary(manifest)
        metadata_ids = metadata_node_ids(manifest)
        prompt_outputs = [
            output
            for node_id, output in node_outputs.items()
            if output.get("node_kind") == "prompt" and node_id not in metadata_ids
        ]
        stage_precheck, stage_aesthetic = _prompt_stage_payloads(node_outputs, manifest)
        score = normalized_result.get("score")
        confidence = normalized_result.get("confidence")
        result = EvaluationResult(
            asset_id=asset_id,
            job_id=job.id,
            strategy_bundle_id=None,
            strategy_snapshot_json=None,
            workflow_version_id=run.workflow_version_id,
            production_run_id=run.id,
            workflow_snapshot_hash=run.snapshot_hash,
            workflow_node_outputs_json=canonical_json(node_outputs),
            normalized_result_json=canonical_json(normalized_result),
            preprocess_json="{}",
            precheck_json=canonical_json(stage_precheck),
            aesthetic_json=(
                canonical_json(stage_aesthetic)
                if stage_aesthetic is not None
                else None
            ),
            scoring_json=canonical_json(normalized_result),
            correction_history_json="[]",
            raw_response_a=canonical_json(prompt_outputs[0]) if prompt_outputs else "{}",
            raw_response_b=(
                canonical_json(prompt_outputs[1]) if len(prompt_outputs) > 1 else None
            ),
            score=(
                float(score)
                if isinstance(score, (int, float)) and not isinstance(score, bool)
                else None
            ),
            level=(
                str(normalized_result["level"])
                if normalized_result.get("level") is not None
                else None
            ),
            confidence=(
                float(confidence)
                if isinstance(confidence, (int, float))
                and not isinstance(confidence, bool)
                else None
            ),
            needs_review=bool(normalized_result.get("needs_review", False)),
            review_stage="initial",
            review_revision=0,
            model_id=legacy["model_id"],
            prompt_a_version=legacy["prompt_a_version"],
            prompt_b_version=legacy["prompt_b_version"],
            risk_review_version=None,
            rubric_version=legacy["rubric_version"],
            engine_version=legacy["engine_version"],
        )
        db.add(result)
        db.flush()
    else:
        existing = _json_object(
            result.normalized_result_json,
            code="native_evaluation_result_conflict",
            message="原生运行已绑定损坏的兼容结果",
        )
        if (
            result.job_id != job.id
            or result.workflow_version_id != run.workflow_version_id
            or result.workflow_snapshot_hash != run.snapshot_hash
            or hash_manifest(existing) != result_hash
        ):
            raise NativeResultFinalizationError(
                "native_evaluation_result_conflict",
                "原生运行已绑定不同的兼容结果",
            )

    fact = NativeEvaluationFact(
        normalized_result=normalized_result,
        result_hash=result_hash,
        workflow_version_id=run.workflow_version_id,
        run_id=run.id,
        node_outputs=node_outputs,
        evaluation_job_id=job.id,
        evaluation_result_id=result.id,
    )
    if run.source_type == "baseline_regression_item":
        from .native_baseline_regression import (
            NativeBaselineRegressionError,
            finalize_native_baseline_item,
        )

        try:
            finalize_native_baseline_item(db, run, fact)
        except NativeBaselineRegressionError as exc:
            raise NativeResultFinalizationError(exc.code, str(exc)) from exc
    # 素材大宽表（ADR-0111）：同一事务内刷新，失败只记日志，不阻断收尾。
    from .wide_material import refresh_material

    refresh_material(db, asset_id, event_kind="evaluated")
    return fact

===== FILE backend/app/workflow_retry_feedback.py =====
"""Frozen opt-in feedback from a settled validation failure, never raw model text."""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import re
from typing import Any, Mapping

from sqlalchemy import select

from .audit import canonical_json
from .models import ProductionStepAttempt, WorkflowModelInvocation


PROFILE = "validation-feedback-v1"
JSON_PROFILE = "validation-feedback-v2"
TRUNCATION_PROFILE = "validation-feedback-v3"
# Workflow model configuration limits max_tokens to 65,536.
_MAX_OUTPUT_TOKENS = 65_536
_JSON_REASONS = frozenset({
    "Expecting value", "Expecting ',' delimiter", "Expecting ':' delimiter",
    "Expecting property name enclosed in double quotes", "Extra data",
    "Unterminated string starting at", "Invalid control character at",
    "Invalid \\escape", "Invalid \\uXXXX escape",
})
_SCHEMA_RETRY_KEY = "schema-retry-v1:root"
_CONSTRAINT_KEYS = (
    "type", "enum", "const", "required", "minLength", "maxLength",
    "minItems", "maxItems", "minimum", "maximum", "additionalProperties",
)
_SEMANTIC_PREFIXES = (
    "redline_reason_conflict:", "missing_evidence:redline:",
    "evidence_conflict:redline:", "missing_evidence:hard_defects:",
    "evidence_without_hit:hard_defects:",
    "metadata_repeated_text:", "metadata_self_correction:", "metadata_length:",
)


def _schema_issue(detail: str, schema: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    match = re.search(r"\$(?:\.[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z_0-9]*|\[[0-9]+\])*", detail)
    if match is None:
        return None
    path = match.group()
    current = schema
    for name, index in re.findall(r"\.([A-Za-z_][A-Za-z_0-9]*)|\[([0-9]+)\]", path):
        current = current.get("properties", {}).get(name) if name else current.get("items")
        if not isinstance(current, Mapping):
            return None
    constraints = {key: current[key] for key in _CONSTRAINT_KEYS if key in current}
    # Large enums stay in the original contract; never present a truncated enum
    # as though it were the complete set of allowed values.
    if len(canonical_json(constraints)) > 1800:
        constraints.pop("enum", None)
        constraints.pop("const", None)
    if len(canonical_json(constraints)) > 1800:
        return None
    return {"path": path, "constraints": constraints}


def _json_parse_issue(failure: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    if failure.get("usage_status") != "provider_reported" or failure.get("finish_reason") != "stop":
        return None
    evidence = failure.get("failure_evidence")
    diagnostic = evidence.get("json_parse") if isinstance(evidence, Mapping) else None
    if not isinstance(diagnostic, Mapping):
        return None
    reason, offset, length = (diagnostic.get(key) for key in ("reason", "offset", "document_chars"))
    if (not isinstance(reason, str) or reason not in _JSON_REASONS
            or type(offset) is not int or type(length) is not int
            or not 0 <= offset <= length or not 1 <= length <= 2_000_000):
        return None
    return {"json_syntax": {"reason": reason, "offset": offset, "document_chars": length}}


def _truncation_issue(source: WorkflowModelInvocation,
                      failure: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    if (source.status != "failed" or source.upstream_status_code != 200
            or source.error_code not in {
                "model_provider_json_truncated", "model_provider_transient_parse"
            } or failure.get("usage_status") != "provider_reported"
            or failure.get("finish_reason") != "length"):
        return None
    usage = failure.get("provider_usage")
    output_tokens = usage.get("output_tokens") if isinstance(usage, Mapping) else None
    cap = source.reserved_output_tokens
    if (type(output_tokens) is not int or type(cap) is not int
            or not 1 <= output_tokens == cap <= _MAX_OUTPUT_TOKENS):
        return None
    return {"truncation_feedback": {"finish_reason": "length",
                                     "output_tokens": output_tokens,
                                     "output_cap_tokens": cap}}


def validation_retry_feedback(db, *, run, attempt, manifest, node_id,
                              invocation_key, schema, transport_mode=None):
    provenance = (manifest.get("resource_policy") or {}).get("source_provenance") or {}
    profile = provenance.get("retry_feedback_profile")
    from .inspiration_metadata_bounds import postcheck_enabled, TEXT_LIMITS, TAG_COUNT, TAG_LENGTH
    if node_id == "production_metadata" and postcheck_enabled(manifest):
        profile = TRUNCATION_PROFILE
    if (not isinstance(profile, str) or profile not in {PROFILE, JSON_PROFILE, TRUNCATION_PROFILE}
            or transport_mode is not None
            or invocation_key not in {"root", _SCHEMA_RETRY_KEY}):
        return None
    if invocation_key == "root" and attempt.attempt_no <= 1:
        return None
    query = select(WorkflowModelInvocation).where(
        WorkflowModelInvocation.production_run_id == run.id,
        WorkflowModelInvocation.node_instance_id == node_id,
    )
    if invocation_key == _SCHEMA_RETRY_KEY:
        # An immediate retry always refers to its own immutable root, including
        # after a quota wait; later calls cannot change the prepared request.
        query = query.where(WorkflowModelInvocation.step_attempt_id == attempt.id,
                            WorkflowModelInvocation.invocation_key == "root")
    else:
        # A new step attempt uses its predecessor's last result. Excluding the
        # current attempt keeps a resumed reservation's dispatch hash stable.
        query = query.join(ProductionStepAttempt,
            ProductionStepAttempt.id == WorkflowModelInvocation.step_attempt_id).where(
                ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == run.id,
                ProductionStepAttempt.step_key == node_id,
                ProductionStepAttempt.attempt_no < attempt.attempt_no,
            )
    source = db.scalar(query.order_by(WorkflowModelInvocation.id.desc()).limit(1))
    if source is None or source.finished_at is None:
        return None
    evidence = json.loads(source.evidence_json or "{}")
    failure = evidence.get("failure") or {}
    result = evidence.get("result") or {}
    issues = []
    if (source.status == "failed" and source.error_code == "structured_output_schema_mismatch"
            and failure.get("usage_status") == "provider_reported"):
        issue = _schema_issue(str(failure.get("detail") or ""), schema)
        if issue is not None:
            issues.append(issue)
    elif (profile in {JSON_PROFILE, TRUNCATION_PROFILE} and source.status == "failed"
            and source.error_code == "model_provider_transient_parse"):
        issue = _json_parse_issue(failure)
        if issue is not None:
            issues.append(issue)
        elif profile == TRUNCATION_PROFILE and source.step_attempt_id != attempt.id:
            issue = _truncation_issue(source, failure)
            if issue is not None:
                issues.append(issue)
    elif (profile == TRUNCATION_PROFILE and source.step_attempt_id != attempt.id
            and source.error_code == "model_provider_json_truncated"):
        issue = _truncation_issue(source, failure)
        if issue is not None:
            issues.append(issue)
    elif source.status == "succeeded":
        validation = result.get("semantic_validation") or {}
        if validation.get("status") == "rejected":
            issues = [{"consistency": reason} for reason in validation.get("reasons", [])
                      if isinstance(reason, str) and reason.startswith(_SEMANTIC_PREFIXES)
                      and re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z_0-9:.-]{1,240}", reason)][:16]
    if not issues:
        return None
    if node_id == "production_metadata" and postcheck_enabled(manifest):
        issues.append({"metadata_text_bounds": {**TEXT_LIMITS, "tags": TAG_COUNT, "tag_chars": TAG_LENGTH}})
    return {
        "profile": profile,
        "source_invocation_id": source.id,
        "source_evidence_hash": hashlib.sha256(source.evidence_json.encode()).hexdigest(),
        "issues": issues,
    }


def append_validation_feedback(user_prompt: str, feedback: Mapping[str, Any]) -> str:
    # Only bounded validator facts and frozen constraints reach the model, not
    # provider bodies, exception prose, truth, credentials or accounting data.
    if any("metadata_text_bounds" in issue or str(issue.get("consistency", "")).startswith(
            ("metadata_repeated_text:", "metadata_self_correction:", "metadata_length:"))
            for issue in feedback["issues"]):
        return user_prompt.rstrip() + (
            "\n\n【上次发布说明的校验反馈】\n"
            "上次输出未通过下方校验，重新看同一张图并返回完整JSON。"
            "cons 用简短中文列出画面中实际可见的不足：水印、界面叠层、遮挡、模糊、旋转、裁切等均须保留。"
            "对文字仅描述缺字、遮挡、裁切或可读性，不引用或转写具体字词，不提出替换词。各项事实只描述一次。"
            "不得统一清空cons，不改变原评分、字段合同或长度限制。\n"
            + canonical_json({"validation_issues": feedback["issues"]})
        )
    if (feedback.get("profile") == TRUNCATION_PROFILE
            and any("truncation_feedback" in issue for issue in feedback["issues"])):
        return user_prompt.rstrip() + (
            "\n\n【上次输出的校验反馈】\n"
            "上次响应达到输出长度上限。重新核对同一输入，只返回完整的JSON对象和所有必填字段；"
            "压缩重复措辞，不输出思维链。保留真实缺陷、视觉判断规则和原有阈值，"
            "不得编造图像证据或为缩短回答省略真实命中。\n"
            + canonical_json({"validation_issues": feedback["issues"]})
        )
    if (feedback.get("profile") in {JSON_PROFILE, TRUNCATION_PROFILE}
            and any("json_syntax" in issue for issue in feedback["issues"])):
        return user_prompt.rstrip() + (
            "\n\n【上次输出的校验反馈】\n"
            "上次响应已完整结束，但不是合法JSON。重新核对同一输入，只返回一个完整JSON对象。"
            "所有键和字符串使用双引号，正确转义字符串；数值位置只写数值，不能写算式、中文说明或占位符；"
            "布尔值使用true/false，空值仅在原合同允许时使用null。数组和对象不能有尾逗号，括号须配对。"
            "下方offset是上次响应中的字符位置，仅用于诊断。原字段合同、评分和视觉判断规则保持，"
            "不得为通过JSON校验编造事实、修改真实命中或省略必填字段。\n"
            + canonical_json({"validation_issues": feedback["issues"]})
        )
    return user_prompt.rstrip() + (
        "\n\n【上次输出的校验反馈】\n"
        "重新核对同一输入并返回完整JSON，重点修正以下字段。原评分、红线和视觉判断规则保持。"
        "字符串长度按每个汉字、英文字母、数字、空格和标点分别计1个字符；超限时重写为完整短句。"
        "缺少的必填数组也须按图像事实明确填写。命中布尔、reason和同key证据应相互一致，"
        "根据原图判断命中与豁免，不得为通过校验虚构证据或抹去真实命中。\n"
        + canonical_json({"validation_issues": feedback["issues"]})
    )

===== FILE backend/app/workflow_route_state.py =====
"""Pure route selection and graph activation for workflow-v3 runs."""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Any, Mapping

from .workflow_conditions import WorkflowConditionError, evaluate_condition


class RouteResolutionError(ValueError):
    def __init__(self, code: str, message: str):
        super().__init__(message)
        self.code = code


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class RouteEvaluation:
    branch: str
    matched: bool
    condition_hash: str
    referenced_facts: Mapping[str, Any]

    def as_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "branch": self.branch,
            "matched": self.matched,
            "condition_hash": self.condition_hash,
            "referenced_facts": dict(self.referenced_facts),
        }


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class RouteDecision:
    branch_node_id: str
    branch_key: str
    mode: str
    activated_branches: tuple[str, ...]
    skipped_branches: tuple[str, ...]
    evaluations: tuple[RouteEvaluation, ...]

    def as_result_payload(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "schema_version": "workflow-route-decision-v1",
            "branch_node_id": self.branch_node_id,
            "branch_key": self.branch_key,
            "mode": self.mode,
            "activated_branches": list(self.activated_branches),
            "skipped_branches": list(self.skipped_branches),
            "evaluations": [item.as_dict() for item in self.evaluations],
        }


def route_decision_from_payload(payload: Mapping[str, Any]) -> RouteDecision:
    """Restore only a fully persisted route decision; never re-evaluate it."""

    if payload.get("schema_version") != "workflow-route-decision-v1":
        raise RouteResolutionError(
            "route_decision_snapshot_invalid",
            "运行中的路线决定快照版本不受支持",
        )
    evaluations_value = payload.get("evaluations")
    if not isinstance(evaluations_value, list):
        raise RouteResolutionError(
            "route_decision_snapshot_invalid",
            "运行中的路线决定缺少条件证据",
        )
    evaluations: list[RouteEvaluation] = []
    for value in evaluations_value:
        if (
            not isinstance(value, Mapping)
            or not isinstance(value.get("branch"), str)
            or not isinstance(value.get("matched"), bool)
            or not isinstance(value.get("condition_hash"), str)
            or not isinstance(value.get("referenced_facts"), Mapping)
        ):
            raise RouteResolutionError(
                "route_decision_snapshot_invalid",
                "运行中的路线条件证据损坏",
            )
        evaluations.append(
            RouteEvaluation(
                branch=value["branch"],
                matched=value["matched"],
                condition_hash=value["condition_hash"],
                referenced_facts=dict(value["referenced_facts"]),
            )
        )
    activated = payload.get("activated_branches")
    skipped = payload.get("skipped_branches")
    if (
        not isinstance(payload.get("branch_node_id"), str)
        or not isinstance(payload.get("branch_key"), str)
        or payload.get("mode") not in {"parallel", "exclusive"}
        or not isinstance(activated, list)
        or any(not isinstance(item, str) for item in activated)
        or not isinstance(skipped, list)
        or any(not isinstance(item, str) for item in skipped)
    ):
        raise RouteResolutionError(
            "route_decision_snapshot_invalid",
            "运行中的路线决定身份损坏",
        )
    return RouteDecision(
        branch_node_id=payload["branch_node_id"],
        branch_key=payload["branch_key"],
        mode=payload["mode"],
        activated_branches=tuple(activated),
        skipped_branches=tuple(skipped),
        evaluations=tuple(evaluations),
    )


def _node_by_id(
    manifest: Mapping[str, Any], node_instance_id: str
) -> Mapping[str, Any]:
    nodes = manifest.get("nodes")
    if isinstance(nodes, list):
        for node in nodes:
            if (
                isinstance(node, Mapping)
                and node.get("node_instance_id") == node_instance_id
            ):
                return node
    raise RouteResolutionError(
        "route_branch_unknown",
        f"工作流中找不到分流节点：{node_instance_id}",
    )


def _branch_edges(
    manifest: Mapping[str, Any], branch_node_id: str
) -> dict[str, Mapping[str, Any]]:
    result: dict[str, Mapping[str, Any]] = {}
    edges = manifest.get("edges")
    if not isinstance(edges, list):
        return result
    for edge in edges:
        if not isinstance(edge, Mapping) or edge.get("from") != branch_node_id:
            continue
        branch = edge.get("branch")
        if isinstance(branch, str) and branch:
            result[branch] = edge
    return result


def resolve_route_decision(
    manifest: Mapping[str, Any],
    branch_node_id: str,
    *,
    input_manifest: Mapping[str, Any],
    upstream_outputs: Mapping[str, Any],
) -> RouteDecision:
    """Resolve one frozen decision without defaults or first-match behavior."""

    node = _node_by_id(manifest, branch_node_id)
    if node.get("kind") != "branch":
        raise RouteResolutionError(
            "route_node_kind_invalid",
            "路线决定只能由分流节点产生",
        )
    config = node.get("config")
    if not isinstance(config, Mapping):
        raise RouteResolutionError(
            "route_branch_config_invalid",
            "分流节点缺少冻结配置",
        )
    branch_key = config.get("branch_key")
    branches = config.get("branches")
    mode = config.get("mode", "parallel")
    if (
        not isinstance(branch_key, str)
        or not isinstance(branches, list)
        or any(not isinstance(item, str) or not item for item in branches)
        or mode not in {"parallel", "exclusive"}
    ):
        raise RouteResolutionError(
            "route_branch_config_invalid",
            "分流节点冻结配置不完整",
        )
    ordered_branches = tuple(branches)
    if mode == "parallel":
        return RouteDecision(
            branch_node_id=branch_node_id,
            branch_key=branch_key,
            mode=mode,
            activated_branches=ordered_branches,
            skipped_branches=(),
            evaluations=(),
        )

    edges = _branch_edges(manifest, branch_node_id)
    facts = {"input": dict(input_manifest), "steps": dict(upstream_outputs)}
    evaluations: list[RouteEvaluation] = []
    for branch in ordered_branches:
        edge = edges.get(branch)
        condition = edge.get("condition") if edge is not None else None
        if not isinstance(condition, Mapping):
            raise RouteResolutionError(
                "exclusive_route_condition_missing",
                f"路线 {branch} 缺少冻结条件",
            )
        try:
            evaluated = evaluate_condition(condition, facts)
        except WorkflowConditionError as exc:
            raise RouteResolutionError(exc.code, str(exc)) from exc
        evaluations.append(
            RouteEvaluation(
                branch=branch,
                matched=evaluated.matched,
                condition_hash=evaluated.condition_hash,
                referenced_facts=evaluated.referenced_facts,
            )
        )
    matched = tuple(item.branch for item in evaluations if item.matched)
    if not matched:
        raise RouteResolutionError(
            "exclusive_route_no_match",
            "按条件选择路线时没有任何路线命中，已停止运行",
        )
    if len(matched) > 1:
        raise RouteResolutionError(
            "exclusive_route_multiple_matches",
            "按条件选择路线时有多条路线同时命中，已停止运行",
        )
    return RouteDecision(
        branch_node_id=branch_node_id,
        branch_key=branch_key,
        mode=mode,
        activated_branches=matched,
        skipped_branches=tuple(
            branch for branch in ordered_branches if branch not in matched
        ),
        evaluations=tuple(evaluations),
    )


def _join_for_branch(
    manifest: Mapping[str, Any], branch_key: str
) -> str | None:
    nodes = manifest.get("nodes")
    if not isinstance(nodes, list):
        return None
    for node in nodes:
        if not isinstance(node, Mapping) or node.get("kind") != "join":
            continue
        config = node.get("config")
        if isinstance(config, Mapping) and config.get("join_for") == branch_key:
            node_id = node.get("node_instance_id")
            return node_id if isinstance(node_id, str) else None
    return None


def _descendants_before_join(
    outgoing: Mapping[str, list[str]], start: str, join_id: str | None
) -> set[str]:
    pending = [start]
    result: set[str] = set()
    while pending:
        current = pending.pop()
        if current == join_id or current in result:
            continue
        result.add(current)
        pending.extend(outgoing.get(current, []))
    return result


def route_skipped_node_ids(
    manifest: Mapping[str, Any], decision: RouteDecision
) -> set[str]:
    """Return nodes owned only by unselected routes, excluding the shared join."""

    outgoing: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    edges = manifest.get("edges")
    if not isinstance(edges, list):
        return set()
    branch_targets: dict[str, str] = {}
    for edge in edges:
        if not isinstance(edge, Mapping):
            continue
        source = edge.get("from")
        target = edge.get("to")
        if isinstance(source, str) and isinstance(target, str):
            outgoing.setdefault(source, []).append(target)
        if source == decision.branch_node_id:
            branch = edge.get("branch")
            if isinstance(branch, str) and isinstance(target, str):
                branch_targets[branch] = target
    join_id = _join_for_branch(manifest, decision.branch_key)
    skipped: set[str] = set()
    for branch in decision.skipped_branches:
        target = branch_targets.get(branch)
        if target is not None:
            skipped.update(_descendants_before_join(outgoing, target, join_id))
    active: set[str] = set()
    for branch in decision.activated_branches:
        target = branch_targets.get(branch)
        if target is not None:
            active.update(_descendants_before_join(outgoing, target, join_id))
    return skipped - active

class WorkflowRuntimeError(ValueError):
    def __init__(self, code: str, message: str, *, status_code: int = 400):
        super().__init__(message)
        self.code = code
        self.status_code = status_code


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ProductionRunRequest:
    workflow_version_id: int
    idempotency_key: str
    category_key: str | None
    queue_class: str
    runtime_context: Mapping[str, Any]
    input_manifest: Mapping[str, Any]
    owner: str
    reason: str = ""
    source_type: str | None = None
    source_id: int | None = None
    source_run_id: int | None = None
    environment: str = "dry_run"
    model_execution_mode: str = "simulated"


def _now(value: datetime | None = None) -> datetime:
    return value or datetime.now(timezone.utc)


def _hash(value: Any) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(canonical_json(value).encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()


def _is_sha256(value: Any) -> bool:
    return (
        isinstance(value, str)
        and len(value) == 64
        and all(character in "0123456789abcdef" for character in value)
    )


def _content_ingress_run_key(event_id: str, snapshot_hash: str) -> str:
    raw = f"content-ingress:{event_id}:{snapshot_hash}"
    if len(raw) <= 200:
        return raw
    event_digest = hashlib.sha256(event_id.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()[:48]
    return f"content-ingress:{event_digest}:{snapshot_hash}"


def _incremental_frozen_contract(
    manifest: Mapping[str, Any],
) -> tuple[int, str]:
    nodes = manifest.get("nodes")
    for node in nodes if isinstance(nodes, list) else []:
        if not isinstance(node, Mapping) or node.get("kind") != "upstream_connector":
            continue
        config = node.get("config")
        snapshot = config.get("connector_snapshot") if isinstance(config, Mapping) else None
        contract_id = snapshot.get("connector_contract_id") if isinstance(snapshot, Mapping) else None
        contract_key = snapshot.get("contract_key") if isinstance(snapshot, Mapping) else None
        if (
            isinstance(contract_id, int)
            and not isinstance(contract_id, bool)
            and isinstance(contract_key, str)
            and contract_key
        ):
            return contract_id, contract_key
    raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
        "incremental_upstream_contract_missing",
        "冻结增量机制缺少上游读取合同",
        status_code=409,
    )


def _authorize_real_execution_request(
    db: Session,
    request: ProductionRunRequest,
    workflow_version: WorkflowVersion,
) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], Mapping[str, Any] | None]:
    """Rebuild paid authority from DB facts; request fields are never authority."""

    runtime = dict(request.runtime_context)
    if (
        request.source_type == "baseline_regression_item"
        and request.source_id is not None
        and runtime.get("run_mode") == "stock_regression"
        and runtime.get("real_execution_confirmed") is True
        and isinstance(runtime.get("real_execution_budget"), Mapping)
        and isinstance(runtime.get("real_execution_node_limits"), Mapping)
    ):
        return runtime, None
    if (
        request.source_type != "content_ingress_event"
        or not isinstance(request.source_id, int)
        or isinstance(request.source_id, bool)
        or request.queue_class != "production_batch"
        or runtime.get("run_mode") != "incremental"
    ):
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
            "real_model_execution_forbidden",
            "真实模型评测只能由已确认费用的存量回归或冻结增量策略创建",
            status_code=403,
        )
    event = db.scalar(
        select(ContentIngressEvent)
        .where(ContentIngressEvent.id == request.source_id)
        .with_for_update()
    )
    package_id = runtime.get("material_package_id")
    package = (
        db.scalar(
            select(MaterialPackage)
            .where(MaterialPackage.id == package_id)
            .with_for_update()
        )
        if isinstance(package_id, int) and not isinstance(package_id, bool)
        else None
    )
    if event is None or event.status != "applied" or package is None:
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
            "real_model_execution_forbidden",
            "真实增量运行缺少已接纳事件或冻结素材包",
            status_code=403,
        )
    record = db.get(ContentRecord, event.content_record_id)
    try:
        mechanism = json.loads(package.mechanism_snapshot_json or "{}")
    except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
            "incremental_snapshot_invalid",
            "冻结增量机制快照损坏",
            status_code=409,
        ) from exc
    frozen_workflow = mechanism.get("workflow_snapshot") if isinstance(mechanism, Mapping) else None
