    frozen_workflow_meta = frozen_workflow.get("workflow") if isinstance(frozen_workflow, Mapping) else None
    frozen_manifest = frozen_workflow_meta.get("manifest") if isinstance(frozen_workflow_meta, Mapping) else None
    frozen_input = (
        mechanism.get("input_manifest")
        if isinstance(mechanism, Mapping)
        else None
    )
    frozen_input_hash = (
        mechanism.get("input_manifest_hash")
        if isinstance(mechanism, Mapping)
        else None
    )
    frozen_asset_id = (
        frozen_input.get("asset_id")
        if isinstance(frozen_input, Mapping)
        else None
    )
    item = (
        db.scalar(
            select(MaterialPackageItem).where(
                MaterialPackageItem.package_id == package.id,
                MaterialPackageItem.asset_id == frozen_asset_id,
            )
        )
        if isinstance(frozen_asset_id, int)
        and not isinstance(frozen_asset_id, bool)
        else None
    )
    snapshot_hash = mechanism.get("snapshot_hash") if isinstance(mechanism, Mapping) else None
    frozen_digest = (
        hashlib.sha256(canonical_json(frozen_workflow).encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
        if isinstance(frozen_workflow, Mapping)
        else None
    )
    current_manifest = persisted_workflow_manifest(workflow_version)
    current_manifest_hash = hashlib.sha256(canonical_json(current_manifest).encode('utf-8')).hexdigest()
    legacy_step_hash = hashlib.sha256(workflow_version.step_manifest_json.encode('utf-8')).hexdigest()
    identity_kind = (
        'canonical_manifest_sha256' if workflow_version.canonical_hash == current_manifest_hash else
        'legacy_step_manifest_sha256' if workflow_version.canonical_hash == legacy_step_hash else None
    )
    if (
        not isinstance(record, ContentRecord)
        or item is None
        or package.status != "incremental_pending"
        or package.source != "production_import"
        or package.category_key != request.category_key
        or package.workflow_version_id != workflow_version.id
        or mechanism.get("schema_version")
        != "category-incremental-mechanism-snapshot-v1"
        or mechanism.get("category_key") != request.category_key
        or mechanism.get("content_ingress_event_db_id") != event.id
        or mechanism.get("content_ingress_event_id") != event.event_id
        or mechanism.get("content_record_id") != record.id
        or mechanism.get("workflow_version_id") != workflow_version.id
        or not _is_sha256(snapshot_hash)
        or frozen_digest != snapshot_hash
        or not isinstance(frozen_manifest, Mapping)
        or frozen_workflow_meta.get("workflow_version_id") != workflow_version.id
        or frozen_workflow_meta.get("canonical_hash") != workflow_version.canonical_hash
        or identity_kind is None
    ):
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
            "incremental_snapshot_mismatch",
            "增量素材包、接入事实与冻结工作流不一致",
            status_code=409,
        )
    # A legacy identity covers only the raw node column. Independently compare
    # the ENTIRE manifest, including edges/resource policy, against the hashed
    # package. Reapply normal connector freezing so legitimate SQL contract
    # enrichment is compared rather than mistaken for manifest drift.
    from .workflow_connector_executor import freeze_workflow_connector_contracts
    frozen_context = frozen_workflow.get('runtime_context')
    frozen_context = frozen_context if isinstance(frozen_context, Mapping) else {}
    try:
        comparable_manifest = freeze_workflow_connector_contracts(
            db, current_manifest, runtime_context=frozen_context)
    except WorkflowConnectorError as exc:
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError('incremental_snapshot_mismatch', str(exc), status_code=409) from exc
    if canonical_json(comparable_manifest) != canonical_json(dict(frozen_manifest)):
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError('incremental_snapshot_mismatch', '当前完整工作流与素材包冻结机制发生漂移', status_code=409)
    verified_identity = {
        'identity_kind': identity_kind,
        'saved_hash': workflow_version.canonical_hash,
        'persisted_manifest_hash': current_manifest_hash,
        'frozen_manifest_hash': hashlib.sha256(canonical_json(dict(frozen_manifest)).encode('utf-8')).hexdigest(),
    }
    from .incremental_input import incremental_autorun_policy
    try:
        policy = incremental_autorun_policy(frozen_workflow)
    except IncrementalInputError as exc:
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError('real_model_execution_forbidden', str(exc), status_code=403) from exc
    frozen_context = frozen_workflow.get('runtime_context', {})
    execution_policy = frozen_context.get('incremental_execution_policy')
    # A new explicit binding authorizes the default enabled policy. Legacy
    # manifest-only real authority keeps its previous explicit enabled gate.
    legacy_policy_root = frozen_manifest.get('resource_policy')
    legacy_policy = legacy_policy_root.get('incremental_autorun') if isinstance(legacy_policy_root, Mapping) else None
    if execution_policy is None and (not isinstance(legacy_policy, Mapping) or legacy_policy.get('enabled') is not True):
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError('real_model_execution_forbidden', '冻结增量策略没有启用真实模型自动运行', status_code=403)
    if execution_policy is not None and (
        execution_policy['binding_id'] != mechanism.get('binding_id')
        or execution_policy['binding_lock_version'] != mechanism.get('binding_lock_version')
    ):
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError('incremental_snapshot_mismatch', '增量运行授权绑定与素材包不一致', status_code=409)
    if (
        not isinstance(policy, Mapping)
        or policy.get("enabled") is not True
        or policy.get("model_execution_mode") != "real"
    ):
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
            "real_model_execution_forbidden",
            "冻结增量策略没有启用真实模型自动运行",
            status_code=403,
        )
    input_manifest = dict(request.input_manifest)
    expected_input_manifest = (
        dict(frozen_input) if isinstance(frozen_input, Mapping) else None
    )
    input_hash = frozen_input_hash if isinstance(frozen_input_hash, str) else ""
    if expected_input_manifest is not None and item is not None:
        try:
            validate_frozen_content_ingress_input(
                db,
                event=event,
                input_manifest=expected_input_manifest,
                input_manifest_hash=input_hash,
                package_category_key=package.category_key,
                package_item_asset_id=item.asset_id,
            )
        except IncrementalInputError as exc:
            raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
                "content_ingress_binding_mismatch",
                str(exc),
                status_code=409,
            ) from exc
    contract_id, contract_key = _incremental_frozen_contract(frozen_manifest)
    existing_source_run = db.scalar(
        select(ProductionRun).where(
            ProductionRun.source_type == "content_ingress_event",
            ProductionRun.source_id == event.id,
        )
    )
    if (
        existing_source_run is not None
        and existing_source_run.idempotency_key != request.idempotency_key
    ):
        from .incremental_reassessment import validate_authority
        if not validate_authority(db, request, package):
            raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
                "content_ingress_run_conflict",
                "同一内容接入事件只能绑定一条工作流运行；失败重评须有独立服务端授权",
                status_code=409,
            )
    if (
        expected_input_manifest is None
        or canonical_json(input_manifest)
        != canonical_json(expected_input_manifest)
        or request.idempotency_key != _content_ingress_run_key(event.event_id, snapshot_hash)
    ):
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
            "content_ingress_binding_mismatch",
            "真实增量运行输入或幂等身份与接入事实不一致",
            status_code=409,
        )
    from .incremental_autorun import _real_execution_plan, IncrementalAutorunError
    try:
        # Source facts above have been hash-checked. Select and price once,
        # retaining that exact route for the eventual executor.
        budget, node_limits, route = _real_execution_plan(db, snapshot=mechanism, include_route=True)
    except IncrementalAutorunError as exc:
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
            exc.code,
            str(exc),
            status_code=exc.status_code,
        ) from exc
    normalized = {
        **runtime,
        "inference_route": route,
        "incremental_workflow_identity": verified_identity,
        "content_ingress_event_db_id": event.id,
        "content_ingress_event_id": event.event_id,
        "content_ingress_payload_hash": event.payload_hash,
        "content_record_id": record.id,
        "material_package_id": package.id,
        "incremental_mechanism_snapshot_hash": snapshot_hash,
        "upstream_intake_contract_version_id": contract_id,
        "upstream_intake_contract_key": contract_key,
        "upstream_intake_payload_hash": input_hash,
        "real_execution_confirmed": True,
        "real_execution_authority": "frozen_incremental_autorun_policy",
        "real_execution_budget": budget,
        "real_execution_node_limits": node_limits,
        # Take authority only from the stored, hash-checked package; caller
        # runtime context and content payload cannot supply this authorization.
        "incremental_execution_policy": execution_policy,
        "incremental_binding_id": frozen_context.get('incremental_binding_id'),
        "incremental_binding_lock_version": frozen_context.get('incremental_binding_lock_version'),
    }
    return normalized, frozen_manifest


def _script_version_for_reference(
    db: Session,
    reference: str,
) -> ScriptVersion:
    script_key, version_name = reference.rsplit("@", 1)
    definition = db.scalar(
        select(ScriptDefinition).where(ScriptDefinition.script_key == script_key)
    )
    if definition is None:
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
            "script_definition_unknown",
            f"工作流引用的脚本不存在：{reference}",
            status_code=409,
        )
    version = db.scalar(
        select(ScriptVersion).where(
            ScriptVersion.script_definition_id == definition.id,
            ScriptVersion.version == version_name,
        )
    )
    if version is None:
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
            "script_version_unknown",
            f"工作流引用的脚本版本不存在：{reference}",
            status_code=409,
        )
    return version


def _dispatch_max_attempts(
    db: Session,
    attempt: ProductionStepAttempt,
) -> int:
    return runtime_step_retry_policy(db, attempt)["max_attempts"]


def _frozen_runtime_node(
    run: ProductionRun,
    step_key: str,
) -> Mapping[str, Any] | None:
    try:
        snapshot = read_run_snapshot_document(run)
    except json.JSONDecodeError:
        return None
    workflow = snapshot.get("workflow")
    manifest = workflow.get("manifest") if isinstance(workflow, Mapping) else None
    nodes = manifest.get("nodes") if isinstance(manifest, Mapping) else None
    return next(
        (
            node
            for node in (nodes if isinstance(nodes, list) else [])
            if isinstance(node, Mapping)
            and node.get("node_instance_id") == step_key
        ),
        None,
    )


def _retry_policy_from_node(
    node: Mapping[str, Any] | None,
    *,
    fallback_max_attempts: int,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    value = node.get("retry_policy") if isinstance(node, Mapping) else None
    if not isinstance(value, Mapping):
        return {
            "kind": "fixed",
            "max_attempts": fallback_max_attempts,
            "delay_seconds": 0.0,
        }
    kind = value.get("kind")
    if kind == "none":
        return {"kind": "none", "max_attempts": 1, "delay_seconds": 0.0}
    # Older immutable workflow snapshots omitted one or both values because
    # the composer script owned them. Preserve those versions with the script
    # defaults while every newly generated v3 manifest freezes explicit values.
    max_attempts = value.get("max_attempts", fallback_max_attempts)
    delay_seconds = value.get("delay_seconds", 0)
    if (
        kind not in {"fixed", "exponential"}
        or not isinstance(max_attempts, int)
        or isinstance(max_attempts, bool)
        or not 1 <= max_attempts <= 5
        or not isinstance(delay_seconds, (int, float))
        or isinstance(delay_seconds, bool)
        or delay_seconds < 0
        or not math.isfinite(float(delay_seconds))
        or delay_seconds > MAX_WORKFLOW_RETRY_DELAY_SECONDS
    ):
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
            "runtime_retry_policy_invalid",
            "冻结节点重试策略损坏",
            status_code=409,
        )
    return {
        "kind": kind,
        "max_attempts": max_attempts,
        "delay_seconds": float(delay_seconds),
    }


def runtime_step_retry_policy(
    db: Session,
    attempt: ProductionStepAttempt,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    script = db.get(ScriptVersion, attempt.script_version_id)
    if script is None:
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
            "runtime_evidence_missing",
            "步骤关联的脚本版本不存在，不能创建运行队列项",
            status_code=409,
        )
    run = db.get(ProductionRun, attempt.run_id)
    node = _frozen_runtime_node(run, attempt.step_key) if run is not None else None
    return _retry_policy_from_node(
        node,
        fallback_max_attempts=script.max_attempts,
    )


def runtime_step_error_policy(
    db: Session,
    attempt: ProductionStepAttempt,
) -> str:
    run = db.get(ProductionRun, attempt.run_id)
    node = _frozen_runtime_node(run, attempt.step_key) if run is not None else None
    value = node.get("error_policy") if isinstance(node, Mapping) else None
    kind = value.get("kind") if isinstance(value, Mapping) else "fail"
    return kind if kind in {"fail", "continue", "skip", "manual_review"} else "fail"


def _retry_policy_delay(policy: Mapping[str, Any], attempt_no: int) -> float:
    base = float(policy.get("delay_seconds") or 0)
    if policy.get("kind") == "exponential":
        return min(
            base * (2 ** max(0, attempt_no - 1)),
            float(MAX_WORKFLOW_RETRY_DELAY_SECONDS),
        )
    return base


def _close_dispatch(
    dispatch: RuntimeDispatchItem,
    *,
    status: str,
    error_code: str = "",
    error_message: str = "",
    now: datetime | None = None,
) -> None:
    if status not in {"completed", "canceled", "dead_lettered"}:
        raise ValueError(f"unsupported dispatch terminal status: {status}")
    dispatch.status = status
    dispatch.last_error_code = error_code
    dispatch.last_error_message = error_message
    dispatch.dead_lettered_at = _now(now) if status == "dead_lettered" else None
    dispatch.lease_owner = None
    dispatch.lease_token = None
    dispatch.leased_at = None
    dispatch.heartbeat_at = None
    dispatch.lease_expires_at = None


def _bounded_business_key(value: str) -> str:
    if len(value) <= 240:
        return value
    digest = hashlib.sha256(value.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
    return f"business-sha256:{digest}"


def _incremental_projection_outbox_request(
    db: Session,
    run: ProductionRun,
    attempt: ProductionStepAttempt,
    output_manifest: Mapping[str, Any],
) -> RuntimeOutboxRequest | None:
    return build_incremental_projection_outbox_request(
        db,
        run,
        step_key=attempt.step_key,
        output_manifest=output_manifest,
    )


def build_incremental_projection_outbox_request(
    db: Session,
    run: ProductionRun,
    *,
    step_key: str,
    output_manifest: Mapping[str, Any],
    review_state: str = "auto",
    change: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
) -> RuntimeOutboxRequest | None:
    """ADR-0064 先发后审：为增量运行的写下游节点生成 Outbox 请求。

    业务身份键只表达素材身份（不含机制版本），同一素材永远对应下游同一行；
    revision 取该身份下已有事件的最大值 +1；review_state=reviewed 只由人工纠偏重发使用，
    机制版本作为普通字段随事件写入供追溯。
    """

    if review_state not in OUTBOX_REVIEW_STATES:
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
            "outbox_review_state_invalid",
            "下游事件 review_state 只能是 auto 或 reviewed",
            status_code=422,
        )
    try:
        snapshot = read_run_snapshot_document(run)
    except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
            "runtime_snapshot_invalid",
            "运行冻结快照无法解析，不能生成下游事件",
            status_code=409,
        ) from exc
    runtime_context = snapshot.get("runtime_context")
    if not isinstance(runtime_context, Mapping):
        return None
    if runtime_context.get("run_mode") != "incremental":
        return None
    workflow = snapshot.get("workflow")
    manifest = workflow.get("manifest") if isinstance(workflow, Mapping) else None
    nodes = manifest.get("nodes") if isinstance(manifest, Mapping) else None
    if not isinstance(nodes, list):
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
            "runtime_snapshot_invalid",
            "增量运行冻结快照缺少工作流节点",
            status_code=409,
        )
    node = next(
        (
            item
            for item in nodes
            if isinstance(item, Mapping)
            and item.get("node_instance_id") == step_key
        ),
        None,
    )
    if node is None:
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
            "runtime_snapshot_invalid",
            "增量运行冻结快照缺少当前节点",
            status_code=409,
        )
    if node.get("kind") != "downstream_projection":
        return None
    from .incremental_routing import validate_run_route, persist_shadow_result
    route = validate_run_route(db, run)
    if route is not None and route["destination"] == "shadow":
        persist_shadow_result(db, run, route=route, step_key=step_key, output_manifest=output_manifest)
        return None
    config = node.get("config")
    connector_key = (
        config.get("connector_key") if isinstance(config, Mapping) else None
    )
    connector_snapshot = (
        config.get("connector_snapshot")
        if isinstance(config, Mapping)
        else None
    )
    if (
        not isinstance(connector_key, str)
        or not connector_key.strip()
        or ".unconfigured." in connector_key
    ):
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
            "downstream_connector_unconfigured",
            "增量工作流的写入下游节点尚未配置目标合同",
            status_code=409,
        )
    target_contract_version_id = (
        connector_snapshot.get("connector_contract_id")
        if isinstance(connector_snapshot, Mapping)
        else None
    )
    if (
        isinstance(target_contract_version_id, bool)
        or not isinstance(target_contract_version_id, int)
        or target_contract_version_id < 1
        or connector_snapshot.get("contract_key") != connector_key
        or connector_snapshot.get("direction") != "downstream"
    ):
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
            "downstream_connector_snapshot_missing",
            "增量工作流的写入下游节点缺少冻结合同版本",
            status_code=409,
        )
    frozen_input = runtime_context.get("input_manifest")
    frozen_input = frozen_input if isinstance(frozen_input, Mapping) else {}
    identity: str
    if isinstance(frozen_input.get("content_key"), str) and str(
        frozen_input["content_key"]
    ).strip():
        identity = str(frozen_input["content_key"]).strip()
    elif frozen_input.get("asset_id") is not None:
        identity = f"asset:{frozen_input['asset_id']}"
    elif run.source_type and run.source_id is not None:
        identity = f"{run.source_type}:{run.source_id}"
    else:
        identity = f"idempotency:{run.idempotency_key}"
    category = run.category_key or str(manifest.get("category_key") or "unknown")
    business_key = _bounded_business_key(
        f"{category}:{identity}:node:{step_key}"
    )
    from .incremental_source_revision import authoritative_source_revision, source_revision_advances
    source_revision = authoritative_source_revision(db, run)
    source_payload = {"source_type": run.source_type, "source_id": run.source_id,
                      "asset_identity": identity, "source_revision": source_revision}
    latest = latest_outbox_event(
        db,
        target_contract_key=connector_key,
        business_idempotency_key=business_key,
        current_read=True,
    )
    if (route is not None or source_revision is not None) and latest is not None and latest.review_state == "reviewed" and review_state == "auto":
        previous_source_payload = load_snapshot_blob(latest.payload_blob)
        if not source_revision_advances(previous_source_payload, source_payload):
            raise WorkflowRuntimeError("incremental_review_precedence", "同一源版本已有人工纠偏，禁止自动结果覆盖", status_code=409)
    revision = int(latest.revision) + 1 if latest is not None else 1
    previous_result: Any = None
    if latest is not None:
        try:
            previous_payload = load_snapshot_blob(latest.payload_blob)
        except Exception:  # pragma: no cover - 旧事件 blob 损坏只影响字段级 diff
            previous_payload = {}
        previous_result = (
            previous_payload.get("result")
            if isinstance(previous_payload, Mapping)
            else None
        )
    change = change if isinstance(change, Mapping) else {}
    change_record = build_change_record(
        trigger_kind=str(change.get("trigger_kind") or "auto_run"),
        actor=str(change.get("actor") or run.requested_by or "runtime"),
        reason=str(change.get("reason") or ""),
        correction_event_id=str(change.get("correction_event_id") or ""),
        previous=latest,
        previous_result=previous_result,
        current_result=dict(output_manifest),
    )
    event_digest = hashlib.sha256(
        canonical_json(
            {
                "target_contract_key": connector_key,
                "target_contract_version_id": target_contract_version_id,
                "business_idempotency_key": business_key,
                "revision": revision,
                "review_state": review_state,
                "mechanism_version_id": run.workflow_version_id,
                "node_instance_id": step_key,
            }
        ).encode("utf-8")
    ).hexdigest()
    wait_metadata = _incremental_projection_wait_metadata(run, step_key)
    payload = {
        "schema_version": "runtime-outbox-payload-v1",
        "target_contract_key": connector_key,
        "target_contract_version_id": target_contract_version_id,
        "target_contract_version": connector_snapshot.get("contract_version"),
        "target_schema_fingerprint": connector_snapshot.get(
            "schema_fingerprint"
        ),
        "business_idempotency_key": business_key,
        "revision": revision,
        "review_state": review_state,
        "asset_identity": identity,
        "change": change_record,
        "mechanism_version_id": run.workflow_version_id,
        "category_key": category,
        "source_type": run.source_type,
        "source_id": run.source_id,
        "origin_run_key": run.run_key,
        **({"source_revision": source_revision} if source_revision is not None else {}),
        **({"incremental_route": route} if route is not None else {}),
        "node_instance_id": step_key,
        "result": dict(output_manifest),
        **(
            {
                "continuation": {
                    "correlation_id": wait_metadata["correlation_id"],
                    "continuation_key": wait_metadata["continuation_key"],
                    "expected_contract_version_id": wait_metadata[
                        "expected_contract_version_id"
                    ],
                }
            }
            if wait_metadata is not None
            else {}
        ),
    }
    return RuntimeOutboxRequest(
        event_key=f"runtime-outbox:{event_digest}",
        target_contract_key=connector_key,
        target_contract_version_id=target_contract_version_id,
        business_idempotency_key=business_key,
        mechanism_version_id=run.workflow_version_id,
        payload=payload,
        node_instance_id=step_key,
        revision=revision,
        review_state=review_state,
    )


def _incremental_projection_wait_metadata(
    run: ProductionRun,
    step_key: str,
) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    try:
        snapshot = read_run_snapshot_document(run)
    except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
            "runtime_snapshot_invalid",
            "运行冻结快照无法解析，不能创建外部等待",
            status_code=409,
        ) from exc
    runtime_context = snapshot.get("runtime_context")
    if (
        not isinstance(runtime_context, Mapping)
        or runtime_context.get("run_mode") != "incremental"
    ):
        return None
    workflow = snapshot.get("workflow")
    manifest = workflow.get("manifest") if isinstance(workflow, Mapping) else None
    nodes = manifest.get("nodes") if isinstance(manifest, Mapping) else None
    if not isinstance(nodes, list):
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
            "runtime_snapshot_invalid",
            "增量运行冻结快照缺少工作流节点",
            status_code=409,
        )
    current_node = next(
        (
            node
            for node in nodes
            if isinstance(node, Mapping)
            and node.get("node_instance_id") == step_key
        ),
        None,
    )
    config = (
        current_node.get("config") if isinstance(current_node, Mapping) else None
    )
    if (
        not isinstance(config, Mapping)
        or config.get("completion_mode", "continue") != "await_follow_up"
    ):
        return None
    continuation_key = config.get("continuation_key")
    resume_node_id = config.get("resume_node_instance_id")
    wait_seconds = config.get("wait_timeout_seconds")
    timeout_policy = config.get("timeout_policy")
    resume_node = next(
        (
            node
            for node in nodes
            if isinstance(node, Mapping)
            and node.get("node_instance_id") == resume_node_id
        ),
        None,
    )
    resume_config = (
        resume_node.get("config") if isinstance(resume_node, Mapping) else None
    )
    connector_snapshot = (
        resume_config.get("connector_snapshot")
        if isinstance(resume_config, Mapping)
        else None
    )
    expected_contract_version_id = (
        connector_snapshot.get("connector_contract_id")
        if isinstance(connector_snapshot, Mapping)
        else None
    )
    if (
        not isinstance(continuation_key, str)
        or not continuation_key
        or not isinstance(resume_node_id, str)
        or not resume_node_id
        or isinstance(wait_seconds, bool)
        or not isinstance(wait_seconds, int)
        or not 1 <= wait_seconds <= 604800
        or timeout_policy not in {"manual_review", "fail"}
        or not isinstance(expected_contract_version_id, int)
        or isinstance(expected_contract_version_id, bool)
        or expected_contract_version_id < 1
    ):
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
            "runtime_continuation_snapshot_invalid",
            "等待外部处理节点的冻结续跑合同不完整",
            status_code=409,
        )
    correlation_digest = hashlib.sha256(
        canonical_json(
            {
                "run_key": run.run_key,
                "request_node_instance_id": step_key,
                "resume_node_instance_id": resume_node_id,
                "continuation_key": continuation_key,
                "expected_contract_version_id": expected_contract_version_id,
            }
        ).encode("utf-8")
    ).hexdigest()
    return {
        "continuation_key": continuation_key,
        "resume_node_instance_id": resume_node_id,
        "wait_timeout_seconds": wait_seconds,
        "timeout_policy": timeout_policy,
        "expected_contract_version_id": expected_contract_version_id,
        "correlation_id": f"wait_{correlation_digest}",
    }


def _image_first_seen_wait_metadata(
    db: Session,
    run: ProductionRun,
    attempt: ProductionStepAttempt,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    try:
        snapshot = read_run_snapshot_document(run)
    except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
            "runtime_snapshot_invalid",
            "运行冻结快照无法解析，不能创建图片查询等待",
            status_code=409,
        ) from exc
    runtime_context = snapshot.get("runtime_context")
    workflow = snapshot.get("workflow")
    manifest = workflow.get("manifest") if isinstance(workflow, Mapping) else None
    nodes = manifest.get("nodes") if isinstance(manifest, Mapping) else None
    node = next(
        (
            item
            for item in nodes or []
            if isinstance(item, Mapping)
            and item.get("node_instance_id") == attempt.step_key
        ),
        None,
    )
    config = node.get("config") if isinstance(node, Mapping) else None
    if (
        not isinstance(runtime_context, Mapping)
        or runtime_context.get("run_mode") != "incremental"
        or not isinstance(node, Mapping)
        or node.get("kind") != "image_first_seen_lookup"
        or not isinstance(config, Mapping)
    ):
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
            "runtime_first_seen_wait_invalid",
            "当前步骤不是可等待回调的增量图片查询节点",
            status_code=409,
        )
    provider_key = config.get("provider_connector_key")
    request_contract_id = config.get("request_contract_version_id")
    response_contract_id = config.get("response_contract_version_id")
    wait_seconds = config.get("wait_timeout_seconds")
    timeout_policy = config.get("timeout_policy")
    if (
        not isinstance(provider_key, str)
        or not provider_key.strip()
        or isinstance(request_contract_id, bool)
        or not isinstance(request_contract_id, int)
        or request_contract_id < 1
        or isinstance(response_contract_id, bool)
        or not isinstance(response_contract_id, int)
        or response_contract_id < 1
        or isinstance(wait_seconds, bool)
        or not isinstance(wait_seconds, int)
        or not 1 <= wait_seconds <= 604800
        or timeout_policy not in {"manual_review", "fail"}
    ):
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
            "runtime_first_seen_wait_invalid",
            "图片查询节点的冻结供应商合同不完整",
            status_code=409,
        )
    request_contract = db.get(ConnectorContractVersion, request_contract_id)
    response_contract = db.get(ConnectorContractVersion, response_contract_id)
    if (
        request_contract is None
        or request_contract.direction != "downstream"
        or request_contract.category_key != run.category_key
        or request_contract.status != "active"
        or response_contract is None
        or response_contract.direction != "upstream"
        or response_contract.category_key != run.category_key
        or response_contract.status != "active"
    ):
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
            "runtime_first_seen_contract_mismatch",
            "图片查询节点的请求或回调合同与运行类目不一致",
            status_code=409,
        )
    correlation_digest = hashlib.sha256(
        canonical_json(
            {
                "run_key": run.run_key,
                "node_instance_id": attempt.step_key,
                "provider_connector_key": provider_key,
                "request_contract_version_id": request_contract.id,
                "response_contract_version_id": response_contract.id,
            }
        ).encode("utf-8")
    ).hexdigest()
    return {
        "provider_connector_key": provider_key.strip(),
        "request_contract": request_contract,
        "response_contract": response_contract,
        "wait_timeout_seconds": wait_seconds,
        "timeout_policy": timeout_policy,
        "correlation_id": f"first_seen_{correlation_digest}",
        "continuation_key": f"first-seen:{correlation_digest[:32]}",
        "runtime_context": runtime_context,
    }


def image_first_seen_wait_exists(db: Session, run_id: int, step_key: str) -> bool:
    """这个首见节点是否已经提交过等待记录（即请求已发出）。

    判断「发请求还是消费回调」必须看这条记录，而不是 attempt_no：第一次尝试在
    登记等待前租约过期时，第二次尝试仍然需要发请求。
    """
    return db.scalar(
        select(WorkflowExternalWait.id)
        .where(
            WorkflowExternalWait.production_run_id == run_id,
            WorkflowExternalWait.request_node_instance_id == step_key,
        )
        .limit(1)
    ) is not None


def begin_image_first_seen_wait(
    db: Session,
    attempt_id: int,
    lease_token: str,
    *,
    now: datetime | None = None,
) -> WorkflowExternalWait:
    initial_attempt = db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, attempt_id)
    if initial_attempt is None:
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
            "step_attempt_not_found",
            "步骤尝试不存在",
            status_code=404,
        )
    run = db.scalar(
        select(ProductionRun)
        .where(ProductionRun.id == initial_attempt.run_id)
        .with_for_update()
    )
    attempt = db.scalar(
        select(ProductionStepAttempt)
        .where(ProductionStepAttempt.id == attempt_id)
        .with_for_update()
        .execution_options(populate_existing=True)
    )
    if run is None or attempt is None:
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
            "runtime_evidence_missing",
            "图片查询步骤关联的运行不存在",
            status_code=409,
        )
    if run.status not in RUNNABLE_RUN_STATUSES:
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
            "run_terminal_state",
            "运行已经停止，不能发起图片查询",
            status_code=409,
        )
    if (
        attempt.status not in {"leased", "running"}
        or attempt.lease_token != lease_token
    ):
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
            "step_lease_stale",
            "图片查询步骤租约已失效",
            status_code=409,
        )
    if image_first_seen_wait_exists(db, run.id, attempt.step_key):
        # 请求已经登记过：这一次尝试只能消费回调，不能再发一次查询。
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
            "first_seen_wait_already_requested",
            "图片查询请求已经登记，等待回调，不能重复发起",
            status_code=409,
        )
    metadata = _image_first_seen_wait_metadata(db, run, attempt)
    try:
        input_manifest = json.loads(attempt.input_manifest_json)
    except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
            "runtime_first_seen_input_invalid",
            "图片查询步骤输入损坏",
            status_code=409,
        ) from exc
    if not isinstance(input_manifest, dict):
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
            "runtime_first_seen_input_invalid",
            "图片查询步骤输入必须是对象",
            status_code=409,
        )
    runtime_input = metadata["runtime_context"].get("input_manifest")
    frozen_input = (
        runtime_input
        if isinstance(runtime_input, Mapping)
        else input_manifest
    )
    identity = (
        str(frozen_input["content_key"]).strip()
        if isinstance(frozen_input.get("content_key"), str)
        and str(frozen_input["content_key"]).strip()
        else f"asset:{frozen_input['asset_id']}"
        if frozen_input.get("asset_id") is not None
        else f"idempotency:{run.idempotency_key}"
    )
    business_key = _bounded_business_key(
        f"{run.category_key or 'unknown'}:{identity}:"
        f"mechanism:{run.workflow_version_id}:node:{attempt.step_key}"
    )
    request_contract = metadata["request_contract"]
    payload = {
        "schema_version": "image-first-seen-request-v1",
        "provider_connector_key": metadata["provider_connector_key"],
        "correlation_id": metadata["correlation_id"],
        "category_key": run.category_key,
        "origin_run_key": run.run_key,
        "node_instance_id": attempt.step_key,
        "input": redact_workflow_secrets(dict(frozen_input)),
    }
    event_digest = hashlib.sha256(
        canonical_json(
            {
                "business_idempotency_key": business_key,
                "correlation_id": metadata["correlation_id"],
                "request_contract_version_id": request_contract.id,
            }
        ).encode("utf-8")
    ).hexdigest()
    outbox = enqueue_runtime_outbox(
        db,
        RuntimeOutboxRequest(
            event_key=f"runtime-outbox:{event_digest}",
            target_contract_key=request_contract.contract_key,
            target_contract_version_id=request_contract.id,
            business_idempotency_key=business_key,
            mechanism_version_id=run.workflow_version_id,
            payload=payload,
            node_instance_id=attempt.step_key,
        ),
    )
    current = _now(now)
    dispatch = db.scalar(
        select(RuntimeDispatchItem).where(
            RuntimeDispatchItem.step_attempt_id == attempt.id
        )
    )
    if dispatch is not None:
        _close_dispatch(
            dispatch,
            status="completed",
            error_code="external_wait_requested",
            error_message="图片查询请求已进入 Outbox，等待供应商回调",
            now=current,
        )
    attempt.status = "retryable"
    attempt.finished_at = current
    attempt.lease_owner = None
    attempt.lease_token = None
    attempt.lease_expires_at = None
    attempt.heartbeat_at = None
    attempt.last_error_code = "external_wait_requested"
    attempt.last_error_message = "图片查询请求已发出，等待供应商回调"
    db.flush()
    wait = create_external_wait(
        db,
        ExternalWaitRequest(
            production_run_id=run.id,
            request_node_instance_id=attempt.step_key,
            resume_node_instance_id=attempt.step_key,
            continuation_key=metadata["continuation_key"],
            outbox_event_id=outbox.id,
            expected_contract_version_id=metadata["response_contract"].id,
            correlation_id=metadata["correlation_id"],
            deadline_at=current
            + timedelta(seconds=metadata["wait_timeout_seconds"]),
            timeout_policy=metadata["timeout_policy"],
            audit_actor=run.requested_by or "runtime",
        ),
    )
    return wait


def _latest_attempts(
    db: Session,
    run_id: int,
) -> dict[str, ProductionStepAttempt]:
    rows = db.scalars(
        select(ProductionStepAttempt)
        .where(ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == run_id)
        .order_by(
            ProductionStepAttempt.step_key,
            ProductionStepAttempt.attempt_no.desc(),
        )
    ).all()
    latest: dict[str, ProductionStepAttempt] = {}
    for row in rows:
        latest.setdefault(row.step_key, row)
    return latest


def fail_reserved_model_invocations_for_runs(
    db: Session,
    production_run_ids: list[int],
    *,
    error_message: str,
) -> int:
    """Conservatively close provider calls before their runs become terminal.

    Once the owning attempt is terminal, lease recovery will no longer see it.
    Without a durable pre/post-dispatch marker, every reserved call must be
    treated as an unknown external outcome so quota leases are released while
    the charged-call reservation remains auditable.
    """

    run_ids = sorted({int(run_id) for run_id in production_run_ids})
    if not run_ids:
        return 0
    # Both callers already hold the owning runs and attempts, serializing
    # reservation/settlement for these runs. Do not lock this discovery scan:
    # MySQL may choose the global status index and lock another run's primary
    # record while evaluating the run filter (secondary -> primary), reversing
    # the primary -> secondary order of fail_model_invocation's status update.
    # The terminal transition below obtains the authoritative primary-key lock.
    invocation_ids = db.scalars(
        select(WorkflowModelInvocation.id)
        .where(
            WorkflowModelInvocation.production_run_id.in_(run_ids),
            WorkflowModelInvocation.status == "reserved",
        )
        .order_by(WorkflowModelInvocation.id)
    ).all()
    for invocation_id in invocation_ids:
        fail_model_invocation(
            db,
            invocation_id=invocation_id,
            error_code="model_invocation_outcome_unknown",
            error_message=error_message,
            evidence={
                "retryable": False,
                "technical_error_type": "unknown_external_state",
                "attempt_count": 0,
            },
            provider_dispatched=True,
        )
    return len(invocation_ids)


def terminalize_runtime_run(
    db: Session,
    run: ProductionRun,
    *,
    status: str,
    error_code: str,
    error_message: str,
    now: datetime | None = None,
    persist_outputs=None,
) -> ProductionRun:
    """Stop every outstanding branch while the caller holds the run lock."""

    if status not in {"failed", "canceled"}:
        raise ValueError(f"unsupported terminal runtime status: {status}")
    current = _now(now)
    attempts = db.scalars(
        select(ProductionStepAttempt)
        .where(ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == run.id)
        .with_for_update()
    ).all()
    fail_reserved_model_invocations_for_runs(
        db,
        [run.id],
        error_message=(
            "运行进入终态，模型供应商执行结果无法确认"
        ),
    )
    for attempt in attempts:
        if attempt.status in NONTERMINAL_ATTEMPT_STATUSES:
            attempt.status = "skipped"
            attempt.finished_at = current
            attempt.last_error_code = error_code
            attempt.last_error_message = error_message
        if attempt.status != "succeeded":
            attempt.lease_owner = None
            attempt.lease_token = None
            attempt.lease_expires_at = None

    dispatches = db.scalars(
        select(RuntimeDispatchItem)
        .join(
            ProductionStepAttempt,
            ProductionStepAttempt.id == RuntimeDispatchItem.step_attempt_id,
        )
        .where(
            ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == run.id,
            RuntimeDispatchItem.status.in_({"queued", "leased"}),
        )
        .with_for_update()
    ).all()
    for dispatch in dispatches:
        _close_dispatch(
            dispatch,
            status="canceled",
            error_code=error_code,
            error_message=error_message,
            now=current,
        )

    if status == "canceled":
        waits = db.scalars(
            select(WorkflowExternalWait)
            .where(
                WorkflowExternalWait.production_run_id == run.id,
                WorkflowExternalWait.state == "waiting",
            )
            .with_for_update()
        ).all()
        for wait in waits:
            wait.state = "cancelled"
            wait.cancelled_at = current

    latest = _latest_attempts(db, run.id)
    run.status = status
    run.completed_steps = sum(
        attempt.status == "succeeded" for attempt in latest.values()
    )
    run.failed_steps = sum(attempt.status == "failed" for attempt in latest.values())
    run.current_step_key = None
    run.error_code = error_code
    run.error_message = error_message
    run.lease_owner = None
    run.lease_token = None
    run.lease_expires_at = None
    run.finished_at = current
    workflow_version = db.get(WorkflowVersion, run.workflow_version_id)
    is_native_baseline = (
        workflow_version is not None
        and workflow_version.workflow_schema_version == "workflow-v3"
        and run.source_type == "baseline_regression_item"
    )
    if is_native_baseline:
        from .native_baseline_regression import (
            NativeBaselineRegressionError,
            finalize_failed_native_baseline_item,
        )

        try:
            finalize_failed_native_baseline_item(db, run)
        except NativeBaselineRegressionError as exc:
            raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
                exc.code,
                str(exc),
                status_code=409,
            ) from exc
    else:
        output_writer = persist_outputs or persist_baseline_workflow_outputs
        output_writer(db, run)
    if (status == "failed" and run.category_key == "inspiration_image"
            and run.source_type == "content_ingress_event"):
        from .inspiration_factory_failure import record_terminal_failure
        record_terminal_failure(db, run)
    db.flush()
    from .wide_material import refresh_for_run

    refresh_for_run(db, run, event_kind="run_state")
    return run


def block_runtime_step(
    db: Session,
    attempt_id: int,
    lease_token: str,
    error_code: str,
    error_message: str,
    *,
    now: datetime | None = None,
) -> ProductionStepAttempt:
    """Stop a deterministic route ambiguity at an explicit human gate."""

    initial_attempt = db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, attempt_id)
    if initial_attempt is None:
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
            "step_attempt_not_found",
            "步骤尝试不存在",
            status_code=404,
        )
    run = db.scalar(
        select(ProductionRun)
        .where(ProductionRun.id == initial_attempt.run_id)
        .with_for_update()
    )
    attempt = db.scalar(
        select(ProductionStepAttempt)
        .where(ProductionStepAttempt.id == attempt_id)
        .with_for_update()
        .execution_options(populate_existing=True)
    )
    if run is None or attempt is None:
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
            "runtime_evidence_missing",
            "步骤关联的运行不存在",
            status_code=409,
        )
    if run.status not in RUNNABLE_RUN_STATUSES:
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
            "run_terminal_state",
            "运行已经停止，旧 Worker 结果不会被接收",
            status_code=409,
        )
    if (
        attempt.status not in {"leased", "running"}
        or attempt.lease_token != lease_token
    ):
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
            "step_lease_stale",
            "步骤租约已失效",
            status_code=409,
        )
    current = _now(now)
    dispatch = db.scalar(
        select(RuntimeDispatchItem).where(
            RuntimeDispatchItem.step_attempt_id == attempt.id
        )
    )
    if dispatch is not None:
        _close_dispatch(
            dispatch,
            status="completed",
            error_code=error_code,
            error_message=error_message,
            now=current,
        )
        # Production sessions disable autoflush. Persist the current dispatch
        # closure before selecting queued siblings, otherwise this leased row is
        # selected again and incorrectly changed to canceled.
        db.flush()
    attempt.status = "blocked"
    attempt.last_error_code = error_code
    attempt.last_error_message = error_message
    attempt.finished_at = current
    attempt.lease_owner = None
    attempt.lease_token = None
    attempt.lease_expires_at = None

    queued_dispatches = db.scalars(
        select(RuntimeDispatchItem)
        .join(
            ProductionStepAttempt,
            ProductionStepAttempt.id == RuntimeDispatchItem.step_attempt_id,
        )
        .where(
            ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == run.id,
            RuntimeDispatchItem.status.in_({"queued", "leased"}),
        )
        .with_for_update()
    ).all()
    for queued in queued_dispatches:
        _close_dispatch(
            queued,
            status="canceled",
            error_code=error_code,
            error_message=error_message,
            now=current,
        )
    run.status = "blocked"
    run.current_step_key = attempt.step_key
    run.blockers_json = canonical_json(
        [
            {
                "code": error_code,
                "message": error_message,
                "step_key": attempt.step_key,
            }
        ]
    )
    run.error_code = error_code
    run.error_message = error_message
    run.lease_owner = None
    run.lease_token = None
    run.lease_expires_at = None
    db.flush()
    return attempt


def _baseline_workflow_identity(
    item: BaselineRegressionItem,
) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    try:
        execution = json.loads(item.run.execution_snapshot_json or "{}")
    except json.JSONDecodeError:
        return None
    workflow_snapshot = execution.get("workflow_snapshot")
    if not isinstance(workflow_snapshot, Mapping):
        return None
    workflow = workflow_snapshot.get("workflow")
    return dict(workflow) if isinstance(workflow, Mapping) else None


def _validate_baseline_workflow_source(
    db: Session,
    *,
    item_id: int,
    baseline_run_id: Any,
    baseline_item_id: Any,
    workflow_version_id: int,
    workflow_canonical_hash: str,
    category_key: str | None,
    lock_item: bool,
) -> BaselineRegressionItem:
    statement = select(BaselineRegressionItem).where(
        BaselineRegressionItem.id == item_id
    )
    if lock_item:
        statement = statement.with_for_update()
    item = db.scalar(statement)
    identity = _baseline_workflow_identity(item) if item is not None else None
    manifest = identity.get("manifest") if isinstance(identity, Mapping) else None
    if (
        item is None
        or baseline_run_id != item.run_id
        or baseline_item_id != item.id
        or category_key != item.run.category_key
        or not isinstance(identity, Mapping)
        or identity.get("workflow_version_id") != workflow_version_id
        or identity.get("canonical_hash") != workflow_canonical_hash
        or not isinstance(manifest, Mapping)
        or manifest.get("category_key") != item.run.category_key
    ):
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
            "baseline_workflow_source_invalid",
            "工作流运行与存量回归冻结的类目、条目或版本身份不一致",
            status_code=409,
        )
    return item


def persist_baseline_workflow_outputs(
    db: Session,
    run: ProductionRun,
) -> None:
    if (
        run.source_type != "baseline_regression_item"
        or run.source_id is None
        or run.status not in {"succeeded", "failed", "canceled"}
    ):
        return
    try:
        run_snapshot = read_run_snapshot_document(run)
    except json.JSONDecodeError:
        run_snapshot = {}
    frozen_runtime = run_snapshot.get("runtime_context", {})
    frozen_workflow = run_snapshot.get("workflow", {})
    if not isinstance(frozen_runtime, Mapping) or not isinstance(
        frozen_workflow, Mapping
    ):
        frozen_runtime = {}
        frozen_workflow = {}
    baseline_run_id = db.scalar(
        select(BaselineRegressionItem.run_id).where(
            BaselineRegressionItem.id == run.source_id
        )
    )
    if baseline_run_id is not None:
        # Every successful child later refreshes all rows in the same baseline
        # run.  Serialize on the shared parent before locking this child so
        # parallel workers cannot form child -> parent / parent -> child cycles.
        db.scalar(
            select(BaselineRegressionRun)
            .where(BaselineRegressionRun.id == baseline_run_id)
            .with_for_update()
        )
    item = _validate_baseline_workflow_source(
        db,
        item_id=run.source_id,
        baseline_run_id=frozen_runtime.get("baseline_run_id"),
        baseline_item_id=frozen_runtime.get("baseline_item_id"),
        workflow_version_id=run.workflow_version_id,
        workflow_canonical_hash=str(frozen_workflow.get("canonical_hash") or ""),
        category_key=run.category_key,
        lock_item=True,
    )
    try:
        snapshot = json.loads(item.result_snapshot_json or "{}")
    except json.JSONDecodeError:
        snapshot = {}
    if not isinstance(snapshot, dict):
        snapshot = {}
    existing_runtime = snapshot.get("workflow_runtime")
    if (
        isinstance(existing_runtime, Mapping)
        and existing_runtime.get("run_key") not in {None, run.run_key}
    ):
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
            "baseline_workflow_output_conflict",
            "该存量回归条目已绑定另一条工作流运行，拒绝覆盖冻结证据",
            status_code=409,
        )
    latest = sorted(
        _latest_attempts(db, run.id).values(),
        key=lambda row: (row.sequence, row.step_key),
    )
    snapshot["workflow_node_outputs"] = {
        row.step_key: {
            "status": row.status,
            "attempt_no": row.attempt_no,
            "input_hash": row.input_hash,
            "output_hash": row.output_hash,
            "output": json.loads(row.output_manifest_json or "{}"),
            **(
                {
                    "error_code": row.last_error_code,
                    "error_message": row.last_error_message,
                }
                if row.last_error_code or row.last_error_message
                else {}
            ),
        }
        for row in latest
    }
    snapshot["workflow_runtime"] = {
        "run_key": run.run_key,
        "status": run.status,
        "snapshot_hash": run.snapshot_hash,
        "environment": run.environment,
    }
    item.result_snapshot_json = canonical_json(snapshot)


def _upstream_identity_columns(input_manifest: Mapping[str, Any] | None) -> dict[str, int | None]:
    """冻结清单里的工厂任务身份抄成列（迁移 119），纠偏页按上游分类筛、按 ID 溯源不再扫 JSON。"""
    task = input_manifest.get("factory_task") if isinstance(input_manifest, Mapping) else None
    values: dict[str, int | None] = {}
    for column, key in (("upstream_class_id", "class_id_1"), ("upstream_task_id", "task_id"),
                        ("upstream_img_id", "img_id"), ("upstream_ll_id", "ll_id")):
        raw = task.get(key) if isinstance(task, Mapping) else None
        values[column] = raw if type(raw) is int and raw > 0 else None
    return values


def create_production_run(
    db: Session,
    request: ProductionRunRequest,
    *,
    actor: str,
) -> ProductionRun:
    if request.environment != "dry_run":
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
            "runtime_environment_forbidden",
            "本阶段运行环境固定为 dry_run",
        )
    if request.model_execution_mode not in {"simulated", "real"}:
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
            "model_execution_mode_invalid",
            "模型执行方式只能是快速模拟或真实模型评测",
        )
    if request.queue_class not in QUEUE_CLASSES:
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
            "queue_class_unsupported",
            "运行只能使用既有五队列",
        )
    workflow_version = db.get(WorkflowVersion, request.workflow_version_id)
    if workflow_version is None:
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
            "workflow_version_not_found",
            "工作流版本不存在",
            status_code=404,
        )
    if workflow_version.status != "active":
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
            "workflow_version_unavailable",
            "只有 active 工作流版本可以创建运行",
            status_code=409,
        )

    runtime_context = dict(request.runtime_context)
    authorized_incremental_manifest: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None
    if request.model_execution_mode == "real":
        runtime_context, authorized_incremental_manifest = (
            _authorize_real_execution_request(
                db,
                request,
                workflow_version,
            )
        )

    if request.source_type == "baseline_regression_item":
        if request.source_id is None:
            raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
                "baseline_workflow_source_invalid",
                "存量回归工作流必须关联具体条目",
                status_code=409,
            )
        _validate_baseline_workflow_source(
            db,
            item_id=request.source_id,
            baseline_run_id=runtime_context.get("baseline_run_id"),
            baseline_item_id=runtime_context.get("baseline_item_id"),
            workflow_version_id=workflow_version.id,
            workflow_canonical_hash=workflow_version.canonical_hash,
            category_key=request.category_key,
            lock_item=True,
        )
        existing_source_run = db.scalar(
            select(ProductionRun).where(
                ProductionRun.source_type == "baseline_regression_item",
                ProductionRun.source_id == request.source_id,
            )
        )
        if (
            existing_source_run is not None
            and existing_source_run.idempotency_key != request.idempotency_key
        ):
            raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
                "baseline_workflow_run_conflict",
                "同一存量回归条目只能绑定一条工作流运行",
                status_code=409,
            )

    from .incremental_routing import authoritative_route
    authoritative_route(db, source_type=request.source_type, source_id=request.source_id,
        category_key=request.category_key, workflow_definition_id=workflow_version.workflow_definition_id,
        claimed_route=runtime_context.get("incremental_route"))
    from .incremental_sla import freeze_incremental_sla
    # Reserved server-owned metadata: callers cannot self-assign a service class
    # or reset their queue clock. Existing run snapshots are never rewritten.
    runtime_context.pop("incremental_sla", None)
    try:
        incremental_sla = freeze_incremental_sla(db, request)
    except ValueError as exc:
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError("incremental_sla_invalid", str(exc), status_code=409) from exc
    if incremental_sla is not None:
        runtime_context["incremental_sla"] = incremental_sla
    sla_columns = {}
    if incremental_sla is not None:
        from datetime import datetime as _dt
        sla_columns = {"sla_service_class": incremental_sla["service_class"],
                       "sla_received_at": _dt.fromisoformat(incremental_sla["first_queued_at"])}
    runtime_context = {
        **runtime_context,
        "environment": request.environment,
        "model_execution_mode": request.model_execution_mode,
        "category_key": request.category_key,
        "queue_class": request.queue_class,
        "input_manifest": dict(request.input_manifest),
    }
    manifest = persisted_workflow_manifest(workflow_version)
    try:
        validate_incremental_intake_binding(
            db,
            manifest=manifest,
            runtime_context=runtime_context,
            input_manifest=request.input_manifest,
            source_type=request.source_type,
            source_id=request.source_id,
            category_key=request.category_key,
            idempotency_key=request.idempotency_key,
        )
    except WorkflowConnectorError as exc:
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
            exc.code,
            str(exc),
            status_code=exc.status_code,
        ) from exc
    if workflow_version.workflow_schema_version == "workflow-v3":
        try:
            assert_native_workflow_executable(manifest)
        except NodeExecutorError as exc:
            raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
                exc.code,
                str(exc),
                status_code=409,
            ) from exc
    if workflow_version.workflow_schema_version in {"workflow-v2", "workflow-v3"}:
        for node in manifest["nodes"]:
            ensure_composer_fixture_version(db, node["kind"])
    try:
        snapshot, snapshot_hash = canonical_workflow_snapshot(
            db,
            workflow_version.id,
            runtime_context,
        )
    except WorkflowRegistryError as exc:
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
            exc.code,
            str(exc),
            status_code=exc.status_code,
        ) from exc
    if authorized_incremental_manifest is not None:
        frozen_manifest = (
            (snapshot.get("workflow") or {}).get("manifest")
            if isinstance(snapshot.get("workflow"), Mapping)
            else None
        )
        if (
            not isinstance(frozen_manifest, Mapping)
            or canonical_json(dict(frozen_manifest))
            != canonical_json(dict(authorized_incremental_manifest))
        ):
            raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
                "incremental_snapshot_mismatch",
                "运行时工作流与素材包冻结机制发生漂移",
                status_code=409,
            )

    existing = db.scalar(
        select(ProductionRun).where(
            ProductionRun.idempotency_key == request.idempotency_key
        )
    )
    if existing is not None:
        if (
            existing.workflow_version_id != workflow_version.id
            or existing.snapshot_hash != snapshot_hash
        ):
            raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
                "run_idempotency_conflict",
                "相同幂等键对应了不同的冻结输入",
                status_code=409,
            )
        return existing

    run_key = "run-" + hashlib.sha256(
        f"{request.idempotency_key}:{snapshot_hash}".encode("utf-8")
    ).hexdigest()[:32]
    steps = (
        list(manifest["steps"])
        if workflow_version.workflow_schema_version == "workflow-v1"
        else composer_runtime_steps(manifest)
    )
    edges = list(manifest["edges"])
    incoming = {step["key"]: 0 for step in steps}
    for edge in edges:
        incoming[edge["to"]] += 1
    roots = [key for key, count in incoming.items() if count == 0]
    nodes_by_id = {
        node["node_instance_id"]: node
        for node in manifest.get("nodes", [])
        if isinstance(node, Mapping)
        and isinstance(node.get("node_instance_id"), str)
    }

    from .module_runtime import execution_pool, EXECUTOR_REVISION
    run = ProductionRun(
        run_key=run_key,
        idempotency_key=request.idempotency_key,
        source_type=request.source_type,
        source_id=request.source_id,
        source_run_id=request.source_run_id,
        workflow_definition_id=workflow_version.workflow_definition_id,
        workflow_version_id=workflow_version.id,
        snapshot_json=canonical_json(snapshot),
        snapshot_hash=snapshot_hash,
        category_key=request.category_key,
        queue_class=request.queue_class,
        status="queued",
        current_step_key=roots[0] if roots else None,
        blockers_json="[]",
        requested_by=actor,
        owner=request.owner,
        reason=request.reason,
        environment=request.environment,
        model_execution_mode=request.model_execution_mode,
        execution_pool=execution_pool(request.source_type, runtime_context.get("run_mode")),
        executor_revision=EXECUTOR_REVISION,
        run_mode=(runtime_context.get("run_mode")
                  if isinstance(runtime_context.get("run_mode"), str) else None),
        **sla_columns,
        **_upstream_identity_columns(request.input_manifest),
        doubao_endpoint_id=(runtime_context.get("inference_route") or {}).get("endpoint_id"),
        total_steps=len(steps),
        completed_steps=0,
        failed_steps=0,
        attempt_count=0,
        error_code="",
        error_message="",
    )
    snapshot_blob = bind_run_snapshot(db, run, snapshot)
    if snapshot_blob.snapshot_hash != snapshot_hash:
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
            "workflow_snapshot_hash_mismatch",
            "工作流冻结快照哈希不一致",
            status_code=409,
        )
    db.add(run)
    db.flush()

    for sequence, step in enumerate(steps):
        is_root = step["key"] in roots
        input_manifest = (
            dict(request.input_manifest)
            if is_root
            else {"pending_upstream": True, "step_key": step["key"]}
        )
        input_hash = _hash(input_manifest)
        script_version = _script_version_for_reference(
            db,
            step["script_version"],
        )
        retry_policy = _retry_policy_from_node(
            nodes_by_id.get(step["key"]),
            fallback_max_attempts=script_version.max_attempts,
        )
        attempt = ProductionStepAttempt(
            run_id=run.id,
            step_key=step["key"],
            step_type=step["type"],
            sequence=sequence,
            script_version_id=script_version.id,
            status="pending",
            attempt_no=1,
            idempotency_key=(
                f"{run.run_key}:{step['key']}:{input_hash}:attempt:1"
            ),
            input_manifest_json=canonical_json(input_manifest),
            input_hash=input_hash,
            output_manifest_json="{}",
            checkpoint_json="{}",
            last_error_code="",
            last_error_message="",
        )
        db.add(attempt)
        db.flush()
        if is_root:
            db.add(
                RuntimeDispatchItem(
                    step_attempt_id=attempt.id,
                    queue_class=request.queue_class,
                    priority=50,
                    status="queued",
                    available_at=_now(),
                    max_attempts=retry_policy["max_attempts"],
                )
            )
    db.flush()
    # 素材大宽表（ADR-0111）：运行创建即登记 run_state；找不到素材就跳过。
    from .wide_material import refresh_for_run

    refresh_for_run(db, run, event_kind="run_state")
    return run


def claim_next_runtime_step(
    db: Session,
    worker_id: str,
    *,
    global_limit: int = 1,
    lease_seconds: int = DEFAULT_LEASE_SECONDS,
    now: datetime | None = None,
) -> int | None:
    from .module_runtime import configured_pool
    claimed = claim_runtime_item(
        db,
        ClaimRequest(
            worker_id=worker_id,
            global_limit=global_limit,
            lease_seconds=lease_seconds,
            now=now,
            execution_pool=configured_pool(),
        ),
    )
    return claimed.attempt_id if claimed is not None else None


def heartbeat_runtime_step(
    db: Session,
    attempt_id: int,
    lease_token: str,
    worker_id: str,
    *,
    lease_seconds: int = DEFAULT_LEASE_SECONDS,
    now: datetime | None = None,
) -> ProductionStepAttempt:
    dispatch = db.scalar(
        select(RuntimeDispatchItem).where(
            RuntimeDispatchItem.step_attempt_id == attempt_id
        )
    )
    if dispatch is None:
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
            "step_lease_stale",
            "步骤租约已失效或不属于当前 Worker",
            status_code=409,
        )
    try:
        heartbeat_runtime_item(
            db,
            dispatch.id,
            lease_token,
            _now(now),
            lease_seconds=lease_seconds,
            worker_id=worker_id,
        )
    except RuntimeQueueError as exc:
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
            (
                "run_terminal_state"
                if exc.code == "run_terminal_state"
                else "step_lease_stale"
            ),
            str(exc).split(": ", 1)[-1],
            status_code=409,
        ) from exc
    attempt = db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, attempt_id)
    if attempt is None:
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
            "step_lease_stale",
            "步骤租约已失效或不属于当前 Worker",
            status_code=409,
        )
    return attempt


def _ready_pending_steps(
    db: Session,
    run: ProductionRun,
    *,
    latest: dict[str, ProductionStepAttempt] | None = None,
) -> list[ProductionStepAttempt]:
    workflow_version = db.get(WorkflowVersion, run.workflow_version_id)
    if workflow_version is None:
        return []
    manifest = persisted_workflow_manifest(workflow_version)
    try:
        run_snapshot = read_run_snapshot_document(run)
    except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
            "runtime_snapshot_invalid",
            "运行冻结快照无法解析，不能判断续跑方式",
            status_code=409,
        ) from exc
    runtime_context = run_snapshot.get("runtime_context")
    run_mode = (
        runtime_context.get("run_mode")
        if isinstance(runtime_context, Mapping)
        else None
    )
    if latest is None:
        latest = _latest_attempts(db, run.id)
    _apply_frozen_route_decisions(db, run, manifest, latest)
    _apply_frozen_short_circuit_decisions(db, run, manifest, latest)
    ready: list[ProductionStepAttempt] = []
    node_kinds = {
        node["node_instance_id"]: node.get("kind")
        for node in manifest.get("nodes", [])
        if isinstance(node, Mapping)
        and isinstance(node.get("node_instance_id"), str)
    }
    nodes_by_id = {
        node["node_instance_id"]: node
        for node in manifest.get("nodes", [])
        if isinstance(node, Mapping)
        and isinstance(node.get("node_instance_id"), str)
    }
    ordered_targets = [
        row.step_key
        for row in sorted(
            latest.values(), key=lambda item: (item.sequence, item.id)
        )
        if row.status == "pending"
    ]
    for target in ordered_targets:
        predecessors = [
            edge["from"]
            for edge in manifest["edges"]
            if edge["to"] == target
        ]
        if not predecessors:
            continue
        target_is_join = node_kinds.get(target) == "join"
        if not all(
            predecessor in latest
            and (
                latest[predecessor].status == "succeeded"
                or (
                    latest[predecessor].status == "skipped"
                    and (
                        latest[predecessor].last_error_code == "short_circuited"
                        or latest[predecessor].last_error_code
                        in FAIL_OPEN_SKIP_REASONS
                        or (
                            target_is_join
                            and latest[predecessor].last_error_code
                            == "route_not_selected"
                        )
                    )
                )
            )
            for predecessor in predecessors
        ):
            continue
        target_attempt = latest.get(target)
        if target_attempt is None or target_attempt.status != "pending":
            continue
        target_node = nodes_by_id.get(target)
        target_config = (
            target_node.get("config")
            if isinstance(target_node, Mapping)
            else None
        )
        if (
            node_kinds.get(target) == "downstream_projection"
            and isinstance(target_config, Mapping)
            and isinstance(target_config.get("mapping"), Mapping)
            and target_config["mapping"].get("schema_version") in {"factory-result-v1", "inspiration-factory-result-v1"}
            and any(key not in latest or latest[key].status not in {"succeeded", "skipped"}
                    for key in nodes_by_id.keys() - {target})
        ):
            # Per-material completion barrier, independent of batch progress.
            # Do not create an early dispatch that will fail/retry while another
            # branch is still executing. Existing projection modes are unchanged.
            continue
        is_continuation = (
            run_mode == "incremental"
            and node_kinds.get(target) == "upstream_connector"
            and isinstance(target_config, Mapping)
            and target_config.get("input_mode", "initial_intake")
            == "continuation_event"
        )
        is_first_seen_callback = (
            run_mode == "incremental"
            and node_kinds.get(target) == "image_first_seen_lookup"
            and target_attempt.attempt_no > 1
        )
        if is_continuation or is_first_seen_callback:
            wait_conditions = [
                WorkflowExternalWait.production_run_id == run.id,
                WorkflowExternalWait.resume_node_instance_id == target,
                WorkflowExternalWait.state == "resumed",
            ]
            if is_continuation:
                wait_conditions.append(
                    WorkflowExternalWait.continuation_key
                    == target_config.get("continuation_key")
                )
            else:
                wait_conditions.append(
                    WorkflowExternalWait.request_node_instance_id == target
                )
            wait = db.scalar(
                select(WorkflowExternalWait).where(*wait_conditions)
            )
            if wait is None or wait.callback_intake_event_id is None:
                continue
            callback = db.get(
                UpstreamIntakeEvent,
                wait.callback_intake_event_id,
            )
            if (
                callback is None
                or callback.production_run_id != run.id
                or callback.contract_version_id
                != wait.expected_contract_version_id
                or callback.payload_hash != wait.callback_payload_hash
                or callback.category_key != run.category_key
                or hashlib.sha256(
                    callback.payload_json.encode("utf-8")
                ).hexdigest()
                != callback.payload_hash
            ):
                raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
                    "runtime_continuation_fact_invalid",
                    "外部续跑事实与冻结等待合同不一致",
                    status_code=409,
                )
            try:
                callback_payload = json.loads(callback.payload_json)
            except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
                raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
                    "runtime_continuation_fact_invalid",
                    "外部续跑事实内容损坏",
                    status_code=409,
                ) from exc
            if not isinstance(callback_payload, dict):
                raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
                    "runtime_continuation_fact_invalid",
                    "外部续跑事实内容必须是对象",
                    status_code=409,
                )
            input_manifest = callback_payload
        else:
            upstream = {
                predecessor: json.loads(
                    latest[predecessor].output_manifest_json
                )
                for predecessor in predecessors
                if latest[predecessor].status == "succeeded"
            }
            input_manifest = {"upstream": upstream}
            target_node = nodes_by_id.get(target, {})
            if (
                (manifest.get("category_key") == "model_3d_su_joint"
                and target_node.get("kind") == "downstream_projection"
                and target_node.get("config", {}).get("mapping", {}).get("schema_version") == "joint-model-projection-v1")
                or (target_node.get("kind") == "downstream_projection"
                    and target_node.get("config", {}).get("mapping", {}).get("schema_version") in {"factory-result-v1", "inspiration-factory-result-v1"})
                or is_legacy_evaluation_projection(manifest, target_node)
            ):
                # Freeze completed facts from this run into the terminal reducer's
                # input hash, including A when intermediate grading nodes skipped.
                input_manifest["upstream"] = {
                    key: json.loads(row.output_manifest_json)
                    for key, row in latest.items()
                    if key != target and row.status == "succeeded" and row.output_manifest_json
                }
        skipped_upstream = sorted(
            predecessor
            for predecessor in (latest.keys() - {target} if
                nodes_by_id.get(target, {}).get("config", {}).get("mapping", {}).get("schema_version") in {"factory-result-v1", "inspiration-factory-result-v1"}
                else predecessors)
            if latest[predecessor].status == "skipped"
            and latest[predecessor].last_error_code
            in {
                "route_not_selected",
                "short_circuited",
                *FAIL_OPEN_SKIP_REASONS,
            }
        )
        if skipped_upstream:
            input_manifest["skipped_upstream"] = skipped_upstream
            input_manifest["skipped_upstream_reasons"] = {
                predecessor: latest[predecessor].last_error_code
                for predecessor in skipped_upstream
            }
        input_hash = _hash(input_manifest)
        target_attempt.input_manifest_json = canonical_json(input_manifest)
        target_attempt.input_hash = input_hash
        target_attempt.idempotency_key = (
            f"{run.run_key}:{target}:{input_hash}:attempt:{target_attempt.attempt_no}"
        )
        existing_dispatch = db.scalar(
            select(RuntimeDispatchItem).where(
                RuntimeDispatchItem.step_attempt_id == target_attempt.id
            )
        )
        if existing_dispatch is None:
            db.add(
                RuntimeDispatchItem(
                    step_attempt_id=target_attempt.id,
                    queue_class=run.queue_class,
                    priority=50,
                    status="queued",
                    available_at=_now(),
                    max_attempts=_dispatch_max_attempts(db, target_attempt),
                )
            )
        ready.append(target_attempt)
    return ready


def _apply_frozen_route_decisions(
    db: Session,
    run: ProductionRun,
    manifest: Mapping[str, Any],
    latest: Mapping[str, ProductionStepAttempt],
) -> None:
    """Materialize persisted decisions without re-evaluating route facts."""

    nodes = manifest.get("nodes")
    if not isinstance(nodes, list):
        return
    node_kinds = {
        node.get("node_instance_id"): node.get("kind")
        for node in nodes
        if isinstance(node, Mapping)
        and isinstance(node.get("node_instance_id"), str)
    }
    incoming: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    edges = manifest.get("edges")
    if isinstance(edges, list):
        for edge in edges:
            if not isinstance(edge, Mapping):
                continue
            source = edge.get("from")
            target = edge.get("to")
            if isinstance(source, str) and isinstance(target, str):
                incoming.setdefault(target, []).append(source)
    branch_ids = {
        node.get("node_instance_id")
        for node in nodes
        if isinstance(node, Mapping) and node.get("kind") == "branch"
    }
    current = _now()
    for branch_id in branch_ids:
        if not isinstance(branch_id, str):
            continue
        branch_attempt = latest.get(branch_id)
        if branch_attempt is None or branch_attempt.status != "succeeded":
            continue
        try:
            branch_output = json.loads(branch_attempt.output_manifest_json or "{}")
        except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
            raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
                "route_decision_snapshot_invalid",
                "运行中的路线决定无法解析",
                status_code=409,
            ) from exc
        payload = branch_output.get("route_decision")
        if not isinstance(payload, Mapping):
            continue
        try:
            decision = route_decision_from_payload(payload)
        except RouteResolutionError as exc:
            raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
                exc.code,
                str(exc),
                status_code=409,
            ) from exc
        for step_key in sorted(route_skipped_node_ids(manifest, decision)):
            attempt = latest.get(step_key)
            if attempt is None or attempt.status == "skipped":
                continue
            if attempt.status not in {"pending", "retryable"}:
                raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
                    "route_activation_conflict",
                    "未选路线已经开始执行，拒绝覆盖运行事实",
                    status_code=409,
                )
            skipped_output = {
                "schema_version": "workflow-node-result-v1",
                "node_instance_id": step_key,
                "node_kind": node_kinds.get(step_key),
                "status": "skipped",
                "upstream_refs": sorted(incoming.get(step_key, [])),
                "skipped_reason": "route_not_selected",
                "route_decision_ref": branch_id,
            }
            output_hash = _hash(skipped_output)
            checkpoint = {
                "schema_version": "runtime-checkpoint-v1",
                "step_key": step_key,
                "input_hash": attempt.input_hash,
                "output_hash": output_hash,
                "status": "skipped",
                "reason": "route_not_selected",
            }
            attempt.status = "skipped"
            attempt.output_manifest_json = canonical_json(skipped_output)
            attempt.output_hash = output_hash
            attempt.checkpoint_json = canonical_json(checkpoint)
            attempt.checkpoint_hash = _hash(checkpoint)
            attempt.finished_at = current
            attempt.last_error_code = "route_not_selected"
            attempt.last_error_message = "本次运行未选择该路线"
            dispatch = db.scalar(
                select(RuntimeDispatchItem).where(
                    RuntimeDispatchItem.step_attempt_id == attempt.id
                )
            )
            if dispatch is not None and dispatch.status in {"queued", "leased"}:
                _close_dispatch(
