                    dispatch,
                    status="canceled",
                    error_code="route_not_selected",
                    error_message="本次运行未选择该路线",
                    now=current,
                )


def _short_circuit_facts(
    latest: Mapping[str, ProductionStepAttempt],
    output_manifest: Mapping[str, Any],
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    business_output = output_manifest.get("output")
    if not isinstance(business_output, Mapping):
        business_output = output_manifest
    facts = dict(business_output)
    facts["output"] = dict(business_output)
    facts["node_result"] = dict(output_manifest)
    node_facts: dict[str, Any] = {}
    for step_key, attempt in latest.items():
        if attempt.status != "succeeded":
            continue
        try:
            parsed = json.loads(attempt.output_manifest_json or "{}")
        except json.JSONDecodeError:
            continue
        if not isinstance(parsed, Mapping):
            continue
        candidate = parsed.get("output")
        node_facts[step_key] = (
            dict(candidate) if isinstance(candidate, Mapping) else dict(parsed)
        )
    facts["nodes"] = node_facts
    return facts


def _attach_frozen_short_circuit_decision(
    db: Session,
    run: ProductionRun,
    attempt: ProductionStepAttempt,
    output_manifest: Mapping[str, Any],
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    workflow_version = db.get(WorkflowVersion, run.workflow_version_id)
    if (
        workflow_version is None
        or workflow_version.workflow_schema_version != "workflow-v3"
    ):
        return dict(output_manifest)
    manifest = persisted_workflow_manifest(workflow_version)
    node = next(
        (
            row
            for row in manifest.get("nodes", [])
            if isinstance(row, Mapping)
            and row.get("node_instance_id") == attempt.step_key
        ),
        None,
    )
    from .upstream_type_short_circuit import effective_short_circuit_rules

    rules = effective_short_circuit_rules(node) if isinstance(node, Mapping) else None
    if not isinstance(rules, list) or not rules:
        return dict(output_manifest)
    try:
        decision = resolve_short_circuit(
            rules,
            _short_circuit_facts(_latest_attempts(db, run.id), output_manifest),
            enabled_levels_from_manifest(manifest),
        )
    except ShortCircuitError as exc:
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError(exc.code, str(exc), status_code=409) from exc
    if decision is None:
        return dict(output_manifest)
    skip_roles = decision.evidence.get("skip_roles")
    skip_roles = skip_roles if isinstance(skip_roles, list) else []
    skip_nodes = decision.evidence.get("skip_nodes")
    skip_nodes = skip_nodes if isinstance(skip_nodes, list) else []
    decision = decision_with_skipped_nodes(
        decision,
        downstream_skipped_nodes(
            manifest,
            source_node_id=attempt.step_key,
            skip_roles=skip_roles,
            skip_nodes=skip_nodes,
        ),
    )
    enriched = dict(output_manifest)
    enriched["short_circuit_decision"] = short_circuit_decision_payload(decision)
    return enriched


def _apply_frozen_short_circuit_decisions(
    db: Session,
    run: ProductionRun,
    manifest: Mapping[str, Any],
    latest: Mapping[str, ProductionStepAttempt],
) -> None:
    nodes = manifest.get("nodes")
    if not isinstance(nodes, list):
        return
    node_kinds = {
        node.get("node_instance_id"): node.get("kind")
        for node in nodes
        if isinstance(node, Mapping)
    }
    incoming: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    for edge in manifest.get("edges", []):
        if (
            isinstance(edge, Mapping)
            and isinstance(edge.get("from"), str)
            and isinstance(edge.get("to"), str)
        ):
            incoming.setdefault(edge["to"], []).append(edge["from"])
    current = _now()
    valid_levels = enabled_levels_from_manifest(manifest)
    for source_attempt in sorted(
        latest.values(), key=lambda row: (row.sequence, row.id)
    ):
        if source_attempt.status != "succeeded":
            continue
        try:
            source_output = json.loads(source_attempt.output_manifest_json or "{}")
        except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
            raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
                "short_circuit_decision_invalid",
                "运行中的短路决定无法解析",
                status_code=409,
            ) from exc
        payload = (
            source_output.get("short_circuit_decision")
            if isinstance(source_output, Mapping)
            else None
        )
        if not isinstance(payload, Mapping):
            continue
        try:
            decision = short_circuit_decision_from_payload(payload)
        except ShortCircuitError as exc:
            raise WorkflowRuntimeError(exc.code, str(exc), status_code=409) from exc
        if decision.target_level not in valid_levels:
            raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
                "short_circuit_target_level_invalid",
                f"短路目标 {decision.target_level} 不在品类冻结等级中",
                status_code=409,
            )
        for step_key in decision.skipped_nodes:
            attempt = latest.get(step_key)
            if attempt is None or attempt.status == "skipped":
                continue
            if attempt.status not in {"pending", "retryable"}:
                raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
                    "short_circuit_activation_conflict",
                    "短路目标节点已经开始执行，拒绝覆盖运行事实",
                    status_code=409,
                )
            skipped_output = {
                "schema_version": "workflow-node-result-v1",
                "node_instance_id": step_key,
                "node_kind": node_kinds.get(step_key),
                "status": "skipped",
                "upstream_refs": sorted(incoming.get(step_key, [])),
                "skipped_reason": "short_circuited",
                "short_circuit_decision": dict(payload),
                "short_circuit_decision_ref": source_attempt.step_key,
            }
            output_hash = _hash(skipped_output)
            checkpoint = {
                "schema_version": "runtime-checkpoint-v1",
                "step_key": step_key,
                "input_hash": attempt.input_hash,
                "output_hash": output_hash,
                "status": "skipped",
                "reason": "short_circuited",
                "short_circuit_rule_key": decision.rule_key,
            }
            attempt.status = "skipped"
            attempt.output_manifest_json = canonical_json(skipped_output)
            attempt.output_hash = output_hash
            attempt.checkpoint_json = canonical_json(checkpoint)
            attempt.checkpoint_hash = _hash(checkpoint)
            attempt.finished_at = current
            attempt.last_error_code = "short_circuited"
            attempt.last_error_message = (
                f"规则 {decision.rule_key} 已直接确定等级 {decision.target_level}"
            )
            dispatch = db.scalar(
                select(RuntimeDispatchItem).where(
                    RuntimeDispatchItem.step_attempt_id == attempt.id
                )
            )
            if dispatch is not None and dispatch.status in {"queued", "leased"}:
                _close_dispatch(
                    dispatch,
                    status="canceled",
                    error_code="short_circuited",
                    error_message=attempt.last_error_message,
                    now=current,
                )


def _cancel_unpublished_incremental_results(
    db: Session, run: ProductionRun, *, error_code: str, now: datetime,
) -> None:
    """Reject uncommitted/pending publication when final result validation fails.

    The final projection and reconciliation share a transaction, so cancellation
    precedes visibility to the independent delivery worker. Already delivered or
    in-flight intermediate projections cannot be retracted here.
    """
    from .downstream_publish_ledger import mark_downstream_publish_outcome

    events = db.scalars(
        select(RuntimeOutboxEvent)
        .join(DownstreamPublishLedger,
              DownstreamPublishLedger.outbox_event_id == RuntimeOutboxEvent.id)
        .where(DownstreamPublishLedger.origin_run_key == run.run_key,
               RuntimeOutboxEvent.status == "queued")
        .order_by(RuntimeOutboxEvent.id).with_for_update()
        .execution_options(populate_existing=True)
    ).all()
    for event in events:
        event.status = "canceled"
        event.last_error_code = "native_result_finalization_failed"
        event.last_error_message = error_code
        event.updated_at = now
        mark_downstream_publish_outcome(
            db, outbox_event_id=event.id, delivery_status="rejected",
            rejection_code="native_result_finalization_failed", now=now,
        )
    if events:
        append_audit_event(
            db, category="workflow_runtime", action="native_result_publication_rejected",
            subject_type="production_run", subject_id=run.id, actor="runtime",
            payload={"run_key":run.run_key,"error_code":error_code,
                     "outbox_event_ids":[event.id for event in events]},
            dedupe_by_content=True,
        )


def _reconcile_locked_runtime_run(
    db: Session,
    run: ProductionRun,
    *,
    now: datetime | None = None,
) -> ProductionRun:
    current = _now(now)
    if run.status not in RUNNABLE_RUN_STATUSES:
        db.flush()
        return run
    latest = _latest_attempts(db, run.id)
    # The owning run is already locked. Route/short-circuit processing mutates
    # these same attempt objects; reuse them rather than scan the set twice.
    ready = _ready_pending_steps(db, run, latest=latest)
    succeeded = [item for item in latest.values() if item.status == "succeeded"]
    skipped = [item for item in latest.values() if item.status == "skipped"]
    failed = [item for item in latest.values() if item.status == "failed"]
    active = sorted(
        (
            item
            for item in latest.values()
            if item.status in {"leased", "running"}
        ),
        key=lambda item: (item.sequence, item.id),
    )
    retryable = sorted(
        (item for item in latest.values() if item.status == "retryable"),
        key=lambda item: (item.sequence, item.id),
    )
    run.completed_steps = len(succeeded) + len(skipped)
    run.failed_steps = len(failed)
    if run.completed_steps == run.total_steps:
        run.status = "succeeded"
        run.current_step_key = None
        run.error_code = ""
        run.error_message = ""
        run.finished_at = current
        run.lease_owner = None
        run.lease_token = None
        run.lease_expires_at = None
        persist_baseline_workflow_outputs(db, run)
        workflow_version = db.get(WorkflowVersion, run.workflow_version_id)
        if (
            workflow_version is not None
            and workflow_version.workflow_schema_version == "workflow-v3"
            and run.source_type in {
                "native_evaluation",
                "baseline_regression_item",
                "content_ingress_event",
            }
        ):
            from .workflow_result_finalizer import (
                NativeResultFinalizationError,
                finalize_native_run,
            )

            try:
                with db.begin_nested():
                    finalize_native_run(db, run)
            except NativeResultFinalizationError as exc:
                if run.source_type == "content_ingress_event":
                    _cancel_unpublished_incremental_results(db, run, error_code=exc.code, now=current)
                return terminalize_runtime_run(
                    db,
                    run,
                    status="failed",
                    error_code=exc.code,
                    error_message=str(exc),
                    now=current,
                )
    else:
        if active:
            run.status = "running"
            run.current_step_key = active[0].step_key
            run.lease_owner = active[0].lease_owner
            run.lease_token = active[0].lease_token
            run.lease_expires_at = active[0].lease_expires_at
        elif retryable:
            run.status = "retryable"
            run.current_step_key = retryable[0].step_key
            run.lease_owner = None
            run.lease_token = None
            run.lease_expires_at = None
        else:
            run.status = "queued"
            run.current_step_key = ready[0].step_key if ready else None
            run.lease_owner = None
            run.lease_token = None
            run.lease_expires_at = None
    db.flush()
    return run


def reconcile_runtime_run(
    db: Session,
    run_id: int,
    *,
    now: datetime | None = None,
) -> ProductionRun:
    run = db.scalar(
        select(ProductionRun)
        .where(ProductionRun.id == run_id)
        .with_for_update()
    )
    if run is None:
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
            "production_run_not_found",
            "生产运行不存在",
            status_code=404,
        )
    return _reconcile_locked_runtime_run(db, run, now=now)


def complete_runtime_step(
    db: Session,
    attempt_id: int,
    lease_token: str,
    output_manifest: Mapping[str, Any],
    *,
    now: datetime | None = None,
) -> ProductionStepAttempt:
    initial_attempt = db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, attempt_id)
    if initial_attempt is None:
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
            "step_attempt_not_found",
            "步骤尝试不存在",
            status_code=404,
        )
    run = db.scalar(
        select(ProductionRun)
        .where(ProductionRun.id == initial_attempt.run_id)
        .with_for_update()
    )
    if run is None:
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
            "production_run_not_found",
            "生产运行不存在",
            status_code=404,
        )
    attempt = db.scalar(
        select(ProductionStepAttempt)
        .where(ProductionStepAttempt.id == attempt_id)
        .with_for_update()
        .execution_options(populate_existing=True)
    )
    if attempt is None:
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
            "step_attempt_not_found",
            "步骤尝试不存在",
            status_code=404,
        )
    output_manifest = _attach_frozen_short_circuit_decision(
        db, run, attempt, output_manifest
    )
    output_hash = _hash(output_manifest)
    if attempt.status == "succeeded":
        if attempt.lease_token == lease_token and attempt.output_hash == output_hash:
            return attempt
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
            "step_completion_conflict",
            "步骤已经以不同输出完成",
            status_code=409,
        )
    if run.status not in RUNNABLE_RUN_STATUSES:
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
            "run_terminal_state",
            "运行已经停止，旧 Worker 结果不会被接收",
            status_code=409,
        )
    if (
        attempt.status not in {"leased", "running"}
        or attempt.lease_token != lease_token
    ):
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
            "step_lease_stale",
            "步骤租约已失效",
            status_code=409,
        )

    current = _now(now)
    checkpoint = {
        "schema_version": "runtime-checkpoint-v1",
        "step_key": attempt.step_key,
        "input_hash": attempt.input_hash,
        "output_hash": output_hash,
    }
    attempt.status = "succeeded"
    attempt.output_manifest_json = canonical_json(output_manifest)
    attempt.output_hash = output_hash
    attempt.checkpoint_json = canonical_json(checkpoint)
    attempt.checkpoint_hash = _hash(checkpoint)
    attempt.finished_at = current
    attempt.heartbeat_at = current
    attempt.last_error_code = ""
    attempt.last_error_message = ""

    outbox_request = _incremental_projection_outbox_request(
        db,
        run,
        attempt,
        output_manifest,
    )
    outbox_event = None
    if outbox_request is not None:
        outbox_event = enqueue_runtime_outbox(db, outbox_request)
        # ADR-0064：平台侧变更日志按发布追加一行（下游只留最新行）
        record_downstream_publish(
            db, event=outbox_event, payload=outbox_request.payload
        )

    dispatch = db.scalar(
        select(RuntimeDispatchItem).where(
            RuntimeDispatchItem.step_attempt_id == attempt.id
        )
    )
    if dispatch is not None:
        _close_dispatch(dispatch, status="completed", now=current)

    run.last_checkpoint_id = attempt.id
    wait_metadata = _incremental_projection_wait_metadata(run, attempt.step_key)
    if wait_metadata is not None:
        if outbox_event is None:
            raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
                "runtime_continuation_outbox_missing",
                "等待外部处理节点没有生成 Outbox 事件",
                status_code=409,
            )
        db.flush()
        create_external_wait(
            db,
            ExternalWaitRequest(
                production_run_id=run.id,
                request_node_instance_id=attempt.step_key,
                resume_node_instance_id=wait_metadata[
                    "resume_node_instance_id"
                ],
                continuation_key=wait_metadata["continuation_key"],
                outbox_event_id=outbox_event.id,
                expected_contract_version_id=wait_metadata[
                    "expected_contract_version_id"
                ],
                correlation_id=wait_metadata["correlation_id"],
                deadline_at=current
                + timedelta(seconds=wait_metadata["wait_timeout_seconds"]),
                timeout_policy=wait_metadata["timeout_policy"],
                audit_actor=run.requested_by or "runtime",
            ),
        )
        latest = _latest_attempts(db, run.id)
        run.completed_steps = sum(
            row.status in {"succeeded", "skipped"} for row in latest.values()
        )
        run.failed_steps = sum(
            row.status == "failed" for row in latest.values()
        )
        db.flush()
    else:
        _reconcile_locked_runtime_run(db, run, now=current)
    return attempt


def recover_expired_runtime_steps(
    db: Session,
    now: datetime | None = None,
) -> int:
    current = _now(now)
    reserve_sqlite_immediate_transaction(db)
    expired = db.scalars(
        select(ProductionStepAttempt).where(
            ProductionStepAttempt.status.in_({"leased", "running"}),
            ProductionStepAttempt.lease_expires_at.is_not(None),
            ProductionStepAttempt.lease_expires_at <= current,
        )
    ).all()
    recovered = 0
    quarantined = 0
    for candidate in expired:
        # 每条候选跑在自己的保存点里：一条收敛不了的坏行只回滚它自己，
        # 不再掀翻整批。原先整批共用一个事务，恢复 tick 撞上一条冲突记录就
        # 中止，后面的候选永远轮不到——一条毒丸能把辅助 worker 永久卡死
        # （137 实测：每 6 秒失败一次，24 条过期租约堆积 1 小时无人收敛）。
        try:
            with db.begin_nested():
                recovered += _recover_one_expired_step(db, candidate, current=current)
        except (WorkflowRuntimeError, WorkflowModelBudgetError) as exc:
            try:
                with db.begin_nested():
                    quarantined += _quarantine_unrecoverable_step(
                        db,
                        candidate,
                        error_code=exc.code,
                        error_message=str(exc),
                        now=current,
                        accounting_conflict=isinstance(exc, WorkflowModelBudgetError),
                    )
            except Exception:
                logger.exception(
                    "运行时恢复隔离失败，跳过 attempt=%s run=%s",
                    candidate.id,
                    candidate.run_id,
                )
        except Exception:
            # 非预期异常（坏数据、代码缺陷）：保存点已回滚这一条，记录后跳过，
            # 不能让它中止整批、把本轮已恢复的其余候选一起回滚。
            logger.exception(
                "运行时恢复跳过一条异常候选 attempt=%s run=%s",
                candidate.id,
                candidate.run_id,
            )
    if quarantined:
        logger.error(
            "运行时恢复隔离了 %s 条无法收敛的步骤，已进入死信待人工处理",
            quarantined,
        )
    db.flush()
    return recovered


def _quarantine_unrecoverable_step(
    db: Session,
    candidate: ProductionStepAttempt,
    *,
    error_code: str,
    error_message: str,
    now: datetime,
    accounting_conflict: bool = False,
) -> int:
    """把收敛不了的步骤移出自动恢复视野，并留在死信里等人工处理。

    不改写任何证据：证据冲突意味着我们无法判断哪一份才是真相，自动改写任何
    一方都是编造。清掉租约让它退出候选集（避免热循环），调度条目落成死信
    （带原始错误码），步骤落 ``failed`` 终态、运行对账成 ``failed``——否则步骤
    永远停在 leased/running，运行永远算在飞（发布闸门排不空），失败台也看不见。
    运行收尾优先走正常终态路径（失败台记录等）；该路径本身就是冲突来源时，
    只把运行状态落成失败，不碰存量回归归并与结果投影。
    """

    attempt = db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, candidate.id)
    if attempt is None or attempt.lease_expires_at is None:
        return 0
    root_code = error_code[:120] or QUARANTINE_ERROR_CODE
    attempt.status = "failed"
    attempt.finished_at = now
    attempt.lease_owner = None
    attempt.lease_token = None
    attempt.lease_expires_at = None
    attempt.last_error_code = QUARANTINE_ERROR_CODE
    attempt.last_error_message = (
        f"运行时恢复无法收敛，已隔离待人工处理（{root_code}）：{error_message}"
    )[:2000]
    dispatch = db.scalar(
        select(RuntimeDispatchItem).where(
            RuntimeDispatchItem.step_attempt_id == attempt.id
        )
    )
    if dispatch is not None and dispatch.status in ("queued", "leased"):
        _close_dispatch(
            dispatch,
            status="dead_lettered",
            error_code=root_code,
            error_message="运行时恢复无法收敛，已隔离待人工处理",
            now=now,
        )
    db.flush()
    try:
        # 运行收尾失败不能连带回滚上面的隔离（清租约 + 死信），否则毒丸又回到候选集。
        with db.begin_nested():
            _fail_quarantined_run(
                db,
                attempt.run_id,
                message=f"步骤 {attempt.step_key} 运行时恢复无法收敛（{root_code}），已隔离待人工处理",
                now=now,
                accounting_conflict=accounting_conflict,
            )
    except Exception:
        logger.exception("隔离步骤的运行收尾失败，保留隔离 run=%s", attempt.run_id)
    logger.error(
        "运行时恢复隔离步骤 attempt=%s run=%s code=%s：%s",
        attempt.id,
        attempt.run_id,
        error_code,
        error_message,
    )
    return 1


QUARANTINE_ERROR_CODE = "runtime_recovery_quarantined"
_TERMINAL_RUN_STATUSES = frozenset({"succeeded", "failed", "canceled"})


def _fail_quarantined_run(
    db: Session, run_id: int, *, message: str, now: datetime,
    accounting_conflict: bool = False,
) -> None:
    run = db.scalar(
        select(ProductionRun).where(ProductionRun.id == run_id).with_for_update()
    )
    if run is None or run.status in _TERMINAL_RUN_STATUSES:
        return
    # 记账冲突时正常终态路径会再去结算预留调用，正是冲突来源：直接只落状态。
    try:
        if accounting_conflict:
            raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
                "model_quota_evidence_invalid", "记账证据冲突，跳过正常终态路径",
            )
        with db.begin_nested():
            terminalize_runtime_run(
                db, run, status="failed", error_code=QUARANTINE_ERROR_CODE,
                error_message=message, now=now,
            )
        return
    except Exception:
        logger.exception(
            "隔离步骤的运行无法走正常终态路径，只落失败状态 run=%s", run_id,
        )
    run = db.get(ProductionRun, run_id)
    if run is None or run.status in _TERMINAL_RUN_STATUSES:
        return
    for other in db.scalars(
        select(ProductionStepAttempt).where(ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == run_id)
    ).all():
        if other.status in NONTERMINAL_ATTEMPT_STATUSES:
            other.status = "skipped"
            other.finished_at = now
            other.last_error_code = QUARANTINE_ERROR_CODE
            other.last_error_message = message
        if other.status != "succeeded":
            other.lease_owner = None
            other.lease_token = None
            other.lease_expires_at = None
    for queued in db.scalars(
        select(RuntimeDispatchItem)
        .join(ProductionStepAttempt,
              ProductionStepAttempt.id == RuntimeDispatchItem.step_attempt_id)
        .where(ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == run_id,
               RuntimeDispatchItem.status.in_({"queued", "leased"}))
    ).all():
        _close_dispatch(queued, status="canceled", error_code=QUARANTINE_ERROR_CODE,
                        error_message=message, now=now)
    # 预留调用保持 reserved：结果不明的费用证据不能在这里被改写或编造实际值。
    latest = _latest_attempts(db, run_id)
    run.status = "failed"
    run.completed_steps = sum(a.status == "succeeded" for a in latest.values())
    run.failed_steps = sum(a.status == "failed" for a in latest.values())
    run.current_step_key = None
    run.error_code = QUARANTINE_ERROR_CODE
    run.error_message = message
    run.lease_owner = None
    run.lease_token = None
    run.lease_expires_at = None
    run.finished_at = now
    db.flush()


def _batch_transport_restart(db, run, attempt, current):
    """Recover only frozen, counted transports; retain live requests until their deadline."""
    from . import batch_endpoint_recovery as recovery
    from .models import ContentIngressEvent, BaselineRegressionItem, BaselineRegressionRun
    try:
        # 快照读取也在保护内：坏快照按「无可安全恢复的证据」收敛，不抛出中止恢复批次。
        snapshot = read_run_snapshot_document(run)
        runtime = snapshot.get("runtime_context", {})
        if recovery.policy(runtime) is None:
            return None
        nodes = snapshot.get("workflow", {}).get("manifest", {}).get("nodes", [])
        node = next((item for item in nodes if item.get("node_instance_id") == attempt.step_key), None)
        if not node or node.get("kind") not in {"prompt", "foreach_image"}:
            return None
        online_limit = runtime["real_execution_node_limits"][attempt.step_key]["online_fallback_limits"]["max_attempts"]
        queued = run.created_at
        if run.source_type == "content_ingress_event":
            event = db.get(ContentIngressEvent, run.source_id)
            if event is None:
                return {"retry": False}
            queued = event.received_at
        elif run.source_type == "baseline_regression_item":
            item = db.get(BaselineRegressionItem, run.source_id)
            aggregate = db.get(BaselineRegressionRun, item.run_id) if item else None
            if aggregate is None:
                return {"retry": False}
            queued = aggregate.created_at
        from .effective_batch_clock import effective_queue_time
        queued = effective_queue_time(db, run_id=run.id, queued_at=queued, now=current)
        from .batch_experiment import batch_only_experiment
        from .incremental_sla import online_fallback_allowed
        batch_only = batch_only_experiment(db, run)
        batch_deadline = recovery.utc(queued) + timedelta(seconds=recovery.DEADLINE_SECONDS)
        rows = db.scalars(select(WorkflowModelInvocation).where(
            WorkflowModelInvocation.production_run_id == run.id,
            WorkflowModelInvocation.node_instance_id == attempt.step_key,
            WorkflowModelInvocation.status.in_(["reserved", "failed", "succeeded"]),
        ).order_by(WorkflowModelInvocation.id)).all()
        groups = {}
        wait_until = current
        for row in rows:
            match = re.fullmatch(r"(transport-v1:[a-f0-9]{64}:)(batch|online):([1-9][0-9]*)(?::reservation:[1-9][0-9]*)?", row.invocation_key)
            if match is None:
                return {"retry": False}
            prefix, mode, _ = match.groups()
            groups.setdefault(prefix, []).append(row)
            if row.status == "reserved":
                if mode == "batch":
                    deadline = (recovery.utc(row.created_at) + timedelta(seconds=recovery.DEADLINE_SECONDS)
                                if batch_only else batch_deadline)
                else:
                    from .workflow_prompt_execution import prompt_execution_node
                    executable = prompt_execution_node(node) or {}
                    timeout = executable.get("config", {}).get("model_snapshot", {}).get("runtime_config", {}).get("timeout_seconds")
                    if type(timeout) not in {int, float} or not 0 < timeout <= 86400:
                        return {"retry": False}
                    deadline = recovery.utc(row.created_at) + timedelta(seconds=timeout + 300)
                wait_until = max(wait_until, deadline)
        if wait_until > current:
            return {"wait_until": wait_until}
        reconciled_prefixes = set()
        if node.get("kind") == "foreach_image":
            from .models import WorkflowForeachItem
            from .workflow_foreach_executor import _reconcile_items_from_invocations, _invocation_key
            children = db.scalars(select(WorkflowForeachItem).where(
                WorkflowForeachItem.parent_step_attempt_id == attempt.id,
            )).all()
            _reconcile_items_from_invocations(db, children, now=current)
            reconciled_prefixes = {
                recovery.invocation_prefix(_invocation_key(child.item_key, child.attempt_count))
                for child in children if child.status == "succeeded"
            }
        retriable = not groups
        resource_waits = []
        evaluation_retry = False
        for prefix, group in groups.items():
            latest = group[-1]
            if latest.status == "succeeded":
                evidence = json.loads(latest.evidence_json or "{}")
                if not isinstance(evidence, Mapping):
                    return {"retry": False}
                semantic = evidence.get("semantic_validation", {})
                if not isinstance(semantic, Mapping):
                    return {"retry": False}
                if semantic.get("status") == "error":
                    return {"retry": False}
                if semantic.get("status") != "rejected":
                    if prefix not in reconciled_prefixes:
                        return {"retry": False}
                    retriable = True  # New parent copies the accepted child; no new paid call.
                    continue
            if latest.status == "failed" and latest.error_code not in {
                "model_invocation_outcome_unknown", "model_provider_429",
                "model_provider_transient_parse", "model_provider_json_truncated",
                "structured_output_schema_mismatch", "model_output_semantic_inconsistency",
            }:
                return {"retry": False}
            decision = recovery.transport_decision(group, prefix=prefix,
                queued_at=queued, now=current, online_limit=online_limit, batch_only=batch_only,
                online_allowed=online_fallback_allowed(run))
            if decision.mode == 'held':
                return {'experiment_hold': True}
            if recovery.is_resource_rejection(latest) and decision.mode != 'exhausted':
                retry_at = recovery.resource_retry_at(group, prefix=prefix, mode=decision.mode) or current
                if decision.mode == 'batch' and not batch_only:
                    retry_at = min(retry_at, batch_deadline)
                resource_waits.append(max(1, (retry_at-current).total_seconds()))
            else:
                evaluation_retry = True
            retriable = retriable or decision.mode != "exhausted"
        if resource_waits and not evaluation_retry:
            return {'retry': True, 'resource_wait_seconds': min(resource_waits),
                    'max_attempts': 3 + online_limit}
        # Runtime crashes before reservation are bounded too; transport counts
        # remain the authority for actual paid calls across runtime attempts.
        return {"retry": retriable, "max_attempts": 3 + online_limit}
    except (AttributeError, KeyError, TypeError, ValueError):
        return {"retry": False}


def _recover_one_expired_step(
    db: Session,
    candidate: ProductionStepAttempt,
    *,
    current: datetime,
) -> int:
    run = db.scalar(
        select(ProductionRun)
        .where(ProductionRun.id == candidate.run_id)
        .with_for_update()
    )
    if run is None:
        return 0
    attempt = db.scalar(
        select(ProductionStepAttempt)
        .where(
            ProductionStepAttempt.id == candidate.id,
            ProductionStepAttempt.status.in_({"leased", "running"}),
            ProductionStepAttempt.lease_expires_at.is_not(None),
            ProductionStepAttempt.lease_expires_at <= current,
        )
        .with_for_update()
        .execution_options(populate_existing=True)
    )
    if attempt is None:
        return 0
    transport_restart = (
        _batch_transport_restart(db, run, attempt, current)
        if run.status in RUNNABLE_RUN_STATUSES else None
    )
    if transport_restart and transport_restart.get('resource_wait_seconds'):
        from .batch_endpoint_recovery import RESOURCE_WAIT_CODE
        fail_runtime_step(db, attempt.id, attempt.lease_token, RESOURCE_WAIT_CODE,
            '恢复已结算的供应商 429 资源等待，原尝试和费用保留', retryable=True,
            retry_after_seconds=transport_restart['resource_wait_seconds'], now=current)
        return 1
    if transport_restart and transport_restart.get("wait_until"):
        deadline = transport_restart["wait_until"]
        attempt.lease_expires_at = deadline
        dispatch = db.scalar(select(RuntimeDispatchItem).where(
            RuntimeDispatchItem.step_attempt_id == attempt.id))
        if dispatch is not None:
            dispatch.lease_expires_at = deadline
        run.lease_expires_at = deadline
        db.flush()
        return 1
    uncertain_invocations = db.scalars(
        select(WorkflowModelInvocation).where(
            WorkflowModelInvocation.step_attempt_id == attempt.id,
            WorkflowModelInvocation.status == "reserved",
        )
    ).all()
    unknown_external_state = bool(uncertain_invocations)
    if unknown_external_state:
        for invocation in uncertain_invocations:
            fail_model_invocation(
                db,
                invocation_id=invocation.id,
                error_code="model_invocation_outcome_unknown",
                error_message="模型调用租约过期，供应商执行结果无法确认",
                evidence={
                    "retryable": False,
                    "technical_error_type": "unknown_external_state",
                    "attempt_count": 0,
                },
            )
    if transport_restart and transport_restart.get('experiment_hold'):
        from .batch_experiment import HOLD_CODE
        fail_runtime_step(db, attempt.id, attempt.lease_token, HOLD_CODE,
            '批量并发实验等待处理：不增加评测尝试，不转在线，保留原费用',
            retryable=True, retry_after_seconds=900, now=current)
        return 1
    if run.status not in RUNNABLE_RUN_STATUSES:
        # run 已经终态（取消/失败/成功）但步骤还挂在 leased/running——原先
        # 这里直接 continue，孤儿步骤永远占着并发槽位：run135 取消后残留的
        # 8 个 running 僵尸恰好占满 global_limit=8，把 run136 整轮饿死在
        # 队列里。终态 run 的步骤只收束，不重派。
        attempt.status = "failed"
        attempt.last_error_code = "STEP_ORPHANED_BY_TERMINAL_RUN"
        attempt.last_error_message = "所属运行已终态，步骤收束回收"
        attempt.finished_at = current
        attempt.lease_expires_at = None
        orphan_dispatch = db.scalar(
            select(RuntimeDispatchItem).where(
                RuntimeDispatchItem.step_attempt_id == attempt.id
            )
        )
        if orphan_dispatch is not None and orphan_dispatch.status in (
            "queued",
            "leased",
        ):
            orphan_dispatch.status = "canceled"
        return 1
    script = db.get(ScriptVersion, attempt.script_version_id)
    dispatch = db.scalar(
        select(RuntimeDispatchItem).where(
            RuntimeDispatchItem.step_attempt_id == attempt.id
        )
    )
    attempt.status = "failed"
    attempt.last_error_code = (
        "model_invocation_outcome_unknown"
        if unknown_external_state
        else "STEP_LEASE_EXPIRED"
    )
    attempt.last_error_message = (
        "模型调用租约过期，供应商执行结果无法确认"
        if unknown_external_state
        else "Worker 租约过期"
    )
    attempt.finished_at = current
    attempt.lease_owner = None
    attempt.lease_token = None
    attempt.lease_expires_at = None
    if dispatch is not None:
        if unknown_external_state:
            _close_dispatch(
                dispatch,
                status="dead_lettered",
                error_code="model_invocation_outcome_unknown",
                error_message=(
                    "模型调用租约过期，供应商执行结果无法确认"
                ),
                now=current,
            )
        else:
            _close_dispatch(
                dispatch,
                status="completed",
                error_code="STEP_LEASE_EXPIRED",
                error_message="Worker 租约过期",
                now=current,
            )
    if unknown_external_state and not (transport_restart and transport_restart.get("retry")):
        error_policy = runtime_step_error_policy(db, attempt)
        if transport_restart is None and error_policy in {"continue", "skip"}:
            _record_fail_open_step(
                db,
                run=run,
                attempt=attempt,
                dispatch=dispatch,
                policy_kind=error_policy,
                error_code="model_invocation_outcome_unknown",
                error_message=(
                    "模型调用结果未知，已禁止自动重试并按节点策略续跑"
                ),
                current=current,
            )
        else:
            db.flush()
            terminalize_runtime_run(
                db,
                run,
                status="failed",
                error_code="model_invocation_outcome_unknown",
                error_message=(
                    "模型调用结果未知，已进入死信并禁止自动重试"
                ),
                now=current,
            )
        return 1
    if script is None:
        if dispatch is not None:
            _close_dispatch(
                dispatch,
                status="dead_lettered",
                error_code="runtime_evidence_missing",
                error_message="步骤关联的脚本版本不存在",
                now=current,
            )
        db.flush()
        terminalize_runtime_run(
            db,
            run,
            status="failed",
            error_code="runtime_evidence_missing",
            error_message="步骤关联的脚本版本不存在",
            now=current,
        )
        return 1
    if transport_restart is not None and not transport_restart.get("retry"):
        terminalize_runtime_run(db, run, status="failed",
            error_code="model_transport_restart_blocked",
            error_message="冻结传输次数耗尽或缺少可安全恢复的模型结果证据", now=current)
        return 1
    retry_policy = runtime_step_retry_policy(db, attempt)
    if transport_restart and transport_restart.get("retry"):
        retry_policy = {"kind": "fixed", "delay_seconds": 1,
                        "max_attempts": transport_restart["max_attempts"]}
    max_attempts = int(retry_policy["max_attempts"])
    if attempt.attempt_no >= max_attempts:
        error_policy = runtime_step_error_policy(db, attempt)
        if transport_restart is None and error_policy in {"continue", "skip"}:
            _record_fail_open_step(
                db,
                run=run,
                attempt=attempt,
                dispatch=dispatch,
                policy_kind=error_policy,
                error_code="STEP_LEASE_EXPIRED",
                error_message="步骤租约过期且达到重试上限",
                current=current,
            )
            return 1
        if dispatch is not None:
            _close_dispatch(
                dispatch,
                status="dead_lettered",
                error_code="STEP_LEASE_EXPIRED",
                error_message="步骤租约过期且达到重试上限",
                now=current,
            )
        db.flush()
        terminalize_runtime_run(
            db,
            run,
            status="failed",
            error_code="STEP_LEASE_EXPIRED",
            error_message="步骤租约过期且达到重试上限",
            now=current,
        )
        return 1
    next_number = attempt.attempt_no + 1
    next_attempt = ProductionStepAttempt(
        run_id=attempt.run_id,
        step_key=attempt.step_key,
        step_type=attempt.step_type,
        sequence=attempt.sequence,
        script_version_id=attempt.script_version_id,
        status="retryable",
        attempt_no=next_number,
        idempotency_key=(
            f"{run.run_key}:{attempt.step_key}:{attempt.input_hash}:"
            f"attempt:{next_number}"
        ),
        input_manifest_json=attempt.input_manifest_json,
        input_hash=attempt.input_hash,
        output_manifest_json="{}",
        checkpoint_json=attempt.checkpoint_json,
        checkpoint_hash=attempt.checkpoint_hash,
        last_error_code="",
        last_error_message="",
    )
    db.add(next_attempt)
    db.flush()
    db.add(
        RuntimeDispatchItem(
            step_attempt_id=next_attempt.id,
            queue_class="recovery",
            priority=100,
            status="queued",
            available_at=current
            + timedelta(
                seconds=_retry_policy_delay(
                    retry_policy,
                    attempt.attempt_no,
                )
            ),
            max_attempts=max_attempts,
        )
    )
    active_siblings = sorted(
        (
            item
            for item in _latest_attempts(db, run.id).values()
            if item.status in {"leased", "running"}
        ),
        key=lambda item: (item.sequence, item.id),
    )
    run.status = "running" if active_siblings else "retryable"
    run.current_step_key = (
        active_siblings[0].step_key if active_siblings else attempt.step_key
    )
    run.error_code = "STEP_LEASE_EXPIRED"
    run.error_message = "步骤租约已回收并进入恢复队列"
    run.lease_owner = active_siblings[0].lease_owner if active_siblings else None
    run.lease_token = active_siblings[0].lease_token if active_siblings else None
    run.lease_expires_at = (
        active_siblings[0].lease_expires_at if active_siblings else None
    )
    return 1


def fail_runtime_step(
    db: Session,
    attempt_id: int,
    lease_token: str,
    error_code: str,
    error_message: str,
    *,
    retryable: bool = True,
    retry_after_seconds: float | None = None,
    now: datetime | None = None,
) -> ProductionStepAttempt:
    initial_attempt = db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, attempt_id)
    if initial_attempt is None:
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
            "step_attempt_not_found",
            "步骤尝试不存在",
            status_code=404,
        )
    run = db.scalar(
        select(ProductionRun)
        .where(ProductionRun.id == initial_attempt.run_id)
        .with_for_update()
    )
    if run is None:
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
            "runtime_evidence_missing",
            "步骤关联的运行不存在",
            status_code=409,
        )
    attempt = db.scalar(
        select(ProductionStepAttempt)
        .where(ProductionStepAttempt.id == attempt_id)
        .with_for_update()
        .execution_options(populate_existing=True)
    )
    if run.status not in RUNNABLE_RUN_STATUSES:
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
            "run_terminal_state",
            "运行已经停止，旧 Worker 失败回报不会被接收",
            status_code=409,
        )
    if (
        attempt is None
        or attempt.status not in {"leased", "running"}
        or attempt.lease_token != lease_token
    ):
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
            "step_lease_stale",
            "步骤租约已失效",
            status_code=409,
        )
    current = _now(now)
    bounded_retry_after = bounded_retry_after_seconds(retry_after_seconds)
    dispatch = db.scalar(
        select(RuntimeDispatchItem).where(
            RuntimeDispatchItem.step_attempt_id == attempt.id
        )
    )
    from .model_quota import LOCAL_QUOTA_WAIT_CODES
    from .batch_endpoint_recovery import DURABLE_WAIT_CODES

    if retryable and error_code in LOCAL_QUOTA_WAIT_CODES | DURABLE_WAIT_CODES | LOCAL_RUNTIME_WAIT_CODES:
        # Capacity/budget and pre-node database waits preserve the execution
        # attempt. Real requests remain charged independently in the ledger.
        if dispatch is None:
            raise WorkflowRuntimeError("runtime_evidence_missing", "步骤调度记录缺失")
        delay = float(bounded_retry_after or 5)
        if error_code in DURABLE_WAIT_CODES and isinstance(retry_after_seconds, (int, float)):
            delay = max(delay, min(86400, retry_after_seconds))
        if error_code == "quota_pool_concurrency_exhausted":
            delay = min(delay, 5)
        dispatch.status = "queued"
        dispatch.available_at = current + timedelta(seconds=max(1, delay))
        dispatch.attempt_count = max(0, dispatch.attempt_count - 1)
        dispatch.last_error_code = error_code
        dispatch.last_error_message = error_message
        dispatch.dead_lettered_at = None
        for row in (dispatch, attempt):
            row.lease_owner = None
            row.lease_token = None
            row.lease_expires_at = None
            row.heartbeat_at = None
        dispatch.leased_at = None
        attempt.status = "retryable"
        attempt.finished_at = None
        attempt.last_error_code = error_code
        attempt.last_error_message = error_message
        siblings = sorted((item for item in _latest_attempts(db, run.id).values()
            if item.status in {"leased", "running"}), key=lambda item: (item.sequence, item.id))
        run.status = "running" if siblings else "retryable"
        run.current_step_key = siblings[0].step_key if siblings else attempt.step_key
        run.error_code = error_code
        run.error_message = error_message
        run.lease_owner = siblings[0].lease_owner if siblings else None
        run.lease_token = siblings[0].lease_token if siblings else None
        run.lease_expires_at = siblings[0].lease_expires_at if siblings else None
        db.flush()
        return attempt
    attempt.status = "failed"
    attempt.last_error_code = error_code
    attempt.last_error_message = error_message
    attempt.finished_at = current
    attempt.lease_owner = None
    attempt.lease_token = None
    attempt.lease_expires_at = None
    script = db.get(ScriptVersion, attempt.script_version_id)
    if script is None:
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
            "runtime_evidence_missing",
            "步骤关联的运行或脚本版本不存在",
            status_code=409,
        )
    retry_policy = runtime_step_retry_policy(db, attempt)
    max_attempts = int(retry_policy["max_attempts"])
    if retryable and attempt.attempt_no < max_attempts:
        if dispatch is not None:
            _close_dispatch(
                dispatch,
                status="completed",
                error_code=error_code,
                error_message=error_message,
                now=current,
            )
        next_number = attempt.attempt_no + 1
        next_attempt = ProductionStepAttempt(
            run_id=attempt.run_id,
            step_key=attempt.step_key,
            step_type=attempt.step_type,
            sequence=attempt.sequence,
            script_version_id=attempt.script_version_id,
            status="retryable",
            attempt_no=next_number,
            idempotency_key=(
                f"{run.run_key}:{attempt.step_key}:{attempt.input_hash}:"
                f"attempt:{next_number}"
            ),
            input_manifest_json=attempt.input_manifest_json,
            input_hash=attempt.input_hash,
            output_manifest_json="{}",
            checkpoint_json=attempt.checkpoint_json,
            checkpoint_hash=attempt.checkpoint_hash,
            last_error_code="",
            last_error_message="",
        )
        db.add(next_attempt)
        db.flush()
        policy_delay = _retry_policy_delay(retry_policy, attempt.attempt_no)
        provider_delay = float(bounded_retry_after or 0)
        db.add(
            RuntimeDispatchItem(
                step_attempt_id=next_attempt.id,
                queue_class="recovery",
                priority=100,
                status="queued",
                available_at=current
                + timedelta(seconds=max(policy_delay, provider_delay)),
                max_attempts=max_attempts,
            )
        )
        active_siblings = sorted(
            (
                item
                for item in _latest_attempts(db, run.id).values()
                if item.status in {"leased", "running"}
            ),
            key=lambda item: (item.sequence, item.id),
        )
        run.status = "running" if active_siblings else "retryable"
        run.current_step_key = (
            active_siblings[0].step_key if active_siblings else attempt.step_key
        )
        run.error_code = error_code
        run.error_message = error_message
        run.lease_owner = active_siblings[0].lease_owner if active_siblings else None
        run.lease_token = active_siblings[0].lease_token if active_siblings else None
        run.lease_expires_at = (
            active_siblings[0].lease_expires_at if active_siblings else None
        )
        db.flush()
        return next_attempt
    if dispatch is not None:
        _close_dispatch(
            dispatch,
            status="dead_lettered",
            error_code=error_code,
            error_message=error_message,
            now=current,
        )
    db.flush()
    terminalize_runtime_run(
        db,
        run,
        status="failed",
        error_code=error_code,
        error_message=error_message,
        now=current,
    )
    return attempt


def _record_fail_open_step(
    db: Session,
    *,
    run: ProductionRun,
    attempt: ProductionStepAttempt,
    dispatch: RuntimeDispatchItem | None,
    policy_kind: str,
    error_code: str,
    error_message: str,
    current: datetime,
) -> ProductionStepAttempt:
    policy_code = f"error_policy_{policy_kind}"
    output_manifest = {
        "schema_version": "workflow-error-policy-evidence-v1",
        "status": "skipped",
        "step_key": attempt.step_key,
        "error_policy": policy_kind,
        "error": {"code": error_code, "message": error_message},
    }
    output_hash = _hash(output_manifest)
    checkpoint = {
        "schema_version": "runtime-checkpoint-v1",
        "step_key": attempt.step_key,
        "input_hash": attempt.input_hash,
        "output_hash": output_hash,
    }
    attempt.status = "skipped"
    attempt.output_manifest_json = canonical_json(output_manifest)
    attempt.output_hash = output_hash
    attempt.checkpoint_json = canonical_json(checkpoint)
    attempt.checkpoint_hash = _hash(checkpoint)
    attempt.last_error_code = policy_code
    attempt.last_error_message = error_message
    attempt.finished_at = current
    attempt.heartbeat_at = current
    attempt.lease_owner = None
    attempt.lease_token = None
    attempt.lease_expires_at = None
    if dispatch is not None:
        _close_dispatch(
            dispatch,
            status="completed",
            error_code=policy_code,
            error_message=error_message,
            now=current,
        )
    run.last_checkpoint_id = attempt.id
    _reconcile_locked_runtime_run(db, run, now=current)
    return attempt


def skip_runtime_step_after_error(
    db: Session,
    attempt_id: int,
    lease_token: str,
    *,
    policy_kind: str,
    error_code: str,
    error_message: str,
    now: datetime | None = None,
) -> ProductionStepAttempt:
    """Record an exhausted/non-retryable failure and keep the graph runnable."""

    if policy_kind not in {"continue", "skip"}:
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
            "runtime_error_policy_invalid",
            "只有继续或跳过策略可以在失败后续跑",
            status_code=409,
        )
    initial_attempt = db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, attempt_id)
    if initial_attempt is None:
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
            "step_attempt_not_found",
            "步骤尝试不存在",
            status_code=404,
        )
    run = db.scalar(
        select(ProductionRun)
        .where(ProductionRun.id == initial_attempt.run_id)
        .with_for_update()
    )
    attempt = db.scalar(
        select(ProductionStepAttempt)
        .where(ProductionStepAttempt.id == attempt_id)
        .with_for_update()
        .execution_options(populate_existing=True)
    )
    if run is None or attempt is None:
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
            "runtime_evidence_missing",
            "步骤关联的运行不存在",
            status_code=409,
        )
    if run.status not in RUNNABLE_RUN_STATUSES:
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
            "run_terminal_state",
            "运行已经停止，旧 Worker 失败回报不会被接收",
            status_code=409,
        )
    if (
        attempt.status not in {"leased", "running"}
        or attempt.lease_token != lease_token
    ):
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
            "step_lease_stale",
            "步骤租约已失效",
            status_code=409,
        )
    current = _now(now)
    dispatch = db.scalar(
        select(RuntimeDispatchItem).where(
            RuntimeDispatchItem.step_attempt_id == attempt.id
        )
    )
    return _record_fail_open_step(
        db,
        run=run,
        attempt=attempt,
        dispatch=dispatch,
        policy_kind=policy_kind,
        error_code=error_code,
        error_message=error_message,
        current=current,
    )


def retry_failed_run_from_checkpoint(
    db: Session,
    run_id: int,
    *,
    now: datetime | None = None,
    actor: str = "operator",
    restart_transport_window: bool = True,
) -> ProductionRun:
    """Resume a terminal failure without replaying successful node facts.

    Automatic retries are bounded by the frozen script policy.  This separate
    operator action creates a new auditable attempt for the failed node and for
    work that terminalization skipped, while keeping every succeeded checkpoint
    immutable.  Frozen route skips remain terminal and are not re-opened.
    """

    run = db.scalar(
        select(ProductionRun)
        .where(ProductionRun.id == run_id)
        .with_for_update()
    )
    if run is None:
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
            "production_run_not_found",
            "生产运行不存在",
            status_code=404,
        )
    if run.status != "failed":
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
            "run_retry_state_invalid",
            "只有 failed 运行可以人工重试",
            status_code=409,
        )
    if run.source_type == "content_ingress_event":
        from .incremental_reassessment import assert_no_replacement
        assert_no_replacement(db, run)
    from .failure_resolution_models import ManualFailureResolution
    if db.scalar(
        select(ManualFailureResolution.id).where(
            ManualFailureResolution.production_run_id == run.id,
            ManualFailureResolution.status == "published",
        ).limit(1)
    ):
        # A person already concluded this material. Re-running would spend
        # money and could publish a model result over the human conclusion.
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
            "run_retry_manual_resolution_exists",
            "这条素材已有人工结论，不能再重跑",
            status_code=409,
        )
    workflow_version = db.get(WorkflowVersion, run.workflow_version_id)
    if workflow_version is None:
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
            "runtime_evidence_missing",
            "运行关联的工作流版本不存在",
            status_code=409,
        )
    manifest = persisted_workflow_manifest(workflow_version)
    latest = _latest_attempts(db, run.id)
    failed = [attempt for attempt in latest.values() if attempt.status == "failed"]
    terminal_error_code = run.error_code
    if len(failed) == 1:
        restart_keys = {
            attempt.step_key
            for attempt in latest.values()
            if attempt.status == "failed"
            or (
                attempt.status == "skipped"
                and attempt.last_error_code == terminal_error_code
            )
        }
    elif not failed and terminal_error_code in RETRYABLE_NATIVE_FINALIZATION_ERRORS:
        nodes = manifest.get("nodes")
        nodes = nodes if isinstance(nodes, list) else []
        restart_keys = {
            str(node.get("node_instance_id"))
            for node in nodes
            if isinstance(node, Mapping)
            and node.get("kind") == "prompt"
            and isinstance(node.get("node_instance_id"), str)
            and node.get("node_instance_id") in latest
        }
        outgoing: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
        for edge in manifest.get("edges", []):
            if not isinstance(edge, Mapping):
                continue
            source = edge.get("from")
            target = edge.get("to")
            if isinstance(source, str) and isinstance(target, str):
                outgoing.setdefault(source, []).append(target)
        frontier = list(restart_keys)
        while frontier:
            source = frontier.pop()
            for target in outgoing.get(source, []):
                if target in latest and target not in restart_keys:
                    restart_keys.add(target)
                    frontier.append(target)
    else:
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
            "run_retry_step_ambiguous",
            "失败运行没有唯一的失败节点，不能安全确定恢复位置",
            status_code=409,
        )
    restart = [latest[key] for key in restart_keys]
    if not restart:
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
            "run_resume_step_missing",
            "没有可恢复的步骤",
            status_code=409,
        )

    incoming = {
        attempt.step_key: []
        for attempt in latest.values()
    }
    for edge in manifest.get("edges", []):
        if not isinstance(edge, Mapping):
            continue
        source = edge.get("from")
        target = edge.get("to")
        if isinstance(source, str) and isinstance(target, str):
            incoming.setdefault(target, []).append(source)

    current = _now(now)
    restarted: dict[str, ProductionStepAttempt] = {}
    for previous in sorted(restart, key=lambda item: (item.sequence, item.id)):
        next_number = previous.attempt_no + 1
        next_attempt = ProductionStepAttempt(
            run_id=previous.run_id,
            step_key=previous.step_key,
            step_type=previous.step_type,
            sequence=previous.sequence,
            script_version_id=previous.script_version_id,
            status="pending",
            attempt_no=next_number,
            idempotency_key=(
                f"{run.run_key}:{previous.step_key}:{previous.input_hash}:"
                f"attempt:{next_number}"
            ),
            input_manifest_json=previous.input_manifest_json,
            input_hash=previous.input_hash,
            output_manifest_json="{}",
            checkpoint_json="{}",
            last_error_code="",
            last_error_message="",
        )
        db.add(next_attempt)
        db.flush()
        restarted[previous.step_key] = next_attempt

    if run.source_type == "baseline_regression_item":
        from .native_baseline_regression import refresh_native_baseline_run

        item = db.scalar(
            select(BaselineRegressionItem)
            .where(BaselineRegressionItem.id == run.source_id)
            .with_for_update()
        )
        if (
            item is None
            or item.production_run_id != run.id
            or item.status != "failed"
            or item.job_id is not None
            or item.evaluation_id is not None
        ):
            raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
                "native_baseline_retry_conflict",
                "存量回归条目已有其他结果，不能覆盖后重试",
                status_code=409,
            )
        item.status = "queued"
        item.result_snapshot_json = "{}"
        item.error_message = ""
        item.finished_at = None
        refresh_native_baseline_run(item.run)

    # 人工重试的语义是「再给它一轮」：重开传输窗口，新一代批量机会 + 24 小时时钟从现在起算。
    # 不改任何旧账本行；没有这条记录，冻结策略会立刻按历史再判 exhausted（2026-09-18 实证 37/40）。
    # 事故恢复（native_baseline_incident）是「把被我们自己打断的调用接着跑」，不开新窗口。
    if restart_transport_window:
        from .effective_batch_clock import record_operator_retry

        record_operator_retry(
            db,
            run_id=run.id,
            actor=actor,
            step_attempt_ids=[attempt.id for attempt in restarted.values()],
            now=current,
        )

    from .incremental_sla import ensure_sla_projection
    ensure_sla_projection(run)  # 迁移 131 前创建的 failed run 重开时补投影（评审 R1）
    run.status = "queued"
    run.current_step_key = None
    run.blockers_json = "[]"
    run.completed_steps = sum(
        attempt.status in {"succeeded", "skipped"}
        for key, attempt in latest.items()
        if key not in restarted
    )
    run.failed_steps = 0
    run.error_code = ""
    run.error_message = ""
    run.next_retry_at = None
    run.finished_at = None
    run.lease_owner = None
    run.lease_token = None
    run.lease_expires_at = None

    for step_key, attempt in restarted.items():
        if incoming.get(step_key):
            continue
        db.add(
            RuntimeDispatchItem(
                step_attempt_id=attempt.id,
                queue_class="recovery",
                priority=100,
                status="queued",
                available_at=current,
                max_attempts=_dispatch_max_attempts(db, attempt),
            )
        )
    ready = _ready_pending_steps(db, run)
    # SessionLocal disables autoflush in production. Materialize recovery
    # dispatches before querying them below; otherwise a valid checkpoint retry
    # is incorrectly rejected as having no runnable node.
    db.flush()
    for attempt in ready:
        dispatch = db.scalar(
            select(RuntimeDispatchItem).where(
                RuntimeDispatchItem.step_attempt_id == attempt.id
            )
        )
        if dispatch is not None:
            dispatch.queue_class = "recovery"
            dispatch.priority = 100
            dispatch.available_at = current
    runnable = sorted(
        (
            attempt
            for attempt in restarted.values()
            if db.scalar(
                select(RuntimeDispatchItem.id).where(
                    RuntimeDispatchItem.step_attempt_id == attempt.id,
                    RuntimeDispatchItem.status == "queued",
                )
            )
            is not None
        ),
        key=lambda item: (item.sequence, item.id),
    )
    if not runnable:
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
            "run_retry_checkpoint_invalid",
            "失败节点的上游检查点不完整，不能安全恢复",
            status_code=409,
        )
    run.current_step_key = runnable[0].step_key
    # The old attempt remains a failure fact, but its dispatch is no longer
    # actionable once a replacement attempt was created. All recovery callers
    # must share this rule (not only the operations bulk-retry endpoint).
    superseded = list(db.scalars(select(RuntimeDispatchItem)
        .join(ProductionStepAttempt, ProductionStepAttempt.id == RuntimeDispatchItem.step_attempt_id)
        .where(ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == run.id, RuntimeDispatchItem.status == "dead_lettered")
        .with_for_update()))
    for dispatch in superseded:
        dispatch.status = "canceled"
    if superseded:
        from .audit import append_audit_event
        append_audit_event(db, category="failure_console", action="dead_letters_superseded",
            subject_type="production_run", subject_id=run.id, actor=actor,
            payload={"dispatch_ids": [item.id for item in superseded],
                     "replacement_attempt_ids": [item.id for item in restarted.values()],
                     "reason": "checkpoint recovery created replacement attempts; old errors and charges retained"})
    db.flush()
    return run


def resume_from_checkpoint(
    db: Session,
    run_id: int,
) -> ProductionRun:
    run = db.get(ProductionRun, run_id)
    if run is None:
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
            "production_run_not_found",
            "生产运行不存在",
            status_code=404,
        )
    if run.status not in {"paused", "retryable", "blocked"}:
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
            "run_resume_state_invalid",
            "当前运行状态不能恢复",
            status_code=409,
        )
    latest = _latest_attempts(db, run.id)
    # Judge by the step, not the run status: a release hold rewrites a blocked
    # run to paused, and resuming it must still not follow an unchosen route.
    if any(attempt.status == "blocked" for attempt in latest.values()):
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
            "run_manual_review_required",
            "该运行停在人工处理节点，不能直接恢复未选择的后续路线",
            status_code=409,
        )
    pending = sorted(
        (
            attempt
            for attempt in latest.values()
            if attempt.status in {"pending", "retryable"}
        ),
        key=lambda item: (item.sequence, item.id),
    )
    if not pending:
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
            "run_resume_step_missing",
            "没有可恢复的步骤",
            status_code=409,
        )
    attempt = pending[0]
    from .incremental_sla import ensure_sla_projection
    # 发布暂存/阻塞后恢复的旧 run 补投影（评审 R1）；放在 batch_job_wait 提前返回之前（复核 F1）
    ensure_sla_projection(run)
    if attempt.last_error_code == "batch_job_wait":
        # Remote work owns this attempt. Resuming a paused run must not turn
        # its retired dispatch back into a second paid model invocation.
        run.status = "running"
        run.current_step_key = attempt.step_key
        db.flush()
        return run
    dispatch = db.scalar(
        select(RuntimeDispatchItem).where(
            RuntimeDispatchItem.step_attempt_id == attempt.id
        )
    )
    if dispatch is None:
        db.add(
            RuntimeDispatchItem(
                step_attempt_id=attempt.id,
                queue_class="recovery",
                priority=100,
                status="queued",
                available_at=_now(),
                max_attempts=_dispatch_max_attempts(db, attempt),
            )
        )
    elif dispatch.status != "queued":
        dispatch.status = "queued"
        dispatch.queue_class = "recovery"
        dispatch.available_at = _now()
    run.status = "queued"
    run.current_step_key = attempt.step_key
    db.flush()
    return run

===== FILE backend/app/workflow_runtime_api.py =====
from __future__ import annotations
from .workflow_snapshot_store import read_run_snapshot_document, prefetch_run_snapshot_blobs

import hashlib
import json
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from typing import Any, Callable, Mapping
from urllib.parse import urlsplit, urlunsplit

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, Query, Response
from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field
from sqlalchemy import func, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .audit import canonical_json
from .authz import has_permission
from .database import get_db
from .models import (
    ProductionRun,
    ProductionStepAttempt,
    RuntimeAuditEvent,
    RuntimeDispatchItem,
    ScriptVersion,
    User,
    WorkflowExternalWait,
    WorkflowVersion,
)
from .runtime_capacity import runtime_capacity_snapshot
from .workflow_runtime import (
    ProductionRunRequest,
    WorkflowRuntimeError,
    create_production_run,
    persist_baseline_workflow_outputs,
    retry_failed_run_from_checkpoint,
    resume_from_checkpoint,
    terminalize_runtime_run,
)


FORBIDDEN_EXECUTABLE_FIELDS = frozenset(
    {"source", "code", "command", "shell", "sql", "script"}
)
RESERVED_MODEL_EXECUTION_FIELDS = frozenset(
    {
        "model_execution_mode",
        "real_execution_confirmed",
        "real_execution_budget",
        "real_execution_node_limits",
    }
)
CONNECTOR_IO_MODES = frozenset(
    {
        "imported_sample",
        "evidence_only",
        "intake_event",
        "transactional_outbox",
        "workflow_input",
        "projection_only",
        "unavailable",
        "imported_evidence",
        "callback_fact",
    }
)


class RuntimeRunCreate(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")

    workflow_version_id: int = Field(gt=0)
    idempotency_key: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=200)
    category_key: str | None = Field(default=None, max_length=40)
    queue_class: str
    runtime_context: dict[str, Any] = Field(default_factory=dict)
    input_manifest: dict[str, Any]
    owner: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=120)
    reason: str = ""
    source_type: str | None = Field(default=None, max_length=60)
    source_id: int | None = None
    source_run_id: int | None = None
    environment: str = "dry_run"


def _runtime_error(exc: WorkflowRuntimeError) -> HTTPException:
    return HTTPException(
        status_code=exc.status_code,
        detail={"code": exc.code, "message": str(exc)},
    )


def _require_write(user: User) -> None:
    if not has_permission(user, "runtime:write"):
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=403,
            detail={
                "code": "runtime_write_forbidden",
                "message": "当前账号没有运行时写权限",
            },
        )


def _scan_executable_fields(value: Any, *, path: str = "$") -> None:
    if isinstance(value, Mapping):
        for key, child in value.items():
            child_path = f"{path}.{key}"
            if str(key).lower() in FORBIDDEN_EXECUTABLE_FIELDS:
                raise HTTPException(
                    status_code=400,
                    detail={
                        "code": "arbitrary_code_field_forbidden",
                        "message": f"运行输入包含禁止字段：{child_path}",
                    },
                )
            _scan_executable_fields(child, path=child_path)
    elif isinstance(value, list):
        for index, child in enumerate(value):
            _scan_executable_fields(child, path=f"{path}[{index}]")


def _scan_reserved_model_execution_fields(
    value: Any, *, path: str = "$.runtime_context"
) -> None:
    if isinstance(value, Mapping):
        for key, child in value.items():
            child_path = f"{path}.{key}"
            if str(key).lower() in RESERVED_MODEL_EXECUTION_FIELDS:
                raise HTTPException(
                    status_code=400,
                    detail={
                        "code": "runtime_model_execution_reserved",
                        "message": f"真实模型执行字段只能由受控业务入口写入：{child_path}",
                    },
                )
            _scan_reserved_model_execution_fields(child, path=child_path)
    elif isinstance(value, list):
        for index, child in enumerate(value):
            _scan_reserved_model_execution_fields(
                child, path=f"{path}[{index}]"
            )


def _run(db: Session, run_key: str) -> ProductionRun:
    row = db.scalar(
        select(ProductionRun).where(ProductionRun.run_key == run_key)
    )
    if row is None:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=404,
            detail={
                "code": "production_run_not_found",
                "message": "生产运行不存在",
            },
        )
    return row


def _allowed_actions(status: str) -> list[str]:
    return {
        "planned": ["cancel"],
        "queued": ["cancel", "pause"],
        "running": ["cancel", "pause"],
        "waiting_external": ["cancel"],
        "paused": ["cancel", "resume"],
        "retryable": ["cancel", "resume"],
        "blocked": ["cancel", "resume"],
        "failed": ["retry"],
    }.get(status, [])


def _external_wait_payload(
    db: Session,
    row: ProductionRun,
) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    wait = db.scalar(
        select(WorkflowExternalWait)
        .where(WorkflowExternalWait.production_run_id == row.id)
        .order_by(WorkflowExternalWait.id.desc())
        .limit(1)
    )
    return _external_wait_value(wait)


def _external_wait_value(
    wait: WorkflowExternalWait | None,
) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    if wait is None:
        return None
    return {
        "id": wait.id,
        "state": wait.state,
        "request_node_instance_id": wait.request_node_instance_id,
        "resume_node_instance_id": wait.resume_node_instance_id,
        "correlation_id": wait.correlation_id,
        "expected_contract_version_id": wait.expected_contract_version_id,
        "timeout_policy": wait.timeout_policy,
        "created_at": wait.created_at,
        "deadline_at": wait.deadline_at,
        "resumed_at": wait.resumed_at,
        "expired_at": wait.expired_at,
        "cancelled_at": wait.cancelled_at,
        "callback_intake_event_id": wait.callback_intake_event_id,
    }


def _frozen_asset_id(row: ProductionRun) -> int | None:
    """The image this run is about, so a list can show it instead of a run key.

    Both incremental and regression runs freeze it at the same place in their
    snapshot. A run whose snapshot predates that, or is unreadable, simply has
    no thumbnail - it must never fail the listing.
    """
    try:
        manifest = read_run_snapshot_document(row)["runtime_context"]["input_manifest"]
    except (TypeError, ValueError, KeyError):
        return None
    value = manifest.get("asset_id") if isinstance(manifest, dict) else None
    return value if isinstance(value, int) and not isinstance(value, bool) and value > 0 else None


def _run_payload(
    db: Session,
    row: ProductionRun,
    *,
    prefetched_external_wait: WorkflowExternalWait | None = None,
    external_wait_prefetched: bool = False,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    workflow = db.get(WorkflowVersion, row.workflow_version_id)
    return {
        "id": row.id,
        "asset_id": _frozen_asset_id(row),
        "run_key": row.run_key,
        "idempotency_key": row.idempotency_key,
        "source_type": row.source_type,
        "source_id": row.source_id,
        "source_run_id": row.source_run_id,
        "workflow_definition_id": row.workflow_definition_id,
        "workflow_version_id": row.workflow_version_id,
        "workflow_version": workflow.version if workflow is not None else "",
        "snapshot_hash": row.snapshot_hash,
        "category_key": row.category_key,
        "queue_class": row.queue_class,
        "status": row.status,
        "current_step_key": row.current_step_key,
        "blockers": json.loads(row.blockers_json),
        "requested_by": row.requested_by,
        "owner": row.owner,
        "reason": row.reason,
        "environment": row.environment,
        "model_execution_mode": row.model_execution_mode,
        "total_steps": row.total_steps,
        "completed_steps": row.completed_steps,
        "failed_steps": row.failed_steps,
        "last_checkpoint_id": row.last_checkpoint_id,
        "attempt_count": row.attempt_count,
        "next_retry_at": row.next_retry_at,
        "error_code": row.error_code,
        "error_message": row.error_message,
        "created_at": row.created_at,
        "updated_at": row.updated_at,
        "started_at": row.started_at,
        "finished_at": row.finished_at,
        "allowed_actions": _allowed_actions(row.status),
        "external_wait": (
            _external_wait_value(prefetched_external_wait)
            if external_wait_prefetched
            else _external_wait_payload(db, row)
        ),
    }


def _safe_connector_evidence(serialized: str | None) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    try:
        payload = json.loads(serialized or "{}")
    except json.JSONDecodeError:
        return None
    if not isinstance(payload, Mapping):
        return None
    io_mode = payload.get("io_mode")
    external_io_performed = payload.get("external_io_performed")
    if (
        io_mode not in CONNECTOR_IO_MODES
        or external_io_performed is not False
    ):
        return None
    evidence: dict[str, Any] = {
        "io_mode": io_mode,
        "external_io_performed": external_io_performed,
    }
    first_seen_mode = io_mode in {
        "unavailable",
        "imported_evidence",
        "callback_fact",
    }
    for key in ("connector_contract_id", "contract_version", "outbox_event_id"):
        value = payload.get(key)
        if value is None and not first_seen_mode:
            evidence[key] = value
        elif (
            isinstance(value, int)
            and not isinstance(value, bool)
            and value > 0
        ):
            evidence[key] = value
    for key in ("schema_fingerprint", "payload_hash"):
        value = payload.get(key)
        if (
            isinstance(value, str)
            and len(value) == 64
            and all(character in "0123456789abcdef" for character in value)
        ):
            evidence[key] = value
    if first_seen_mode:
        output = payload.get("output")
        if not isinstance(output, Mapping):
            return None
        status = output.get("status")
        supplier_first_seen_at = output.get("web_first_seen_at")
        source_url = output.get("source_url")
        provider = output.get("provider")
        match_kind = output.get("match_kind")
        confidence = output.get("confidence")
        checked_at = output.get("checked_at")
        if (
            status not in {"found", "not_found", "unavailable", "failed"}
            or (
                supplier_first_seen_at is not None
                and not isinstance(supplier_first_seen_at, str)
            )
            or (source_url is not None and not isinstance(source_url, str))
            or not isinstance(provider, str)
            or match_kind not in {"exact", "partial", "similar", "unknown"}
            or not isinstance(confidence, (int, float))
            or isinstance(confidence, bool)
            or not isinstance(checked_at, str)
        ):
            return None
        evidence["first_seen"] = {
            "status": status,
            "supplier_first_seen_at": supplier_first_seen_at,
            "source_url": _safe_public_source_url(source_url),
            "provider": provider,
            "match_kind": match_kind,
            "confidence": confidence,
            "checked_at": checked_at,
        }
    return evidence


def _safe_public_source_url(value: str | None) -> str | None:
    """Keep a public evidence locator without supplier credentials or signatures."""

    if value is None:
        return None
    try:
        parsed = urlsplit(value)
        if parsed.scheme.lower() not in {"http", "https"} or not parsed.hostname:
            return None
        host = parsed.hostname
        if ":" in host:
            host = f"[{host}]"
        port = parsed.port
    except ValueError:
        return None
    netloc = f"{host}:{port}" if port is not None else host
    return urlunsplit((parsed.scheme.lower(), netloc, parsed.path, "", ""))


def _append_runtime_audit(
    db: Session,
    row: ProductionRun,
    *,
    action: str,
    actor: str,
    details: Mapping[str, Any],
) -> RuntimeAuditEvent:
    existing_count = db.scalar(
        select(RuntimeAuditEvent.id)
        .where(
            RuntimeAuditEvent.entity_type == "production_run",
            RuntimeAuditEvent.entity_key == row.run_key,
        )
        .order_by(RuntimeAuditEvent.id.desc())
        .limit(1)
    )
    ordinal = int(existing_count or 0) + 1
    event_key = hashlib.sha256(
        f"{row.run_key}:{action}:{actor}:{ordinal}:{canonical_json(details)}".encode(
            "utf-8"
        )
    ).hexdigest()
    event = RuntimeAuditEvent(
        event_key=event_key,
        entity_type="production_run",
        entity_key=row.run_key,
        action=action,
        actor=actor,
        details_json=canonical_json(dict(details)),
    )
    db.add(event)
    db.flush()
    return event


def _pause_run(db: Session, row: ProductionRun) -> ProductionRun:
    locked = db.scalar(
        select(ProductionRun)
        .where(ProductionRun.id == row.id)
        .with_for_update()
        .execution_options(populate_existing=True)
    )
    if locked is None:
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
            "production_run_not_found",
            "生产运行不存在",
            status_code=404,
        )
    row = locked
    if row.status not in {"queued", "running", "retryable"}:
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
            "run_pause_state_invalid",
            "当前运行状态不能暂停",
