            status_code=409,
        )
    attempts = db.scalars(
        select(ProductionStepAttempt).where(
            ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == row.id,
            ProductionStepAttempt.status.in_({"leased", "running"}),
        )
    ).all()
    for attempt in attempts:
        attempt.status = "retryable"
        attempt.lease_owner = None
        attempt.lease_token = None
        attempt.lease_expires_at = None
        attempt.heartbeat_at = None
        dispatch = db.scalar(
            select(RuntimeDispatchItem).where(
                RuntimeDispatchItem.step_attempt_id == attempt.id
            )
        )
        if dispatch is not None:
            dispatch.status = "canceled"
            dispatch.lease_owner = None
            dispatch.lease_token = None
            dispatch.leased_at = None
            dispatch.heartbeat_at = None
            dispatch.lease_expires_at = None
    row.status = "paused"
    row.lease_owner = None
    row.lease_token = None
    row.lease_expires_at = None
    row.heartbeat_at = None
    db.flush()
    return row


def _cancel_run(db: Session, row: ProductionRun) -> ProductionRun:
    locked = db.scalar(
        select(ProductionRun)
        .where(ProductionRun.id == row.id)
        .with_for_update()
        .execution_options(populate_existing=True)
    )
    if locked is None:
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
            "production_run_not_found",
            "生产运行不存在",
            status_code=404,
        )
    row = locked
    if row.status in {"succeeded", "canceled"}:
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
            "run_cancel_state_invalid",
            "当前运行已经结束，不能取消",
            status_code=409,
        )
    return terminalize_runtime_run(
        db,
        row,
        status="canceled",
        error_code="RUN_CANCELED",
        error_message="运行已由用户取消",
        now=datetime.now(timezone.utc),
        persist_outputs=persist_baseline_workflow_outputs,
    )


def build_workflow_runtime_router(
    require_user: Callable[..., Any],
) -> APIRouter:
    router = APIRouter(prefix="/api/runtime", tags=["workflow-runtime"])

    @router.get("/capacity")
    def get_runtime_capacity(
        _user: User = Depends(require_user),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return runtime_capacity_snapshot(db)

    @router.get("/runs")
    def list_runs(
        status: str | None = None,
        queue_class: str | None = None,
        category_key: str | None = None,
        limit: int = Query(default=100, ge=1, le=500),
        offset: int = Query(default=0, ge=0),
        _user: User = Depends(require_user),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        statement = select(ProductionRun)
        if status:
            statement = statement.where(ProductionRun.status == status)
        if queue_class:
            statement = statement.where(ProductionRun.queue_class == queue_class)
        if category_key:
            statement = statement.where(ProductionRun.category_key == category_key)
        total = db.scalar(
            select(func.count()).select_from(statement.subquery())
        ) or 0
        rows = db.scalars(
            statement.order_by(
                ProductionRun.created_at.desc(),
                ProductionRun.id.desc(),
            ).offset(offset).limit(limit)
        ).all()
        prefetch_run_snapshot_blobs(db, rows)
        waits_by_run_id: dict[int, WorkflowExternalWait] = {}
        if rows:
            latest_wait_ids = (
                select(func.max(WorkflowExternalWait.id))
                .where(
                    WorkflowExternalWait.production_run_id.in_(
                        [row.id for row in rows]
                    )
                )
                .group_by(WorkflowExternalWait.production_run_id)
            )
            waits = db.scalars(
                select(WorkflowExternalWait).where(
                    WorkflowExternalWait.id.in_(latest_wait_ids)
                )
            ).all()
            waits_by_run_id = {
                wait.production_run_id: wait for wait in waits
            }
        return {
            "items": [
                _run_payload(
                    db,
                    row,
                    prefetched_external_wait=waits_by_run_id.get(row.id),
                    external_wait_prefetched=True,
                )
                for row in rows
            ],
            "total": total,
            "offset": offset,
            "limit": limit,
            "has_more": offset + len(rows) < total,
        }

    @router.post("/runs")
    def create_run(
        payload: RuntimeRunCreate,
        response: Response,
        user: User = Depends(require_user),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        _require_write(user)
        if payload.source_type == "baseline_regression_item":
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=400,
                detail={
                    "code": "runtime_source_type_reserved",
                    "message": "该来源类型只能由存量回归启动器内部创建",
                },
            )
        _scan_executable_fields(payload.runtime_context)
        _scan_reserved_model_execution_fields(payload.runtime_context)
        _scan_executable_fields(payload.input_manifest)
        existing = db.scalar(
            select(ProductionRun).where(
                ProductionRun.idempotency_key == payload.idempotency_key
            )
        )
        try:
            row = create_production_run(
                db,
                ProductionRunRequest(
                    workflow_version_id=payload.workflow_version_id,
                    idempotency_key=payload.idempotency_key,
                    category_key=payload.category_key,
                    queue_class=payload.queue_class,
                    runtime_context=payload.runtime_context,
                    input_manifest=payload.input_manifest,
                    owner=payload.owner,
                    reason=payload.reason,
                    source_type=payload.source_type,
                    source_id=payload.source_id,
                    source_run_id=payload.source_run_id,
                    environment=payload.environment,
                ),
                actor=user.username,
            )
        except WorkflowRuntimeError as exc:
            db.rollback()
            raise _runtime_error(exc) from exc
        duplicate = existing is not None
        if not duplicate:
            _append_runtime_audit(
                db,
                row,
                action="create",
                actor=user.username,
                details={
                    "status": row.status,
                    "workflow_version_id": row.workflow_version_id,
                    "snapshot_hash": row.snapshot_hash,
                    "environment": row.environment,
                },
            )
        db.commit()
        db.refresh(row)
        response.status_code = 200 if duplicate else 201
        return {**_run_payload(db, row), "duplicate": duplicate}

    @router.get("/runs/{run_key}")
    def get_run(
        run_key: str,
        _user: User = Depends(require_user),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return _run_payload(db, _run(db, run_key))

    @router.get("/runs/{run_key}/timeline")
    def get_timeline(
        run_key: str,
        _user: User = Depends(require_user),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        row = _run(db, run_key)
        attempts = db.scalars(
            select(ProductionStepAttempt)
            .where(ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == row.id)
            .order_by(
                ProductionStepAttempt.sequence,
                ProductionStepAttempt.attempt_no,
                ProductionStepAttempt.id,
            )
        ).all()
        items: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        for attempt in attempts:
            script = db.get(ScriptVersion, attempt.script_version_id)
            dispatch = db.scalar(
                select(RuntimeDispatchItem).where(
                    RuntimeDispatchItem.step_attempt_id == attempt.id
                )
            )
            items.append(
                {
                    "id": attempt.id,
                    "step_key": attempt.step_key,
                    "step_type": attempt.step_type,
                    "sequence": attempt.sequence,
                    "attempt_no": attempt.attempt_no,
                    "status": attempt.status,
                    "script_version_id": attempt.script_version_id,
                    "script_version": script.version if script is not None else "",
                    "queue_class": dispatch.queue_class if dispatch is not None else None,
                    "input_hash": attempt.input_hash,
                    "output_hash": attempt.output_hash,
                    "checkpoint_hash": attempt.checkpoint_hash,
                    "lease_owner": attempt.lease_owner,
                    "lease_expires_at": attempt.lease_expires_at,
                    "last_error_code": attempt.last_error_code,
                    "last_error_message": attempt.last_error_message,
                    "started_at": attempt.started_at,
                    "finished_at": attempt.finished_at,
                    "connector_evidence": _safe_connector_evidence(
                        attempt.output_manifest_json
                    ),
                }
            )
        return {"run_key": run_key, "items": items}

    @router.get("/runs/{run_key}/snapshot")
    def get_snapshot(
        run_key: str,
        _user: User = Depends(require_user),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        row = _run(db, run_key)
        return {
            "run_key": run_key,
            "snapshot_hash": row.snapshot_hash,
            "snapshot": read_run_snapshot_document(row),
        }

    @router.post("/runs/{run_key}/pause")
    def pause_run(
        run_key: str,
        user: User = Depends(require_user),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        _require_write(user)
        row = _run(db, run_key)
        try:
            _pause_run(db, row)
        except WorkflowRuntimeError as exc:
            db.rollback()
            raise _runtime_error(exc) from exc
        _append_runtime_audit(
            db,
            row,
            action="pause",
            actor=user.username,
            details={"status": row.status},
        )
        db.commit()
        db.refresh(row)
        return _run_payload(db, row)

    @router.post("/runs/{run_key}/resume")
    def resume_run(
        run_key: str,
        user: User = Depends(require_user),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        _require_write(user)
        row = _run(db, run_key)
        try:
            resume_from_checkpoint(db, row.id)
        except WorkflowRuntimeError as exc:
            db.rollback()
            raise _runtime_error(exc) from exc
        _append_runtime_audit(
            db,
            row,
            action="resume",
            actor=user.username,
            details={"status": row.status},
        )
        db.commit()
        db.refresh(row)
        return _run_payload(db, row)

    @router.post("/runs/{run_key}/retry")
    def retry_run(
        run_key: str,
        user: User = Depends(require_user),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        _require_write(user)
        row = _run(db, run_key)
        if row.status != "failed":
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=409,
                detail={
                    "code": "run_retry_state_invalid",
                    "message": "只有 failed 运行可以人工重试",
                },
            )
        try:
            retry_failed_run_from_checkpoint(db, row.id, actor=user.username)
        except WorkflowRuntimeError as exc:
            db.rollback()
            raise _runtime_error(exc) from exc
        _append_runtime_audit(
            db,
            row,
            action="retry",
            actor=user.username,
            details={"status": row.status},
        )
        db.commit()
        db.refresh(row)
        return _run_payload(db, row)

    @router.post("/runs/{run_key}/cancel")
    def cancel_run(
        run_key: str,
        user: User = Depends(require_user),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        _require_write(user)
        row = _run(db, run_key)
        try:
            _cancel_run(db, row)
        except WorkflowRuntimeError as exc:
            db.rollback()
            raise _runtime_error(exc) from exc
        _append_runtime_audit(
            db,
            row,
            action="cancel",
            actor=user.username,
            details={"status": row.status},
        )
        db.commit()
        db.refresh(row)
        return _run_payload(db, row)

    return router

===== FILE backend/app/workflow_short_circuit.py =====
"""Frozen, deterministic workflow level short-circuit decisions.

The module deliberately owns no database state.  A workflow freezes rules and
its category level scale; runtime evaluates those two immutable facts against a
completed node result and persists the returned decision with the run evidence.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import math
from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass, replace
from typing import Any, Mapping, Sequence

from .prompt_output_contract import json_values_equal


RULE_VERSION = "short-circuit-rule-v1"
DECISION_VERSION = "short-circuit-decision-v1"
SUPPORTED_OPERATORS = frozenset({"eq", "in", "exists", "gte", "lte"})
PROTECTED_EXECUTION_ROLES = frozenset({"audit", "outbox", "projection"})
PROTECTED_NODE_KINDS = frozenset({"downstream_projection"})
_MISSING = object()


def _is_finite_number(value: Any) -> bool:
    if isinstance(value, bool) or not isinstance(value, (int, float)):
        return False
    return isinstance(value, int) or math.isfinite(value)


class ShortCircuitError(ValueError):
    def __init__(self, code: str, message: str) -> None:
        super().__init__(message)
        self.code = code


class ShortCircuitConflict(ShortCircuitError):
    pass


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ShortCircuitRule:
    rule_key: str
    when: Mapping[str, Any]
    target_level: str
    priority: int = 0
    skip_roles: tuple[str, ...] = ()
    skip_nodes: tuple[str, ...] = ()
    rule_version: str = RULE_VERSION


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ShortCircuitDecision:
    rule_key: str
    target_level: str
    evidence: Mapping[str, Any]
    skipped_nodes: tuple[str, ...] = ()
    rule_version: str = RULE_VERSION
    schema_version: str = DECISION_VERSION


def _rule_from_value(value: ShortCircuitRule | Mapping[str, Any]) -> ShortCircuitRule:
    if isinstance(value, ShortCircuitRule):
        rule = value
    elif isinstance(value, Mapping):
        skip_roles = value.get("skip_roles", ())
        if not isinstance(skip_roles, (list, tuple)) or not all(
            isinstance(item, str) and item.strip() for item in skip_roles
        ):
            raise ShortCircuitError(
                "short_circuit_skip_roles_invalid",
                "短路规则的 skip_roles 必须是非空字符串数组",
            )
        skip_nodes = value.get("skip_nodes", ())
        if not isinstance(skip_nodes, (list, tuple)) or not all(
            isinstance(item, str) and item.strip() for item in skip_nodes
        ):
            raise ShortCircuitError(
                "short_circuit_skip_nodes_invalid",
                "短路规则的 skip_nodes 必须是非空字符串数组",
            )
        rule = ShortCircuitRule(
            rule_key=value.get("rule_key"),
            when=value.get("when"),
            target_level=value.get("target_level"),
            priority=value.get("priority", 0),
            skip_roles=tuple(skip_roles),
            skip_nodes=tuple(skip_nodes),
            rule_version=value.get("rule_version", RULE_VERSION),
        )
    else:
        raise ShortCircuitError(
            "short_circuit_rule_invalid", "短路规则必须是对象"
        )
    if not isinstance(rule.rule_key, str) or not rule.rule_key.strip():
        raise ShortCircuitError(
            "short_circuit_rule_key_invalid", "短路规则必须有稳定 rule_key"
        )
    if not isinstance(rule.when, Mapping):
        raise ShortCircuitError(
            "short_circuit_condition_invalid", "短路规则 when 必须是对象"
        )
    if not isinstance(rule.target_level, str) or not rule.target_level:
        raise ShortCircuitError(
            "short_circuit_target_level_invalid", "短路规则必须指定目标等级"
        )
    if isinstance(rule.priority, bool) or not isinstance(rule.priority, int):
        raise ShortCircuitError(
            "short_circuit_priority_invalid", "短路规则 priority 必须是整数"
        )
    if rule.rule_version != RULE_VERSION:
        raise ShortCircuitError(
            "short_circuit_rule_version_unsupported",
            f"短路规则版本必须是 {RULE_VERSION}",
        )
    return rule


def _lookup_path(facts: Mapping[str, Any], raw_path: str) -> tuple[bool, Any]:
    path = raw_path[2:] if raw_path.startswith("$.") else raw_path
    if not path:
        return True, facts
    current: Any = facts
    for part in path.split("."):
        if isinstance(current, Mapping) and part in current:
            current = current[part]
            continue
        if isinstance(current, (list, tuple)) and part.isdigit():
            index = int(part)
            if 0 <= index < len(current):
                current = current[index]
                continue
        return False, _MISSING
    return True, current


def _validate_condition_definition(condition: Mapping[str, Any]) -> None:
    """Validate a frozen rule without depending on whether a fact path exists."""

    composite_keys = [key for key in ("all", "any") if key in condition]
    if composite_keys:
        if len(composite_keys) != 1 or len(condition) != 1:
            raise ShortCircuitError(
                "short_circuit_condition_invalid",
                "组合短路条件只能声明 all 或 any 之一",
            )
        mode = composite_keys[0]
        children = condition[mode]
        if not isinstance(children, list) or not children:
            raise ShortCircuitError(
                "short_circuit_condition_invalid", f"{mode} 必须是非空条件数组"
            )
        for child in children:
            if not isinstance(child, Mapping):
                raise ShortCircuitError(
                    "short_circuit_condition_invalid", "组合条件的子项必须是对象"
                )
            _validate_condition_definition(child)
        return
    if set(condition) != {"path", "op", "value"}:
        raise ShortCircuitError(
            "short_circuit_condition_invalid",
            "叶子短路条件只能声明 path、op 和 value",
        )
    path = condition.get("path")
    op = condition.get("op")
    value = condition.get("value")
    if not isinstance(path, str) or not path.strip() or op not in SUPPORTED_OPERATORS:
        raise ShortCircuitError(
            "short_circuit_condition_invalid",
            "短路条件必须包含 path 及 eq/in/exists/gte/lte 运算符",
        )
    if op == "exists" and not isinstance(value, bool):
        raise ShortCircuitError(
            "short_circuit_condition_invalid",
            "exists 条件的 value 必须是布尔值",
        )
    if op == "in" and not isinstance(value, (list, tuple)):
        raise ShortCircuitError(
            "short_circuit_condition_invalid", "in 条件的 value 必须是数组"
        )
    if op in {"gte", "lte"} and (
        not _is_finite_number(value)
    ):
        raise ShortCircuitError(
            "short_circuit_condition_invalid",
            "gte/lte 条件的 value 必须是有限数值",
        )


def _leaf_condition(
    condition: Mapping[str, Any], facts: Mapping[str, Any]
) -> tuple[bool, list[dict[str, Any]]]:
    path = condition.get("path")
    op = condition.get("op")
    if not isinstance(path, str) or not path.strip() or op not in SUPPORTED_OPERATORS:
        raise ShortCircuitError(
            "short_circuit_condition_invalid",
            "短路条件必须包含 path 及 eq/in/exists/gte/lte 运算符",
        )
    exists, actual = _lookup_path(facts, path)
    expected = condition.get("value")
    if op == "exists":
        if not isinstance(expected, bool):
            raise ShortCircuitError(
                "short_circuit_condition_invalid",
                "exists 条件的 value 必须是布尔值",
            )
        matched = exists is expected
    elif not exists:
        matched = False
    elif op == "eq":
        matched = json_values_equal(actual, expected)
    elif op == "in":
        if not isinstance(expected, (list, tuple)):
            raise ShortCircuitError(
                "short_circuit_condition_invalid", "in 条件的 value 必须是数组"
            )
        matched = any(json_values_equal(actual, item) for item in expected)
    else:
        if (
            isinstance(actual, bool)
            or isinstance(expected, bool)
            or not isinstance(actual, (int, float))
            or not isinstance(expected, (int, float))
        ):
            matched = False
        else:
            matched = actual >= expected if op == "gte" else actual <= expected
    if not matched:
        return False, []
    evidence = {
        "path": path,
        "op": op,
        "expected": expected,
        "actual": actual if exists else None,
    }
    return True, [evidence]


def _evaluate_condition(
    condition: Mapping[str, Any], facts: Mapping[str, Any]
) -> tuple[bool, list[dict[str, Any]]]:
    composite_keys = [key for key in ("all", "any") if key in condition]
    if composite_keys:
        if len(composite_keys) != 1 or len(condition) != 1:
            raise ShortCircuitError(
                "short_circuit_condition_invalid",
                "组合短路条件只能声明 all 或 any 之一",
            )
        mode = composite_keys[0]
        children = condition[mode]
        if not isinstance(children, list) or not children:
            raise ShortCircuitError(
                "short_circuit_condition_invalid", f"{mode} 必须是非空条件数组"
            )
        evaluations: list[tuple[bool, list[dict[str, Any]]]] = []
        for child in children:
            if not isinstance(child, Mapping):
                raise ShortCircuitError(
                    "short_circuit_condition_invalid", "组合条件的子项必须是对象"
                )
            evaluations.append(_evaluate_condition(child, facts))
        if mode == "all":
            if not all(item[0] for item in evaluations):
                return False, []
            return True, [fact for _, rows in evaluations for fact in rows]
        for matched, rows in evaluations:
            if matched:
                return True, rows
        return False, []
    return _leaf_condition(condition, facts)


def resolve_short_circuit(
    rules: Sequence[ShortCircuitRule | Mapping[str, Any]],
    facts: Mapping[str, Any],
    valid_levels: Sequence[str],
) -> ShortCircuitDecision | None:
    """Resolve the highest-priority matching frozen rule, fail-closed on ties."""

    if not isinstance(facts, Mapping):
        raise ShortCircuitError(
            "short_circuit_facts_invalid", "短路判定事实必须是对象"
        )
    levels = tuple(valid_levels)
    if not levels or not all(isinstance(level, str) and level for level in levels):
        raise ShortCircuitError(
            "short_circuit_level_scale_invalid", "工作流缺少冻结等级列表"
        )
    normalized = sorted(
        (_rule_from_value(value) for value in rules),
        key=lambda item: (-item.priority, item.rule_key),
    )
    for rule in normalized:
        _validate_condition_definition(rule.when)
        if rule.target_level not in levels:
            raise ShortCircuitError(
                "short_circuit_target_level_invalid",
                f"短路目标 {rule.target_level} 不在品类冻结等级中",
            )
    matched: list[tuple[ShortCircuitRule, list[dict[str, Any]]]] = []
    for rule in normalized:
        is_match, evidence = _evaluate_condition(rule.when, facts)
        if is_match:
            matched.append((rule, evidence))
    if not matched:
        return None
    highest_priority = matched[0][0].priority
    winners = [item for item in matched if item[0].priority == highest_priority]
    target_levels = {item[0].target_level for item in winners}
    if len(target_levels) > 1:
        keys = "、".join(item[0].rule_key for item in winners)
        raise ShortCircuitConflict(
            "short_circuit_conflict",
            f"同优先级短路规则得出不同等级（{keys}），必须转人工复核",
        )
    winner, winner_evidence = winners[0]
    return ShortCircuitDecision(
        rule_key=winner.rule_key,
        target_level=winner.target_level,
        evidence={
            "matched_rule_keys": [item[0].rule_key for item in winners],
            "matched_conditions": winner_evidence,
            "priority": winner.priority,
            "skip_roles": list(winner.skip_roles),
            "skip_nodes": list(winner.skip_nodes),
        },
        rule_version=winner.rule_version,
    )


def validate_short_circuit_rules(
    rules: Any, valid_levels: Sequence[str]
) -> tuple[ShortCircuitRule, ...]:
    if not isinstance(rules, list) or not rules:
        raise ShortCircuitError(
            "short_circuit_rules_invalid", "short_circuit_rules 必须是非空数组"
        )
    levels = tuple(valid_levels)
    normalized: list[ShortCircuitRule] = []
    seen: set[str] = set()
    for value in rules:
        rule = _rule_from_value(value)
        if rule.rule_key in seen:
            raise ShortCircuitError(
                "short_circuit_rule_key_duplicate",
                f"短路规则 rule_key 重复：{rule.rule_key}",
            )
        seen.add(rule.rule_key)
        if rule.target_level not in levels:
            raise ShortCircuitError(
                "short_circuit_target_level_invalid",
                f"短路目标 {rule.target_level} 不在品类冻结等级中",
            )
        _validate_condition_definition(rule.when)
        normalized.append(rule)
    return tuple(sorted(normalized, key=lambda item: (-item.priority, item.rule_key)))


def decision_with_skipped_nodes(
    decision: ShortCircuitDecision, skipped_nodes: Sequence[str]
) -> ShortCircuitDecision:
    return replace(decision, skipped_nodes=tuple(sorted(set(skipped_nodes))))


def short_circuit_decision_payload(decision: ShortCircuitDecision) -> dict[str, Any]:
    payload = asdict(decision)
    payload["skipped_nodes"] = list(decision.skipped_nodes)
    return payload


def short_circuit_decision_from_payload(
    payload: Mapping[str, Any],
) -> ShortCircuitDecision:
    if payload.get("schema_version") != DECISION_VERSION:
        raise ShortCircuitError(
            "short_circuit_decision_invalid", "短路决定版本无效"
        )
    skipped = payload.get("skipped_nodes", [])
    evidence = payload.get("evidence")
    if not isinstance(skipped, (list, tuple)) or not all(
        isinstance(item, str) for item in skipped
    ) or not isinstance(evidence, Mapping):
        raise ShortCircuitError(
            "short_circuit_decision_invalid", "短路决定证据无效"
        )
    rule = _rule_from_value(
        {
            "rule_key": payload.get("rule_key"),
            "when": {"path": "decision", "op": "exists", "value": True},
            "target_level": payload.get("target_level"),
            "priority": 0,
            "skip_roles": evidence.get("skip_roles", []),
            "skip_nodes": evidence.get("skip_nodes", []),
            "rule_version": payload.get("rule_version"),
        }
    )
    return ShortCircuitDecision(
        rule_key=rule.rule_key,
        target_level=rule.target_level,
        evidence=dict(evidence),
        skipped_nodes=tuple(skipped),
        rule_version=rule.rule_version,
    )


def enabled_levels_from_manifest(manifest: Mapping[str, Any]) -> tuple[str, ...]:
    """Read enabled levels from the frozen workflow, with legacy L1-L5 fallback."""

    def levels_from(value: Any) -> tuple[str, ...]:
        if not isinstance(value, Mapping):
            return ()
        enabled = value.get("enabled_levels")
        if isinstance(enabled, list) and all(isinstance(item, str) for item in enabled):
            return tuple(enabled)
        rows = value.get("levels")
        if isinstance(rows, list):
            result = tuple(
                str(row["level"])
                for row in rows
                if isinstance(row, Mapping)
                and isinstance(row.get("level"), str)
                and row.get("enabled", True) is True
            )
            if result:
                return result
        thresholds = value.get("thresholds")
        if isinstance(thresholds, list):
            result = tuple(
                str(row["level"])
                for row in thresholds
                if isinstance(row, Mapping) and isinstance(row.get("level"), str)
            )
            if result:
                return result
        return ()

    resource = manifest.get("resource_policy")
    resource = resource if isinstance(resource, Mapping) else {}
    for candidate in (resource, resource.get("level_scale")):
        if found := levels_from(candidate):
            return found
    projection = resource.get("legacy_projection_snapshot")
    if isinstance(projection, Mapping):
        fields = projection.get("workflow_fields")
        contract = fields.get("contract") if isinstance(fields, Mapping) else None
        if isinstance(contract, Mapping):
            if found := levels_from(contract.get("level_scale")):
                return found
    nodes = manifest.get("nodes")
    if isinstance(nodes, list):
        for node in nodes:
            if not isinstance(node, Mapping):
                continue
            config = node.get("config")
            if (
                not isinstance(config, Mapping)
                or config.get("module_type_key") != "score_level_matcher"
            ):
                continue
            snapshot = config.get("component_snapshot")
            raw = snapshot.get("content_json") if isinstance(snapshot, Mapping) else None
            if isinstance(raw, str):
                try:
                    content = json.loads(raw)
                except json.JSONDecodeError:
                    continue
                if found := levels_from(content):
                    return found
    # Old frozen mechanisms predate explicit level_scale snapshots and are
    # contractually L1-L5.  New editor-created rules always freeze a scale.
    return ("L1", "L2", "L3", "L4", "L5")


def downstream_skipped_nodes(
    manifest: Mapping[str, Any],
    *,
    source_node_id: str,
    skip_roles: Sequence[str],
    skip_nodes: Sequence[str] = (),
) -> tuple[str, ...]:
    nodes = manifest.get("nodes")
    edges = manifest.get("edges")
    if not isinstance(nodes, list) or not isinstance(edges, list):
        return ()
    adjacency: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    for edge in edges:
        if (
            isinstance(edge, Mapping)
            and isinstance(edge.get("from"), str)
            and isinstance(edge.get("to"), str)
        ):
            adjacency.setdefault(edge["from"], []).append(edge["to"])
    descendants: set[str] = set()
    pending = list(adjacency.get(source_node_id, ()))
    while pending:
        node_id = pending.pop()
        if node_id in descendants:
            continue
        descendants.add(node_id)
        pending.extend(adjacency.get(node_id, ()))
    wanted_roles = set(skip_roles)
    wanted_nodes = set(skip_nodes)
    skipped: list[str] = []
    for node in nodes:
        if not isinstance(node, Mapping):
            continue
        node_id = node.get("node_instance_id")
        config = node.get("config")
        role = config.get("execution_role") if isinstance(config, Mapping) else None
        if (
            manifest.get("category_key") == "model_3d_su_joint"
            and isinstance(config, Mapping)
            and isinstance(config.get("output_contract"), Mapping)
            and config["output_contract"].get("name") == "model_3d_su_joint_call_a"
        ):
            # A also owns the independent KG result, even when grading short-circuits.
            continue
        if (
            isinstance(node_id, str)
            and node_id in descendants
            and (role in wanted_roles or node_id in wanted_nodes)
            and role not in PROTECTED_EXECUTION_ROLES
            and node.get("kind") not in PROTECTED_NODE_KINDS
        ):
            skipped.append(node_id)
    return tuple(sorted(skipped))

===== FILE backend/app/workflow_snapshot_backfill.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
from dataclasses import dataclass

from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .database_capabilities import DatabaseCapabilities
from .models import ProductionRun, WorkflowSnapshotBlob
from .workflow_snapshot_store import (
    canonical_snapshot_bytes,
    get_or_create_snapshot_blob,
)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SnapshotBackfillReport:
    scanned: int
    bound: int
    reused: int
    logical_bytes: int
    physical_bytes: int
    avoided_bytes: int
    mismatches: tuple[int, ...]
    last_id: int
    complete: bool


class SnapshotBackfillError(RuntimeError):
    def __init__(self, message: str, *, report: SnapshotBackfillReport):
        super().__init__(message)
        self.report = report


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class _PreparedSnapshot:
    run: ProductionRun
    document: dict[str, object]
    canonical_size_bytes: int


def _remaining_unbound_run_exists(db: Session, *, after_id: int) -> bool:
    return (
        db.scalar(
            select(ProductionRun.id)
            .where(
                ProductionRun.id > after_id,
                ProductionRun.snapshot_blob_id.is_(None),
            )
            .order_by(ProductionRun.id)
            .limit(1)
        )
        is not None
    )


def backfill_workflow_snapshots(
    db: Session,
    *,
    batch_size: int,
    after_id: int = 0,
) -> SnapshotBackfillReport:
    if batch_size <= 0:
        raise ValueError("batch_size 必须大于 0")
    if after_id < 0:
        raise ValueError("after_id 不能小于 0")

    statement = (
        select(ProductionRun)
        .where(
            ProductionRun.id > after_id,
            ProductionRun.snapshot_blob_id.is_(None),
        )
        .order_by(ProductionRun.id)
        .limit(batch_size)
    )
    capabilities = DatabaseCapabilities.for_dialect(
        db.get_bind().dialect.name
    )
    statement = capabilities.claim_lock_statement(statement)
    runs = list(db.scalars(statement).all())
    last_id = runs[-1].id if runs else after_id
    if not runs:
        return SnapshotBackfillReport(
            scanned=0,
            bound=0,
            reused=0,
            logical_bytes=0,
            physical_bytes=0,
            avoided_bytes=0,
            mismatches=(),
            last_id=last_id,
            complete=not _remaining_unbound_run_exists(
                db,
                after_id=after_id,
            ),
        )

    prepared: list[_PreparedSnapshot] = []
    mismatches: list[int] = []
    for run in runs:
        try:
            document = json.loads(run.snapshot_json)
        except json.JSONDecodeError:
            mismatches.append(run.id)
            continue
        if not isinstance(document, dict):
            mismatches.append(run.id)
            continue
        canonical = canonical_snapshot_bytes(document)
        if hashlib.sha256(canonical).hexdigest() != run.snapshot_hash:
            mismatches.append(run.id)
            continue
        prepared.append(
            _PreparedSnapshot(
                run=run,
                document=document,
                canonical_size_bytes=len(canonical),
            )
        )

    if mismatches:
        report = SnapshotBackfillReport(
            scanned=len(runs),
            bound=0,
            reused=0,
            logical_bytes=0,
            physical_bytes=0,
            avoided_bytes=0,
            mismatches=tuple(mismatches),
            last_id=last_id,
            complete=False,
        )
        raise SnapshotBackfillError(
            "历史运行快照与已记录哈希不一致，整批已停止",
            report=report,
        )

    logical_bytes = 0
    physical_bytes = 0
    reused = 0
    savepoint = db.begin_nested()
    try:
        for item in prepared:
            canonical = canonical_snapshot_bytes(item.document)
            snapshot_hash = hashlib.sha256(canonical).hexdigest()
            existing = db.scalar(
                select(WorkflowSnapshotBlob).where(
                    WorkflowSnapshotBlob.snapshot_hash == snapshot_hash
                )
            )
            blob = get_or_create_snapshot_blob(db, item.document)
            item.run.snapshot_blob_id = blob.id
            item.run.snapshot_blob = blob
            logical_bytes += item.canonical_size_bytes
            if existing is None:
                physical_bytes += blob.compressed_size_bytes
            else:
                reused += 1
        db.flush()
        savepoint.commit()
    except Exception:
        savepoint.rollback()
        raise

    complete = not _remaining_unbound_run_exists(db, after_id=last_id)
    return SnapshotBackfillReport(
        scanned=len(runs),
        bound=len(prepared),
        reused=reused,
        logical_bytes=logical_bytes,
        physical_bytes=physical_bytes,
        avoided_bytes=max(logical_bytes - physical_bytes, 0),
        mismatches=(),
        last_id=last_id,
        complete=complete,
    )

===== FILE backend/app/workflow_snapshot_compaction.py =====
"""Bounded, reversible removal of duplicate stock snapshot text.

The caller owns the outer transaction and must durably save the plan before
committing apply. No blob, business result, truth, or timestamp is rewritten.
Only byte-identical canonical text can be reconstructed from the existing blob.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import re
from collections.abc import Mapping
from typing import Any

from sqlalchemy import select, text
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .models import ProductionRun, WorkflowSnapshotBlob
from .workflow_snapshot_store import canonical_snapshot_bytes, load_snapshot_blob


class SnapshotCompactionError(RuntimeError):
    pass


TERMINAL = frozenset({"succeeded", "failed", "canceled"})
SOURCES = frozenset({"baseline_regression_item", "baseline_regression"})
MAX_BATCH = 200
FORMAT = "stock-snapshot-compaction-v1"


def _hash(value: bytes) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(value).hexdigest()


def _json(value: Any) -> bytes:
    return json.dumps(value, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True,
                      separators=(",", ":"), default=str).encode("utf-8")


def plan_digest(plan: Mapping[str, Any]) -> str:
    return _hash(_json(dict(plan)))


def _clean_session(db: Session) -> None:
    if db.new or db.dirty or db.deleted:
        raise SnapshotCompactionError("compaction requires a clean dedicated session")


def _eligible(row: Mapping[str, Any]) -> bool:
    return (row["source_type"] in SOURCES and row["status"] in TERMINAL
            and row["run_mode"] == "stock_regression"
            and row["execution_pool"] == "stock"
            and row["snapshot_blob_id"] is not None
            and row["lease_owner"] is None and row["lease_token"] is None
            and row["held_by_release"] is None)


def _state_hash(row: Mapping[str, Any]) -> str:
    return _hash(_json({k: v for k, v in row.items() if k != "snapshot_json"}))


def _original(db: Session, row: Mapping[str, Any], *, locked: bool = False) -> bytes:
    statement = select(WorkflowSnapshotBlob).where(
        WorkflowSnapshotBlob.id == row["snapshot_blob_id"]
    ).execution_options(populate_existing=True)
    if locked:
        statement = statement.with_for_update()
    blob = db.scalar(statement)
    if blob is None or blob.snapshot_hash != row["snapshot_hash"]:
        raise SnapshotCompactionError("missing or mismatched frozen snapshot blob")
    return canonical_snapshot_bytes(load_snapshot_blob(blob))


def plan_batch(db: Session, *, after_id: int, through_id: int,
               batch_size: int = 100) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Read one keyset page; noncanonical source bytes remain inline."""
    _clean_session(db)
    if (type(after_id) is not int or type(through_id) is not int
            or not 0 <= after_id <= through_id or type(batch_size) is not int
            or not 1 <= batch_size <= MAX_BATCH):
        raise SnapshotCompactionError("invalid bounded compaction scope")
    table = ProductionRun.__table__
    c = table.c
    rows = db.execute(select(table).where(
        c.id > after_id, c.id <= through_id, c.source_type.in_(SOURCES),
        c.run_mode == "stock_regression", c.execution_pool == "stock",
        c.status.in_(TERMINAL), c.snapshot_blob_id.is_not(None),
        c.snapshot_json.is_not(None), c.lease_owner.is_(None),
        c.lease_token.is_(None), c.held_by_release.is_(None),
    ).order_by(c.id).limit(batch_size)).mappings().all()
    planned, skipped = [], []
    for row in rows:
        original = _original(db, row)
        inline = row["snapshot_json"].encode("utf-8")
        if canonical_snapshot_bytes(json.loads(inline)) != original:
            raise SnapshotCompactionError("inline and blob snapshot documents differ")
        if inline != original:
            skipped.append({"id": row["id"], "reason": "source_not_canonical"})
            continue
        planned.append({"id": row["id"], "state_sha256": _state_hash(row),
                        "source_sha256": _hash(inline), "utf8_bytes": len(inline)})
    return {"format": FORMAT, "after_id": after_id, "through_id": through_id,
            "last_scanned_id": rows[-1]["id"] if rows else through_id,
            "scanned": len(rows), "skipped": skipped, "rows": planned}


def _validate(plan: dict[str, Any], expected_sha256: str) -> None:
    if (set(plan) != {"format", "after_id", "through_id", "last_scanned_id",
                      "scanned", "skipped", "rows"}
            or plan.get("format") != FORMAT
            or not isinstance(expected_sha256, str)
            or not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{64}", expected_sha256)
            or plan_digest(plan) != expected_sha256):
        raise SnapshotCompactionError("compaction plan digest or format mismatch")
    if (any(type(plan[k]) is not int for k in
            ("after_id", "through_id", "last_scanned_id", "scanned"))
            or not 0 <= plan["after_id"] <= plan["last_scanned_id"] <= plan["through_id"]
            or not 0 <= plan["scanned"] <= MAX_BATCH
            or not isinstance(plan["rows"], list)
            or len(plan["rows"]) > plan["scanned"]):
        raise SnapshotCompactionError("invalid compaction plan bounds")
    previous = plan["after_id"]
    for row in plan["rows"]:
        if (not isinstance(row, dict)
                or set(row) != {"id", "state_sha256", "source_sha256", "utf8_bytes"}
                or type(row["id"]) is not int
                or not previous < row["id"] <= plan["last_scanned_id"]
                or type(row["utf8_bytes"]) is not int or row["utf8_bytes"] < 2
                or any(not isinstance(row[k], str)
                       or not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{64}", row[k])
                       for k in ("state_sha256", "source_sha256"))):
            raise SnapshotCompactionError("invalid compaction plan row")
        previous = row["id"]


def execute_batch(db: Session, plan: dict[str, Any], *, expected_sha256: str,
                  restore: bool = False) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Revalidate a pinned batch under row locks; caller must commit explicitly.

    A replay recognizes only exact already-applied bytes/state. Restore uses
    the same pre-clearing plan, preventing a reconstructed but different source.
    """
    _clean_session(db)
    _validate(plan, expected_sha256)
    changed, already, changed_bytes = 0, 0, 0
    table = ProductionRun.__table__
    if db.get_bind().dialect.name == "sqlite":
        connection = db.connection()
        if not connection.connection.driver_connection.in_transaction:
            connection.exec_driver_sql("BEGIN")
    with db.begin_nested():
        pending = []
        for item in plan["rows"]:
            row = db.execute(select(table).where(table.c.id == item["id"])
                             .with_for_update()).mappings().one_or_none()
            if row is None or not _eligible(row) or _state_hash(row) != item["state_sha256"]:
                raise SnapshotCompactionError("run changed or left the terminal stock scope")
            original = _original(db, row, locked=True)
            if len(original) != item["utf8_bytes"] or _hash(original) != item["source_sha256"]:
                raise SnapshotCompactionError("original snapshot bytes cannot be restored")
            inline = row["snapshot_json"]
            if inline is not None and inline.encode("utf-8") != original:
                raise SnapshotCompactionError("inline snapshot bytes changed")
            if (restore and inline is not None) or (not restore and inline is None):
                already += 1
            else:
                pending.append((item, original))
        for item, original in pending:
            # Native SQL deliberately avoids SQLAlchemy's updated_at onupdate.
            result = db.execute(text(
                "UPDATE production_runs SET snapshot_json=:value WHERE id=:id"
            ), {"id": item["id"], "value": original.decode("utf-8") if restore else None})
            if result.rowcount != 1:
                raise SnapshotCompactionError("snapshot update did not affect exactly one row")
            after = db.execute(select(table).where(table.c.id == item["id"])).mappings().one()
            if (_state_hash(after) != item["state_sha256"]
                    or after["snapshot_json"] != (original.decode("utf-8") if restore else None)):
                raise SnapshotCompactionError("snapshot update changed other run data")
            changed += 1
            changed_bytes += item["utf8_bytes"]
    return {"format": FORMAT, "plan_sha256": expected_sha256,
            "action": "restore" if restore else "compact", "changed": changed,
            "already_applied": already, "inline_bytes_changed": changed_bytes,
            "physical_bytes_reclaimed": None, "requires_outer_commit": True}

===== FILE backend/app/workflow_snapshot_store.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import gzip
import hashlib
import json
from collections.abc import Mapping
from typing import Any

from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.exc import IntegrityError
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
from sqlalchemy.orm.attributes import set_committed_value

from .audit import canonical_json
from .models import ProductionRun, WorkflowSnapshotBlob


class SnapshotStoreError(RuntimeError):
    def __init__(self, code: str, message: str):
        super().__init__(f"{code}: {message}")
        self.code = code


def canonical_snapshot_bytes(snapshot: Mapping[str, Any]) -> bytes:
    return canonical_json(dict(snapshot)).encode("utf-8")


def get_or_create_snapshot_blob(
    db: Session,
    snapshot: Mapping[str, Any],
) -> WorkflowSnapshotBlob:
    canonical = canonical_snapshot_bytes(snapshot)
    snapshot_hash = hashlib.sha256(canonical).hexdigest()
    existing = db.scalar(
        select(WorkflowSnapshotBlob).where(
            WorkflowSnapshotBlob.snapshot_hash == snapshot_hash
        )
    )
    if existing is not None:
        load_snapshot_blob(existing)
        return existing

    compressed = gzip.compress(canonical, mtime=0)
    blob = WorkflowSnapshotBlob(
        snapshot_hash=snapshot_hash,
        canonical_size_bytes=len(canonical),
        compressed_size_bytes=len(compressed),
        compression="gzip",
        payload_bytes=compressed,
    )
    try:
        with db.begin_nested():
            db.add(blob)
            db.flush()
    except IntegrityError as exc:
        existing = db.scalar(
            select(WorkflowSnapshotBlob).where(
                WorkflowSnapshotBlob.snapshot_hash == snapshot_hash
            )
        )
        if existing is None:
            raise exc
        load_snapshot_blob(existing)
        return existing
    return blob


def load_snapshot_blob(blob: WorkflowSnapshotBlob) -> dict[str, Any]:
    if blob.compression != "gzip":
        raise SnapshotStoreError(
            "snapshot_blob_corrupt",
            "快照压缩格式不受支持",
        )
    if len(blob.payload_bytes) != blob.compressed_size_bytes:
        raise SnapshotStoreError(
            "snapshot_blob_corrupt",
            "快照压缩字节数不一致",
        )
    try:
        canonical = gzip.decompress(blob.payload_bytes)
    except (EOFError, OSError) as exc:
        raise SnapshotStoreError(
            "snapshot_blob_corrupt",
            "快照无法解压",
        ) from exc
    if len(canonical) != blob.canonical_size_bytes:
        raise SnapshotStoreError(
            "snapshot_blob_corrupt",
            "快照规范字节数不一致",
        )
    if hashlib.sha256(canonical).hexdigest() != blob.snapshot_hash:
        raise SnapshotStoreError(
            "snapshot_blob_corrupt",
            "快照哈希不一致",
        )
    try:
        document = json.loads(canonical)
    except (UnicodeDecodeError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
        raise SnapshotStoreError(
            "snapshot_blob_corrupt",
            "快照不是合法 JSON",
        ) from exc
    if not isinstance(document, dict):
        raise SnapshotStoreError(
            "snapshot_blob_corrupt",
            "快照 JSON 根节点必须是对象",
        )
    if canonical_snapshot_bytes(document) != canonical:
        raise SnapshotStoreError(
            "snapshot_blob_corrupt",
            "快照不是规范 JSON",
        )
    return document


def read_run_snapshot_text(run: ProductionRun) -> str:
    """Return the frozen source text, or a verified canonical blob fallback.

    Existing source text is intentionally returned unchanged: older snapshots
    may have noncanonical whitespace while their hash covers canonical JSON.
    """
    if run.snapshot_json:
        return run.snapshot_json
    if run.snapshot_blob_id is None:
        raise SnapshotStoreError("snapshot_blob_missing", "运行快照缺少内容引用")
    blob = run.snapshot_blob
    if blob is None or blob.id != run.snapshot_blob_id:
        raise SnapshotStoreError("snapshot_blob_missing", "运行快照内容不存在")
    document = load_snapshot_blob(blob)
    if blob.snapshot_hash != run.snapshot_hash:
        raise SnapshotStoreError("snapshot_blob_corrupt", "运行快照哈希不一致")
    return canonical_snapshot_bytes(document).decode("utf-8")


def read_run_snapshot_document(run: ProductionRun) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Decode a run snapshot without weakening the existing text contract."""
    return json.loads(read_run_snapshot_text(run))


def prefetch_run_snapshot_blobs(db: Session, runs: list[ProductionRun]) -> None:
    """Load only missing-text runs in one batch before iterating a result page."""
    compact = [run for run in runs if not run.snapshot_json]
    ids = {run.snapshot_blob_id for run in compact if run.snapshot_blob_id is not None}
    blobs = {blob.id: blob for blob in db.scalars(
        select(WorkflowSnapshotBlob).where(WorkflowSnapshotBlob.id.in_(ids))
    )} if ids else {}
    for run in compact:
        # Populate only the relationship. Refreshing entire runs would overwrite
        # a caller's unflushed status/lease changes when autoflush is disabled.
        set_committed_value(run, "snapshot_blob", blobs.get(run.snapshot_blob_id))


def bind_run_snapshot(
    db: Session,
    run: ProductionRun,
    snapshot: Mapping[str, Any],
) -> WorkflowSnapshotBlob:
    blob = get_or_create_snapshot_blob(db, snapshot)
    canonical = canonical_snapshot_bytes(snapshot)
    run.snapshot_json = canonical.decode("utf-8")
    run.snapshot_hash = blob.snapshot_hash
    run.snapshot_blob_id = blob.id
    run.snapshot_blob = blob
    return blob

===== FILE backend/app/workflow_v3.py =====

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class WorkflowV3ValidationError:
    path: str
    code: str
    message: str

    def as_dict(self) -> dict[str, str]:
        return {"path": self.path, "code": self.code, "message": self.message}


_TOP_LEVEL_FIELDS = frozenset(
    {

        "category_key",

        "output_schema",

        "retry_policy",

_QUEUE_CLASSES = frozenset(
    {"validation", "interactive", "production_batch", "canary", "recovery"}
)
_RUN_MODES = frozenset(
    {"labeling", "evaluation", "prompt_regression", "stock_regression", "incremental"}
)
_JOIN_MODES = frozenset({"all"})
_ERROR_POLICY_KINDS = frozenset({"fail", "continue", "skip", "manual_review"})
_RETRY_POLICY_KINDS = frozenset({"none", "fixed", "exponential"})
_ERROR_POLICY_FIELDS = frozenset({"kind"})
_RETRY_POLICY_FIELDS = frozenset({"kind", "max_attempts", "delay_seconds"})
MAX_WORKFLOW_RETRY_DELAY_SECONDS = 86_400
_COMMON_NODE_CONFIG_FIELDS = frozenset({"execution_role", "short_circuit_rules"})
_PROMPT_CONFIG_FIELDS = frozenset(
    {
        "asset_key",
        "input_modality",
        "prompt_version_id",
        "model_registry_id",
        "max_tokens",
        "thinking_level",
        "thinking_enabled",
        "temperature",
        "structured_output",
        "output_contract",
        "output_contract_key",
        "output_contract_snapshot",
        "component_snapshot",
        "model_snapshot",
        "vision_input_policy",
        "vision_capability_snapshot",
        "vision_policy_hash",
        # 多次采样投票（本地模型免费）：同一提示词跑 N 次取中位数，消掉边界票的抖动
        "vote_samples",
        "vote_temperature",
        "pairwise_anchors",
        "pairwise_samples",
        "pairwise_prompt",
        "response_format_mode",
        "precheck_context_mode",
        "production_title_normalization_profile",
    }
)
VOTE_MAX_SAMPLES = 9
PAIRWISE_MAX_SAMPLES = 5
# 09-19 实测：json_schema 严格约束解码在 .205 的 xgrammar 上单素材慢 1.85 倍；
# json_object 只约束"是 JSON"，字段仍由平台按冻结合同校验并可重问一次。
RESPONSE_FORMAT_MODES = frozenset({"contract", "json_object"})
_PROMPT_REQUIRED_CONFIG_FIELDS = frozenset(
    {
        "asset_key",
        "prompt_version_id",
        "model_registry_id",
        "max_tokens",
        "thinking_level",
    }
)
_FOREACH_ITEM_EXECUTOR_FIELDS = (_PROMPT_CONFIG_FIELDS - {
    "production_title_normalization_profile", "precheck_context_mode",
}) | frozenset({"kind"})
_SAFE_DOT_PATH_PATTERN = re.compile(
    r"^\$\.[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_-]*(?:\.[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_-]*)*$"
)

            "mapping",

            "field_contract_snapshot",

    "prompt": _PROMPT_CONFIG_FIELDS,
    "foreach_image": frozenset(

            "items_path",

            "max_items",

    "aggregate_case": frozenset(
        {
            "foreach_node_id",

            "score_path",

            "score_buckets",

            "failure_projection",
            # 素材（案例）维度：把图片总数、分数分布、各特征取值计数并进终态结果。
            # 逐版本开关，已冻结的旧版本不带这个键，输出与结果哈希保持原样。
            "emit_material_summary",

            "emit_image_grades",

            # 冻结在版本里，改口径要发新版本，旧回归的口径不会被追溯改写。
            "image_grade_bands",

}

_MERGE_STRATEGIES = frozenset(
    strategy
    for definition in module_type_catalog()
    for strategy in definition.merge_strategies
)


def _workflow_vision_code(code: str) -> str:
    return code if code.startswith("workflow_") else f"workflow_{code}"


def _parse_frozen_or_authored_vision_policy(
    value: Mapping[str, Any] | None,
    *,
    capability: VisionCapabilityProfile,
    legacy_if_missing: bool,
) -> VisionInputPolicy:
    """Parse authored v1 policies and the one exact frozen legacy projection."""

    if isinstance(value, Mapping) and value.get("version") == "legacy-default":
        expected = parse_vision_input_policy(
            None,
            capability=capability,
            legacy_if_missing=True,
        )
        if canonical_json(dict(value)) != canonical_json(expected.as_dict()):
            raise VisionInputPolicyError(
                "vision_input_policy_invalid",
                "历史兼容图片策略快照与平台固定语义不一致",
            )
        return expected
    return parse_vision_input_policy(
        value,
        capability=capability,
        legacy_if_missing=legacy_if_missing,
    )


def _synthetic_policy_capability(value: Mapping[str, Any]) -> VisionCapabilityProfile:
    profile_key = value.get("provider_profile")
    if profile_key == QWEN_205_PROFILE["profile_key"]:
        # Authored shape validation needs the real built-in profile. The
        # managed validator below still checks the selected model's provider
        # and both registry/source capabilities; this grants no model binding.
        return parse_vision_capability_profile(
            QWEN_205_PROFILE, provider="local_qwen", model_id="synthetic-validation-profile"
        )
    raw_profile = dict(SEED_20_PROFILE)
    if isinstance(profile_key, str) and profile_key:
        raw_profile["profile_key"] = profile_key
    return parse_vision_capability_profile(
        raw_profile,
        provider="workflow-contract",
        model_id="synthetic-validation-profile",
    )


def validate_authored_vision_input_policy(
    value: Any,
) -> VisionInputPolicy:
    """Validate policy shape without pretending to know the selected model.

    Model-specific support is deliberately checked by managed validation.
    """

    if not isinstance(value, Mapping):
        raise VisionInputPolicyError(
            "vision_input_policy_invalid",
            "vision_input_policy 必须是对象",
        )
    capability = _synthetic_policy_capability(value)
    return _parse_frozen_or_authored_vision_policy(
        value,
        capability=capability,
        legacy_if_missing=False,
    )


def _model_capabilities(model: ModelRegistryEntry) -> tuple[str, ...]:
    try:
        value = json.loads(model.capabilities_json or "[]")
    except json.JSONDecodeError:
        value = []
    if not isinstance(value, list):
        return ()
    return tuple(item for item in value if isinstance(item, str))


def resolve_prompt_vision_policy(
    config: Mapping[str, Any],
    *,
    model: ModelRegistryEntry,
    source_model: ModelConfig | None = None,
) -> tuple[VisionCapabilityProfile, VisionInputPolicy]:
    """Resolve the exact publish/runtime vision contract for one prompt node."""

    if config.get("input_modality", "image") != "image":
        if any(
            key in config
            for key in (
                "vision_input_policy",
                "vision_capability_snapshot",
                "vision_policy_hash",
            )
        ):
            raise VisionInputPolicyError(
                "vision_policy_not_applicable",
                "纯文本提示词节点不能配置图片输入策略",
            )
        raise VisionInputPolicyError(
            "vision_policy_not_applicable",
            "纯文本提示词节点没有图片输入策略",
        )
    if "vision" not in _model_capabilities(model):
        raise VisionInputPolicyError(
            "vision_capability_missing",
            "图片提示词节点所选模型未声明 vision 能力",
        )
    try:
        raw_capability = json.loads(model.vision_capability_profile_json or "{}")
    except (json.JSONDecodeError, TypeError) as exc:
        raise VisionInputPolicyError(
            "vision_capability_profile_invalid",
            "模型图片能力档案不是合法 JSON 对象",
        ) from exc
    capability = parse_vision_capability_profile(
        raw_capability,
        provider=model.provider,
        model_id=model.model_id,
    )
    if source_model is not None:
        try:
            source_raw = json.loads(
                source_model.vision_capability_profile_json or "{}"
            )
        except (json.JSONDecodeError, TypeError) as exc:
            raise VisionInputPolicyError(
                "vision_capability_source_drift",
                "运行模型图片能力档案不是合法 JSON 对象",
            ) from exc
        source_capability = parse_vision_capability_profile(
            source_raw,
            provider=source_model.provider,
            model_id=source_model.model_id,
        )
        if not same_vision_capability(
            source_capability,
            capability,
            provider=source_model.provider,
            model_id=source_model.model_id,
        ):
            raise VisionInputPolicyError(
                "vision_capability_source_drift",
                "模型注册表与运行模型的图片能力档案不一致",
            )
    policy = _parse_frozen_or_authored_vision_policy(
        config.get("vision_input_policy"),
        capability=capability,
        legacy_if_missing=True,
    )
    capability_snapshot = config.get("vision_capability_snapshot")
    if capability_snapshot is not None and not same_vision_capability(
        capability_snapshot,
        capability,
        provider=model.provider,
        model_id=model.model_id,
    ):
        raise VisionInputPolicyError(
            "workflow_vision_capability_snapshot_drift",
            "工作流冻结的图片能力档案与当前所选模型不一致",
        )
    expected_hash = vision_policy_hash(policy, capability)
    frozen_hash = config.get("vision_policy_hash")
    if frozen_hash is not None and frozen_hash != expected_hash:
        raise VisionInputPolicyError(
            "workflow_vision_policy_hash_drift",
            "工作流图片策略哈希与冻结策略或能力档案不一致",
        )
    return capability, policy


def rule_execution_fingerprint(node: Mapping[str, Any]) -> str:
    config = node["config"]
    payload = {"rule_module_version_id": config["rule_module_version_id"]}
    return hashlib.sha256(canonical_json(payload).encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()


def _error(
    errors: list[WorkflowV3ValidationError],
    path: str,
    code: str,
    message: str,
) -> None:
    errors.append(WorkflowV3ValidationError(path, code, message))


def _validate_schema(
    errors: list[WorkflowV3ValidationError], path: str, value: Any
) -> None:
    if not isinstance(value, Mapping) or value.get("type") != "object":
        _error(errors, path, "workflow_schema_invalid", "Schema 必须是对象类型")


def _positive_int(value: Any) -> bool:
    return isinstance(value, int) and not isinstance(value, bool) and value > 0


def _non_empty_text(value: Any) -> bool:
    return isinstance(value, str) and bool(value.strip())


def _validate_wait_settings(
    errors: list[WorkflowV3ValidationError],
    *,
    path: str,
    config: Mapping[str, Any],
) -> None:
    timeout = config.get("wait_timeout_seconds")
    if not _positive_int(timeout) or int(timeout) > 604800:
        _error(
            errors,
            f"{path}.wait_timeout_seconds",
            "workflow_continuation_timeout_invalid",
            "等待外部结果的时限必须为 1 秒至 7 天",
        )
    if config.get("timeout_policy") not in {"manual_review", "fail"}:
        _error(
            errors,
            f"{path}.timeout_policy",
            "workflow_continuation_timeout_policy_invalid",
            "等待超时后必须选择人工处理或失败",
        )


def _validate_first_seen_output_schema(
    errors: list[WorkflowV3ValidationError], path: str, schema: Any
) -> None:
    if not isinstance(schema, Mapping):
        return
    fields = {
        "status",
        "web_first_seen_at",
        "source_url",
        "provider",
        "match_kind",
        "confidence",
        "checked_at",
        "evidence",
    }
    properties = schema.get("properties")
    required = schema.get("required")
    if (
        not isinstance(properties, Mapping)
        or set(properties) != fields
        or not isinstance(required, list)
        or set(required) != fields
        or schema.get("additionalProperties") is not False
    ):
        _error(
            errors,
            path,
            "workflow_first_seen_output_schema_invalid",
            "图片最早出现时间节点必须使用平台固定输出字段",
        )


def _validate_prompt_config(
    errors: list[WorkflowV3ValidationError],
    *,
    path: str,
    config: Mapping[str, Any],
    nested: bool = False,
    category_key: str | None = None,
) -> None:
    try:
        precheck_context_mode(config, category_key=category_key)
    except ValueError as exc:
        _error(
            errors, f"{path}.precheck_context_mode",
            "workflow_prompt_precheck_context_invalid", str(exc),
        )
    if nested:
        for field in sorted(set(config) - _FOREACH_ITEM_EXECUTOR_FIELDS):
            _error(
                errors,
                f"{path}.{field}",
                "workflow_node_config_field_unknown",
                "逐图执行器包含未批准的提示词字段",
            )
        missing = sorted(_PROMPT_REQUIRED_CONFIG_FIELDS - set(config))
        if missing:
            _error(
                errors,
                path,
                "workflow_prompt_config_incomplete",
                f"逐图提示词配置缺少：{','.join(missing)}",
            )
        if config.get("kind") != "prompt":
            _error(
                errors,
                f"{path}.kind",
                "foreach_item_executor_invalid",
                "逐图执行器当前只能选择平台提示词节点",
            )
    if config.get("input_modality", "image") not in {"image", "text"}:
        _error(
            errors,
            f"{path}.input_modality",
            "workflow_prompt_input_modality_invalid",
            "提示词输入方式必须选择图片或纯文本",
        )
    if config.get("vision_input_policy") is not None:
        try:
            validate_authored_vision_input_policy(config.get("vision_input_policy"))
        except VisionInputPolicyError as exc:
            _error(
                errors,
                f"{path}.vision_input_policy",
                _workflow_vision_code(exc.code),
                str(exc),
            )
    capability_snapshot = config.get("vision_capability_snapshot")
    if capability_snapshot is not None:
        if not isinstance(capability_snapshot, Mapping):
            _error(
                errors,
                f"{path}.vision_capability_snapshot",
                "workflow_vision_capability_snapshot_invalid",
                "冻结图片能力档案必须是对象",
            )
        elif config.get("vision_input_policy") is None:
            _error(
                errors,
                f"{path}.vision_input_policy",
                "workflow_vision_policy_snapshot_incomplete",
                "冻结图片能力档案必须同时包含图片输入策略",
            )
    frozen_policy_hash = config.get("vision_policy_hash")
    if (capability_snapshot is None) != (frozen_policy_hash is None):
        _error(
            errors,
            path,
            "workflow_vision_policy_snapshot_incomplete",
            "运行时图片能力快照与策略哈希必须同时存在",
        )
    if frozen_policy_hash is not None and (
        not isinstance(frozen_policy_hash, str)
        or re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{64}", frozen_policy_hash) is None
    ):
        _error(
            errors,
            f"{path}.vision_policy_hash",
            "workflow_vision_policy_hash_invalid",
            "图片策略哈希必须是 64 位小写十六进制 SHA-256",
        )
    max_tokens = config.get("max_tokens")
    if (
        not isinstance(max_tokens, int)
        or isinstance(max_tokens, bool)
        or not 1 <= max_tokens <= 65536
    ):
        _error(
            errors,
            f"{path}.max_tokens",
            "workflow_model_max_tokens_invalid",
            "最大 token 必须是 1 至 65536 的整数",
        )
    if config.get("thinking_level") not in {
        "none",
        "auto",
        "low",
        "medium",
        "high",
    }:
        _error(
            errors,
            f"{path}.thinking_level",
            "workflow_model_thinking_level_invalid",
            "思考层级不受支持",
        )
    if "vote_samples" in config:
        vote_samples = config.get("vote_samples")
        if (isinstance(vote_samples, bool) or not isinstance(vote_samples, int)
                or not 1 <= vote_samples <= VOTE_MAX_SAMPLES):
            _error(
                errors,
                f"{path}.vote_samples",
                "workflow_model_vote_samples_invalid",
                f"投票采样次数必须是 1 至 {VOTE_MAX_SAMPLES} 的整数",
            )
    if "vote_temperature" in config:
        vote_temperature = config.get("vote_temperature")
        if (isinstance(vote_temperature, bool) or not isinstance(vote_temperature, (int, float))
                or not 0 <= vote_temperature <= 2):
            _error(
                errors,
                f"{path}.vote_temperature",
                "workflow_model_vote_temperature_invalid",
                "投票采样温度必须在 0 到 2 之间",
            )
    if "pairwise_anchors" in config and not isinstance(config.get("pairwise_anchors"), bool):
        _error(errors, f"{path}.pairwise_anchors", "workflow_model_pairwise_anchors_invalid",
               "成对锚点比较开关必须是布尔值")
    if "pairwise_samples" in config:
        pairwise_samples = config.get("pairwise_samples")
        if (isinstance(pairwise_samples, bool) or not isinstance(pairwise_samples, int)
                or not 1 <= pairwise_samples <= PAIRWISE_MAX_SAMPLES):
            _error(errors, f"{path}.pairwise_samples", "workflow_model_pairwise_samples_invalid",
                   f"每张锚点的比较次数必须是 1 至 {PAIRWISE_MAX_SAMPLES} 的整数")
    if "pairwise_prompt" in config:
        pairwise_prompt = config.get("pairwise_prompt")
        if not isinstance(pairwise_prompt, str) or not pairwise_prompt.strip() or len(pairwise_prompt) > 4000:
            _error(errors, f"{path}.pairwise_prompt", "workflow_model_pairwise_prompt_invalid",
                   "成对比较提示词必须是 1 到 4000 字的非空字符串")
    if "response_format_mode" in config and config.get("response_format_mode") not in RESPONSE_FORMAT_MODES:
        _error(errors, f"{path}.response_format_mode", "workflow_model_response_format_mode_invalid",
               "输出格式模式必须是 contract（按合同发 json_schema）或 json_object（只发宽松 JSON，由平台按合同校验）")
    if "thinking_enabled" in config:
        thinking_enabled = config.get("thinking_enabled")
        thinking_level = config.get("thinking_level")
        if not isinstance(thinking_enabled, bool):
            _error(
                errors,
                f"{path}.thinking_enabled",
                "workflow_model_thinking_enabled_invalid",
                "思考开关必须是布尔值",
            )
        elif (thinking_enabled and thinking_level == "none") or (
            not thinking_enabled and thinking_level != "none"
        ):
            _error(
                errors,
                f"{path}.thinking_enabled",
                "workflow_model_thinking_configuration_inconsistent",
                "思考开关与思考层级不一致",
            )
    if config.get("output_contract") is not None:
        try:
            output_contract = parse_output_contract(config.get("output_contract"))
        except PromptOutputContractError as exc:
            _error(
                errors,
                f"{path}.output_contract",
                "workflow_prompt_output_contract_invalid",
                f"提示词结构化输出合同不合法：{exc}",
            )
        else:
            if (
                output_contract.mode != "none"
                and config.get("structured_output") is False
            ):
                _error(
                    errors,
                    f"{path}.structured_output",
                    "workflow_prompt_structured_output_required",
                    "启用输出合同时必须同时启用结构化输出",
                )


def _validate_safe_dot_path(
    errors: list[WorkflowV3ValidationError], *, path: str, value: Any
) -> None:
    if not isinstance(value, str) or _SAFE_DOT_PATH_PATTERN.fullmatch(value) is None:
        _error(
            errors,
            path,
            "foreach_path_invalid",
            "逐图与聚合路径只能使用 $.字段.子字段 形式，不能包含表达式或数组选择器",
        )


def _validate_foreach_config(
    errors: list[WorkflowV3ValidationError], *, path: str, config: Mapping[str, Any]
) -> None:
    _validate_safe_dot_path(errors, path=f"{path}.items_path", value=config.get("items_path"))
    _validate_safe_dot_path(
        errors,
        path=f"{path}.item_identity_path",
        value=config.get("item_identity_path"),
    )
    max_items = config.get("max_items")
    if (
        not isinstance(max_items, int)
        or isinstance(max_items, bool)
        or not 1 <= max_items <= 10_000
    ):
        _error(
            errors,
            f"{path}.max_items",
            "foreach_max_items_invalid",
            "逐图数量硬门禁必须是 1 至 10000 的整数",
        )
    max_parallel = config.get("max_parallel_items")
    if (
        not isinstance(max_parallel, int)
        or isinstance(max_parallel, bool)
        or not 1 <= max_parallel <= 64
    ):
        _error(
            errors,
            f"{path}.max_parallel_items",
            "foreach_parallelism_invalid",
            "逐图节点内部并发必须是 1 至 64 的整数",
        )
    if config.get("failure_policy") not in {"fail_open", "fail_closed"}:
        _error(
            errors,
            f"{path}.failure_policy",
            "foreach_failure_policy_invalid",
            "逐图失败策略必须选择保留失败并继续或失败即终止",
        )
    executor = config.get("item_executor")
    if not isinstance(executor, Mapping):
        _error(
            errors,
            f"{path}.item_executor",
            "foreach_item_executor_invalid",
            "逐图节点必须配置一个提示词执行器",
        )
    else:
        _validate_prompt_config(
            errors,
            path=f"{path}.item_executor",
            config=executor,
            nested=True,
        )


def _validate_aggregate_config(
    errors: list[WorkflowV3ValidationError], *, path: str, config: Mapping[str, Any]
) -> None:
    if not _non_empty_text(config.get("foreach_node_id")):
        _error(
            errors,
            f"{path}.foreach_node_id",
            "aggregate_foreach_node_invalid",
            "案例聚合必须选择一个逐图节点",
        )
    statuses = config.get("included_statuses")
    allowed_statuses = {"succeeded", "failed", "skipped"}
    if (
        not isinstance(statuses, list)
        or not statuses
        or any(status not in allowed_statuses for status in statuses)
        or len(statuses) != len(set(statuses))
    ):
        _error(
            errors,
            f"{path}.included_statuses",
            "aggregate_statuses_invalid",
            "案例聚合状态必须是无重复的成功、失败或跳过状态数组",
        )
    _validate_safe_dot_path(errors, path=f"{path}.score_path", value=config.get("score_path"))
    feature_paths = config.get("feature_paths")
    if (
        not isinstance(feature_paths, Mapping)
        or len(feature_paths) > 64
        or any(not _non_empty_text(key) for key in feature_paths)
    ):
        _error(
            errors,
            f"{path}.feature_paths",
            "aggregate_feature_paths_invalid",
            "特征聚合路径必须是最多 64 项的命名对象",
        )
    else:
        for key, value in feature_paths.items():
            _validate_safe_dot_path(
                errors,
                path=f"{path}.feature_paths.{key}",
                value=value,
            )
    try:
        normalize_score_buckets(config.get("score_buckets"))
    except WorkflowCaseAggregationError:
        _error(
            errors,
            f"{path}.score_buckets",
            "aggregate_score_buckets_invalid",
            "分数区间必须是不重叠的有限左闭右开区间",
        )
    if config.get("empty_policy") not in {"fail", "emit_empty"}:
        _error(
            errors,
            f"{path}.empty_policy",
            "aggregate_empty_policy_invalid",
            "空案例必须选择失败或输出空聚合",
        )
    if config.get("failure_projection") not in {"include", "exclude"}:
        _error(
            errors,
            f"{path}.failure_projection",
            "aggregate_failure_projection_invalid",
            "失败图片必须选择保留或排除投影",
        )
    if "emit_material_summary" in config and not isinstance(
        config["emit_material_summary"], bool
    ):
        _error(
            errors,
            f"{path}.emit_material_summary",
            "aggregate_material_summary_invalid",
            "素材维度聚合摘要开关必须是布尔值",
        )
    if "emit_image_grades" in config and not isinstance(
        config["emit_image_grades"], bool
    ):
        _error(
            errors,
            f"{path}.emit_image_grades",
            "aggregate_image_grades_invalid",
            "图片维度逐图档位开关必须是布尔值",
        )
    if "image_grade_bands" in config:
        from .design_case_image_grade import (
            DesignCaseImageGradeError,
            normalize_grade_bands,
        )

        try:
            normalize_grade_bands(config["image_grade_bands"])
        except DesignCaseImageGradeError as exc:
            _error(
                errors,
                f"{path}.image_grade_bands",
                exc.code,
                str(exc),
            )


def _validate_node(
    errors: list[WorkflowV3ValidationError], node: Any, index: int,
    *, category_key: str | None = None,
) -> str | None:
    path = f"nodes[{index}]"
    if not isinstance(node, Mapping):
        _error(errors, path, "workflow_node_invalid", "节点必须是对象")
        return None
    unknown = sorted(set(node) - _NODE_FIELDS)
    for field in unknown:
        _error(
            errors,
            f"{path}.{field}",
            "workflow_contract_field_unknown",
            "节点包含未批准字段",
        )
    node_id = node.get("node_instance_id")
    if not isinstance(node_id, str) or not node_id.strip():
        _error(
            errors,
            f"{path}.node_instance_id",
            "workflow_node_identity_invalid",
            "节点必须有稳定实例标识",
        )
        node_id = None
    kind = node.get("kind")
    if kind not in _NODE_CONFIG_FIELDS:
        _error(
            errors,
            f"{path}.kind",
            "workflow_node_kind_unsupported",
            "节点类型不受支持",
        )
        return node_id
    display_name = node.get("display_name")
    if not isinstance(display_name, str) or not display_name.strip():
        _error(
            errors,
            f"{path}.display_name",
            "workflow_node_display_name_invalid",
            "节点必须填写运营可读名称",
        )
    config = node.get("config")
    if not isinstance(config, Mapping):
        _error(
            errors,
            f"{path}.config",
            "workflow_node_config_invalid",
            "节点配置必须是对象",
        )
        return node_id
    for field in sorted(
        set(config) - _NODE_CONFIG_FIELDS[kind] - _COMMON_NODE_CONFIG_FIELDS
    ):
        _error(
            errors,
            f"{path}.config.{field}",
            "workflow_node_config_field_unknown",
            "节点配置包含未批准字段",
        )
    missing = sorted(_REQUIRED_CONFIG_FIELDS[kind] - set(config))
    if missing:
        code = (
            "workflow_prompt_config_incomplete"
            if kind == "prompt"
            else "workflow_rule_config_incomplete"
            if kind == "rule_module"
            else "workflow_node_config_incomplete"
        )
        _error(
            errors,
            f"{path}.config",
            code,
            f"节点配置缺少：{','.join(missing)}",
        )
    if kind == "prompt":
        _validate_prompt_config(
            errors, path=f"{path}.config", config=config, category_key=category_key
        )
    elif kind == "foreach_image":
        _validate_foreach_config(
            errors, path=f"{path}.config", config=config
        )
    elif kind == "aggregate_case":
        _validate_aggregate_config(
            errors, path=f"{path}.config", config=config
        )
    if kind in {"upstream_connector", "downstream_projection"}:
        contract_version_id = config.get("connector_contract_version_id")
        if contract_version_id is not None and (
            isinstance(contract_version_id, bool)
            or not isinstance(contract_version_id, int)
            or contract_version_id < 1
        ):
            _error(
                errors,
                f"{path}.config.connector_contract_version_id",
                "workflow_connector_contract_version_invalid",
                "接口合同版本必须是正整数",
            )
    if kind == "upstream_connector":
        input_mode = config.get("input_mode", "initial_intake")
        if input_mode not in {"initial_intake", "continuation_event"}:
            _error(
                errors,
