                f"{path}.config.input_mode",
                "workflow_upstream_input_mode_invalid",
                "读取步骤必须选择初始接入或接收外部处理结果",
            )
        if input_mode == "continuation_event":
            if not _non_empty_text(config.get("continuation_key")):
                _error(
                    errors,
                    f"{path}.config.continuation_key",
                    "workflow_continuation_key_invalid",
                    "接收外部处理结果必须填写稳定关联标识",
                )
            if not _positive_int(config.get("connector_contract_version_id")):
                _error(
                    errors,
                    f"{path}.config.connector_contract_version_id",
                    "workflow_continuation_contract_version_required",
                    "接收外部处理结果必须锁定接口合同版本",
                )
    if kind == "downstream_projection":
        completion_mode = config.get("completion_mode", "continue")
        if completion_mode not in {"continue", "await_follow_up"}:
            _error(
                errors,
                f"{path}.config.completion_mode",
                "workflow_downstream_completion_mode_invalid",
                "写入下游后必须选择继续处理或等待对方处理",
            )
        if completion_mode == "await_follow_up":
            for field in ("continuation_key", "resume_node_instance_id"):
                if not _non_empty_text(config.get(field)):
                    _error(
                        errors,
                        f"{path}.config.{field}",
                        "workflow_continuation_key_invalid",
                        "等待外部处理必须填写关联标识和接收结果步骤",
                    )
            _validate_wait_settings(errors, path=f"{path}.config", config=config)
    if kind == "image_first_seen_lookup":
        if not _non_empty_text(config.get("provider_connector_key")):
            _error(
                errors,
                f"{path}.config.provider_connector_key",
                "workflow_first_seen_provider_invalid",
                "图片最早出现时间节点必须选择逻辑供应商",
            )
        for field in ("request_contract_version_id", "response_contract_version_id"):
            if not _positive_int(config.get(field)):
                _error(
                    errors,
                    f"{path}.config.{field}",
                    "workflow_connector_contract_version_invalid",
                    "图片查询节点必须锁定请求和响应合同版本",
                )
        _validate_wait_settings(errors, path=f"{path}.config", config=config)
        _validate_first_seen_output_schema(
            errors, f"{path}.output_schema", node.get("output_schema")
        )
    if kind == "branch":
        branch_key = config.get("branch_key")
        if not isinstance(branch_key, str) or not branch_key.strip():
            _error(
                errors,
                f"{path}.config.branch_key",
                "workflow_branch_key_invalid",
                "分支组必须有稳定标识",
            )
        branches = config.get("branches")
        if (
            not isinstance(branches, list)
            or len(branches) < 2
            or any(not isinstance(item, str) or not item for item in branches)
            or len(branches) != len(set(branches))
        ):
            _error(
                errors,
                f"{path}.config.branches",
                "workflow_branch_config_invalid",
                "分支至少包含两个不重复名称",
            )
        mode = config.get("mode", "parallel")
        if mode not in {"parallel", "exclusive"}:
            _error(
                errors,
                f"{path}.config.mode",
                "workflow_branch_mode_invalid",
                "分支模式必须选择同时执行或按条件选择一条路线",
            )
    if kind == "join":
        join_for = config.get("join_for")
        if not isinstance(join_for, str) or not join_for.strip():
            _error(
                errors,
                f"{path}.config.join_for",
                "workflow_join_branch_unknown",
                "汇合步骤必须选择对应的分支组",
            )
        if config.get("mode") not in _JOIN_MODES:
            _error(
                errors,
                f"{path}.config.mode",
                "workflow_join_mode_invalid",
                "当前汇合步骤必须等待全部路线完成",
            )
    for field in (
        "input_schema",
        "output_schema",
    ):
        _validate_schema(errors, f"{path}.{field}", node.get(field))
    error_policy = node.get("error_policy")
    if not isinstance(error_policy, Mapping):
        _error(
            errors,
            f"{path}.error_policy",
            "workflow_error_policy_invalid",
            "错误策略必须是对象",
        )
    else:
        for field in sorted(set(error_policy) - _ERROR_POLICY_FIELDS):
            _error(
                errors,
                f"{path}.error_policy.{field}",
                "workflow_error_policy_field_unknown",
                "错误策略包含未批准字段",
            )
        if error_policy.get("kind") not in _ERROR_POLICY_KINDS:
            _error(
                errors,
                f"{path}.error_policy.kind",
                "workflow_error_policy_invalid",
                "错误策略必须选择失败、继续、跳过或人工处理",
            )
    retry_policy = node.get("retry_policy")
    if not isinstance(retry_policy, Mapping):
        _error(
            errors,
            f"{path}.retry_policy",
            "workflow_retry_policy_invalid",
            "重试策略必须是对象",
        )
    else:
        for field in sorted(set(retry_policy) - _RETRY_POLICY_FIELDS):
            _error(
                errors,
                f"{path}.retry_policy.{field}",
                "workflow_retry_policy_field_unknown",
                "重试策略包含未批准字段",
            )
        retry_kind = retry_policy.get("kind")
        if retry_kind not in _RETRY_POLICY_KINDS:
            _error(
                errors,
                f"{path}.retry_policy.kind",
                "workflow_retry_policy_invalid",
                "重试策略必须选择不重试、固定间隔或递增间隔",
            )
        elif retry_kind != "none":
            max_attempts = retry_policy.get("max_attempts")
            delay_seconds = retry_policy.get("delay_seconds")
            if (
                not isinstance(max_attempts, int)
                or isinstance(max_attempts, bool)
                or not 1 <= max_attempts <= 5
                or not isinstance(delay_seconds, (int, float))
                or isinstance(delay_seconds, bool)
                or delay_seconds < 0
                or not math.isfinite(float(delay_seconds))
                or delay_seconds > MAX_WORKFLOW_RETRY_DELAY_SECONDS
            ):
                _error(
                    errors,
                    f"{path}.retry_policy",
                    "workflow_retry_policy_invalid",
                    "重试策略必须声明 1 至 5 次尝试和不超过 86400 秒的有限等待时间",
                )
    evidence = node.get("evidence_fields")
    if not isinstance(evidence, list) or any(
        not isinstance(item, str) or not item for item in evidence
    ):
        _error(
            errors,
            f"{path}.evidence_fields",
            "workflow_evidence_fields_invalid",
            "证据字段必须是文本数组",
        )
    return node_id


def _validate_edges_and_cycle(
    errors: list[WorkflowV3ValidationError],
    edges: Any,
    nodes_by_id: Mapping[str, Mapping[str, Any]],
) -> tuple[dict[str, list[str]], dict[str, list[str]]]:
    node_ids = set(nodes_by_id)
    outgoing = {node_id: [] for node_id in node_ids}
    incoming = {node_id: [] for node_id in node_ids}
    parsed_edges: list[tuple[str, str, str | None, Any, str]] = []
    if not isinstance(edges, list):
        _error(errors, "edges", "workflow_edges_invalid", "连线必须是数组")
        return outgoing, incoming
    for index, edge in enumerate(edges):
        path = f"edges[{index}]"
        if not isinstance(edge, Mapping):
            _error(errors, path, "workflow_edge_invalid", "连线必须是对象")
            continue
        for field in sorted(set(edge) - _EDGE_FIELDS):
            _error(
                errors,
                f"{path}.{field}",
                "workflow_contract_field_unknown",
                "连线包含未批准字段",
            )
        source = edge.get("from")
        target = edge.get("to")
        if source not in node_ids or target not in node_ids:
            _error(
                errors,
                path,
                "workflow_edge_node_unknown",
                "连线引用不存在的节点",
            )
            continue
        branch = edge.get("branch")
        if branch is not None and (
            not isinstance(branch, str) or not branch.strip()
        ):
            _error(
                errors,
                f"{path}.branch",
                "workflow_branch_edge_invalid",
                "路线名称必须是非空文本",
            )
            branch = None
        outgoing[source].append(target)
        incoming[target].append(source)
        parsed_edges.append((source, target, branch, edge.get("condition"), path))
    indegree = {key: len(value) for key, value in incoming.items()}
    ready = [key for key, value in indegree.items() if value == 0]
    visited = 0
    while ready:
        current = ready.pop()
        visited += 1
        for target in outgoing[current]:
            indegree[target] -= 1
            if indegree[target] == 0:
                ready.append(target)
    if node_ids and visited != len(node_ids):
        _error(errors, "edges", "workflow_cycle", "流程图中存在循环依赖")

    if node_ids:
        entries = [node_id for node_id in node_ids if not incoming[node_id]]
        terminals = [node_id for node_id in node_ids if not outgoing[node_id]]
        if len(entries) != 1:
            _error(
                errors,
                "edges",
                "workflow_entry_not_unique",
                "工作流必须有且只有一个入口步骤",
            )
        if len(terminals) != 1:
            _error(
                errors,
                "edges",
                "workflow_terminal_not_unique",
                "工作流必须有且只有一个终点步骤",
            )

    branch_nodes: dict[str, tuple[str, list[str]]] = {}
    duplicate_branch_keys: set[str] = set()
    for node_id, node in nodes_by_id.items():
        kind = node.get("kind")
        if kind != "branch" and len(outgoing[node_id]) > 1:
            _error(
                errors,
                f"nodes.{node_id}",
                "workflow_split_requires_branch",
                "一个步骤分出多条路线时必须先使用“分成多条路线”步骤",
            )
        if kind != "join" and len(incoming[node_id]) > 1:
            _error(
                errors,
                f"nodes.{node_id}",
                "workflow_merge_requires_join",
                "多条路线汇合时必须使用“汇合多条路线”步骤",
            )
        config = node.get("config")
        if kind != "branch" or not isinstance(config, Mapping):
            continue
        branch_key = config.get("branch_key")
        branches = config.get("branches")
        if not isinstance(branch_key, str) or not branch_key.strip():
            continue
        if branch_key in branch_nodes:
            duplicate_branch_keys.add(branch_key)
            _error(
                errors,
                f"nodes.{node_id}.config.branch_key",
                "workflow_branch_key_duplicate",
                "分支组标识不能重复",
            )
            continue
        branch_nodes[branch_key] = (
            node_id,
            list(branches) if isinstance(branches, list) else [],
        )

    edges_by_source: dict[str, list[tuple[str, str | None, Any, str]]] = {
        node_id: [] for node_id in node_ids
    }
    for source, target, branch, condition, path in parsed_edges:
        edges_by_source[source].append((target, branch, condition, path))
        if nodes_by_id[source].get("kind") != "branch" and branch is not None:
            _error(
                errors,
                f"{path}.branch",
                "workflow_branch_edge_forbidden",
                "只有分路线步骤的连线可以设置路线名称",
            )

        if nodes_by_id[source].get("kind") != "branch" and condition is not None:
            _error(
                errors,
                f"{path}.condition",
                "workflow_condition_edge_forbidden",
                "只有按条件选择路线的步骤可以设置条件",
            )

    def ancestors(node_id: str) -> set[str]:
        pending = list(incoming[node_id])
        result: set[str] = set()
        while pending:
            current = pending.pop()
            if current in result:
                continue
            result.add(current)
            pending.extend(incoming[current])
        return result

    for node_id, declared in branch_nodes.values():
        node_config = nodes_by_id[node_id].get("config")
        mode = (
            node_config.get("mode", "parallel")
            if isinstance(node_config, Mapping)
            else "parallel"
        )
        used = [
            branch for _, branch, _, _ in edges_by_source[node_id] if branch
        ]
        for _, branch, condition, path in edges_by_source[node_id]:
            if branch is None:
                _error(
                    errors,
                    f"{path}.branch",
                    "workflow_branch_edge_required",
                    "分路线步骤的每条连线都必须选择路线名称",
                )
            elif branch not in declared:
                _error(
                    errors,
                    f"{path}.branch",
                    "workflow_branch_edge_unknown",
                    "连线使用了未声明的路线名称",
                )
            if mode == "exclusive":
                if condition is None:
                    _error(
                        errors,
                        f"{path}.condition",
                        "workflow_exclusive_condition_required",
                        "按条件选择路线时，每条路线都必须配置条件",
                    )
                else:
                    for condition_error in validate_condition(
                        condition,
                        allowed_steps=ancestors(node_id),
                        allowed_roots={"steps"},
                        path=f"{path}.condition",
                    ):
                        _error(
                            errors,
                            condition_error.path,
                            condition_error.code,
                            condition_error.message,
                        )
            elif condition is not None:
                _error(
                    errors,
                    f"{path}.condition",
                    "workflow_parallel_condition_forbidden",
                    "同时执行多条路线时不能配置筛选条件",
                )
        for branch in declared:
            count = used.count(branch)
            if count == 0:
                _error(
                    errors,
                    f"nodes.{node_id}.config.branches",
                    "workflow_branch_edge_missing",
                    f"路线 {branch} 尚未连接后续步骤",
                )
            elif count > 1:
                _error(
                    errors,
                    f"nodes.{node_id}.config.branches",
                    "workflow_branch_edge_duplicate",
                    f"路线 {branch} 只能连接一个直接后续步骤",
                )

    def reaches(start: str, target: str) -> bool:
        pending = [start]
        seen: set[str] = set()
        while pending:
            current = pending.pop()
            if current == target:
                return True
            if current in seen:
                continue
            seen.add(current)
            pending.extend(outgoing[current])
        return False

    joins_by_branch: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    for node_id, node in nodes_by_id.items():
        if node.get("kind") != "join":
            continue
        config = node.get("config")
        join_for = config.get("join_for") if isinstance(config, Mapping) else None
        if not isinstance(join_for, str) or join_for not in branch_nodes:
            _error(
                errors,
                f"nodes.{node_id}.config.join_for",
                "workflow_join_branch_unknown",
                "汇合步骤引用了不存在的分支组",
            )
            continue
        joins_by_branch.setdefault(join_for, []).append(node_id)
        branch_node_id, _ = branch_nodes[join_for]
        for target, branch, _, _ in edges_by_source[branch_node_id]:
            if branch is not None and not reaches(target, node_id):
                _error(
                    errors,
                    f"nodes.{node_id}.config.join_for",
                    "workflow_branch_route_not_joined",
                    f"路线 {branch} 没有到达这个汇合步骤",
                )
        if len(incoming[node_id]) < 2:
            _error(
                errors,
                f"nodes.{node_id}",
                "workflow_join_inputs_insufficient",
                "汇合步骤至少需要两条输入路线",
            )

    for branch_key, (node_id, _) in branch_nodes.items():
        if branch_key in duplicate_branch_keys:
            continue
        count = len(joins_by_branch.get(branch_key, []))
        if count == 0:
            _error(
                errors,
                f"nodes.{node_id}.config.branch_key",
                "workflow_branch_join_missing",
                "分出的路线必须由一个对应汇合步骤收拢",
            )
        elif count > 1:
            _error(
                errors,
                f"nodes.{node_id}.config.branch_key",
                "workflow_branch_join_duplicate",
                "同一分支组只能由一个汇合步骤收拢",
            )
    return outgoing, incoming


def _validate_duplicate_rules(
    errors: list[WorkflowV3ValidationError], nodes: list[Any]
) -> None:
    seen: dict[str, str] = {}
    for index, node in enumerate(nodes):
        if not isinstance(node, Mapping) or node.get("kind") != "rule_module":
            continue
        config = node.get("config")
        if not isinstance(config, Mapping) or not _REQUIRED_CONFIG_FIELDS[
            "rule_module"
        ].issubset(config):
            continue
        fingerprint = rule_execution_fingerprint(node)
        current = str(node.get("node_instance_id", f"nodes[{index}]"))
        if fingerprint in seen:
            _error(
                errors,
                f"nodes[{index}].config",
                "duplicate_rule_module_execution",
                f"与节点 {seen[fingerprint]} 重复执行同一规则模块版本",
            )
        else:
            seen[fingerprint] = current


def _validate_short_circuit_rule_keys(
    errors: list[WorkflowV3ValidationError], nodes: list[Any]
) -> None:
    """A rule key identifies one frozen rule across the entire workflow."""

    seen: dict[str, tuple[int, int]] = {}
    for node_index, node in enumerate(nodes):
        if not isinstance(node, Mapping):
            continue
        config = node.get("config")
        rules = (
            config.get("short_circuit_rules")
            if isinstance(config, Mapping)
            else None
        )
        if not isinstance(rules, list):
            continue
        for rule_index, rule in enumerate(rules):
            rule_key = rule.get("rule_key") if isinstance(rule, Mapping) else None
            if not isinstance(rule_key, str) or not rule_key.strip():
                continue
            previous = seen.get(rule_key)
            if previous is None:
                seen[rule_key] = (node_index, rule_index)
                continue
            first_node, first_rule = previous
            _error(
                errors,
                (
                    f"nodes[{node_index}].config.short_circuit_rules"
                    f"[{rule_index}].rule_key"
                ),
                "short_circuit_rule_key_duplicate",
                (
                    f"短路规则键 {rule_key} 已由 nodes[{first_node}]"
                    f".config.short_circuit_rules[{first_rule}] 使用；"
                    "同一工作流内必须全局唯一"
                ),
            )


def _validate_short_circuit_skip_nodes(
    errors: list[WorkflowV3ValidationError],
    nodes_by_id: Mapping[str, Mapping[str, Any]],
    outgoing: Mapping[str, list[str]],
) -> None:
    for source_id, node in nodes_by_id.items():
        config = node.get("config")
        rules = (
            config.get("short_circuit_rules")
            if isinstance(config, Mapping)
            else None
        )
        if not isinstance(rules, list):
            continue
        descendants: set[str] = set()
        pending = list(outgoing.get(source_id, ()))
        while pending:
            target = pending.pop()
            if target in descendants:
                continue
            descendants.add(target)
            pending.extend(outgoing.get(target, ()))
        for rule_index, rule in enumerate(rules):
            skip_nodes = rule.get("skip_nodes") if isinstance(rule, Mapping) else None
            if not isinstance(skip_nodes, list):
                continue
            for target_index, target in enumerate(skip_nodes):
                path = (
                    f"nodes[{source_id}].config.short_circuit_rules"
                    f"[{rule_index}].skip_nodes[{target_index}]"
                )
                target_node = nodes_by_id.get(target) if isinstance(target, str) else None
                if target_node is None:
                    _error(
                        errors,
                        path,
                        "short_circuit_skip_node_unknown",
                        "短路显式跳过节点不在当前冻结工作流中",
                    )
                    continue
                if target not in descendants:
                    _error(
                        errors,
                        path,
                        "short_circuit_skip_node_not_downstream",
                        "短路只能显式跳过当前节点的下游节点",
                    )
                    continue
                target_config = target_node.get("config")
                target_role = (
                    target_config.get("execution_role")
                    if isinstance(target_config, Mapping)
                    else None
                )
                if (
                    target_role in PROTECTED_EXECUTION_ROLES
                    or target_node.get("kind") in PROTECTED_NODE_KINDS
                ):
                    _error(
                        errors,
                        path,
                        "short_circuit_skip_node_protected",
                        "审计、发件箱和下游投影节点不能被短路跳过",
                    )


def _is_external_wait_node(node: Mapping[str, Any]) -> bool:
    config = node.get("config")
    if node.get("kind") == "image_first_seen_lookup":
        return True
    return (
        node.get("kind") == "downstream_projection"
        and isinstance(config, Mapping)
        and config.get("completion_mode", "continue") == "await_follow_up"
    )


def _validate_external_wait_isolation(
    errors: list[WorkflowV3ValidationError],
    nodes_by_id: Mapping[str, Mapping[str, Any]],
    outgoing: Mapping[str, list[str]],
) -> None:
    """等待外部回调的节点不能落在「同时执行」的并行路线里。

    进入外部等待时整条运行被置为 waiting_external；此时兄弟路线上在飞的付费调用会被
    当成运行已终态而中断，回调恢复后运行也无可派发项、就此卡住。运行时不支持这种拓扑，
    保存时就拒绝（2026-09-26 复审裁决：先在编排侧禁止，不改运行时）。
    """

    waits = [node_id for node_id, node in nodes_by_id.items() if _is_external_wait_node(node)]
    if not waits:
        return

    def reach(start: str) -> set[str]:
        result: set[str] = set()
        pending = [start]
        while pending:
            current = pending.pop()
            if current in result:
                continue
            result.add(current)
            pending.extend(outgoing.get(current, []))
        return result

    for branch_id, node in nodes_by_id.items():
        config = node.get("config")
        mode = config.get("mode", "parallel") if isinstance(config, Mapping) else "parallel"
        targets = list(dict.fromkeys(outgoing.get(branch_id, [])))
        if node.get("kind") != "branch" or mode == "exclusive" or len(targets) < 2:
            continue
        arms = [reach(target) for target in targets]
        for wait_id in waits:
            inside = sum(wait_id in arm for arm in arms)
            if 0 < inside < len(arms):
                _error(
                    errors,
                    f"nodes.{wait_id}",
                    "workflow_external_wait_in_parallel_branch",
                    "等待外部回调的步骤不能放在同时执行的多条路线里；请移到汇合之后或改为按条件选择路线",
                )


def _validate_continuation_pairs(
    errors: list[WorkflowV3ValidationError],
    nodes_by_id: Mapping[str, Mapping[str, Any]],
    outgoing: Mapping[str, list[str]],
    incoming: Mapping[str, list[str]],
) -> None:
    waits: dict[str, list[tuple[str, Mapping[str, Any]]]] = {}
    resumes: dict[str, list[tuple[str, Mapping[str, Any]]]] = {}
    for node_id, node in nodes_by_id.items():
        config = node.get("config")
        if not isinstance(config, Mapping):
            continue
        if (
            node.get("kind") == "downstream_projection"
            and config.get("completion_mode", "continue") == "await_follow_up"
        ):
            key = config.get("continuation_key")
            if _non_empty_text(key):
                waits.setdefault(str(key), []).append((node_id, config))
        if (
            node.get("kind") == "upstream_connector"
            and config.get("input_mode", "initial_intake") == "continuation_event"
        ):
            key = config.get("continuation_key")
            if _non_empty_text(key):
                resumes.setdefault(str(key), []).append((node_id, config))
            if not incoming.get(node_id):
                _error(
                    errors,
                    f"nodes.{node_id}.config.input_mode",
                    "workflow_continuation_upstream_is_entry",
                    "接收外部处理结果不能作为流程入口",
                )

    all_keys = set(waits) | set(resumes)
    for key, wait_rows in waits.items():
        for wait_id, wait_config in wait_rows:
            declared_resume = wait_config.get("resume_node_instance_id")
            resume_node = (
                nodes_by_id.get(str(declared_resume))
                if _non_empty_text(declared_resume)
                else None
            )
            resume_config = (
                resume_node.get("config")
                if isinstance(resume_node, Mapping)
                else None
            )
            if (
                not isinstance(resume_node, Mapping)
                or resume_node.get("kind") != "upstream_connector"
                or not isinstance(resume_config, Mapping)
                or resume_config.get("input_mode", "initial_intake")
                != "continuation_event"
                or resume_config.get("continuation_key") != key
            ):
                _error(
                    errors,
                    f"nodes.{wait_id}.config.resume_node_instance_id",
                    "workflow_continuation_pair_mismatch",
                    "等待步骤选择的接收节点与外部续跑标识不一致",
                )
    for key in sorted(all_keys):
        wait_rows = waits.get(key, [])
        resume_rows = resumes.get(key, [])
        if len(wait_rows) > 1 or len(resume_rows) > 1:
            _error(
                errors,
                "nodes",
                "workflow_continuation_key_duplicate",
                f"外部续跑标识 {key} 在一个工作流版本内只能使用一对",
            )
        if len(wait_rows) != 1 or len(resume_rows) != 1:
            for node_id, _ in wait_rows + resume_rows:
                _error(
                    errors,
                    f"nodes.{node_id}.config.continuation_key",
                    "workflow_continuation_pair_missing",
                    "等待步骤与接收外部结果步骤必须成对出现",
                )
            continue
        wait_id, wait_config = wait_rows[0]
        resume_id, _ = resume_rows[0]
        if (
            wait_config.get("resume_node_instance_id") != resume_id
            or outgoing.get(wait_id) != [resume_id]
            or incoming.get(resume_id) != [wait_id]
        ):
            _error(
                errors,
                f"nodes.{wait_id}.config.resume_node_instance_id",
                "workflow_continuation_pair_mismatch",
                "等待步骤必须唯一连接到匹配的接收外部结果步骤",
            )


def _validate_knowledge_graph_bindings(
    errors: list[WorkflowV3ValidationError], nodes: list[Any]
) -> None:
    prompt_contracts: set[str] = set()
    prompt_contract_hashes: dict[str, set[str]] = {}
    projection_contracts: list[tuple[int, str]] = []
    for index, node in enumerate(nodes):
        if not isinstance(node, Mapping):
            continue
        config = node.get("config")
        if not isinstance(config, Mapping):
            continue
        kind = node.get("kind")
        key_field = (
            "output_contract_key"
            if kind == "prompt"
            else "field_contract_key"
            if kind == "downstream_projection"
            else None
        )
        if key_field is None or key_field not in config:
            continue
        contract_key = config.get(key_field)
        contract = (
            get_knowledge_graph_contract(contract_key, config.get("output_contract_snapshot" if kind == "prompt" else "field_contract_snapshot"))
            if isinstance(contract_key, str)
            else None
        )
        if contract is None:
            _error(
                errors,
                f"nodes[{index}].config.{key_field}",
                "workflow_kg_output_contract_unknown",
                "知识图谱字段合同不存在或未登记",
            )
            continue
        if kind == "prompt":
            prompt_contracts.add(contract.contract_key)
            prompt_contract_hashes.setdefault(contract.contract_key, set()).add(contract.snapshot()["contract_hash"])
            explicit = config.get("output_contract")
            if explicit is not None:
                schema = explicit.get("schema") if isinstance(explicit, Mapping) else None
                if (not isinstance(explicit, Mapping)
                    or explicit.get("mode") != "json_schema"
                    or _schema_shape(schema) != _schema_shape(contract.output_schema)):
                    _error(
                        errors,
                        f"nodes[{index}].config.output_contract",
                        "workflow_kg_explicit_schema_mismatch",
                        f"结构化输出与所选字段版本 {contract.contract_key} 不一致；"
                        "请重新选择字段版本。新增视角请选择 v2，其他新增字段需先登记，不能仅修改提示词或 JSON。",
                    )
            snapshot = config.get("output_contract_snapshot")
            if snapshot is not None and snapshot != contract.snapshot():
                _error(
                    errors,
                    f"nodes[{index}].config.output_contract_snapshot",
                    "workflow_kg_contract_snapshot_invalid",
                    "提示词节点的知识图谱字段快照与登记版本不一致",
                )
            if snapshot is not None and node.get("output_schema") != contract.output_schema:
                _error(
                    errors,
                    f"nodes[{index}].output_schema",
                    "workflow_kg_output_schema_mismatch",
                    "提示词输出 Schema 与冻结的知识图谱字段合同不一致",
                )
        else:
            projection_contracts.append((index, contract.contract_key))
            snapshot = config.get("field_contract_snapshot")
            if snapshot is not None and snapshot != contract.snapshot():
                _error(
                    errors,
                    f"nodes[{index}].config.field_contract_snapshot",
                    "workflow_kg_contract_snapshot_invalid",
                    "下游节点的知识图谱字段快照与登记版本不一致",
                )
    for index, contract_key in projection_contracts:
        snapshot = nodes[index]["config"].get("field_contract_snapshot")
        if snapshot is not None and prompt_contract_hashes.get(contract_key) != {snapshot.get("contract_hash")}:
            _error(errors, f"nodes[{index}].config.field_contract_snapshot", "workflow_kg_contract_mismatch",
                   "下游字段定义与提示词的冻结字段版本不一致")
        if contract_key not in prompt_contracts:
            _error(
                errors,
                f"nodes[{index}].config.field_contract_key",
                "workflow_kg_contract_mismatch",
                "下游知识图谱字段合同与本工作流的提示词输出合同不一致",
            )


def _output_keys(node: Mapping[str, Any]) -> set[str]:
    schema = node.get("output_schema")
    if not isinstance(schema, Mapping):
        return set()
    properties = schema.get("properties")
    return set(properties) if isinstance(properties, Mapping) else set()


def _validate_join_collisions(
    errors: list[WorkflowV3ValidationError],
    nodes_by_id: Mapping[str, Mapping[str, Any]],
    incoming: Mapping[str, list[str]],
) -> None:
    for node_id, node in nodes_by_id.items():
        if node.get("kind") != "join" or len(incoming.get(node_id, [])) < 2:
            continue
        counts: dict[str, int] = {}
        for source in incoming[node_id]:
            for key in _output_keys(nodes_by_id[source]):
                counts[key] = counts.get(key, 0) + 1
        collisions = {key for key, count in counts.items() if count > 1}
        if not collisions:
            continue
        config = node.get("config")
        merge_policy = (
            config.get("merge_policy") if isinstance(config, Mapping) else None
        )
        unresolved = {
            key
            for key in collisions
            if not isinstance(merge_policy, Mapping)
            or merge_policy.get(key) not in _MERGE_STRATEGIES
        }
        if unresolved:
            _error(
                errors,
                f"nodes.{node_id}.config.merge_policy",
                "workflow_output_collision",
                f"多分支同时写入字段且未选择合并方式：{','.join(sorted(unresolved))}",
            )


def validate_workflow_v3(
    manifest: Mapping[str, Any],
) -> list[WorkflowV3ValidationError]:
    errors: list[WorkflowV3ValidationError] = []
    if not isinstance(manifest, Mapping):
        return [
            WorkflowV3ValidationError(
                "manifest", "workflow_manifest_invalid", "工作流必须是对象"
            )
        ]
    for field in sorted(set(manifest) - _TOP_LEVEL_FIELDS):
        _error(
            errors,
            field,
            "workflow_contract_field_unknown",
            "工作流包含未批准字段",
        )
    if manifest.get("schema_version") != "workflow-v3":
        _error(
            errors,
            "schema_version",
            "workflow_schema_version_unsupported",
            "工作流规格必须是 workflow-v3",
        )
    for field in ("category_key", "lane_key"):
        if not isinstance(manifest.get(field), str) or not manifest[field].strip():
            _error(
                errors,
                field,
                "workflow_identity_invalid",
                "类目和链路标识不能为空",
            )
    if manifest.get("queue_class") not in _QUEUE_CLASSES:
        _error(
            errors,
            "queue_class",
            "queue_class_unsupported",
            "队列类型不受支持",
        )
    run_modes = manifest.get("run_modes")
    if (
        not isinstance(run_modes, list)
        or not run_modes
        or any(mode not in _RUN_MODES for mode in run_modes)
        or len(set(run_modes)) != len(run_modes)
    ):
        _error(
            errors,
            "run_modes",
            "workflow_run_mode_unsupported",
            "运行范围必须是无重复的既有模式",
        )
    if not isinstance(manifest.get("resource_policy"), Mapping):
        _error(
            errors,
            "resource_policy",
            "resource_policy_invalid",
            "资源策略必须是对象",
        )
    _validate_schema(errors, "input_schema", manifest.get("input_schema"))
    _validate_schema(errors, "output_schema", manifest.get("output_schema"))
    nodes = manifest.get("nodes")
    if not isinstance(nodes, list) or not nodes:
        _error(errors, "nodes", "workflow_nodes_empty", "工作流至少需要一个节点")
        nodes = []
    node_ids: set[str] = set()
    nodes_by_id: dict[str, Mapping[str, Any]] = {}
    for index, node in enumerate(nodes):
        node_id = _validate_node(
            errors, node, index, category_key=manifest.get("category_key"),
        )
        if node_id is None:
            continue
        if node_id in node_ids:
            _error(
                errors,
                f"nodes[{index}].node_instance_id",
                "workflow_node_identity_duplicate",
                "节点实例标识不可重复",
            )
        node_ids.add(node_id)
        if isinstance(node, Mapping):
            nodes_by_id[node_id] = node
    for index, node in enumerate(nodes):
        if not isinstance(node, Mapping) or node.get("kind") != "aggregate_case":
            continue
        config = node.get("config")
        foreach_node_id = (
            config.get("foreach_node_id") if isinstance(config, Mapping) else None
        )
        source = nodes_by_id.get(str(foreach_node_id))
        if source is None or source.get("kind") != "foreach_image":
            _error(
                errors,
                f"nodes[{index}].config.foreach_node_id",
                "aggregate_foreach_node_invalid",
                "案例聚合引用的节点必须是当前工作流中的逐图节点",
            )
    valid_levels = enabled_levels_from_manifest(manifest)
    for index, node in enumerate(nodes):
        if not isinstance(node, Mapping):
            continue
        config = node.get("config")
        if not isinstance(config, Mapping):
            continue
        if "production_title_normalization_profile" in config:
            from .production_title_normalization import frozen_title_profile
            try:
                frozen_title_profile(node, str(manifest.get("category_key")))
            except ValueError as exc:
                _error(errors, f"nodes[{index}].config.production_title_normalization_profile",
                       "workflow_title_normalization_invalid", str(exc))
        role = config.get("execution_role")
        if role is not None and (
            not isinstance(role, str) or not role.strip() or len(role) > 80
        ):
            _error(
                errors,
                f"nodes[{index}].config.execution_role",
                "workflow_execution_role_invalid",
                "执行角色必须是 1 至 80 字符的稳定标识",
            )
        rules = config.get("short_circuit_rules")
        if rules is None:
            continue
        try:
            validate_short_circuit_rules(rules, valid_levels)
        except ShortCircuitError as exc:
            _error(
                errors,
                f"nodes[{index}].config.short_circuit_rules",
                exc.code,
                str(exc),
            )
    outgoing, incoming = _validate_edges_and_cycle(
        errors, manifest.get("edges"), nodes_by_id
    )
    _validate_continuation_pairs(errors, nodes_by_id, outgoing, incoming)
    _validate_external_wait_isolation(errors, nodes_by_id, outgoing)
    _validate_short_circuit_rule_keys(errors, nodes)
    _validate_short_circuit_skip_nodes(errors, nodes_by_id, outgoing)
    _validate_duplicate_rules(errors, nodes)
    _validate_knowledge_graph_bindings(errors, nodes)
    _validate_join_collisions(errors, nodes_by_id, incoming)
    deduplicated: dict[tuple[str, str], WorkflowV3ValidationError] = {}
    for item in errors:
        deduplicated.setdefault((item.path, item.code), item)
    return list(deduplicated.values())


def _asset_for_node(
    db: Session,
    *,
    category_key: str,
    asset_key: Any,
) -> WorkflowNodeAsset | None:
    if not isinstance(asset_key, str) or not asset_key:
        return None
    return db.scalar(
        select(WorkflowNodeAsset).where(
            WorkflowNodeAsset.category_key == category_key,
            WorkflowNodeAsset.asset_key == asset_key,
        )
    )


def validate_managed_workflow_v3(
    db: Session, manifest: Mapping[str, Any]
) -> list[WorkflowV3ValidationError]:
    errors = validate_workflow_v3(manifest)
    category_key = manifest.get("category_key")
    if not isinstance(category_key, str) or not category_key:
        return errors
    profile = db.scalar(
        select(EvaluationCategoryProfile).where(
            EvaluationCategoryProfile.category_key == category_key
        )
    )
    if profile is None or profile.status == "retired":
        _error(
            errors,
            "category_key",
            "workflow_category_unknown",
            "工作流所属类目不存在或已经停用",
        )
    nodes = manifest.get("nodes")
    if not isinstance(nodes, list):
        return errors
    for index, node in enumerate(nodes):
        if not isinstance(node, Mapping):
            continue
        kind = node.get("kind")
        if kind not in {"prompt", "rule_module", "foreach_image"}:
            continue
        config = node.get("config")
        if not isinstance(config, Mapping):
            continue
        path = f"nodes[{index}].config"
        if kind == "foreach_image":
            executor = config.get("item_executor")
            if not isinstance(executor, Mapping):
                continue
            config = executor
            kind = "prompt"
            path = f"{path}.item_executor"
        asset = _asset_for_node(
            db,
            category_key=category_key,
            asset_key=config.get("asset_key"),
        )
        if asset is None:
            _error(
                errors,
                f"{path}.asset_key",
                "workflow_component_asset_unknown",
                "当前类目不存在所选组件节点",
            )
            continue
        if asset.status != "active":
            _error(
                errors,
                f"{path}.asset_key",
                "workflow_component_asset_unavailable",
                "所选组件节点已经停用",
            )
        if kind == "prompt":
            if asset.kind != "prompt":
                _error(
                    errors,
                    f"{path}.asset_key",
                    "workflow_component_kind_mismatch",
                    "所选组件不是提示词节点",
                )
                continue
            version_id = config.get("prompt_version_id")
            prompt = (
                db.get(PromptVersion, version_id)
                if isinstance(version_id, int) and not isinstance(version_id, bool)
                else None
            )
            if prompt is None:
                _error(
                    errors,
                    f"{path}.prompt_version_id",
                    "workflow_prompt_version_unknown",
                    "提示词版本不存在",
                )
            elif prompt.category_key != category_key:
                _error(
                    errors,
                    f"{path}.prompt_version_id",
                    "workflow_prompt_category_mismatch",
                    "提示词版本不属于当前类目",
                )
            elif db.scalar(
                select(WorkflowNodeAssetBinding.id).where(
                    WorkflowNodeAssetBinding.asset_id == asset.id,
                    WorkflowNodeAssetBinding.prompt_version_id == prompt.id,
                )
            ) is None:
                _error(
                    errors,
                    f"{path}.prompt_version_id",
                    "workflow_component_version_unbound",
                    "提示词版本尚未绑定到该节点",
                )
            model_id = config.get("model_registry_id")
            model = (
                db.get(ModelRegistryEntry, model_id)
                if isinstance(model_id, int) and not isinstance(model_id, bool)
                else None
            )
            if model is None or not model.active:
                _error(
                    errors,
                    f"{path}.model_registry_id",
                    "workflow_model_unavailable",
                    "所选模型不存在或已经停用",
                )
            elif (
                model.role not in {"main", "benchmark"}
                or model.source_model_config_id is None
                or db.get(ModelConfig, model.source_model_config_id) is None
            ):
                _error(
                    errors,
                    f"{path}.model_registry_id",
                    "workflow_model_role_unsupported",
                    "工作流只能选择主评测或基准模型",
                )
            elif (
                isinstance(config.get("max_tokens"), int)
                and not isinstance(config.get("max_tokens"), bool)
                and config["max_tokens"] > model.max_tokens
            ):
                _error(
                    errors,
                    f"{path}.max_tokens",
                    "workflow_model_max_tokens_exceeds_limit",
                    f"最大 token 不能超过所选模型上限 {model.max_tokens}",
                )
            elif config.get("output_contract_key") and not model.structured_output:
                _error(
                    errors,
                    f"{path}.model_registry_id",
                    "workflow_kg_structured_output_required",
                    "知识图谱字段合同只能使用支持结构化输出的模型",
                )
            output_contract = None
            if config.get("output_contract") is not None:
                try:
                    output_contract = parse_output_contract(
                        config.get("output_contract")
                    )
                except PromptOutputContractError:
                    pass
            if (
                output_contract is not None
                and output_contract.mode != "none"
                and model is not None
            ):
                try:
                    capabilities = json.loads(model.capabilities_json or "[]")
                except json.JSONDecodeError:
                    capabilities = []
                if (
                    model.structured_output is not True
                    or not isinstance(capabilities, list)
                    or "structured_output" not in capabilities
                ):
                    _error(
                        errors,
                        f"{path}.model_registry_id",
                        "workflow_prompt_structured_output_unsupported",
                        "严格输出合同只能使用已声明结构化输出能力的模型",
                    )
                if (
                    output_contract.mode == "json_schema"
                    and model.protocol != "openai_chat"
                ):
                    _error(
                        errors,
                        f"{path}.model_registry_id",
                        "workflow_prompt_json_schema_protocol_unsupported",
                        "当前仅允许 OpenAI Chat 兼容协议发送严格 JSON Schema",
                    )
            requires_vision = config.get("input_modality", "image") == "image"
            has_vision_config = any(
                key in config
                for key in (
                    "vision_input_policy",
                    "vision_capability_snapshot",
                    "vision_policy_hash",
                )
            )
            source_model = (
                db.get(ModelConfig, model.source_model_config_id)
                if model is not None and model.source_model_config_id is not None
                else None
            )
            if model is not None and (requires_vision or has_vision_config):
                try:
                    resolve_prompt_vision_policy(
                        config,
                        model=model,
                        source_model=source_model,
                    )
                except VisionInputPolicyError as exc:
                    code = _workflow_vision_code(exc.code)
                    field = (
                        "vision_policy_hash"
                        if code == "workflow_vision_policy_hash_drift"
                        else "vision_capability_snapshot"
                        if code == "workflow_vision_capability_snapshot_drift"
                        else "vision_input_policy"
                    )
                    _error(
                        errors,
                        f"{path}.{field}",
                        code,
                        str(exc),
                    )
            continue
        if asset.kind != "rule_module" or asset.module_type_key != config.get(
            "module_type_key"
        ):
            _error(
                errors,
                f"{path}.asset_key",
                "workflow_component_kind_mismatch",
                "所选规则节点的模块类型不一致",
            )
            continue
        version_id = config.get("rule_module_version_id")
        version = (
            db.get(RuleModuleVersion, version_id)
            if isinstance(version_id, int) and not isinstance(version_id, bool)
            else None
        )
        if version is None:
            _error(
                errors,
                f"{path}.rule_module_version_id",
                "workflow_rule_version_unknown",
                "规则模块版本不存在",
            )
        elif (
            version.asset_id != asset.id
            or version.module_type_key != asset.module_type_key
        ):
            _error(
                errors,
                f"{path}.rule_module_version_id",
                "workflow_component_version_unbound",
                "规则模块版本不属于该节点",
            )
    deduplicated: dict[tuple[str, str], WorkflowV3ValidationError] = {}
    for item in errors:
        deduplicated.setdefault((item.path, item.code), item)
    return list(deduplicated.values())


def _prompt_content(prompt: PromptVersion) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "stage": prompt.stage,
        "system_prompt": prompt.system_prompt,
        "user_prompt": prompt.user_prompt,
        "rubric_version": prompt.rubric_version,
    }



def _pinned_legacy_revision_bundle(
    db: Session, resource_policy: Mapping[str, Any], category_key: str
) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    """候选/迁移工作流若钉住了源修订，投影就按该修订算，而不是按当前 active 修订。

    否则从候选修订物化出来的工作流会被 active 修订的工作流级字段（如
    image_defects_enabled）覆盖，导致模型 schema 与执行/收尾口径不一致。
    没有钉住修订、或修订不属于本类目时返回 None，回落 active 修订（行为不变）。
    """
    identity = resource_policy.get("migration_identity")
    if not isinstance(identity, Mapping):
        return None
    revision_id = identity.get("legacy_revision_id")
    if not isinstance(revision_id, int) or isinstance(revision_id, bool):
        return None
    from .category_evaluation_v3_revisions import revision_bundle
    from .models import CategoryEvaluationV3Revision

    revision = db.get(CategoryEvaluationV3Revision, revision_id)
    if revision is None or revision.category_key != category_key:
        return None
    return revision_bundle(revision)

def freeze_component_references(
    db: Session, manifest: Mapping[str, Any]
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    frozen = deepcopy(dict(manifest))
    for node in frozen.get("nodes", []):
        if not isinstance(node, dict):
            continue
        config = node.get("config")
        if not isinstance(config, dict):
            continue
        if (
            node.get("kind") == "aggregate_case"
            and config.get("emit_image_grades") is True
            and config.get("image_grade_bands") is None
        ):
            from .design_case_image_grade import DEFAULT_IMAGE_GRADE_BANDS, bands_payload

            # Freeze defaults into the new version, never into the caller's
            # draft or an existing published manifest.
            config["image_grade_bands"] = bands_payload(DEFAULT_IMAGE_GRADE_BANDS)
        prompt_config = (
            config
            if node.get("kind") == "prompt"
            else config.get("item_executor")
            if node.get("kind") == "foreach_image"
            else None
        )
        if isinstance(prompt_config, dict) and "thinking_enabled" not in prompt_config:
            prompt_config["thinking_enabled"] = False
            prompt_config["thinking_level"] = "none"
        if node.get("kind") == "upstream_connector":
            config.setdefault("input_mode", "initial_intake")
        elif node.get("kind") == "downstream_projection":
            config.setdefault("completion_mode", "continue")
        key_field = (
            "output_contract_key"
            if node.get("kind") == "prompt"
            else "field_contract_key"
            if node.get("kind") == "downstream_projection"
            else None
        )
        if key_field is None or not isinstance(config.get(key_field), str):
            continue
        contract = get_knowledge_graph_contract(config[key_field], config.get("output_contract_snapshot" if node.get("kind") == "prompt" else "field_contract_snapshot"))
        if contract is None:
            continue
        if node.get("kind") == "prompt":
            config["output_contract_snapshot"] = contract.snapshot()
            node["output_schema"] = contract.output_schema
            if config.get("output_contract") is None:
                config["output_contract"] = knowledge_graph_strict_output_contract(contract.contract_key, contract.snapshot())
        else:
            config["field_contract_snapshot"] = contract.snapshot()
    errors = validate_managed_workflow_v3(db, frozen)
    if errors:
        from .workflow_registry import WorkflowRegistryError

        vision_error = next(
            (
                item
                for item in errors
                if item.code.startswith("workflow_vision_")
            ),
            None,
        )
        if vision_error is not None:
            raise WorkflowRegistryError(
                vision_error.code,
                vision_error.message,
            )
        raise WorkflowRegistryError(
            "workflow_manifest_invalid",
            canonical_json(
                {"ok": False, "errors": [item.as_dict() for item in errors]}
            ),
        )
    from .evaluation_mechanism_migration import split_legacy_revision
    from .worker_v3_authoritative import (
        V3AuthoritativeError,
        v3_authoritative_category,
    )

    resource_policy = frozen.setdefault("resource_policy", {})
    category_key = frozen.get("category_key")
    pinned_bundle = _pinned_legacy_revision_bundle(db, resource_policy, str(category_key))
    try:
        base_bundle = (
            pinned_bundle
            if pinned_bundle is not None
            else v3_authoritative_category(db, str(category_key))
        )
    except V3AuthoritativeError as exc:
        resource_policy["legacy_projection_snapshot"] = {
            "schema_version": "legacy-projection-workflow-fields-v1",
            "available": False,
            "reason_code": exc.code,
        }
    else:
        projection_slices = split_legacy_revision(base_bundle)
        workflow_fields = projection_slices.workflow_fields
        resource_policy["legacy_projection_snapshot"] = {
            "schema_version": "legacy-projection-workflow-fields-v1",
            "available": True,
            "presence": sorted(projection_slices.presence),
            "workflow_fields": workflow_fields,
            "workflow_fields_hash": hashlib.sha256(
                canonical_json(workflow_fields).encode("utf-8")
            ).hexdigest(),
        }
    for node in frozen["nodes"]:
        config = node["config"]
        kind = node["kind"]
        if kind == "foreach_image":
            config = config["item_executor"]
            kind = "prompt"
        if kind == "prompt":
            prompt = db.get(PromptVersion, config["prompt_version_id"])
            model = db.get(ModelRegistryEntry, config["model_registry_id"])
            assert prompt is not None and model is not None
            source_model = (
                db.get(ModelConfig, model.source_model_config_id)
                if model.source_model_config_id is not None
                else None
            )
            assert source_model is not None
            if config.get("input_modality", "image") == "image":
                capability, policy = resolve_prompt_vision_policy(
                    config,
                    model=model,
                    source_model=source_model,
                )
                config["vision_input_policy"] = policy.as_dict()
                config["vision_capability_snapshot"] = capability.as_dict()
                config["vision_policy_hash"] = vision_policy_hash(
                    policy,
                    capability,
                )
            config.setdefault("temperature", model.temperature)
            config.setdefault("structured_output", model.structured_output)
            content = _prompt_content(prompt)
            config["component_snapshot"] = {
                "kind": "prompt",
                "version_id": prompt.id,
                "version": prompt.version,
                "content": content,
                "content_hash": canonical_component_hash(content),
            }
            try:
                capabilities = json.loads(model.capabilities_json or "[]")
            except json.JSONDecodeError:
                capabilities = []
            model_snapshot = {
                "model_registry_id": model.id,
                "name": model.name,
                "provider": model.provider,
                "protocol": model.protocol,
                "model_id": model.model_id,
                "source_model_config_id": source_model.id,
                "capabilities": capabilities
                if isinstance(capabilities, list)
                else [],
                "structured_output": model.structured_output,
                "runtime_config": build_model_config_snapshot(source_model),
            }
            model_snapshot["snapshot_hash"] = hashlib.sha256(
                canonical_json(model_snapshot).encode("utf-8")
            ).hexdigest()
            config["model_snapshot"] = model_snapshot
        elif kind == "rule_module":
            version = db.get(
                RuleModuleVersion, config["rule_module_version_id"]
            )
            assert version is not None
            config["component_snapshot"] = {
                "kind": "rule_module",
                "version_id": version.id,
                "version": str(version.version),
                "module_type_key": version.module_type_key,
                "content_json": version.content_json,
                "content_hash": version.content_hash,
            }
    from .recommendation_calibration import manifest_profile

    try:
        manifest_profile(frozen)
    except ValueError as exc:
        from .workflow_registry import WorkflowRegistryError

        raise WorkflowRegistryError("workflow_recommendation_calibration_invalid", str(exc)) from exc
    return frozen

===== FILE backend/app/workflow_working_copies.py =====
"""Editable workflow-v3 canvases and immutable mechanism generation."""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from copy import deepcopy
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from typing import Any, Mapping

from sqlalchemy import select, update
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .dimension_schema_registry import canonical_json
from .evaluation_component_catalog import MODULE_TYPE_KEYS
from .models import (
    PromptVersion,
    WorkflowDefinition,
    WorkflowNodeAsset,
    WorkflowNodeAssetBinding,
    WorkflowVersion,
    WorkflowWorkingCopy,
)
from .workflow_registry import (
    WorkflowRegistryError,
    create_workflow_version,
    persisted_workflow_manifest,
)
from .workflow_v3 import freeze_component_references


class WorkflowWorkingCopyError(ValueError):
    def __init__(
        self, code: str, message: str, *, status_code: int = 400
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__(message)
        self.code = code
        self.status_code = status_code


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class EditableWorkflowTemplate:
    manifest: dict[str, Any]
    source_workflow_version_id: int
    compatibility_applied: bool


def _require_definition(db: Session, definition_id: int) -> WorkflowDefinition:
    definition = db.get(WorkflowDefinition, definition_id)
    if definition is None:
        raise WorkflowWorkingCopyError(
            "workflow_definition_not_found",
            "工作流定义不存在",
            status_code=404,
        )
    if definition.status != "active":
        raise WorkflowWorkingCopyError(
            "workflow_definition_retired",
            "已停用工作流不能继续编辑",
            status_code=409,
        )
    return definition


def _require_v3_manifest(manifest: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    if not isinstance(manifest, Mapping) or manifest.get(
        "schema_version"
    ) != "workflow-v3":
        raise WorkflowWorkingCopyError(
            "workflow_working_copy_schema_invalid",
            "工作副本必须使用 workflow-v3",
        )
    try:
        return json.loads(canonical_json(dict(manifest)))
    except (TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
        raise WorkflowWorkingCopyError(
            "workflow_working_copy_json_invalid",
            "工作副本不是合法 JSON",
        ) from exc


def get_or_create_workflow_working_copy(
    db: Session,
    *,
    workflow_definition_id: int,
    initial_manifest: Mapping[str, Any],
    actor: str,
) -> WorkflowWorkingCopy:
    _require_definition(db, workflow_definition_id)
    existing = db.scalar(
        select(WorkflowWorkingCopy).where(
            WorkflowWorkingCopy.workflow_definition_id
            == workflow_definition_id
        )
    )
    if existing is not None:
        return existing
    normalized = _require_v3_manifest(initial_manifest)
    row = WorkflowWorkingCopy(
        workflow_definition_id=workflow_definition_id,
        base_workflow_version_id=None,
        manifest_json=canonical_json(normalized),
        lock_version=1,
        updated_by=actor,
    )
    db.add(row)
    db.flush()
    return row


def require_workflow_working_copy(
    db: Session, workflow_definition_id: int
) -> WorkflowWorkingCopy:
    _require_definition(db, workflow_definition_id)
    row = db.scalar(
        select(WorkflowWorkingCopy).where(
            WorkflowWorkingCopy.workflow_definition_id
            == workflow_definition_id
        )
    )
    if row is None:
        raise WorkflowWorkingCopyError(
            "workflow_working_copy_not_found",
            "工作流尚未创建工作副本",
            status_code=404,
        )
    return row


def _operator_owned_node(node: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    normalized = deepcopy(dict(node))
    config = normalized.get("config")
    if isinstance(config, dict):
        for field in (
            "component_snapshot",
            "model_snapshot",
            "connector_snapshot",
            "field_contract_snapshot",
            "output_contract_snapshot",
            "vision_capability_snapshot",
            "vision_policy_hash",
        ):
            config.pop(field, None)
    return normalized


def _legacy_prompt_node(
    db: Session,
    *,
    category_key: str,
    node: Mapping[str, Any],
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    config = node.get("config")
    if not isinstance(config, Mapping):
        raise WorkflowWorkingCopyError(
            "legacy_prompt_config_invalid",
            "历史提示词节点配置损坏，无法生成兼容编辑模板",
            status_code=409,
        )
    asset_key = config.get("asset_key")
    prompt_ref = config.get("prompt_ref")
    asset = db.scalar(
        select(WorkflowNodeAsset).where(
            WorkflowNodeAsset.category_key == category_key,
            WorkflowNodeAsset.asset_key == asset_key,
            WorkflowNodeAsset.kind == "prompt",
        )
    )
    if asset is None:
        raise WorkflowWorkingCopyError(
            "legacy_prompt_asset_missing",
            f"历史提示词节点 {asset_key} 尚未进入统一组件目录",
            status_code=409,
        )
    versions = db.scalars(
        select(PromptVersion)
        .join(
            WorkflowNodeAssetBinding,
            WorkflowNodeAssetBinding.prompt_version_id == PromptVersion.id,
        )
        .where(WorkflowNodeAssetBinding.asset_id == asset.id)
        .order_by(PromptVersion.id.desc())
    ).all()
    prompt = next(
        (
            item
            for item in versions
            if f"prompt:{item.stage}@{item.version}" == prompt_ref
        ),
        None,
    )
    if prompt is None:
        raise WorkflowWorkingCopyError(
            "legacy_prompt_version_missing",
            f"历史提示词引用 {prompt_ref} 已不可用",
            status_code=409,
        )
    converted_config = {
        key: deepcopy(value)
        for key, value in config.items()
        if key != "prompt_ref"
    }
    converted_config["prompt_version_id"] = prompt.id
    return {
        "node_instance_id": node.get("key"),
        "kind": "prompt",
        "display_name": node.get("display_name") or asset.display_name,
        "config": converted_config,
        "input_schema": deepcopy(node.get("input_schema", {"type": "object"})),
        "output_schema": deepcopy(node.get("output_schema", {"type": "object"})),
        "error_policy": deepcopy(node.get("error_policy", {"kind": "fail"})),
        "retry_policy": deepcopy(
            node.get(
                "retry_policy",
                {"kind": "fixed", "max_attempts": 1, "delay_seconds": 0},
            )
        ),
        "evidence_fields": deepcopy(node.get("evidence_fields", [])),
    }


def editable_workflow_template(
    db: Session, workflow_definition_id: int
) -> EditableWorkflowTemplate:
    """Build a non-mutating operator template for an existing workflow.

    Historical imports intentionally keep the old A/B workflow and the split
    seven-module revision as separate immutable facts.  The editor, however,
    must present one complete mechanism.  This view combines those facts only
    when the newest v3 version contains no prompt nodes.
    """

    definition = _require_definition(db, workflow_definition_id)
    versions = db.scalars(
        select(WorkflowVersion)
        .where(WorkflowVersion.workflow_definition_id == definition.id)
        .order_by(WorkflowVersion.created_at.desc(), WorkflowVersion.id.desc())
    ).all()
    v3_version = next(
        (row for row in versions if row.workflow_schema_version == "workflow-v3"),
        None,
    )
    if v3_version is None:
        raise WorkflowWorkingCopyError(
            "workflow_editable_template_missing",
            "没有找到可编辑的评测机制版本",
            status_code=404,
        )
    v3_manifest = persisted_workflow_manifest(v3_version)
    v3_nodes = v3_manifest.get("nodes")
    if not isinstance(v3_nodes, list):
        raise WorkflowWorkingCopyError(
            "workflow_editable_template_invalid",
            "评测机制节点数据损坏",
            status_code=409,
        )
    if any(
        isinstance(node, Mapping) and node.get("kind") == "prompt"
        for node in v3_nodes
    ):
        editable = deepcopy(v3_manifest)
        editable["nodes"] = [
            _operator_owned_node(node)
            for node in v3_nodes
            if isinstance(node, Mapping)
        ]
        return EditableWorkflowTemplate(editable, v3_version.id, False)

    v2_version = next(
        (
            row
            for row in versions
            if row.workflow_schema_version == "workflow-v2"
            and any(
                isinstance(node, Mapping) and node.get("kind") == "prompt"
                for node in persisted_workflow_manifest(row).get("nodes", [])
            )
        ),
        None,
    )
    if v2_version is None:
        editable = deepcopy(v3_manifest)
        editable["nodes"] = [
            _operator_owned_node(node)
            for node in v3_nodes
            if isinstance(node, Mapping)
        ]
        return EditableWorkflowTemplate(editable, v3_version.id, False)

    v2_manifest = persisted_workflow_manifest(v2_version)
    prompt_nodes = [
        _legacy_prompt_node(
            db,
            category_key=str(v3_manifest.get("category_key") or ""),
            node=node,
        )
        for node in v2_manifest.get("nodes", [])
        if isinstance(node, Mapping) and node.get("kind") == "prompt"
    ]
    prompt_by_asset = {
        str(node["config"].get("asset_key")): node for node in prompt_nodes
    }
    rule_by_type = {
        str(node.get("config", {}).get("module_type_key")): _operator_owned_node(node)
        for node in v3_nodes
        if isinstance(node, Mapping) and node.get("kind") == "rule_module"
    }
    missing_modules = sorted(set(MODULE_TYPE_KEYS) - set(rule_by_type))
    if missing_modules:
        raise WorkflowWorkingCopyError(
            "workflow_editable_template_modules_missing",
            f"历史评测机制缺少规则模块：{','.join(missing_modules)}",
            status_code=409,
        )
    upstream = next(
        (
            _operator_owned_node(node)
            for node in v3_nodes
            if isinstance(node, Mapping) and node.get("kind") == "upstream_connector"
        ),
        None,
    )
    downstream = next(
        (
            _operator_owned_node(node)
            for node in reversed(v3_nodes)
            if isinstance(node, Mapping) and node.get("kind") == "downstream_projection"
        ),
        None,
    )
    if upstream is None or downstream is None:
        raise WorkflowWorkingCopyError(
            "workflow_editable_template_boundary_missing",
            "历史评测机制缺少读取或输出步骤",
            status_code=409,
        )
    ordered_nodes = [upstream]
    prompt_a = prompt_by_asset.get("prompt_a")
    prompt_b = prompt_by_asset.get("prompt_b")
    if prompt_a is not None:
        ordered_nodes.append(prompt_a)
    ordered_nodes.extend(
        [rule_by_type["eligibility_redline"], rule_by_type["track_routing"]]
    )
    if prompt_b is not None:
        ordered_nodes.append(prompt_b)
    ordered_nodes.extend(
        [rule_by_type[key] for key in MODULE_TYPE_KEYS[2:]]
    )
    ordered_nodes.append(downstream)
    edges = [
        {"from": source["node_instance_id"], "to": target["node_instance_id"]}
        for source, target in zip(ordered_nodes, ordered_nodes[1:])
    ]
    previous = None
    for node in ordered_nodes:
        if node.get("kind") == "rule_module" and previous is not None:
            node["config"]["input_bindings"] = {
                "legacy_input": f"{previous}.output"
            }
        previous = node["node_instance_id"]
    editable = deepcopy(v3_manifest)
    editable.update(
        {
            "lane_key": str(v2_manifest.get("lane_key") or v3_manifest.get("lane_key")),
            "run_modes": deepcopy(v2_manifest.get("run_modes") or v3_manifest.get("run_modes")),
            "nodes": ordered_nodes,
            "edges": edges,
            "layout": {
                node["node_instance_id"]: {"x": 64 + index * 260, "y": 96}
                for index, node in enumerate(ordered_nodes)
            },
        }
    )
    return EditableWorkflowTemplate(editable, v3_version.id, True)


def save_workflow_working_copy(
    db: Session,
    *,
    workflow_definition_id: int,
    expected_lock_version: int,
    manifest: Mapping[str, Any],
    actor: str,
) -> WorkflowWorkingCopy:
    require_workflow_working_copy(db, workflow_definition_id)
    normalized = _require_v3_manifest(manifest)
    result = db.execute(
        update(WorkflowWorkingCopy)
        .where(
            WorkflowWorkingCopy.workflow_definition_id
            == workflow_definition_id,
            WorkflowWorkingCopy.lock_version == expected_lock_version,
        )
        .values(
            manifest_json=canonical_json(normalized),
            lock_version=WorkflowWorkingCopy.lock_version + 1,
            updated_by=actor,
            updated_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc),
        )
        .execution_options(synchronize_session=False)
    )
    if result.rowcount != 1:
        raise WorkflowWorkingCopyError(
            "workflow_working_copy_conflict",
            "工作流已被其他人更新，请刷新后重试",
            status_code=409,
        )
    db.expire_all()
    return require_workflow_working_copy(db, workflow_definition_id)


def _next_numeric_version(db: Session, definition_id: int) -> str:
    values = db.scalars(
        select(WorkflowVersion.version).where(
            WorkflowVersion.workflow_definition_id == definition_id
        )
    ).all()
    numeric = [int(value) for value in values if str(value).isdigit()]
    return str(max(numeric, default=0) + 1)


def create_immutable_evaluation_mechanism_version(
    db: Session,
    *,
    definition: WorkflowDefinition,
    version: str,
    manifest: Mapping[str, Any],
    created_by: str,
    enforce_authoring_policy: bool = False,
) -> WorkflowVersion:
    from .workflow_node_executors import (
        NodeExecutorError,
        assert_native_workflow_executable,
    )

    try:
        frozen = freeze_component_references(db, manifest)
        assert_native_workflow_executable(frozen)
        if enforce_authoring_policy:
            # 只拦运营发布的新机制版本；启动迁移回填历史修订时不加严。
            from .mechanism_authoring_policy import (
                workflow_authoring_policy_errors,
            )

            policy_errors = workflow_authoring_policy_errors(frozen)
            if policy_errors:
                code, _ = policy_errors[0]
                raise WorkflowWorkingCopyError(
                    code,
                    "；".join(message for _, message in policy_errors),
                    status_code=422,
                )
        row = create_workflow_version(
            db,
            definition=definition,
            version=version,
            manifest=frozen,
            created_by=created_by,
        )
    except WorkflowRegistryError as exc:
        raise WorkflowWorkingCopyError(
            exc.code, str(exc), status_code=exc.status_code
        ) from exc
    except NodeExecutorError as exc:
        raise WorkflowWorkingCopyError(
            exc.code, str(exc), status_code=409
        ) from exc
    row.status = "active"
    row.validation_report_json = canonical_json({"ok": True, "errors": []})
    db.flush()
    return row


def generate_evaluation_mechanism_version(
    db: Session,
    *,
    workflow_definition_id: int,
    expected_lock_version: int,
    created_by: str,
) -> WorkflowVersion:
    definition = _require_definition(db, workflow_definition_id)
    working = require_workflow_working_copy(db, workflow_definition_id)
    if working.lock_version != expected_lock_version:
        raise WorkflowWorkingCopyError(
            "workflow_working_copy_conflict",
            "工作流已被其他人更新，请刷新后重试",
            status_code=409,
        )
    manifest = json.loads(working.manifest_json)
    row = create_immutable_evaluation_mechanism_version(
        db,
        definition=definition,
        version=_next_numeric_version(db, definition.id),
        manifest=manifest,
        created_by=created_by,
        enforce_authoring_policy=True,
    )
    working.base_workflow_version_id = row.id
    db.flush()
    return row


def legacy_v2_to_v3_working_copy(
    manifest: Mapping[str, Any],
    *,
    prompt_version_ids: Mapping[str, int],
    rule_module_version_ids: Mapping[str, int],
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    if manifest.get("schema_version") != "workflow-v2":
        raise WorkflowWorkingCopyError(
            "legacy_workflow_schema_invalid",
            "只有 workflow-v2 可以转换为 workflow-v3 工作副本",
        )
    legacy_nodes = manifest.get("nodes")
    if not isinstance(legacy_nodes, list):
        raise WorkflowWorkingCopyError(
            "legacy_workflow_nodes_invalid", "旧工作流节点配置损坏"
        )
    converted_nodes: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    layout: dict[str, Any] = {}
    for index, raw in enumerate(legacy_nodes):
        if not isinstance(raw, Mapping):
            raise WorkflowWorkingCopyError(
                "legacy_workflow_node_invalid", f"旧工作流第 {index + 1} 个节点损坏"
            )
        node_key = raw.get("key")
        kind = raw.get("kind")
        if not isinstance(node_key, str) or not node_key:
            raise WorkflowWorkingCopyError(
                "legacy_workflow_node_identity_invalid", "旧工作流节点缺少稳定 key"
            )
        config = deepcopy(raw.get("config", {}))
        if not isinstance(config, dict):
            config = {}
        next_kind = kind
        if kind == "prompt":
            prompt_ref = config.pop("prompt_ref", None)
            prompt_version_id = prompt_version_ids.get(str(prompt_ref))
            if prompt_version_id is None:
                raise WorkflowWorkingCopyError(
                    "legacy_prompt_mapping_missing",
                    f"旧提示词引用尚未映射：{prompt_ref}",
                )
            config["prompt_version_id"] = prompt_version_id
        elif kind in {"rule_contract", "level_matcher"}:
            module_type_key = (
                "eligibility_redline"
                if kind == "rule_contract"
                else "score_level_matcher"
            )
            version_id = rule_module_version_ids.get(module_type_key)
            if version_id is None:
                raise WorkflowWorkingCopyError(
                    "legacy_rule_mapping_missing",
                    f"旧规则节点尚未映射：{module_type_key}",
                )
            next_kind = "rule_module"
            config = {
                "asset_key": module_type_key,
                "module_type_key": module_type_key,
                "rule_module_version_id": version_id,
                "input_bindings": {"legacy_input": "previous.output"},
                "scope": "legacy_projection",
            }
        position = raw.get("ui_position")
        if isinstance(position, Mapping):
            layout[node_key] = deepcopy(dict(position))
        converted_nodes.append(
            {
                "node_instance_id": node_key,
                "kind": next_kind,
                "display_name": raw.get("display_name") or node_key,
                "config": config,
                "input_schema": deepcopy(raw.get("input_schema", {"type": "object"})),
                "output_schema": deepcopy(raw.get("output_schema", {"type": "object"})),
                "error_policy": deepcopy(raw.get("error_policy", {"kind": "fail"})),
                "retry_policy": deepcopy(
                    raw.get(
                        "retry_policy",
                        {"kind": "fixed", "max_attempts": 1, "delay_seconds": 0},
                    )
                ),
                "evidence_fields": deepcopy(raw.get("evidence_fields", [])),
            }
        )
    converted = {
        "schema_version": "workflow-v3",
        "category_key": manifest.get("category_key"),
        "lane_key": manifest.get("lane_key"),
        "run_modes": deepcopy(manifest.get("run_modes", [])),
        "nodes": converted_nodes,
        "edges": deepcopy(manifest.get("edges", [])),
        "queue_class": manifest.get("queue_class"),
        "input_schema": deepcopy(manifest.get("input_schema", {"type": "object"})),
        "output_schema": deepcopy(manifest.get("output_schema", {"type": "object"})),
        "resource_policy": deepcopy(manifest.get("resource_policy", {})),
    }
    if layout:
        converted["layout"] = layout
    return converted

===== FILE backend/app/xlsx_writer.py =====
"""最小可用的 xlsx 生成器，支持在单元格里嵌入图片。

平台没有 openpyxl 之类的依赖，``label_export`` 早先手写了一份只能写文字的
生成器。存量回归导出要让运营对着缩略图逐张核对，必须把图片打进包里，因此
这里把 xlsx 的骨架抽成可复用的一份，并补上 drawing/media 那几个部件。

写出来的包按 ECMA-376 的部件顺序组织；worksheet 里 ``drawing`` 必须排在
``autoFilter`` 之后，顺序错了 Excel 会判定文件损坏。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import io
import re
import zipfile
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Any, Iterable, Sequence
from xml.sax.saxutils import escape

# 每像素 9525 EMU（English Metric Unit），Office 的固定换算。
EMU_PER_PIXEL = 9525
# 行高单位是磅；1 磅 ≈ 4/3 像素。
PIXELS_PER_POINT = 4 / 3

_INVALID_XML = re.compile(r"[\x00-\x08\x0b\x0c\x0e-\x1f]")
# 单元格文本以这些字符开头时，Excel 会当公式执行。
_FORMULA_PREFIXES = ("=", "+", "-", "@", "\t", "\r")

_MIME_EXTENSIONS = {
    "image/jpeg": "jpeg",
    "image/jpg": "jpeg",
    "image/png": "png",
    "image/gif": "gif",
    "image/webp": "webp",
}


class XlsxWriterError(ValueError):
    pass


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SheetImage:
    """锚在某个单元格左上角的图片。行列都是 1 起算。"""

    row: int
    column: int
    data: bytes
    mime_type: str
    width_px: int
    height_px: int
    offset_px: int = 2
