    def extension(self) -> str:
        ext = _MIME_EXTENSIONS.get(self.mime_type.lower())
        if ext is None:
            raise XlsxWriterError(f"不支持嵌入的图片类型：{self.mime_type}")
        return ext


@dataclass
class Sheet:
    name: str
    headers: Sequence[str]
    rows: Sequence[Sequence[Any]]
    column_widths: Sequence[float] = ()
    row_heights: dict[int, float] = field(default_factory=dict)
    images: Sequence[SheetImage] = ()
    freeze_header: bool = True
    auto_filter: bool = True


def spreadsheet_safe_text(value: Any) -> Any:
    """挡住 CSV/XLSX 公式注入：以危险字符开头的文本前置单引号。"""

    if isinstance(value, str) and value.startswith(_FORMULA_PREFIXES):
        return "'" + value
    return value


def _xml_text(value: Any) -> str:
    return escape(_INVALID_XML.sub("", str(value)))


def column_name(index: int) -> str:
    result = ""
    while index:
        index, remainder = divmod(index - 1, 26)
        result = chr(65 + remainder) + result
    return result


def _cell_xml(reference: str, value: Any, *, header: bool = False) -> str:
    style = ' s="1"' if header else ""
    if isinstance(value, (int, float)) and not isinstance(value, bool):
        return f'<c r="{reference}"{style}><v>{value}</v></c>'
    if value is None or value == "":
        return f'<c r="{reference}"{style}/>'
    return (
        f'<c r="{reference}" t="inlineStr"{style}><is><t xml:space="preserve">'
        f"{_xml_text(value)}</t></is></c>"
    )


def _sheet_xml(sheet: Sheet, *, has_drawing: bool) -> str:
    rendered = [tuple(sheet.headers), *[tuple(row) for row in sheet.rows]]
    row_xml: list[str] = []
    for row_index, row in enumerate(rendered, start=1):
        cells = "".join(
            _cell_xml(f"{column_name(col)}{row_index}", value, header=row_index == 1)
            for col, value in enumerate(row, start=1)
        )
        height = sheet.row_heights.get(row_index)
        attrs = f' ht="{height:g}" customHeight="1"' if height else ""
        row_xml.append(f'<row r="{row_index}"{attrs}>{cells}</row>')
    last_column = column_name(max(1, len(sheet.headers)))
    last_row = max(1, len(rendered))
    cols = "".join(
        f'<col min="{index}" max="{index}" width="{width:g}" customWidth="1"/>'
        for index, width in enumerate(sheet.column_widths, start=1)
    )
    panes = (
        '<sheetViews><sheetView workbookViewId="0">'
        '<pane ySplit="1" topLeftCell="A2" activePane="bottomLeft" state="frozen"/>'
        "</sheetView></sheetViews>"
        if sheet.freeze_header
        else '<sheetViews><sheetView workbookViewId="0"/></sheetViews>'
    )
    parts = [
        '<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" standalone="yes"?>',
        '<worksheet xmlns="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/spreadsheetml/2006/main" '
        'xmlns:r="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/officeDocument/2006/relationships">',
        f'<dimension ref="A1:{last_column}{last_row}"/>',
        panes,
        '<sheetFormatPr defaultRowHeight="18"/>',
        f"<cols>{cols}</cols>" if cols else "",
        f"<sheetData>{''.join(row_xml)}</sheetData>",
        f'<autoFilter ref="A1:{last_column}{last_row}"/>' if sheet.auto_filter else "",
        # drawing 必须排在 autoFilter 之后，顺序错了 Excel 判定文件损坏。
        '<drawing r:id="rId1"/>' if has_drawing else "",
        "</worksheet>",
    ]
    return "".join(parts)


def _drawing_xml(images: Sequence[SheetImage]) -> str:
    anchors: list[str] = []
    for index, image in enumerate(images, start=1):
        off = image.offset_px * EMU_PER_PIXEL
        anchors.append(
            "<xdr:oneCellAnchor>"
            f"<xdr:from><xdr:col>{image.column - 1}</xdr:col><xdr:colOff>{off}</xdr:colOff>"
            f"<xdr:row>{image.row - 1}</xdr:row><xdr:rowOff>{off}</xdr:rowOff></xdr:from>"
            f'<xdr:ext cx="{image.width_px * EMU_PER_PIXEL}" '
            f'cy="{image.height_px * EMU_PER_PIXEL}"/>'
            "<xdr:pic><xdr:nvPicPr>"
            f'<xdr:cNvPr id="{index}" name="image{index}"/><xdr:cNvPicPr/>'
            "</xdr:nvPicPr>"
            f'<xdr:blipFill><a:blip r:embed="rId{index}"/><a:stretch><a:fillRect/>'
            "</a:stretch></xdr:blipFill>"
            "<xdr:spPr><a:xfrm><a:off x=\"0\" y=\"0\"/>"
            f'<a:ext cx="{image.width_px * EMU_PER_PIXEL}" '
            f'cy="{image.height_px * EMU_PER_PIXEL}"/></a:xfrm>'
            '<a:prstGeom prst="rect"><a:avLst/></a:prstGeom></xdr:spPr>'
            "</xdr:pic><xdr:clientData/></xdr:oneCellAnchor>"
        )
    return (
        '<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" standalone="yes"?>'
        '<xdr:wsDr xmlns:xdr="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/drawingml/2006/spreadsheetDrawing" '
        'xmlns:a="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/drawingml/2006/main" '
        'xmlns:r="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/officeDocument/2006/relationships">'
        + "".join(anchors)
        + "</xdr:wsDr>"
    )


_STYLES_XML = (
    '<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" standalone="yes"?>'
    '<styleSheet xmlns="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/spreadsheetml/2006/main">'
    '<fonts count="2"><font><sz val="11"/><name val="Arial"/></font>'
    '<font><b/><color rgb="FFFFFFFF"/><sz val="11"/><name val="Arial"/></font></fonts>'
    '<fills count="3"><fill><patternFill patternType="none"/></fill>'
    '<fill><patternFill patternType="gray125"/></fill>'
    '<fill><patternFill patternType="solid"><fgColor rgb="FF304238"/>'
    '<bgColor indexed="64"/></patternFill></fill></fills>'
    '<borders count="1"><border><left/><right/><top/><bottom/><diagonal/></border></borders>'
    '<cellStyleXfs count="1"><xf numFmtId="0" fontId="0" fillId="0" borderId="0"/></cellStyleXfs>'
    '<cellXfs count="2"><xf numFmtId="0" fontId="0" fillId="0" borderId="0" xfId="0"/>'
    '<xf numFmtId="0" fontId="1" fillId="2" borderId="0" xfId="0" '
    'applyFont="1" applyFill="1" applyAlignment="1"><alignment vertical="top" wrapText="1"/></xf>'
    '</cellXfs>'
    '<cellStyles count="1"><cellStyle name="Normal" xfId="0" builtinId="0"/></cellStyles>'
    "</styleSheet>"
)


def build_xlsx(sheets: Sequence[Sheet]) -> bytes:
    if not sheets:
        raise XlsxWriterError("至少需要一个工作表")
    output = io.BytesIO()
    media_extensions: set[str] = set()
    for sheet in sheets:
        for image in sheet.images:
            media_extensions.add(image.extension())

    with zipfile.ZipFile(output, "w", zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as book:
        overrides = "".join(
            f'<Override PartName="/xl/worksheets/sheet{index}.xml" '
            'ContentType="application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.'
            'spreadsheetml.worksheet+xml"/>'
            for index in range(1, len(sheets) + 1)
        ) + "".join(
            f'<Override PartName="/xl/drawings/drawing{index}.xml" '
            'ContentType="application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.drawing+xml"/>'
            for index, sheet in enumerate(sheets, start=1)
            if sheet.images
        )
        defaults = "".join(
            f'<Default Extension="{ext}" ContentType="image/{"jpeg" if ext == "jpeg" else ext}"/>'
            for ext in sorted(media_extensions)
        )
        book.writestr(
            "[Content_Types].xml",
            '<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" standalone="yes"?>'
            '<Types xmlns="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/package/2006/content-types">'
            '<Default Extension="rels" ContentType="application/vnd.openxmlformats-package.relationships+xml"/>'
            '<Default Extension="xml" ContentType="application/xml"/>'
            f"{defaults}"
            '<Override PartName="/xl/workbook.xml" ContentType="application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet.main+xml"/>'
            f"{overrides}"
            '<Override PartName="/xl/styles.xml" ContentType="application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.styles+xml"/>'
            "</Types>",
        )
        book.writestr(
            "_rels/.rels",
            '<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" standalone="yes"?>'
            '<Relationships xmlns="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/package/2006/relationships">'
            '<Relationship Id="rId1" Type="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/officeDocument/2006/relationships/officeDocument" Target="xl/workbook.xml"/>'
            "</Relationships>",
        )
        sheet_tags = "".join(
            f'<sheet name="{_xml_text(sheet.name)}" sheetId="{index}" r:id="rId{index}"/>'
            for index, sheet in enumerate(sheets, start=1)
        )
        book.writestr(
            "xl/workbook.xml",
            '<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" standalone="yes"?>'
            '<workbook xmlns="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/spreadsheetml/2006/main" '
            'xmlns:r="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/officeDocument/2006/relationships">'
            f"<sheets>{sheet_tags}</sheets></workbook>",
        )
        sheet_rels = "".join(
            f'<Relationship Id="rId{index}" Type="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/officeDocument/2006/relationships/worksheet" Target="worksheets/sheet{index}.xml"/>'
            for index in range(1, len(sheets) + 1)
        )
        book.writestr(
            "xl/_rels/workbook.xml.rels",
            '<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" standalone="yes"?>'
            '<Relationships xmlns="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/package/2006/relationships">'
            f"{sheet_rels}"
            f'<Relationship Id="rId{len(sheets) + 1}" Type="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/officeDocument/2006/relationships/styles" Target="styles.xml"/>'
            "</Relationships>",
        )
        book.writestr("xl/styles.xml", _STYLES_XML)

        media_index = 0
        for index, sheet in enumerate(sheets, start=1):
            book.writestr(
                f"xl/worksheets/sheet{index}.xml",
                _sheet_xml(sheet, has_drawing=bool(sheet.images)),
            )
            if not sheet.images:
                continue
            book.writestr(
                f"xl/worksheets/_rels/sheet{index}.xml.rels",
                '<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" standalone="yes"?>'
                '<Relationships xmlns="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/package/2006/relationships">'
                f'<Relationship Id="rId1" Type="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/officeDocument/2006/relationships/drawing" Target="../drawings/drawing{index}.xml"/>'
                "</Relationships>",
            )
            book.writestr(f"xl/drawings/drawing{index}.xml", _drawing_xml(sheet.images))
            rels: list[str] = []
            for position, image in enumerate(sheet.images, start=1):
                media_index += 1
                target = f"image{media_index}.{image.extension()}"
                book.writestr(f"xl/media/{target}", image.data)
                rels.append(
                    f'<Relationship Id="rId{position}" '
                    'Type="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/officeDocument/2006/relationships/image" '
                    f'Target="../media/{target}"/>'
                )
            book.writestr(
                f"xl/drawings/_rels/drawing{index}.xml.rels",
                '<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" standalone="yes"?>'
                '<Relationships xmlns="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/package/2006/relationships">'
                + "".join(rels)
                + "</Relationships>",
            )
    return output.getvalue()

===== FILE backend/tests/conftest.py =====
"""Ensure ``backend/`` is importable as the package root for tests.

The existing convention runs pytest from ``backend/`` (see AGENTS.md), which
puts ``app`` on ``sys.path`` implicitly.  When pytest is instead invoked from
the repository root (for example ``python3 -m pytest -q backend/tests``), the
default ``prepend`` import mode only adds ``backend/tests`` to ``sys.path`` and
``import app`` would fail.  Adding the tests' parent directory here makes the
suite import-stable under both working directories without changing any test
behaviour; when ``backend/`` is already present the insert is a no-op.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import os
import re
import sys
import uuid
from pathlib import Path

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, event, text
from sqlalchemy.engine import Engine, make_url

_BACKEND_ROOT = str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1])
if _BACKEND_ROOT not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, _BACKEND_ROOT)
_REPOSITORY_ROOT = str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2])
if _REPOSITORY_ROOT not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, _REPOSITORY_ROOT)

from app.database_capabilities import assert_mysql_runtime_contract  # noqa: E402
from app.database import configure_mysql_dbapi_connection  # noqa: E402
from app.mysql_integration import (  # noqa: E402
    mysql_database_url_from_references,
)


def _configure_mysql_test_connection(
    dbapi_connection: object,
    _connection_record: object,
) -> None:
    configure_mysql_dbapi_connection(dbapi_connection)


@pytest.fixture
def pg_engine() -> Engine:
    """Create one disposable PostgreSQL database when explicitly configured."""
    configured = os.getenv("POSTGRES_TEST_URL", "").strip()
    if not configured:
        pytest.skip("POSTGRES_TEST_URL 未配置")
    maintenance_url = make_url(configured)
    if maintenance_url.get_backend_name() != "postgresql":
        pytest.fail("POSTGRES_TEST_URL 必须使用 PostgreSQL")
    if maintenance_url.database not in {"postgres", "template1"}:
        pytest.fail("POSTGRES_TEST_URL 必须指向 postgres 或 template1 维护库")

    database_name = f"labelsystem_test_{uuid.uuid4().hex}"
    if re.fullmatch(r"labelsystem_test_[0-9a-f]{32}", database_name) is None:
        pytest.fail("临时 PostgreSQL 数据库名不安全")
    admin_engine = create_engine(
        maintenance_url,
        isolation_level="AUTOCOMMIT",
        pool_pre_ping=True,
    )
    test_engine: Engine | None = None
    try:
        with admin_engine.connect() as connection:
            connection.exec_driver_sql(f'CREATE DATABASE "{database_name}"')
        test_engine = create_engine(
            maintenance_url.set(database=database_name),
            pool_pre_ping=True,
        )
        yield test_engine
    finally:
        if test_engine is not None:
            test_engine.dispose()
        with admin_engine.connect() as connection:
            connection.execute(
                text(
                    "SELECT pg_terminate_backend(pid) FROM pg_stat_activity "
                    "WHERE datname = :database_name AND pid <> pg_backend_pid()"
                ),
                {"database_name": database_name},
            )
            connection.exec_driver_sql(f'DROP DATABASE IF EXISTS "{database_name}"')
        admin_engine.dispose()


@pytest.fixture
def mysql_engine() -> Engine:
    """Create one guarded disposable MySQL schema when explicitly configured."""
    configured = os.getenv("MYSQL_TEST_CONFIG", "").strip()
    if not configured:
        pytest.skip("MYSQL_TEST_CONFIG 未配置")
    maintenance_url = mysql_database_url_from_references(
        Path(configured),
        role="test",
        database=None,
    )
    if (
        maintenance_url.get_backend_name() != "mysql"
        or maintenance_url.get_driver_name() != "pymysql"
    ):
        pytest.fail("MySQL 测试配置必须解析为 mysql+pymysql")
    if maintenance_url.database not in {None, "mysql"}:
        pytest.fail("MySQL 测试配置必须不选默认库或指向 mysql 维护库")

    database_name = f"labelsystem_test_{uuid.uuid4().hex}"
    if re.fullmatch(r"labelsystem_test_[0-9a-f]{32}", database_name) is None:
        pytest.fail("临时 MySQL 数据库名不安全")
    quoted_database = f"`{database_name}`"
    admin_engine = create_engine(
        maintenance_url,
        isolation_level="AUTOCOMMIT",
        pool_pre_ping=True,
    )
    event.listen(admin_engine, "connect", _configure_mysql_test_connection)
    test_engine: Engine | None = None
    created = False
    try:
        with admin_engine.connect() as connection:
            health = assert_mysql_runtime_contract(connection)
            if bool(getattr(health, "read_only", False)):
                pytest.fail("MySQL 测试实例处于只读状态")
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                f"CREATE DATABASE {quoted_database} CHARACTER SET utf8mb4 "
                "COLLATE utf8mb4_0900_as_cs"
            )
            created = True
        test_engine = create_engine(
            maintenance_url.set(database=database_name),
            pool_pre_ping=True,
            pool_recycle=900,
            pool_reset_on_return="rollback",
        )
        event.listen(test_engine, "connect", _configure_mysql_test_connection)
        yield test_engine
    finally:
        if test_engine is not None:
            test_engine.dispose()
        if created:
            with admin_engine.connect() as connection:
                connection.exec_driver_sql(
                    f"DROP DATABASE IF EXISTS {quoted_database}"
                )
        admin_engine.dispose()

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
from typing import Any

from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.audit import canonical_json
from app.connector_contracts import create_connector_contract_version
from app.evaluation_component_catalog import (
    MODULE_TYPE_KEYS,
    canonical_component_hash,
)
from app.evaluation_mechanism_migration import split_legacy_revision
from app.models import (
    CategoryEvaluationV3Config,
    EvaluationCategoryProfile,
    ModelConfig,
    ModelRegistryEntry,
    PromptVersion,
    RuleModuleVersion,
    WorkflowNodeAsset,
    WorkflowNodeAssetBinding,
    WorkflowDefinition,
    WorkflowVersion,
)
from app.workflow_composer_runtime import ensure_composer_fixture_version
from app.vision_input_policy import SEED_20_PROFILE
from tests.v3_contract_fixtures import add_active_v3_contract


def _node(
    node_instance_id: str,
    kind: str,
    display_name: str,
    config: dict[str, Any],
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "node_instance_id": node_instance_id,
        "kind": kind,
        "display_name": display_name,
        "config": config,
        "input_schema": {"type": "object"},
        "output_schema": {"type": "object"},
        "error_policy": {"kind": "fail"},
        "retry_policy": {
            "kind": "fixed",
            "max_attempts": 2,
            "delay_seconds": 0,
        },
        "evidence_fields": ["request_id"],
    }


def incremental_manifest(
    category_key: str,
    *,
    prompt_a: PromptVersion,
    prompt_b: PromptVersion,
    model: ModelRegistryEntry,
    module_contents: dict[str, dict[str, Any]],
    run_modes: list[str] | None = None,
    lane_key: str = "incremental_evaluation",
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    def prompt_node(prompt: PromptVersion) -> dict[str, Any]:
        prompt_content = {
            "stage": prompt.stage,
            "system_prompt": prompt.system_prompt,
            "user_prompt": prompt.user_prompt,
            "rubric_version": prompt.rubric_version,
        }
        return _node(
            f"prompt_{prompt.stage.lower()}",
            "prompt",
            f"提示词 {prompt.stage}",
            {
                "asset_key": f"prompt_{prompt.stage.lower()}",
                "prompt_version_id": prompt.id,
                "model_registry_id": model.id,
                "max_tokens": min(model.max_tokens, 2048),
                "thinking_level": "high",
                "component_snapshot": {
                    "kind": "prompt",
                    "version_id": prompt.id,
                    "version": prompt.version,
                    "content_hash": canonical_component_hash(prompt_content),
                    "content": prompt_content,
                },
                "model_snapshot": {
                    "model_registry_id": model.id,
                    "name": model.name,
                    "model_id": model.model_id,
                },
            },
        )

    def module_node(module_type_key: str, index: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return _node(
            f"{module_type_key}_{index}",
            "rule_module",
            module_type_key,
            {
                "asset_key": module_type_key,
                "module_type_key": module_type_key,
                "rule_module_version_id": 100 + index,
                "input_bindings": {"previous": "previous.output"},
                "scope": "incremental",
                "component_snapshot": {
                    "kind": "rule_module",
                    "version_id": 100 + index,
                    "version": "1",
                    "module_type_key": module_type_key,
                    "content_hash": "b" * 64,
                    "content_json": canonical_json(
                        module_contents[module_type_key]
                    ),
                },
            },
        )

    nodes = [
        _node(
            "upstream",
            "upstream_connector",
            "读取增量素材",
            {"connector_key": f"{category_key}-input"},
        ),
        prompt_node(prompt_a),
        module_node("eligibility_redline", 1),
        module_node("track_routing", 2),
        prompt_node(prompt_b),
        *[
            module_node(module_type_key, index)
            for index, module_type_key in enumerate(MODULE_TYPE_KEYS[2:], start=3)
        ],
        _node(
            "downstream",
            "downstream_projection",
            "写入增量结果",
            {"connector_key": f"{category_key}-output"},
        ),
    ]
    return {
        "schema_version": "workflow-v3",
        "category_key": category_key,
        "lane_key": lane_key,
        "run_modes": (
            run_modes
            if run_modes is not None
            else ["evaluation", "stock_regression", "incremental"]
        ),
        "nodes": nodes,
        "edges": [
            {
                "from": source["node_instance_id"],
                "to": target["node_instance_id"],
            }
            for source, target in zip(nodes, nodes[1:])
        ],
        "queue_class": "production_batch",
        "input_schema": {"type": "object"},
        "output_schema": {"type": "object"},
        "resource_policy": {"max_parallel": 4},
    }


def seed_incremental_versions(
    db: Session,
    *,
    category_key: str = "space_image",
    count: int = 2,
    run_modes: list[str] | None = None,
    connector_overrides: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] | None = None,
) -> list[WorkflowVersion]:
    for direction in ("upstream", "downstream"):
        suffix = "input" if direction == "upstream" else "output"
        create_connector_contract_version(
            db,
            contract_key=f"{category_key}-{suffix}",
            direction=direction,
            category_key=category_key,
            logical_locator=f"logical.{category_key}.{suffix}",
            adapter_key=(connector_overrides or {}).get(direction, {}).get("adapter_key", "api-json-v1"),
            schema_fingerprint=(connector_overrides or {}).get(direction, {}).get("schema_fingerprint", hashlib.sha256(
                f"{category_key}:{direction}:fixture-v1".encode("utf-8")
            ).hexdigest()),
            field_mapping={"content_id": "content_id"},
            cursor_contract=(
                {
                    "field": "sequence",
                    "kind": "integer",
                    "ordering": "ascending",
                    "out_of_order": "delay",
                }
                if direction == "upstream"
                else {}
            ),
            idempotency_contract={
                "field": (
                    "event_id"
                    if direction == "upstream"
                    else "business_idempotency_key"
                )
            },
            batch_size=100,
            timeout_seconds=30,
            owner="test",
            status="active",
            created_by="test",
        )
    profile = db.scalar(
        select(EvaluationCategoryProfile).where(
            EvaluationCategoryProfile.category_key == category_key
        )
    )
    if profile is None:
        profile = EvaluationCategoryProfile(
            category_key=category_key,
            display_name=f"{category_key} 测试类目",
            status="active",
            allowed_mime_types_json='["image/jpeg"]',
            preprocess_config_json="{}",
            pipeline_config_json="{}",
            created_by="test",
        )
        db.add(profile)
        db.flush()

    active_contract = db.scalar(
        select(CategoryEvaluationV3Config).where(
            CategoryEvaluationV3Config.category_key == category_key,
            CategoryEvaluationV3Config.status == "active",
        )
    )
    if active_contract is None:
        artifacts = add_active_v3_contract(db, category_key)
    else:
        artifacts = {
            "contract": json.loads(active_contract.contract_json),
            "classification_map": json.loads(
                active_contract.classification_map_json
            ),
            "subcategory_dimensions": json.loads(
                active_contract.subcategory_dimensions_json
            ),
        }
    module_slices = split_legacy_revision(
        {
            "category_key": category_key,
            "revision": 1,
            "contract_hash": "c" * 64,
            **artifacts,
        }
    )
    module_contents = {
        key: dict(module_slices[key]) for key in MODULE_TYPE_KEYS
    }

    source_model = ModelConfig(
        name=f"{category_key} 增量模型",
        provider="fixture",
        base_url="https://fixture.invalid",
        api_path="/dry-run",
        model_id=f"{category_key}-incremental-model",
        max_tokens=4096,
        thinking_mode="high",
        structured_output=True,
        active=True,
        vision_capability_profile_json=canonical_json(dict(SEED_20_PROFILE)),
    )
    prompt_a = PromptVersion(
        category_key=category_key,
        pipeline_scope="shared",
        stage="A",
        name="提示词 A",
        version="incremental-a-v1",
        system_prompt="A",
        user_prompt="",
        rubric_version="incremental-rubric-v1",
        status="published",
        created_by="test",
    )
    prompt_b = PromptVersion(
        category_key=category_key,
        pipeline_scope="shared",
        stage="B",
        name="提示词 B",
        version="incremental-b-v1",
        system_prompt="B",
        user_prompt="",
        rubric_version="incremental-rubric-v1",
        status="published",
        created_by="test",
    )
    db.add_all([source_model, prompt_a, prompt_b])
    db.flush()
    model = ModelRegistryEntry(
        role="main",
        name=source_model.name,
        provider=source_model.provider,
        protocol=source_model.protocol,
        base_url=source_model.base_url,
        api_path=source_model.api_path,
        model_id=source_model.model_id,
        max_tokens=source_model.max_tokens,
        thinking_mode=source_model.thinking_mode,
        structured_output=source_model.structured_output,
        active=True,
        vision_capability_profile_json=canonical_json(dict(SEED_20_PROFILE)),
        source_model_config_id=source_model.id,
        created_by="test",
    )
    db.add(model)
    db.flush()
    for kind in (
        "upstream_connector",
        "prompt",
        "rule_module",
        "downstream_projection",
    ):
        ensure_composer_fixture_version(db, kind)

    definition = db.scalar(
        select(WorkflowDefinition).where(
            WorkflowDefinition.workflow_key == f"{category_key}.incremental"
        )
    )
    if definition is None:
        definition = WorkflowDefinition(
            workflow_key=f"{category_key}.incremental",
            name=f"{category_key} 增量评测",
            description="增量机制测试工作流",
            owner="test",
            allowed_categories_json=f'["{category_key}"]',
            status="active",
            created_by="test",
        )
        db.add(definition)
        db.flush()
    versions: list[WorkflowVersion] = []
    for number in range(1, count + 1):
        manifest = incremental_manifest(
            category_key,
            prompt_a=prompt_a,
            prompt_b=prompt_b,
            model=model,
            module_contents=module_contents,
            run_modes=run_modes,
            lane_key=f"incremental_{number}",
        )
        # Managed native validation requires actual component assets/versions,
        # not only the frozen JSON used by the former legacy projection gate.
        for node in manifest["nodes"]:
            if node["kind"] not in {"prompt", "rule_module"}:
                continue
            config = node["config"]
            asset = db.scalar(select(WorkflowNodeAsset).where(
                WorkflowNodeAsset.category_key == category_key,
                WorkflowNodeAsset.asset_key == config["asset_key"],
            ))
            if asset is None:
                asset = WorkflowNodeAsset(
                    category_key=category_key, asset_key=config["asset_key"],
                    kind=node["kind"], display_name=node["display_name"],
                    module_type_key=config.get("module_type_key"), created_by="test",
                )
                db.add(asset)
                db.flush()
                if node["kind"] == "prompt":
                    db.add(WorkflowNodeAssetBinding(
                        asset_id=asset.id, prompt_version_id=config["prompt_version_id"],
                        created_by="test",
                    ))
                else:
                    snapshot = config["component_snapshot"]
                    db.add(RuleModuleVersion(
                        asset_id=asset.id,
                        version=1, module_type_key=config["module_type_key"],
                        content_json=snapshot["content_json"],
                        content_hash=snapshot["content_hash"],
                        source_working_copy_revision=1, created_by="test",
                    ))
            if node["kind"] == "rule_module":
                db.flush()
                module = db.scalar(select(RuleModuleVersion).where(
                    RuleModuleVersion.asset_id == asset.id,
                ))
                config["rule_module_version_id"] = module.id
                config["component_snapshot"]["version_id"] = module.id
        db.flush()
        digest = hashlib.sha256(
            canonical_json(manifest).encode("utf-8")
        ).hexdigest()
        version = WorkflowVersion(
            workflow_definition_id=definition.id,
            version=str(number),
            status="active",
            workflow_schema_version="workflow-v3",
            step_manifest_json=canonical_json(manifest["nodes"]),
            edge_manifest_json=canonical_json(manifest["edges"]),
            input_schema_json=canonical_json(manifest["input_schema"]),
            output_schema_json=canonical_json(manifest["output_schema"]),
            resource_policy_json=canonical_json(
                {
                    **manifest["resource_policy"],
                    "queue_class": manifest["queue_class"],
                    "category_key": category_key,
                    "lane_key": manifest["lane_key"],
                    "run_modes": manifest["run_modes"],
                }
            ),
            canonical_hash=digest,
            validation_report_json=canonical_json(
                {"ok": True, "errors": []}
            ),
            created_by="test",
        )
        db.add(version)
        db.flush()
        versions.append(version)
    return versions

===== FILE backend/tests/test_account_permissions.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

from sqlalchemy import create_engine
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.authz import has_permission
from app.database import Base
from app.models import User


def _user(role: str) -> User:
    return User(username=role, password_hash="x", display_name=role, role=role, is_admin=role == "admin")


def test_role_permission_matrix() -> None:
    assert has_permission(_user("admin"), "models:write")
    assert has_permission(_user("admin"), "incremental_mechanisms:activate")
    for role in ("category_manager", "product_rd"):
        for permission in ("reviews:write", "regressions:run", "production:rerun"):
            assert has_permission(_user(role), permission)
        assert not has_permission(_user(role), "models:write")
        assert not has_permission(_user(role), "incremental_mechanisms:activate")
    assert has_permission(_user("product_rd"), "evaluation_components:write")
    assert has_permission(_user("product_rd"), "evaluation_components:version")
    assert not has_permission(_user("category_manager"), "drafts:write")
    for role in ("viewer", "manager", "reviewer", "analyst", "unknown"):
        assert has_permission(_user(role), "models:read")
        assert has_permission(_user(role), "results:export")
        for permission in ("reviews:write", "models:write", "assets:write", "regressions:run", "drafts:write"):
            assert not has_permission(_user(role), permission)


def test_user_role_persists() -> None:
    engine = create_engine("sqlite:///:memory:")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    with Session(engine) as db:
        db.add(_user("manager"))
        db.commit()
        persisted = db.query(User).one()
        assert persisted.role == "manager"
        assert not has_permission(persisted, "automation:write")

===== FILE backend/tests/test_adaptive_admission.py =====
import asyncio
import pytest


def test_capacity_backlog_excludes_paused_and_terminal_materials(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    from sqlalchemy.orm import Session, sessionmaker
    from app import adaptive_admission, database
    from app.models import ProductionRun
    from test_workflow_runtime import _engine, _seed_active_workflow
    from test_runtime_long_tail_scheduling import ready
    engine = _engine(tmp_path)
    with Session(engine) as db:
        version = _seed_active_workflow(db)
        for index, status in enumerate(('queued','paused','failed','succeeded')):
            attempt, _ = ready(db, version, f'backlog-{index}')
            db.get(ProductionRun, attempt.run_id).status = status
        db.commit()
    monkeypatch.setattr(database,'SessionLocal',sessionmaker(bind=engine))
    assert adaptive_admission._queued_count() == 1
    engine.dispose()


def test_growth_requires_fresh_evidence_and_stays_inside_validated_ceiling():
    from app.adaptive_admission import AdmissionPolicy, Sample
    p = AdmissionPolicy(ceiling=48, initial=8)
    assert p.observe(None) == 8
    sample = Sample(queued=100, completed=20, cpu_percent=20, memory_available_mb=6000, phase_seconds=.02)
    for n in range(10): p.observe(Sample(100, 20 * (n + 1), 20, 6000, .02))
    assert p.limit == 48
    assert p.observe(Sample(100, 20, 95, 6000, .02)) < 48


def test_pressure_reduces_and_cooldown_prevents_immediate_regrowth():
    from app.adaptive_admission import AdmissionPolicy, Sample
    p = AdmissionPolicy(ceiling=96, initial=24)
    assert p.observe(Sample(100, 20, 30, 500, .02)) == 12
    assert p.observe(Sample(100, 20, 20, 6000, .02)) == 12
    assert p.observe(Sample(100, 20, 20, 6000, .02)) == 12
    assert p.observe(Sample(100, 20, 20, 6000, .02)) > 12


def test_short_completion_windows_do_not_freeze_healthy_long_wait_capacity():
    from app.adaptive_admission import AdmissionPolicy, Sample
    p = AdmissionPolicy(ceiling=96, initial=8)
    sample = Sample(100, 20, 20, 6000, .02)
    assert p.observe(sample) == 16
    assert p.observe(sample) > 16
    q = AdmissionPolicy(ceiling=96, initial=8)
    assert q.observe(Sample(100, 0, 20, 6000, .02)) == 16


def test_lowering_gate_drains_existing_work_without_cancelling():
    from app.adaptive_admission import AdmissionGate
    async def scenario():
        gate = AdmissionGate(2)
        release = asyncio.Event()
        entered=[]
        async def job(i):
            async with gate:
                entered.append(i)
                await release.wait()
        a,b = asyncio.create_task(job(1)),asyncio.create_task(job(2))
        while len(entered)<2: await asyncio.sleep(0)
        await gate.resize(1)
        c=asyncio.create_task(job(3))
        await asyncio.sleep(.01)
        assert entered == [1,2]
        assert not a.cancelled() and not b.cancelled()
        release.set()
        await asyncio.gather(a,b,c)
        assert entered == [1,2,3]
    asyncio.run(scenario())


def test_runtime_accepts_bounded_high_io_slots():
    from app.bounded_runtime import BoundedRuntime
    async def scenario():
        async with BoundedRuntime(slots=768): pass
        with pytest.raises(ValueError): BoundedRuntime(slots=4097)
    asyncio.run(scenario())


def test_consumer_uses_dynamic_admission_for_actual_work():
    from app.adaptive_admission import AdmissionGate
    from app.async_runtime_worker import consume
    async def scenario():
        live = peak = done = 0
        running = True
        gate=AdmissionGate(2)
        async def step():
            nonlocal live, peak, done, running
            live += 1
            peak=max(peak,live)
            await asyncio.sleep(.01)
            live -= 1
            done += 1
            if done >= 4: running=False
            return True
        await consume(step,slots=24,should_continue=lambda:running,poll_seconds=.01,admission=gate)
        assert peak == 2
        assert done >= 4
    asyncio.run(scenario())


def test_provider_latency_and_errors_do_not_multiply_local_capacity_cuts():
    from app.adaptive_admission import AdmissionPolicy, Sample
    p=AdmissionPolicy(ceiling=96,initial=8)
    assert p.observe(Sample(100,20,20,6000,.02))==16
    assert p.observe(Sample(100,0,20,6000,.02,provider_error_rate=1)) > 16
    # Supplier pacing owns errors; local pressure still drains admission.
    previous = p.limit
    assert p.observe(Sample(100,0,95,6000,.02,provider_error_rate=1)) < previous


def test_idle_polling_is_bounded_by_admitted_population():
    from app.adaptive_admission import AdmissionGate
    from app.async_runtime_worker import consume
    async def scenario():
        calls=0
        loop=asyncio.get_running_loop()
        end=loop.time()+.06
        async def step():
            nonlocal calls
            calls+=1
            return False
        await consume(step, slots=100, should_continue=lambda:loop.time()<end,
                      poll_seconds=.01, admission=AdmissionGate(1))
        assert calls <= 8
    asyncio.run(scenario())


def test_invalid_sample_cannot_increase_admission():
    from app.adaptive_admission import AdmissionPolicy, Sample
    for sample in [Sample(100,-1,10,6000,.1), Sample(100,20,-1,6000,.1),
                   Sample(100,20,10,6000,-1), Sample(100,20,10,6000,.1,fresh=False)]:
        p=AdmissionPolicy(ceiling=48,initial=8)
        assert p.observe(sample)==8


def test_sampler_rejects_slow_database_observation(monkeypatch):
    from app import adaptive_admission as module
    sampler=module.CapacitySampler()
    sampler._cpu=[0]*8
    monkeypatch.setattr(module,'_resource_snapshot',lambda:([10,0,0,90,0,0,0,0],6000))
    monkeypatch.setattr(module,'_queued_count',lambda:100)
    times=iter([0.,6.])
    monkeypatch.setattr(module.time,'monotonic',lambda:next(times))
    sample=sampler.collect(completed=10,phase_seconds=.01)
    assert not sample.fresh
    assert module.AdmissionPolicy(ceiling=96,initial=8).observe(sample)==8


def test_runtime_counters_exclude_claim_ticks_without_provider():
    from app.bounded_runtime import BoundedRuntime,ProviderWait
    async def scenario():
        async with BoundedRuntime(slots=2) as runtime:
            def no_provider():
                if False: yield
                return True
            async def call(): return 'returned'
            def provider():
                yield ProviderWait(call,1)
                return True
            for _ in range(5): await runtime.run(no_provider())
            await runtime.run(provider())
            assert runtime.provider_completed==1
            assert runtime.provider_errors==0
    asyncio.run(scenario())


def test_gated_database_errors_keep_poll_backoff(caplog):
    from app.adaptive_admission import AdmissionGate
    from app.async_runtime_worker import consume
    async def scenario():
        calls=0
        loop=asyncio.get_running_loop()
        end=loop.time()+.035
        async def step():
            nonlocal calls
            calls+=1
            raise RuntimeError('synthetic DB failure')
        await consume(step,slots=20,should_continue=lambda:loop.time()<end,
                      poll_seconds=.01,admission=AdmissionGate(1))
        assert calls<=5
    asyncio.run(scenario())


def test_worker_loop_connects_provider_counters_to_real_gate(monkeypatch):
    from app import async_runtime_worker as worker, database, optimization_automation, workflow_fixture_executor
    from app.adaptive_admission import Sample, AdmissionGate
    from app.bounded_runtime import ProviderWait
    observations=[]
    resized=[]
    class Session:
        def __enter__(self): return self
        def __exit__(self,*args): pass
        def commit(self): pass
    class Sampler:
        def collect(self,**values):
            observations.append(values)
            return Sample(100,values['completed'],95,6000,.01)
    async def process(worker_id, *, runtime):
        async def call():
            await asyncio.sleep(.005)
            return True
        def operation():
            yield ProviderWait(call,1)
            return True
        return await runtime.run(operation())
    original=AdmissionGate.resize
    async def resize(self,limit):
        resized.append(limit)
        await original(self,limit)
    monkeypatch.setattr(database,'SessionLocal',Session)
    monkeypatch.setattr(optimization_automation,'record_automation_worker_status',lambda *a,**k:None)
    monkeypatch.setattr(workflow_fixture_executor,'process_runtime_step_async',process)
    monkeypatch.setattr(worker,'CapacitySampler',Sampler)
    monkeypatch.setattr(AdmissionGate,'resize',resize)
    monkeypatch.setenv('WORKER_RUNTIME_AUTOTUNE','1')
    monkeypatch.setenv('WORKER_RUNTIME_VALIDATED_SLOTS','24')
    async def scenario():
        # 2026-09-17: a 0.13 s window left no sample at all on a 2-CPU isolated
        # runner (the worker's first tick pays for lazy imports), so the release
        # acceptance flaked by position in the run. The assertions only need
        # the first two samples; a wider window changes nothing else.
        end=asyncio.get_running_loop().time()+1.0
        await worker.run_async_worker(slots=24,should_continue=lambda:asyncio.get_running_loop().time()<end,sample_seconds=.03)
    asyncio.run(scenario())
    assert observations and observations[0]['completed']>0
    # Admission now starts at the validated ceiling (24) and halves on pressure.
    assert resized[:2]==[12,6]
    assert all(x['provider_errors']==0 for x in observations)


def test_single_slow_phase_holds_but_two_consecutive_cut():
    from app.adaptive_admission import AdmissionPolicy, Sample
    p = AdmissionPolicy(ceiling=60, initial=10)
    # run 367: one cold 13 s anchor preparation with an idle host
    assert p.observe(Sample(200, 20, 38, 12000, 13.3)) == 10
    assert p.reason == 'phase_watch'
    # a healthy sample resets the strike; growth stays possible
    assert p.observe(Sample(200, 20, 38, 12000, .5)) == 20
    assert p.observe(Sample(200, 20, 38, 12000, 9.0)) == 20
    assert p.observe(Sample(200, 20, 38, 12000, 10.0)) == 10
    assert p.reason == 'local_resource_pressure'
    # CPU/memory pressure still cuts on the first sample
    q = AdmissionPolicy(ceiling=60, initial=10)
    assert q.observe(Sample(200, 20, 90, 12000, .1)) == 5


def test_idle_host_recovers_in_double_steps():
    from app.adaptive_admission import AdmissionPolicy, Sample
    p = AdmissionPolicy(ceiling=60, initial=12)
    assert p.observe(Sample(200, 20, 12, 12000, .5)) == 24
    assert p.observe(Sample(200, 20, 12, 12000, .5)) == 48
    assert p.observe(Sample(200, 20, 12, 12000, .5)) == 60
    assert p.reason == 'validated_ceiling'
    q = AdmissionPolicy(ceiling=60, initial=12)
    assert q.observe(Sample(200, 20, 60, 12000, .5)) == 18


def test_burst_contention_phase_under_eight_seconds_does_not_cut():
    from app.adaptive_admission import AdmissionPolicy, Sample
    p = AdmissionPolicy(ceiling=60, initial=10)
    # run 369 burst start: 6.3 s then 5.0 s phases with the host 40 % busy
    assert p.observe(Sample(200, 20, 40, 12000, 6.3)) == 10
    assert p.reason == 'hold_for_headroom'
    assert p.observe(Sample(200, 20, 40, 12000, 5.0)) == 10
    assert p.observe(Sample(200, 20, 40, 12000, 0.5)) == 20
    assert p.reason == 'backlog_with_headroom'
